### [1] [Coupling Evaporation, Chemical Reactions, and Crystallization: A Population-Balance Framework Applied to Two-Step Perovskite Crystallization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26591)
*K. Segadlo,O. J. J. Ronsin,J. Harting*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个简化的零维种群平衡模型（zero-dimensional population-balance model），耦合蒸发、化学反应与结晶过程，用于解析蒸发诱导的两步结晶路径，并给出定量LaMer曲线与"供给限制/速率限制"的分析框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对蒸发与结晶路径耦合机制的认识有限，阻碍了对溶液法制备钙钛矿光活性层（perovskite photoactive layers）结晶过程的调控；明确这些机制并学会选择与调节它们，是实现目标晶体形貌、进而提升太阳能电池性能与稳定性的关键。

Method: 构建一个简单且通用的零维种群平衡模型，将蒸发、化学反应与结晶三者耦合；针对遵循两步结晶路径（溶剂蒸发先诱导结晶性前驱体形成，随后再结晶为最终相）的溶剂-溶质体系，在参数空间中进行探索。

Result: 模型可预测干燥过程中薄膜组成的演化，并提供定量的LaMer曲线；据此提出"供给限制（supply limitations）"与"速率限制（rate limitations）"的术语体系，用以从主导工艺条件的角度解释结晶动力学。

Conclusion: 该零维种群平衡模型为研究蒸发薄膜中的结晶路径提供了简单通用的工具，其"供给限制/速率限制"框架有助于将结晶动力学与可控工艺条件相联系，从而服务于钙钛矿薄膜形貌的理性调控。

Abstract: A limited understanding of how evaporation couples to crystallization pathways hampers control over the formation of solution-processed perovskite photoactive layers. Identifying these mechanisms-and learning how to select and tune them-is essential for achieving the desired crystal morphology and, ultimately, improving solar-cell performance and stability. To investigate crystallization pathways in evaporating thin films, we develop a simple and general zero-dimensional population-balance model that couples evaporation, chemical reactions, and crystallization. We use the model to explore a range of parameters for a solvent-solute system that follows a two-step crystallization pathway: solvent evaporation first induces formation of a crystalline precursor, which subsequently recrystallizes into the final phase. The model predicts the evolution of the film composition during drying and provides quantitative LaMer curves. Based on these results, we introduce a vocabulary of "supply limitations" and "rate limitations" that enables the interpretation of crystallization kinetics in terms of the governing process conditions.

</details>


### [2] [Challenging Atomistic Foundation Models on Disordered Carbons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26701)
*Joseph Hart,Samuel G. H. Brookes,Fabian L. Thiemann,Christoph Schran*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该工作以跨相态、不同杂化方式及多孔骨架的纯碳与含O/H无序碳为测试体系，对六种主流基础机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potentials, MLIPs）进行压力测试；发现低DFT能量与力误差不足以揭示非物理外推与动力学失效，MACE-MH-1综合最稳健，且训练覆盖度（如OMAT系列共享结构偏差）至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础机器学习原子间势宣称具备广泛可迁移性（transferability），但其域外（out-of-domain）可靠性难以确立；现有评估多依赖密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）能量与力误差，缺乏对实际分子动力学行为的检验，故需针对复杂无序碳体系开展应用导向的验证。

Method: 选取覆盖多相态、竞争性杂化（competing hybridisations）与多孔骨架的纯碳及含O/H无序碳体系；先以DFT能量、力误差及结合曲线（binding curves）对六种基础MLIPs进行初筛，再对其中四种开展熔化—淬火—退火（melt-quench-anneal）分子动力学模拟并评估所得结构可观测量。

Result: 结合曲线与含O/H轨迹暴露出低DFT误差无法反映的非物理外推（unphysical extrapolation）与动力学失效；所测模型中MACE-MH-1最为稳健，兼具低DFT误差与稳定MD。基于OMAT的模型虽架构不同却共享结构偏差，说明训练覆盖度的重要性。四种模型生成的纳米多孔碳结构均再现了主要实验特征与专用碳势的特征信号；引入O/H后短程有序（short-range order）基本不变，但孔径分布（pore-size distributions）向大孔方向移动。

Conclusion: 结果表明基础MLIPs需针对具体应用开展验证，单一误差指标不足以判定可靠性；同时将纳米多孔碳的分子动力学建模推进至更接近实验真实的结构。

Abstract: Foundation machine-learned interatomic potentials (MLIPs) promise broad transferability, yet their out-of-domain reliability is hard to establish. Here, we use pure and O/H-containing disordered carbon spanning multiple phases, competing hybridisations and porous frameworks to stress-test six popular foundation MLIPs. We first screen all six on density functional theory (DFT) energy and force errors and binding curves, then subject four to melt-quench-anneal molecular dynamics and evaluate the resulting structural observables. Binding curves and O/H-containing trajectories reveal unphysical extrapolation and dynamical failures that low DFT energy and force errors fail to reveal. Of the models tested, MACE-MH-1 is the most consistently robust, with low DFT errors and stable MD. Despite differing architectures, OMAT-based models share structural biases, highlighting the importance of training coverage. All four models generate structures of nanoporous carbon that reproduce the principal experimental and specialised carbon potential signatures. Incorporating O/H leaves short-range order largely unchanged but shifts pore-size distributions towards larger pores. These results emphasise the need for application-specific validation and extend molecular dynamics modelling of nanoporous carbon towards experimentally realistic structures.

</details>

### [3] [Multimessenger Probes of Primordial Black Holes with Gravitational Wave and 21 cm Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24067)
*Leah Jenks*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文指出，原初黑洞（primordial black holes, PBHs）蒸发引起的21厘米全局信号（21 cm global signal）改变，与同一标量扰动（scalar perturbations）产生的引力波（gravitational waves）信号之间存在互补性，二者可共同构成原初物理的多信使探针（multimessenger probe）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初黑洞由早期宇宙曲率扰动（curvature perturbations）坍缩形成，其形成机制本身会产生引力波，而黑洞蒸发又会改变宇宙暗 ages 期间的21厘米信号，两类观测彼此独立，因而存在联合探测的动机。

Method: 文章基于原初黑洞蒸发效应分析21厘米全局信号的改变，并将其与同源标量扰动产生的引力波信号在频率与红移范围上作对应比较，评估两类观测的互补性（complementarity）。

Result: 对质量范围 $M_{\rm PBH}\sim 5\times10^{13}\,{\rm g}-10^{17}\,{\rm g}$ 的原初黑洞，引力波信号覆盖 $f_{\rm GW}\sim 10^{-3}\,{\rm Hz}-20\,{\rm Hz}$，涉及LISA、deci-Hz及地面探测频段；对应的21厘米全局信号出现在 $z\sim 300-30$。

Conclusion: 两类观测完全独立且均在近未来实验能力范围内，因此联合测量可作为探索原初物理的新型多信使探针。

Abstract: Primordial black holes (PBHs) formed from the collapse of curvature perturbations in the early universe are known to have an associated gravitational wave signal sourced by the same scalar perturbations. In this work, we show that there is a complementarity between these gravitational waves and modifications of the 21 cm global signal during the cosmic dark ages due to PBH evaporation. For PBHs in the range $ M_{\rm PBH} \sim 5 \times 10^{13} \, {\rm g} - 10^{17} \, {\rm g}$, there is a correlated signal in gravitational waves in the LISA, deci-Hz, and ground based regimes from $f_{\rm GW} \sim 10^{-3} \, {\rm Hz} - 20 \, {\rm Hz}$ and in the 21 cm global signal from $z \sim 300 - 30$. These observations are entirely independent of each other and within the reach of near-future experiments, making a combined measurement a novel multimessenger probe of primordial physics.

</details>


### [4] [Gravitational Instability of Cold Axion Dark Matter Beyond Minimal Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25984)
*Bryan Cordero-Patino,Álvaro Duenas-Vidal,Jorge Segovia*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文构建了与引力非最小耦合的轴子冷暗物质模型，并在流体力学极限下评估其引力不稳定性，为解释JWST高红移大质量星系观测提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 詹姆斯·韦布空间望远镜（James Webb Space Telescope, JWST）在高红移处观测到出乎意料的大质量星系，促使学界重新审视暗物质（Dark Matter, DM）模型，特别是暗物质与引力的耦合方式，这已成为宇宙学（Cosmology）的核心议题。

Method: 构建一个与引力存在非最小耦合（non-minimal coupling）的轴子（axion）冷暗物质（Cold Dark Matter, CDM）模型，并在此框架下于流体力学极限（hydrodynamic limit）中评估引力不稳定性（gravitational instability）。

Result: 结果表明，引力不稳定性依赖于非最小耦合参数，从而提供了一种与JWST高红移观测相对齐的新工具。

Conclusion: 在轴子冷暗物质框架中引入引力的非最小耦合，可通过调节耦合参数影响结构形成的引力不稳定性，为协调高红移大质量星系观测与暗物质模型提供可行方向。

Abstract: Following recent observations of unexpectedly massive galaxies at high redshift by the James Webb Space Telescope, the revision of Dark Matter (DM) models --specifically regarding how DM couples to gravity-- has become a central topic in Cosmology. In this paper, we develop an axion model for Cold Dark Matter (CDM) with a non-minimal coupling to gravity. From this framework, we evaluate the gravitational instability in the hydrodynamic limit. Our results demonstrate how gravitational instabilities depend on the non-minimal coupling parameter, providing a novel tool for aligning with the high-redshift observations from the James Webb Space Telescope.

</details>


### [5] [Beyond dust in local cosmography: perturbative Vlasov predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25145)
*Chris Clarkson,Roy Maartens*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 宇宙学（cosmography）中常用的尘埃近似（dust approximation）忽略了结构形成过程中的速度弥散，该弥散产生的压强梯度会在局域距离-红移关系（distance-redshift relation）中引入本征偶极（intrinsic dipole）。本文用线性 Vlasov 微扰理论（linear Vlasov perturbation theory）计算该偶极、膨胀（expansion）与剪切（shear）的相应变化，结果表明其远小于观测到的低红移偶极，后者近观测者行为由物质系与 CMB 系之间的相对运动主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尘埃近似在宇宙学制图（cosmography）中忽略了结构形成产生的速度弥散（velocity dispersion），而该弥散导致各向同性与各向异性压强（isotropic and anisotropic pressure），其梯度即使粒子沿测地线（geodesic）运动也会加速平均物质流。因此有必要评估该效应对局域距离-红移关系的影响。

Method: 在 FLRW 背景（FLRW background）上采用线性 Vlasov 微扰理论，并以基于暗晕估计（halo estimates）的平均弥散历史作为输入，计算本征偶极以及膨胀与剪切的相应改变；同时考虑压强对结构增长的修正，及其对剪切和空间梯度的影响。

Result: 在最低阶红移展开中，最大修正出现在剪切项；而在下一阶，膨胀梯度（expansion gradient）的变化可能产生更大的偶极。整体而言，所得本征偶极远小于观测到的低红移偶极，后者在近观测者处的行为主要由物质系与 CMB 系之间的相对运动决定。

Conclusion: 压强效应可在局域距离-红移关系中产生本征偶极并修正剪切与膨胀梯度，但量级不足以解释观测到的低红移偶极。此外，所假设的 FLRW 背景使宇宙学原理（cosmological principle）无法被独立检验，数值预言亦依赖于所采用的 LCDM 宇宙学模型。

Abstract: The dust approximation used in cosmography neglects the velocity dispersion generated during structure formation. This dispersion produces isotropic and anisotropic pressure, whose gradients accelerate the mean matter flow even though individual particles follow geodesics. The acceleration introduces an intrinsic dipole into the local distance-redshift relation. We calculate this dipole and the accompanying changes in expansion and shear using linear Vlasov perturbation theory about an FLRW background, with a mean dispersion history motivated by halo estimates. The assumed FLRW background prevents an independent test of the cosmological principle; the numerical predictions also depend on the adopted LCDM cosmology. Pressure also changes the growth of structure, modifying the shear and the spatial gradients that govern how the distance anisotropy varies with redshift. The largest correction at the lowest order in redshift is to the shear, while changes in the expansion gradient can produce a larger dipole at the next order. The resulting intrinsic dipole is far smaller than the observed low-redshift dipole, whose behaviour nearest the observer is dominated by motion between the matter and CMB frames.

</details>


### [6] [Mean-Field Backreaction in Single-Field Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26669)
*Matteo Braglia,Gabriele Franciolini,Nadir Ijaz,Marco Peloso*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文以半解析的平均场方法研究小尺度功率增强的单场暴胀模型中标量反作用，指出Bunch–Davies真空紫外模式会显著污染反作用估计，除非采用绝热减除；结果可重现格点计算，但在USR与OF模型中可靠性不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期关于小尺度增强功率的单场暴胀模型中，线性微扰理论的有效性及反作用（backreaction）的作用存在争论，需要澄清反作用估计是否稳健。

Method: 在解耦极限（decoupling limit）下采用平均场（mean-field）框架半解析地计算标量反作用，考察含瞬态超慢滚（ultra-slow-roll, USR）阶段与势能振荡特征（oscillatory feature, OF）的两类流行模型，并比较绝热减除（adiabatic subtraction）对紫外（UV）模式贡献的影响。

Result: Bunch–Davies真空中的紫外模式会显著影响反作用估计，除非被绝热减除移除；平均场处理可重现近期格点（lattice）结果，但在某些情形下显露出对UV贡献的虚假敏感性。OF模型中大量粒子产生使放大的物理模式与UV真空尾部分离，该敏感性降低，格点结果更可靠。

Conclusion: 在USR情形下，经绝热减除的谱给出的背景演化反作用比此前推断的更温和；格点结果的可靠性依赖于UV贡献是否被恰当处理，且在OF模型中更可信。

Abstract: The validity of linear perturbation theory and the role of backreaction in single-field inflationary models that enhance power at small scales have recently been under debate. We investigate scalar backreaction semi-analytically in a mean-field approach within the decoupling limit, considering popular models with a transient ultra-slow-roll (USR) stage or an oscillatory feature (OF) in the potential. We find that ultraviolet (UV) modes in the Bunch--Davies vacuum can substantially affect backreaction estimates unless removed by adiabatic subtraction. Our mean-field treatment can reproduce recent lattice results, but reveals a spurious sensitivity to UV contributions in some cases. This sensitivity is reduced in the OF model when copious particle production separates the amplified physical modes from the UV vacuum tail, making lattice results more reliable in this regime. In USR, instead, adiabatically subtracted spectra yield milder backreaction on the background evolution than previously inferred.

</details>


### [7] [Cosmological implications of two mock TianQin standard siren catalogs generated under different background cosmological models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26727)
*Jun-Xian Li,Shuang Wang,Liang-Gui Zhu*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本研究定量考察背景宇宙学模型选择对天琴（TianQin）模拟标准汽笛（standard siren）星表及其宇宙学推论的影响。结果表明两套星表差异很小（距离-红移关系最大相对差 1.48%），但基于不同模型生成的星表在联合观测下对动力学暗能量（dynamical dark energy）的偏好程度不同（分别为 2.3σ 与 3.8σ）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往天琴标准汽笛研究均采用 ΛCDM 模型生成模拟数据，背景模型选择对星表及暗能量（dark energy, DE）推论的影响尚未被定量评估，本文即针对这一空白展开分析。

Method: 分别采用 ΛCDM 模型与红移分箱暗能量重构模型（wbin 模型）生成两套模拟天琴标准汽笛星表 TQSS1 与 TQSS2，源为大质量黑洞双星并合产生的亮标准汽笛；在 Chevallier-Polarski-Linder（CPL）模型中单独及联合 DESI DR2 重子声学振荡（BAO）、Planck 2018 宇宙微波背景（CMB）与 DESY5 Ia 型超新星（SN Ia）数据分析其宇宙学推论。

Result: 两套星表差异较小，距离-红移关系最大相对差为 1.48%；单独使用任一星表给出的宇宙学约束与超新星数据相近，可作为主流观测的补充；TQSS1+DESI DR2+CMB+DESY5 与 TQSS2+DESI DR2+CMB+DESY5 仍偏好动力学暗能量，置信度分别为 2.3σ 与 3.8σ；采用不同大质量黑洞种子模型不影响星表的宇宙学推论。

Conclusion: 模拟标准汽笛星表本身差异有限，但其对动力学暗能量的偏好程度依赖于生成星表所采用的背景宇宙学模型；因此在暗能量相关的标准汽笛预测研究中考虑不同宇宙学模型具有重要性。

Abstract: Space-borne gravitational wave (GW) detectors such as TianQin are expected to detect GW standard sirens for cosmological parameter estimation. All previous studies of TianQin adopted the $Λ$CDM model to generate mock standard siren data. In this work, we quantitatively investigate how the choice of background cosmological model affects mock TianQin standard siren (TQSS) catalogs and the corresponding cosmological implications. Adopting the $Λ$CDM model and a redshift-binned dark energy (DE) reconstruction model (the wbin model), we consider the bright standard sirens from massive black hole binary mergers and generate two mock TQSS catalogs, i.e. TQSS1 and TQSS2, respectively. We investigate the difference of the catalogs and further explore their cosmological implications within the Chevallier-Polarski-Linder (CPL) model, both individually and in combination with DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) data, $\textit{Planck}$ 2018 cosmic microwave background (CMB) data, and DESY5 type Ia supernova (SN Ia) data. We find that there are only small differences between TQSS1 and TQSS2 catalogs, with a maximum relative difference of $1.48\%$ in the distance-redshift relation. In addition, either the TQSS1 or TQSS2 catalog alone gives cosmological constraints similar to those obtained from SN data, implying that these two catalogs can serve as a complement to mainstream cosmological observations. Most importantly, we find that both TQSS1+DESI DR2+CMB+DESY5 and TQSS2+DESI DR2+CMB+DESY5 are still expected to favor dynamical DE, with these two combinations showing a preference for dynamical DE at $2.3σ$ and $3.8σ$, respectively. Adopting different massive black hole seed models does not impact the cosmological implications of the mock TQSS catalogs. Our study indicates the importance of considering different cosmological models in the standard siren forecasts for DE.

</details>

### [8] [A vertex operator algebra for the S-fold realisation of a non-unitary Haagerup--Izumi modular fusion category](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26341)
*David E Evans*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 论证 Flohr 提出的强有理顶点算子代数（strongly rational vertex operator algebra）$V_{11}$ 存在，并刻画其表示范畴的辫子张量结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $V_{11}$ 由 Flohr 提出，但其存在性及表示范畴的精确结构此前未获确立，需要给出严格构造与范畴等价证明。

Method: 构造强有理顶点算子代数 $V_{11}$，并利用辫子范畴（braided category）的分解工具，将其表示范畴表示为半离子范畴（semion category）与 Ostrik 扭曲 Haagerup 范畴（twisted Haagerup category）经 Galois 自同构 $\sigma_2:\zeta_{117}\mapsto\zeta_{117}^2$ 作用后的 Drinfeld 中心（$\mathcal{Z}$）的张量积。

Result: 得到辫子范畴等价 $\mathrm{Rep}\,V_{11}\simeq \mathrm{Sem}\otimes\mathcal{Z}(\sigma_2\mathcal{F})$，其中 $\mathrm{Sem}$ 取生成元维数为 $-1$ 的球面结构（spherical structure）。

Conclusion: $V_{11}$ 确实存在且为强有理，其表示范畴可完全由已知范畴（Sem 与 $\mathcal{Z}(\sigma_2\mathcal{F})$）的张量积描述，为相关有理共形场论（rational conformal field theory）的分类提供依据。

Abstract: We show that there exists a strongly rational vertex operator algebra $V_{11}$, proposed by Flohr, whose representation theory $\mathrm{Rep}\,V_{11}\simeq\mathrm{Sem}\otimes\mathcal Z(σ_2\mathcal F)$ as braided categories. Here $\mathrm{Sem}$ is the semion category (with the spherical structure in which its generator has dimension $-1$), $\mathcal F$ is Ostrik's twisted Haagerup category and $σ_2$ is the Galois automorphism $ζ_{117}\mapstoζ_{117}^2$.

</details>

### [9] [Gate-defined quantum dot and charge noise analysis in a Forksheet transistor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24136)
*Dominic Leclerc,Claude Rohrbacher,Alexis Morel,Joffrey Rivard,Romain Ritzenthaler,Christian Lupien,Hans Mertens,Naoto Horiguchi,Eva Dupont-Ferrier*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 14纳米nMOS叉片晶体管（Forksheet transistor）在低至10 mK下仍保持晶体管工作特性，并可栅控形成单电子区量子点（quantum dot），其低频电荷噪声（charge noise）平均幅度约10 μeV/√Hz（1 Hz），最低3.3 μeV/√Hz，显示叉片工艺在量子-经典CMOS单片共集成方面的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向未来量子处理器（quantum processor）中量子功能与经典CMOS功能的单片共集成（monolithic cointegration）需求，需要评估先进CMOS器件在深低温下的可用性及作为量子点的噪声性能。

Method: 对14 nm nMOS叉片晶体管进行低至10 mK的低温电学表征，验证其晶体管工作特性，并将其调谐至单电子区（single-electron regime）形成栅定义量子点；在不同电荷占据下开展低频电荷噪声测量。

Result: 器件在低温下保持晶体管工作且特性有所改善；量子点区测得1 Hz处平均电荷噪声幅度⟨S₀⟩ = 10 μeV/√Hz，最低测量值为3.3 μeV/√Hz。

Conclusion: 叉片技术（Forksheet technology）兼具低温晶体管功能与可调量子点及较低电荷噪声，具备在未来量子处理器中实现量子与经典CMOS功能单片共集成的潜力。

Abstract: We report the cryogenic characterization of a 14-nm nMOS Forksheet transistor down to 10 mK. We demonstrate that the device retains transistor operation with improved characteristics at cryogenic temperatures, while also forming a gate-defined quantum dot that can be tuned to the single-electron regime. In the quantum-dot regime, we perform low-frequency charge-noise measurements across different charge occupations and observe an average charge-noise amplitude of $\langle S_0\rangle = 10\;μ\mathrm{eV}/\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$ at 1 Hz, with a minimum measured value of $3.3\;μ\mathrm{eV}/\sqrt{\mathrm{Hz}}$. These results highlight the potential of Forksheet technology for the monolithic cointegration of quantum and classical CMOS functionalities in future quantum processors.

</details>


### [10] [Boundary Element Modeling for THz-SNOM Near-Field Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24151)
*Noah Reich,Richard Kim,Thomas Koschny,Jigang Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文建立了一种边界元法（boundary element method, BEM），用于模拟导电原子力显微镜（atomic force microscopy, AFM）针尖在外加背景场下的静电场近场响应，并以远少的网格单元高精度复现了COMSOL三维准静态（quasi-static）模拟的场衰减行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太赫兹扫描近场光学显微镜（terahertz scanning near-field optical microscopy, THz-SNOM）的近场响应对针尖几何高度敏感，因此需要理解几何参数如何控制场的局域与衰减，以面向特定实验的空间分辨率需求选择针尖设计；而系统研究该依赖关系需要准确且计算高效的建模手段。

Method: 采用边界元法（BEM）建立模型：将针尖简化为封闭的钝锥（blunt-cone）几何，表面离散为面板并在顶点（apex）附近加密网格；用修正的格林函数（Green's function）计算面板间相互作用，并通过增广线性系统（augmented linear system）同时施加导体表面的等势边界条件与整体电荷中性条件，以求解感应表面电荷分布。

Result: 将BEM得到的近场衰减与三维准静态COMSOL Multiphysics模拟结果对比，BEM准确重现了COMSOL的场衰减行为，同时所需网格单元少若干数量级，显著降低了计算成本。

Conclusion: BEM为系统研究THz近场局域（near-field confinement）对AFM针尖几何的依赖提供了准确且计算高效的框架；该框架可推广至更复杂、更贴近实验的针尖几何，为实现面向应用特定空间分辨率需求的理性针尖选择与设计提供途径。

Abstract: Terahertz scanning near-field optical microscopy (THz-SNOM) enables spatially and spectrally resolved investigation of low-energy collective and topological states in quantum materials and nano-structured devices by exploiting the strongly localized near field generated by a polarized atomic force microscopy (AFM) tip. Because the THz near-field response is highly sensitive to tip geometry, understanding how geometric parameters control field confinement and decay is essential for selecting tip designs that provide the spatial resolution required for specific experiments. In this work, we develop a boundary element method (BEM) for modeling the electrostatic near-field of a conducting AFM tip in response to an external background field. The tip is represented by a simplified closed, blunt-cone geometry with its surface discretized into panels using increased mesh density near the apex. A modified Green's function is used to calculate interactions between surface panels, while an augmented linear system simultaneously enforces the equipotential boundary condition on the conducting surface and overall charge neutrality to determine the induced surface-charge distribution. We validate the BEM by comparing the resulting near-field decay with that obtained from a three-dimensional quasi-static COMSOL Multiphysics simulation. The BEM accurately reproduces the COMSOL field-decay behavior while requiring orders of magnitude fewer mesh elements and substantially reducing computational cost. These results demonstrate that the BEM provides an accurate and computationally efficient framework for systematically investigating the dependence of THz near-field confinement on AFM tip geometry. This framework can be extended to more complex and experimentally realistic tip geometries, providing a route toward rational tip selection and design for application-specific spatial-resolution requirements.

</details>


### [11] [A Chiral Superlattice Route to Spin-Split Topological Antiferromagnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24242)
*Thao Dinh,Mengke Liu,Jian-Xiang Qiu,Sougata Mardanya,Suk Hyun Sung,Xuan Hoang Le,Christopher Broyles,Chengfeng Zhu,Qiaozhi Xu,Haotian Chen,Zack Rehfuss,Yu-Fei Liu,Xiaoyu Zeng,Houchen Li,Peng Guo,Jinchen Liu,Tianye Huang,Jingtian Shi,Michael Smith,Joanna M. Blawat,John Singleton,Ross McDonald,Ivan E. Arvizo,Dongtao Cui,Shao-Liang Zheng,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Vineet Kumar Sharma,Sudip Ghorai,Barun Ghosh,Hsin Lin,Ting Yong Lim,Tay-Rong Chang,Arun Bansil,Ivar Martin,Ashvin Vishwanath,Weiwei Xie,Claudia Felser,Jairo Sinova,Anyuan Gao,Mikhail D. Lukin,Jennifer E. Hoffman,Sugata Chowdhury,Qiong Ma,Kai Sun,Ismail El Baggari,Sheng Ran,Talieh S. Ghiasi,Hongkun Park,Philip Kim,Su-Yang Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文在共线反铁磁体（collinear antiferromagnet）UOTe 中发现可自发形成的手性超晶格（chiral superlattice），后者等效于有限波矢下冻结的手性声子，通过调制轨道布洛赫波函数（orbital Bloch wavefunction）与量子几何（quantum geometry）产生大贝里曲率（Berry curvature），并与共线反铁磁序耦合，实现巨大反常霍尔角与自旋极化电流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性（chirality）与磁性（magnetism）的相互作用横跨粒子物理、凝聚态、化学与生命起源等领域。原始 UOTe 为无贝里曲率、无自旋劈裂能带的共线反铁磁体，缺乏产生自旋劈裂拓扑现象的内禀机制，本文旨在探索新的实现路径。

Method: 识别 UOTe 中手性超晶格的自发形成并视其为有限波矢冻结的手性声子；通过非线性霍尔效应（nonlinear Hall effect）探测手性超晶格诱导的贝里曲率，利用自旋汉勒进动（spin Hanle precession）测量检验自旋极化电流，并结合化学离子尺寸与层间-层内能量竞争分析超调制形成机制。

Result: 手性超晶格势强烈调制电子的轨道布洛赫波函数与量子几何，产生可被非线性霍尔效应探测的大贝里曲率；在 150 K 附近、接近奈尔温度（$T_N$）处，该贝里曲率与共线反铁磁序耦合，产生约 0.14 的巨大反常霍尔角（anomalous Hall angle），属块体磁体中最大者之一；自旋汉勒进动测量表明手性超晶格可从共线反铁磁体产生自旋极化电流。

Conclusion: 研究基于化学离子尺寸与物理的层间-层内能量竞争阐明了超调制的形成机制，并提出可发现类似键失配超晶格（bond-mismatch superlattice）的设计原则，给出通过实空间量子几何工程实现自旋劈裂拓扑现象的手性超晶格路径。

Abstract: The interplay between chirality and magnetism is a profound topic, spanning particle physics, condensed matter, chemistry and origin of life. In this paper, we study this interplay in the new antiferromagnet (AFM) UOTe, and we report a chiral superlattice route to spin-split topological phenomena from collinear antiferromagnetism. Pristine UOTe is a collinear AFM without Berry curvature or spin-split bands. However, we identify the spontaneous formation of a chiral superlattice in UOTe, which can be viewed as frozen chiral phonons at finite wave vector. When electrons move through the chiral superlattice, their orbital Bloch wavefunction and quantum geometry are modulated by the strong chiral superlattice potential, generating large Berry curvature in the chiral superlattice, which we detect by the nonlinear Hall effect. At 150 K, the chiral superlattice induced Berry curvature couples to the collinear AFM order, generating a giant anomalous Hall angle ~0.14 abruptly near $T_N$ that is among the largest in bulk magnets. Moreover, our spin Hanle precession measurement shows that the chiral superlattice also generates spin-polarized current from the collinear AFM, a long-standing goal in spintronics. We shed light on the supermodulation formation mechanism based on chemical ion size and physical interlayer-intralayer energy competitions, using which we propose a design principle to discover similar bond-mismatch superlattices, providing a chiral superlattice pathway through real space engineering of quantum geometry.

</details>


### [12] [Water-Driven Porous Silicon as a Platform for Blue Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24460)
*Marc Thelen,Julia Brandt,Stella Gries,Manuel Brinker,Alexander Petrov,Patrick Huber*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该观点文章（perspective）提出多孔硅（porous silicon）可作为一种能够像生物体一样"控制水"的工程材料，并给出三条发展方向：孔隙层级化、表面改性与限域水（confined water）性质调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活体生物通过控制水来输送物质、传递能量并驱动化学反应，而现有工程材料尚缺乏对水的类似精细调控能力；多孔硅在孔隙结构、表面化学与半导体骨架上的可设计性，使其具备填补这一空白的潜力。

Method: 以观点/展望（perspective）形式，基于多孔硅的孔隙结构、表面化学与半导体骨架可在多尺度上按半导体工艺精度加工的特性，归纳其与水相互作用产生的多种效应，并提出三条整合与拓展路径。

Result: 多孔硅中的液体（多为水）可在数纳米宽的孔隙中以连续介质方式流动，并通过毛细管应力（capillary stress）与电吸附应力（electrosorption stress）使骨架产生应变，进而改变结构色（structural color）、切换透明性，以及调制或产生电信号。

Conclusion: 三条方向为：孔隙层级（pore hierarchy）可缓解界面面积与传输速度之间的经典权衡，并支持从平面薄膜到三维构件的功能放大；表面修饰成为可按位置设定、并可借助可切换涂层按需调节的设计变量；仅数分子宽的孔隙会改变水本身的性质，使其在缺乏类体相体积时呈现介电各向异性（dielectrically anisotropic）与高电导。由此，多孔硅有望从响应性材料（responsive material）转变为自适应、水驱动的材料（adaptive, water-driven material）。

Abstract: Living organisms control water to transport matter, transfer energy and drive chemical reactions. In this perspective, we argue that porous silicon can give engineering materials comparable control over water. Its pore architecture, surface chemistry and semiconducting skeleton can be shaped across length scales with the precision of semiconductor processing and, like the water it can host, it relies on one of the most abundant chemical elements on Earth. Liquids in porous silicon, most often water, can flow as a continuum through pores a few nanometers wide, strain the skeleton through capillary and electrosorption stresses, shift structural color, switch transparency, and modulate or generate electrical signals. We describe three directions to combine and extend these effects and overcome their limitations. Pore hierarchy resolves the classical trade-off between interface area and transport speed by combining large and small pores and thereby allows functions to be scaled from thin planar films to three-dimensional components. Surface modification becomes a design variable that tunes the interaction between water and the pore wall. It can be set locally and, with switchable coatings, on demand. Finally, pores only a few molecules wide alter the properties of water itself. In the absence of a bulk-like volume, water can become dielectrically anisotropic and highly conductive. Altogether, these steps turn porous silicon from a responsive material into an adaptive, water-driven material.

</details>


### [13] [Turning Number and the Geometry of Disorder-Induced Topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24637)
*Lars Emmrich,Michael Potthoff*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出以动量空间哈密顿量映射（momentum-space Hamiltonian map）的几何不变性（如转折数（turning number））补充传统拓扑不变量，并在一维手征SSH模型（chiral Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model）中揭示由布鲁赫环（Bloch loop）切向量消失驱动的新相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑分类（topological classification）主要依赖标准拓扑不变量，难以刻画超越非相互作用干净极限（noninteracting clean limit）的量子系统；作者希望利用由零频单粒子格林函数（zero-frequency single-particle Green's function）导出的动量空间哈密顿量映射的几何结构，获得更精细的拓扑刻画。

Method: 以带无序（disorder）的一维手征Su-Schrieffer-Heeger模型为范式，其拓扑哈密顿量（topological Hamiltonian）定义了一条平面布鲁赫环（planar Bloch loop）；在常规绕数（winding number）之外引入刻画其切向量绕转的整数转折数（turning number），并结合渐近分析（asymptotic analysis）与数值计算。

Result: 识别出在拓扑哈密顿量仍保持有限且谱有能隙（spectrally gapped）时发生的转折数转变，其唯一驱动因素是布鲁赫环切向量的消失；由此得到在绕数分类之外还包含额外拓扑结构的扩展相图（extended phase diagram），渐近分析对相应转变区间的出现及其无序依赖的预测与数值结果一致。

Conclusion: 动量空间哈密顿量映射的几何可作为超出常规分类的额外拓扑结构来源，为量子系统的精细拓扑表征提供了新途径。

Abstract: The geometry of momentum-space Hamiltonian maps derived from the zero-frequency single-particle Green's function provides a route to a refined topological characterization of quantum systems beyond the noninteracting clean limit. The central idea is to complement standard topological invariants by invariants that characterize the geometry of the map itself. As a paradigmatic example, we study the one-dimensional chiral Su-Schrieffer-Heeger model with disorder, where the topological Hamiltonian defines a planar Bloch loop. In addition to its conventional winding number, this loop possesses an integer turning number that characterizes the winding of its tangent vector. We identify turning-number transitions that occur while the topological Hamiltonian remains finite and spectrally gapped, driven solely by a vanishing tangent vector of the Bloch loop, and obtain an extended phase diagram with additional topological structure beyond the winding-number classification. Asymptotic analysis predicts the emergence and disorder dependence of the corresponding transition regime, in agreement with the numerical results. Our findings establish the geometry of momentum-space Hamiltonian maps as an additional source of topological structure beyond conventional classifications.

</details>


### [14] [Topological Splitting of Sondheimer Oscillations in Thin-Film Weyl Semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24967)
*Toshihito Osada*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文基于玻尔兹曼输运理论研究拓扑效应对外尔半金属薄膜磁阻中Sondheimer振荡的影响，发现贝里曲率与轨道磁矩使振荡分裂为两支并产生频率随磁场变化的啁啾特征，机制源于轨道-塞曼效应导致的费米面形变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sondheimer振荡源于薄膜厚度与回旋轨道螺距的匹配（commensurability），但拓扑能带中的贝里曲率（Berry curvature）与轨道磁矩（orbital magnetic moment）对该振荡的影响尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用玻尔兹曼输运理论（Boltzmann transport theory），研究贝里曲率与轨道磁矩对薄膜外尔半金属（Weyl semimetal）磁阻中Sondheimer振荡的修正作用。

Result: Sondheimer振荡分裂为两支具有不同频率的分支，且每支因频率依赖磁场而呈现啁啾（chirped）特征；这些特征源自轨道-塞曼效应（orbital-Zeeman effect）引起的费米面（Fermi surface）形变。

Conclusion: 拓扑效应显著改变Sondheimer振荡的频谱结构，且该机制同样适用于狄拉克半金属（Dirac semimetal），为拓扑半金属薄膜磁输运研究提供了可推广的判据。

Abstract: We discuss topological effects on Sondheimer oscillations in the magnetoresistance of thin-film Weyl semimetals. The Sondheimer oscillations are periodic in magnetic field and originate from the commensurability between the film thickness and the cyclotron-orbit pitch. Using Boltzmann transport theory, we investigate the effects of Berry curvature and orbital magnetic moment on the oscillations. We find that the Sondheimer oscillations split into two branches with different frequencies and that each branch becomes chirped because its frequency depends on magnetic field. These features originate from the orbital-Zeeman-induced deformation of the Fermi surface. The same mechanism also applies to Dirac semimetals.

</details>


### [15] [Unconventional spin momentum locking and topological domains in twisted slabs of topological insulator Bi$_2$Te$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25190)
*Thomas Naimer,Jaroslav Fabian*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 摘要报告：以第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）研究将 Bi₂Te₃ 薄板（slab）分成两半后，通过扭转、平移与层间距调控界面，探索其对拓扑狄拉克态（topological Dirac states）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维拓扑绝缘体（three-dimensional topological insulator）Bi₂Te₃ 表面狄拉克态具有自旋-动量锁定（spin-momentum locking），通常表现为面内 Rashba 型自旋织构（tangential Rashba-like spin texture）；通过分割薄板并修饰界面或可改变这些性质，其中扭转自由度（twisting degree of freedom）尤值关注。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），并行两条路线：一是考察不同堆叠构型（stacking configurations），以提取适用于小扭转角莫尔结构（small twist angle Moiré structures）的参数；二是计算公度扭转超胞（commensurate twisted supercells）的性质。

Result: 堆叠构型分析显示仅部分堆叠具有非平庸拓扑（non-trivial topology），从而在莫尔尺度（Moiré scale）上形成拓扑畴（topological domains）；公度扭转超胞计算则揭示出平带（flat bands，可经外部压力（external pressure）调节）以及具有非常规自旋-动量锁定（unconventional spin-momentum locking）的径向自旋织构（radial spin textures）。

Conclusion: 界面扭转、平移与层间距的改变可显著调控 Bi₂Te₃ 薄板的拓扑与自旋性质；扭转体系兼具可调平带与拓扑畴特征，为莫尔工程（Moiré engineering）下的拓扑与自旋物态设计提供依据（摘要未展开具体应用）。

Abstract: The three-dimensional topological insulator Bi$_2$Te$_3$ hosts topological Dirac states on its two-dimensional surfaces. These Dirac states show spin-momentum locking, which usually implies a tangential Rashba-like spin texture. One can alter the properties of the Dirac states by dividing a slab of such a material into two thinner halves, modifying the interface by twisting, shifting, and changing the interlayer distance. Using first-principles calculations, we investigate such modified slabs of Bi$_2$Te$_3$. The twisting degree of freedom deserves special attention. To this end, we follow two approaches. On the one hand, we probe different stacking configurations in order to extract parameters relevant for small twist angle Moiré structures. Indeed, we show that breaking parity and time-reversal symmetries using combined electric and magnetic fields unlocks nonstandard axion electrodynamics via the controllable activation of various tensorial ME couplings, thus, going beyond the standard axion picture. ### [17] [Chiral symmetry and the orbital susceptibility of disordered honeycomb flakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25555)
*B. Murzaliev*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在无中性蜂窝状薄片中，手征（子晶格）对称性使化学势在每个无序实现中都固定于最小能隙中心，从而使罕见的近简并可被无序利用；当无序能闭合该能隙且磁场仍能混合两个能级时，平均轨道磁化率（orbital susceptibility）随温度按 ln(1/T) 增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小尺寸无序颗粒的轨道磁化率由占据态与空态几乎相触的稀有样本主导，但在混沌颗粒中能级排斥（level repulsion）及化学势对齐要求压制了此类近简并，使平均磁化率保持有限。作者旨在指出手征对称性可去除后一条件，从而考察磁化率可能出现的低温发散。

Result: 平均轨道磁化率在低温 T 下按 ln(1/T) 增长；其是否发生取决于跃迁几何：紧凑六边形薄片上正的最近邻跃迁使能隙保持打开，而第三近邻跃迁允许能隙闭合。手征随机矩阵模型中对数前系数为 C_n=√(2/n)·Γ[(n+1)/2]/Γ(n/2)，接近 1；化学势失谐会截断该对数。

Conclusion: 手征（子晶格）对称性可使命运固定的化学势与无序诱导的近简并共存，导致轨道磁化率的对数温度依赖；该效应存在与否由跃迁几何决定，且对数发散在化学势失谐时被截断。

Abstract: The orbital susceptibility of a small disordered grain is dominated by rare samples in which an occupied and an empty level nearly touch. In a chaotic grain such near-degeneracies are suppressed by level repulsion and must also coincide with the chemical potential, so the mean susceptibility stays finite. In a neutral honeycomb flake, chiral (sublattice) symmetry removes the second condition: It places the chemical potential in the middle of the smallest gap, in every disorder realization. Here we show that the mean orbital susceptibility then grows as $\ln(1/T)$ at low temperature $T$ if disorder can close this gap while a magnetic field still mixes the two levels, and that the hopping geometry decides whether it can: Positive nearest-neighbor hopping on a compact hexagonal flake keeps the gap open, third-neighbor hopping allows it to close. In a chiral random-matrix model with $n$ level pairs the coefficient of the logarithm is $C_n=\sqrt{2/n}\,Γ[(n+1)/2]/Γ(n/2)$, close to unity, and a detuned chemical potential cuts the logarithm off.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在阐明磁场驱动下动量空间单极子（monopoles）的动力学，并为强关联拓扑材料（strongly correlated topological materials）中的输运理解提供统一框架。

Conclusion: 结果阐明了磁场驱动下动量空间单极子的动力学，并为理解强关联拓扑材料中的输运提供了分析框架。

Abstract: We illustrate the field-driven anomalous Hall engineering via the momentum-space motion of Weyl nodes using pyrochlore iridates under a [111] magnetic field as an example. Tracking the position of Weyl nodes during the spin rotation, we demonstrate macroscopic anomalous Hall conductivity variations are fundamentally driven by Weyl node motions by comparing the full multi-band Kubo formula with the geometric Weyl node separation. The discrepancy occurs only near a critical spin rotation angle, where several Weyl nodes come into close proximity. We reveal that the evolution of anomalous Hall conductivity under the magnetic field agrees with reported experiments in an order-of-magnitude and non-monotonicity. These results elucidate the field-driven dynamics of momentum-space monopoles and provide a framework for understanding transport in strongly correlated topological materials.

</details>


### [19] [Phase engineering of odd parity state in a quantum dot Josephson junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26015)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 调控量子点约瑟夫森结（quantum-dot Josephson junctions, QDJJs）中的费米子宇称（fermion parity）是获取新奇超导物理的途径，但通常采用电学调控；本文转而研究由相位驱动的宇称转变。

Abstract: Controlling fermion parity in quantum-dot Josephson junctions (QDJJs) enables access to novel superconducting physics. While parity transitions are usually tuned electrically, we study phase-induced transitions in a strongly coupled QDJJ using the superconducting phase difference across the dot terminals, denoted by $φ$. We find that this phase allows control over the ground state parity, with parity transitions observed as the Andreev bound states (ABSs) energies crossing zero. </details>

*Md Afsar Reja,Awadhesh Narayan*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交错磁体（altermagnets）兼具铁磁体与反铁磁体特征，其中动量依赖自旋劈裂遵循奇宇称形状因子的奇波磁体（odd-wave magnets）为研究非常规磁性与拓扑现象提供了新平台，但如何在其中直接调控拓扑与响应尚待探索。

Method: 采用双圆偏振光驱动奇宇称磁体，通过调节两束光的相对振幅比实现对外部光场的连续调控，并系统分析由此产生的拓扑相变与非线性霍尔响应，同时建立相应的选择定则（selection rules）。

Conclusion: 奇宇称磁体的拓扑性质与非线性响应均可由双圆偏振光驱动直接调控，表明该光场是一种有效的拓扑相与非互易响应调控手段。

We show that a cascade of topological phase transitions occur as the relative amplitude ratio of the two beams is varied. These are accompanied by sharp changes of the quantum metric. Our results show that both the topology and the nonlinear responses in odd-parity magnets can be directly controlled by a bicircular drive.

</details>


### [21] [Spatiotemporal Imaging of Thermally Driven Metal-Insulator Phase Domain Propagation in VO2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26367)
*Ilaria Delbono,Raouf Amara,Matias Feldman,Hankun Yang,Cédric Leau,Carlo Scian,Erwan Dandeu,Sébastien Royer,Debora Pierucci,Emmanuel Lhuillier,Franck Vidal,Tiziana Cesca,Hilton B. de Aguiar,Jose Ordonez-Miranda,Danièle Fournier,Raj Pandya,Boris Kalinic,Giovanni Mattei,James K. Utterback*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 利用频闪光学散射显微术与有限元模拟，揭示VO2薄膜中绝缘体-金属相界的非线性热驱动运动规律，发现相界输运在局域上遵循扩散行为，但介观相前传播快于热扩散预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相界（phase boundary）的性质与动力学，尤其是相变材料中的固-固转变（solid-solid transition），仍缺乏细致认识，是相变研究的核心挑战。

Method: 采用频闪光学散射显微术（stroboscopic optical scattering microscopy）结合有限元模拟（finite-element simulations），观测VO2薄膜中衍射极限光致金属相畴的横向时空演化，并通过激发通量、膜厚与衬底热导率调控相界动力学。

Result: 数据与无拟合参数的非线性热输运模拟（nonlinear thermal transport simulations）一致，表明该区域内相界输运为局域扩散式；潜热（latent heat）起热惯性作用，抑制输运并延长相共存；尽管由傅里叶定律（Fourier's law）描述的局域热梯度驱动，介观相前（mesoscale phase front）传播却快于热扩散预期。

Conclusion: 该"悖论"源于温度场演化与相界等温线（isotherm）运动之间的区别；研究建立了热耗散与介观相前运动的联系，为VO2光子学应用中的相变动力学调控提供框架，并有望推广至广泛的一阶与二阶相变体系。

Abstract: Achieving a detailed picture of the nature and dynamics of phase boundaries remains a major challenge, particularly solid-solid transitions central to phase-change materials. Here, we use stroboscopic optical scattering microscopy and finite-element simulations to study the nonlinear thermally driven motion of the mesoscale insulator-metal phase boundary in VO2 thin films. We visualize the lateral spatiotemporal evolution of a diffraction-limited photoinduced metallic phase domain, resolving its outward propagation, contraction, and ultimate disappearance. The phase boundary dynamics are governed by heat flow, which can be controlled by excitation fluence, film thickness, and substrate thermal conductivity. Parameter-free agreement between the data and nonlinear thermal transport simulations identify the phase boundary transport as locally diffusive under this regime. We quantify how the latent heat acts as a thermal inertia, suppressing transport and prolonging the phase coexistence. Paradoxically, despite being driven by the underlying thermal gradient locally described by Fourier's law, the mesoscale phase front propagates faster than expected from thermal diffusion. This phenomenon arises from the distinction between temperature-field evolution and phase-boundary isotherm motion. These results establish a link between heat dissipation and mesoscale phase-front motion, providing a framework for controlling phase-transition kinetics relevant to photonics applications of VO2. We anticipate that this approach generalizes to a broad class of first- and second-order transitions.

</details>


### [22] [Probing the physical origins of 1/f noise by nonlinear transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26592)
*Nicolas Fontaine,Bertrand Reulet*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 在直流与交流偏置的隧道结（tunnel junctions）非线性电子输运中，低频噪声（low frequency noise）不仅存在于电阻，也存在于非线性系数，且二者存在关联；这些测量可归因于势垒高度（barrier heights）的涨落，结两侧势垒高度的涨落近乎完全反相关（anti-correlated）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注隧道结非线性输运中的低频噪声行为，旨在检验噪声是否仅局限于电阻，还是同样出现在非线性系数中，并厘清其微观来源。

Method: 对直流（dc）和交流（ac）偏置下的隧道结开展低频噪声测量，并借助隧道结电子输运的通用理论模型，将测量结果与势垒高度涨落建立联系。

Result: 电阻与非线性系数均表现出类 1/f 噪声（$1/f$-like noise），且两者之间存在相关性；结两侧的势垒高度存在强烈涨落，且这些涨落几乎完全反相关。

Conclusion: 隧道结非线性输运的低频噪声可归因于势垒高度涨落，其中两侧势垒涨落的近完全反相关是主要实验发现；该结果将噪声谱与势垒参数涨落直接关联。

Abstract: We report measurements of low frequency noise in the nonlinear electron transport of tunnel junctions that are dc or ac biased. We show that not only the resistance but also the nonlinear coefficients exhibit $1/f$-like noise, and that correlations among them exist. Using the common theoretical model for electron transport in tunnel junctions, we relate our measurements to fluctuations of barrier heights. We show in particular that the barrier heights on either side of the junction have strong fluctuations that are almost perfectly anti-correlated.

</details>


### [23] [Strain-induced disorder from Schottky gates on high mobility 2DEGs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26607)
*Sambit Mohapatra,Lukas Berg,Jean-Baptiste Cuvellier,Yan Sun,Abdelhanin Aassime,Alan Durnez,Antonella Cavanna,Dominique Mailly,Ulf Gennser*

TL;DR: 该研究证明，GaAs/AlGaAs异质结中金属肖特基栅（Schottky gate）导致的二维电子气（2DEG）迁移率下降源于应变效应（strain effects），而非其他机制；无图案栅中金属薄膜的无序颗粒结构起到与超晶格栅等效的应变场来源作用。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静电栅（electrostatic gates）对分数量子霍尔效应（fractional quantum Hall effect）等关联现象研究至关重要，但常用的金属肖特基栅易损害电子迁移率并抑制分数量子霍尔态，其机理尚需厘清。

Method: 采用特殊设计的纳米结构超晶格栅（nanostructured superlattice gates），通过测量共度振荡（commensurability oscillations）来探测应变效应，并将实验结果与COMSOL Multiphysics仿真对比；同时构建简化模型分析无图案栅中金属薄膜颗粒无序的作用。

Result: 实验结果表明迁移率降低由应变效应引起，且与COMSOL Multiphysics模拟吻合良好；简化模型显示，常规无图案栅中金属薄膜的无序颗粒特性可产生穿透至2DEG的应变场，其作用与超晶格相当。

Abstract: Electrostatic gates on two-dimensional electron gases (2DEGs) in GaAs/AlGaAs heterostructures are essential for numerous studies of correlation driven phenomena, many performed within the fractional quantum Hall effect. However, the most commonly used type of gates, the metallic Schottky gate, has the propensity to adversely impact the electron mobility and suppress the fractional quantum Hall states. Here we demonstrate through commensurability oscillations under specially designed nanostructured superlattice gates, that strain effects are responsible for the mobility reduction. The experimental results agree well with COMSOL Multiphysics simulations. In a regular, unpatterned gate the disordered, granular nature of the metal film fills the same role as the superlattice in creating a strain field penetrating down to the 2DEG, as we show with a simplified model. These results will help in understanding, improving and finding novel methods to reduce strain-induced disorder in gated high-mobility 2DEGs.

</details>


### [24] [Proximity Transient Absorption in van der Waals Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26715)
*Neema Rafizadeh,Ting Zheng,Hui Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统瞬态吸收光谱（transient absorption spectroscopy）通常将瞬态光学信号归因于承载被测光学跃迁材料内部的激发，该默认假设限制了对难以直接探测材料中载流子动力学的光学研究。论文旨在检验瞬态光学信号能否源自空间上分离的邻近层激发，从而拓展超快光谱的适用范围。

Method: 构建石墨烯/hBN/WS₂范德华异质结构（van der Waals heterostructures），以原子级薄的hBN作为间隔层实现两层电子学隔离；在石墨烯中光激发载流子，同时监测WS₂单层激子共振处的瞬态吸收响应，并系统改变hBN厚度以检验信号的邻近起源。

Result: 石墨烯中的光激发载流子通过库仑屏蔽在WS₂激子共振处诱导出强瞬态光学响应，即使两层保持电子学隔离；所得信号随石墨烯而非WS₂的载流子动力学演化。信号随hBN厚度增加而系统性下降，在间距接近2 nm时仍保持可观幅度，证实其邻近起源。

Conclusion: WS₂激子可作为邻近材料中电子过程的光学换能器（optical transducer）。PTA提供了一种非接触式（noncontact）光学调控与探测手段，可用于调控另一材料并探测难以直接研究的载流子动力学；更广泛地，该工作确立了超快光谱的新模态，并表明范德华异质结构中的瞬态光学信号可源于空间分离层中的激发。

Abstract: Transient absorption spectroscopy is a widely used technique for investigating ultrafast electronic dynamics, where transient optical signals are generally interpreted as arising from excitations within the material hosting the probed optical transition. A distinct form of transient absorption spectroscopy, termed proximity transient absorption (PTA), is demonstrated, in which the probed optical transition and the monitored photoexcited carriers reside in different materials. Using graphene/hBN/WS$_2$ heterostructures, photoexcited carriers in graphene induce a strong transient optical response at the excitonic resonance of a WS$_2$ monolayer through Coulomb screening, even when the two layers remain electronically isolated by an atomically thin hBN spacer. The resulting signal follows the carrier dynamics of graphene rather than those of WS$_2$, demonstrating that the WS$_2$ exciton acts as an optical transducer of electronic processes in a neighboring material. The signal decreases systematically with increasing hBN thickness, confirming its proximity origin while remaining substantial for separations approaching 2 nm. PTA provides a noncontact approach for optically modulating one material using photoexcited carriers in another and enables probing of carrier dynamics in materials that are otherwise difficult to investigate directly. More broadly, PTA establishes a new modality of ultrafast spectroscopy and reveals that transient optical signals in van der Waals heterostructures can originate from excitations residing in spatially separated layers.

</details>

Method: 基于BESIII的J/ψ数据，通过辐射跃迁过程J/ψ→γη/η'获得η与η'样本，并对其衰变进行振幅与截面等精密测量分析；文中未给出更具体的方法细节。

Conclusion: BESIII凭借大统计量J/ψ样本，在η与η'的衰变机制、形状因子及稀有衰变等方面取得多项进展，凸显该数据样本在轻介子谱学研究中的独特价值。

Abstract: The world's largest sample of $J/ψ$ events accumulated with the BESIII detector offers a unique opportunity to investigate $η$ and $η'$ physics via the radiative transition processes $J/ψ\to γη/η'$. A selection of recent highlights in light meson spectroscopy achieved at BESIII is reviewed in these proceedings, including evidence for the cusp effect in $η' \to π^0π^0η$, precision studies of the decay mechanisms of $η\to 3π$ and $η' \to 4π$, electromagnetic transition form factor measurements with $η/η' \to γe^+e^-$ and $η' \to π^+π^-l^+l^-$, as well as searches for rare decays of $η$ and $η'$ into lepton pairs, including $η\to l^+l^-$ and $η/η' \to l^+l^-l^+l^-$~($l = e, μ$).

### [26] [ceci n'est pas un tagger, can an LLM flavour-tag a jet?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24954)
*Andrea Coccaro,Andrea Milici,Lucio Derin*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该研究探讨大语言模型（large language models, LLMs）能否仅凭预训练知识与喷注（jet）重建组分，直接对强子喷注进行味分类（flavour tagging），并在公开ATLAS样本上取得非平凡判别力且附带自然语言推理依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLMs融入高能物理（high-energy physics）大型合作组的分析流程，尚不清楚其预训练所获知识能否支持对物理数据的定量推断；作者因此提出超越现有应用范围的简单问题：LLM能否对强子喷注的味进行分类。

Method: 要求模型基于公开ATLAS味标记（flavour-tagging）样本的重建组分（reconstructed constituents），直接输出喷注的味概率（b、c、轻味、τ），并在受限条件下禁止编写代码或实现常规算法，仅依赖预训练知识与给定输入完成推断；全部代码通过GitHub仓库公开以复现实验。

Result: 两项互补结果：其一，模型实现了非平凡的味间判别，并与近期ATLAS味标记算法进行了比较；其二，每次预测均伴随自然语言推理说明（natural language rationale），其中援引的物理概念与真实味相关，并再现了各喷注类别传统上关联的特征。

Conclusion: 味标记为更广泛的问题提供了实验场：如何主动利用数十年研究积累的科学知识语料，以及LLMs能否作为任务特定算法（task-specific algorithms）的通用科学背景来源。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly capable of reasoning about scientific problems, effectively mastering and synthesizing entire fields of research and providing, for the first time, access to a distilled and queryable form of scientific knowledge. As the integration of LLMs rapidly spreads across the large collaborations of high-energy physics, aiming to re-think and optimize analysis workflows, it remains unclear whether the knowledge acquired during pretraining can support quantitative inference on physical data. Moving beyond the applications currently pursued by the community, we pose a simple question: can an LLM classify the flavour of a hadronic jet? An LLM is therefore asked to assign flavour probabilities (b, c, light, $τ$) to jets from a public ATLAS flavour-tagging sample, using only reconstructed constituents. Under a small set of constraints, the model is not allowed to write code or implement conventional algorithms and must instead perform the inference directly from its pretrained knowledge and the available inputs. We report two complementary results and make publicly available, through a GitHub repository, all the code required to reproduce the experiment and obtain the quantitative results presented in this work. First, the model achieves non-trivial discrimination between flavours, which we compare with recent ATLAS flavour-tagging algorithms. Second, the model accompanies each prediction with a natural language rationale. The physical concepts invoked in these rationales correlate with the truth flavour and reproduce the features traditionally associated with each jet category. Flavour tagging thus provides a laboratory for broader questions: how can we actively leverage the corpus of scientific knowledge accumulated over decades of research? Could LLMs serve as a general source of scientific context for task-specific algorithms?

</details>

Conclusion: BESIII 的大统计量粲偶素数据为检验 LNV/BNV、CLFV 与粲偶素弱衰变等新物理敏感通道提供了重要实验平台；具体结论与限制需参见所汇总的已发表文献。

Abstract: Up to now, the Standard Model (SM) remains confronted with several outstanding issues, including the matter-antimatter asymmetry in the universe, as well as the masses, oscillations, and hierarchical structure of neutrinos. These challenges are closely tied to the naturalness and stability of the SM, and have motivated numerous proposals for new physics beyond the SM. The BESIII experiment has accumulated the world's largest data samples at the $J/ψ$ and $ψ(3686)$ resonances, providing an ideal environment for probing signatures of physics beyond the SM. This proceeding presents a summary of recent results published by the BESIII Collaboration on the searches for lepton/baryon number violation (LNV/BNV), charged lepton flavor violation (CLFV), and charmonium weak decays.

</details>

Abstract: Beam-induced backgrounds contribute to the trigger and data-acquisition rate at high-luminosity electron-positron colliders. Their dependence on beam and machine conditions motivates data-driven studies alongside simulation. A data-driven machine-learning method for trigger-level beam-background suppression is developed using experimental data recorded by BESIII. Compact neural-network classifiers are trained and evaluated for two trigger configurations: one based only on tracking and time-of-flight information, and one based only on electromagnetic-calorimeter information. Each classifier uses 48 binary Level-1 trigger conditions of the BESIII detector. The models distinguish hadron events from beam-background events and are evaluated against the established trigger selection at matched hadron-event efficiency. Relative to the established trigger selection, the beam-background false-positive rate is reduced by 20-60% across the studied configurations and center-of-mass energies, with stable suppression performance as tested using random-trigger data. Hardware-oriented synthesis indicates that the compact network is suitable for low-latency FPGA implementation. The proposed method can reduce the fraction of beam-background events in the trigger stream in future BESIII trigger upgrades. It also provides a reference for trigger optimization at future high-luminosity electron-positron colliders.

### [29] [Measurements of time-dependent b-hadron properties in LHCb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26188)
*Lucas Foreman*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于LHCb Run 2数据，报告三项含时（time-dependent）测量：$B_s^0\to J/ψπ^+π^-$的CKM相位$φ_s$、$B^0\to J/ψρ^0(770)$中企鹅（penguin）贡献与$2β$、以及$B_s^0\to K_Sμ^+μ^-$中首个$b\to s l^+l^-$过程的含时CP破坏测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用LHCb Run 2数据提高$φ_s$在特定衰变道的精度、约束企鹅贡献对新物理的污染，并首次将含时CP破坏测量拓展至$b\to s l^+l^-$过程。

Method: 采用$b$强子（$b$-hadrons）含时性质的测量方法，分别分析$B_s^0\to J/ψπ^+π^-$、$B^0\to J/ψρ^0(770)$与$B_s^0\to K_Sμ^+μ^-$衰变，并将$φ_s$结果纳入LHCb全局平均（global average）。

Result: 得$φ_s=-77\pm34(\text{stat})\pm7(\text{syst})$ mrad，全局平均为$φ_s=-41\pm17$ mrad；$B^0\to J/ψρ^0(770)$首次观测到CP破坏，$2β^{\text{eff}}_{ccd}=0.710\pm0.084\pm0.051$ rad，企鹅贡献$Δφ_s=5.0\pm4.6$ mrad为最严格约束；$B_s^0\to K_Sμ^+μ^-$给出$C=-0.13\pm0.32(\text{stat})\pm0.04(\text{syst})$、$S=+0.82\pm0.29(\text{stat})\pm0.05(\text{syst})$。

Conclusion: 三项结果提升了$φ_s$的测定精度、给出目前最严格的企鹅贡献约束，并实现$b\to s l^+l^-$过程中含时CP破坏的首次测量。

Abstract: These proceedings present the latest measurements of time-dependent properties of $b$-hadrons using the LHCb Run 2 data set. Three results are presented. The first of these is a measurement of the CKM phase $φ_s$ with $B_s^0\to J/ψπ^+π^-$. This result improves the determination in this channel, obtaining a result of $φ_s = -77\pm34(\text{stat})\pm7(\text{syst})$ mrad. This result is included in the LHCb global average to obtain $φ_s = -41\pm17$ mrad. Additionally, a determination of the penguin contributions, $Δφ_s$, to measurements of $φ_s$ using $B^0\to J/ψρ^0(770)$ is presented. This measurement constitutes the first observation of CP-violation in $B_0 \to J/ψρ^0(770)$ decays. The CKM phase $2β$ is measured and is determined to be $2β^{\text{eff}}_{ccd} = 0.710\pm0.084\pm0.051$ rad. The most stringent constraint on penguin contributions is obtained and determined to be $Δφ_s = 5.0\pm4.6$ mrad. Finally, the first measurement of time-dependent CP-violation in a $b\to s l^+ l^-$ process using the decay $B_s^0 \to K_S μ^+ μ^- $ is presented. The time-dependent coefficients of this decay are determined to be $C=-0.13\pm0.32(\text{stat})\pm0.04(\text{syst})$ and $S=+0.82\pm0.29(\text{stat})\pm0.05(\text{syst})$.

### [30] [Neural Bayesian Online Calibration with Deficient Simulators for MADMAX Dark Matter Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26343)
*Nabil Salama*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一个面向实验的贝叶斯在线校准（Bayesian online calibration）框架，利用可直接测量的模拟器输出与潜在科学量，联合校准存在缺陷的模拟器并推断不可观测量；该框架已应用于MADMAX暗物质实验，实验验证正在进行中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学实验常依赖有缺陷的模拟器（deficient simulators）与硬件，其指令设定与实际达到的状态存在偏差，从而限制了可靠优化与推断。因此需要一种能在实验中在线校准模拟器、并同时处理测量与定位不确定性的方法。

Method: 采用贝叶斯在线校准框架：先用预校准数据（pre-calibration data）学习状态依赖的有效模拟器参数，并用归一化流（normalizing flow）实现基于测量历史的摊销后验推断（amortized posterior inference）；校准过程中传播测量与定位不确定性，并按期望改进（expected improvement）与信息增益（information gain）的权衡来选择硬件指令。

Result: 框架被应用于MADMAX暗物质实验，以实测反射率校准有缺陷的disk-booster模拟器，并推断不可观测的轴子信号功率增强因子（axion-signal power boost factor）。摘要未给出定量结果，MADMAX实验上的实验验证仍在进行中。

Conclusion: 该框架可在存在模拟器缺陷与硬件不确定性的条件下，同时完成模拟器在线校准与潜在科学量的后验推断，并通过信息增益驱动的指令选择提升校准效率；其实际有效性尚待MADMAX实验验证完成。

Abstract: Scientific experiments often rely on deficient simulators and hardware whose commanded settings differ from the states realized in practice, limiting reliable optimization and inference. A Bayesian online calibration framework is introduced for experiments with at least one directly measurable simulator output and potentially latent quantities of scientific interest. Pre-calibration data are used to learn state-dependent effective simulator parameters, while a normalizing flow enables amortized posterior inference from measurement histories. During calibration, measurement and positioning uncertainties are propagated, and hardware commands are selected by balancing expected improvement with information gain. The framework is applied to the MADMAX dark matter experiment, where measured reflectivity is used to calibrate a deficient disk-booster simulator and infer the unobservable axion-signal power boost factor. Experimental validation on the MADMAX experiment is ongoing.

</details>

Zhu,Lin Zhu,S. H. Zhu,T. J. Zhu,W. D. Zhu,W. J. Zhu,W. Z. Zhu,Y. C. Zhu,Z. A. Zhu,X. Y. Zhuang,M. Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用 BESIII 探测器在 3.00–3.12 GeV 的 284 pb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻ 碰撞数据，首次测定 J/ψ→Σ⁺Σ̄⁻ 过程中强相互作用与电磁相互作用振幅的相对相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强相互作用与电磁相互作用振幅的相对相位（relative phase）此前未在 J/ψ 衰变到超子-反超子对（hyperon-antihyperon pair）的过程中被实验测定，该量可用于检验微扰 QCD（perturbative QCD）预言与 SU(3) 味对称性（SU(3) flavor symmetry）研究。

Method: 基于 BESIII 探测器在质心能量（center-of-mass energy）3.00–3.12 GeV 采集的 284 pb⁻¹ e⁺e⁻ 碰撞数据，对 J/ψ→Σ⁺Σ̄⁻ 过程进行振幅分析以提取强、电磁振幅的相对相位及分支比（branching fraction）。

Result: 相对相位测得为 (107.9 ± 24.9)° 或 (−107.6 ± 24.3)°（不确定度含统计与系统误差）；相应分支比 B(J/ψ→Σ⁺Σ̄⁻) 为 (1.14 ± 0.04)×10⁻³（正相位解）或 (1.19 ± 0.04)×10⁻³（负相位解）。

Conclusion: 这是 J/ψ 衰变到超子-反超子对过程中强-电磁相位差的首次实验测定，结果为检验微扰 QCD 预言和 SU(3) 味对称性研究提供重要输入。

Abstract: Using 284 pb$^{-1}$ of $e^{+}e^{-}$ collision data collected with the BESIII detector at center-of-mass energies between 3.00 and 3.12 GeV, the relative phase between the strong and electromagnetic interaction amplitudes in the process $J/ψ\to Σ^+ \barΣ^-$ is determined. The relative phase is measured to be $(107.9 \pm 24.9)^{\circ}$ or $(-107.6 \pm 24.3)^{\circ}$, where the uncertainty includes both statistical and systematic sources. This is the first experimental determination of the strong-electromagnetic phase difference in $J/ψ$ decays to a hyperon-antihyperon pair. The branching fraction $\mathcal{B}_{J/ψ\to Σ^+ \barΣ^-}$ is measured to be $(1.14 \pm 0.04)\times 10^{-3}$ for the positive phase solution or $(1.19 \pm 0.04) \times 10^{-3}$ for the negative phase solution. These results provide important input for testing perturbative QCD predictions and studies of SU(3) flavor symmetry.

</details>

### [32] [Arithmetic Constraints on Charges of Symmetric Monopoles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24139)
*Paul Cusson*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文针对规范群（gauge group）为 SU(N)（N≥3）、最大对称性破缺（maximal symmetry breaking）且谱数据（spectral data）足够一般的欧氏 BPS 磁单极子（Euclidean BPS monopoles），给出其磁荷（magnetic charges）必须满足的同余关系（congruence relations），并据此排除 SO(3) 的某些子群作为对称群。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于判定给定磁荷的 BPS 磁单极子可能具有哪些对称性，即通过磁荷的算术约束来限制其允许的 SO(3) 对称子群（symmetry subgroups）。

Method: 方法为在 SU(N)（N≥3）、最大对称性破缺与足够一般谱数据的假设下，推导磁荷所必须满足的同余关系（congruence relations）。

Result: 所得同余关系使得具有给定磁荷的单极子可以排除 SO(3) 的若干子群作为可能的对称群；特别地，轴对称情形（axially symmetric case）仅在非一般（non-generic）设定下才可能承载非平凡磁荷。

Conclusion: 磁荷的同余约束构成限制 BPS 磁单极子对称性的有效工具，并表明轴对称非平凡磁荷情形属于非一般的特殊设定。

Abstract: For Euclidean BPS monopoles with gauge group $SU(N)$, maximal symmetry breaking, and sufficiently generic spectral data, we produce necessary congruence relations that their magnetic charges must satisfy when $N \geq 3$. As such, a monopole as above with given charges can have certain subgroups of $SO(3)$ ruled out as possible groups of symmetries. In particular, the axially symmetric case may only admit non-trivial magnetic charges in the non-generic setting.

</details>


### [33] [Smooth Quasi--Einstein Lifts of Locally Embeddable Three-Dimensional CR Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24455)
*Masoud Ganji*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该文证明实解析拟爱因斯坦提升定理（quasi–Einstein lifting theorem）的 $C^\infty$ 光滑版本：每个光滑、局部 CR 可嵌入、严格伪凸的三维 CR 流形，对任意实宇宙学常数，均存在局部光滑 Lorentz 拟爱因斯坦提升，其零测地线汇（null congruence）无旋、无剪切且带扭转，外尔张量（Weyl tensor）为 Petrov II 或 D 型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知实解析情形下有 Hill–Lewandowski–Nurowski 的拟爱因斯坦提升定理，而全局构造与部分局部结果分别受限于 Sasakian 情形或更窄的设定，因此需要把局部提升推广到光滑（$C^\infty$）范畴。

Method: 证明归结为约化半线性接触方程（reduced semilinear contact equation）的光滑局部可解性，通过将 $L^\infty$ 压缩（contraction）与次椭圆正则性（subelliptic regularity）相结合来完成。

Result: 对任意实宇宙学常数，均得到局部光滑 Lorentz 拟爱因斯坦提升，且其零测地线汇为测地、无剪切、带扭转，外尔张量具有 Petrov II 或 D 型；该结论超越 Sasakian 全局构造及相关局部结果的范围。

Conclusion: 拟爱因斯坦提升定理可由实解析情形推广至 $C^\infty$ 光滑情形，其关键在于约化半线性接触方程的光滑局部可解性。

Abstract: We prove a $C^\infty$ analogue of the real-analytic quasi--Einstein lifting theorem of Hill, Lewandowski and Nurowski \cite[Theorem~2.5]{HLN2008}. Every smooth, locally CR-embeddable, strictly pseudoconvex three-dimensional CR manifold admits, for each real cosmological constant, a local smooth Lorentzian quasi--Einstein lift whose null congruence is geodesic, shearfree and twisting and whose Weyl tensor has Petrov type~$\mathrm{II}$ or $\mathrm{D}$. The result extends local lifting beyond the Sasakian setting of the global constructions in \cite{GSS2020} and the local results in \cite{GGS2026}. The proof establishes smooth local solvability of the reduced semilinear contact equation by combining an $L^\infty$ contraction with subelliptic regularity.

</details>

<details>

Method: 摘要未描述具体方法，仅指出研究围绕闭局部对称时空（closed locally symmetric spacetimes）的几何（geometry）与动力学（dynamics）展开。

Result: 摘要仅称呈现“若干结果”（some results），未给出任何具体结果、定理或数值。

Conclusion: 摘要未给出结论内容；仅可确认研究对象为闭局部对称时空（closed locally symmetric spacetimes）的几何与动力学。

Abstract: We present some results concerning the geometry and dynamics of closed locally symmetric spacetimes.

</details>

### [35] [Onset of Chaos and Orbital Instabilities around Rotating Weyl Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23976)
*Shubham Kala,M. Yousaf,Pradeep Singh,Faisal Javed,Hemwati Nandan,G. Mustafa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本研究利用Poincare截面、Lyapunov指数、FLI、KS熵与DIG混沌图，考察Weyl共形引力中旋转黑洞附近光子轨道的非线性混沌特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清 relativistic frame dragging 与高阶曲率修正如何共同控制光子混沌，并评估非线性光子动力学作为强引力区偏离广义相对论（General Relativity）探针的潜力。

Method: 在Weyl共形引力（Weyl conformal gravity）框架下数值追踪旋转黑洞附近的光子轨道，结合Poincare截面、Lyapunov指数、快速Lyapunov指标（Fast Lyapunov Indicators, FLI）、Kolmogorov-Sinai（KS）熵及离散混沌指标（Discrete Indicator of Chaos, DIG）图进行混沌诊断。

Result: 随参数ξ与γ增大，不变环面（invariant tori）逐步变形并最终破裂，形成共振岛（resonance islands）与随机层（stochastic layers），在近极端自旋区域产生大范围混沌区；Lyapunov指数升高、FLI增长加快、KS熵增大，DIG图显示光子球附近规则区收缩、混沌域扩张。

Conclusion: 共形引力修正会破坏零测地圆轨道（null circular orbits）的稳定性，参考系拖曳（frame dragging）与高阶曲率项共同主导光子混沌，非线性光子动力学可作为强引力区检验广义相对论偏离的潜在探针。

Abstract: We investigate nonlinear photon motion in the vicinity of a rotating black hole (BH) within Weyl conformal gravity (WCG) by employing Poincare sections, Lyapunov exponents, Fast Lyapunov Indicators (FLI), Kolmogorov-Sinai (KS) entropy, and Discrete Indicator of Chaos (DIG) maps. The joint influence of BH spin and higher order curvature corrections, governed by the parameters $ξ$ and $γ$, induces a transition from nearly integrable trajectories to markedly chaotic dynamics in the strong field domain. As $ξ$ and $γ$ increase, the invariant tori are increasingly deformed and eventually break up. Resonance islands and stochastic layers then form, producing large chaotic regions in phase space, especially near the extremal spin regime. Increasing Lyapunov exponents, faster FLI growth, and higher KS entropy provide quantitative measures of the enhanced dynamical instability, the increased sensitivity to initial conditions, and the corresponding rate of information production. DIG maps show shrinking regular regions and expanding chaotic domains around the photon sphere, indicating that conformal gravity corrections destabilize null circular orbits. Taken together, these findings clarify how relativistic frame dragging and higher order curvature terms jointly control photon chaos and point to nonlinear photon dynamics as a potential probe of departures from General Relativity in the strong-gravity regime.

</details>


### [36] [Astrophysical Signatures of Charged Anisotropic Compact Star in f(R,T) Gravity via Minimal Geometric Deformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23993)
*Hira Shadab,M. Sharif,Tayyab Naseer*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在f(R,T)理论框架下，结合电磁场效应与最小几何形变（minimal geometric deformation）方法，构建了两个新的各向异性（anisotropic）恒星解，并以LMC X-4的质量与半径验证其物理可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在f(R,T)引力理论中引入电磁场与各向异性流体，以拓展致密星内部解的研究，并借助最小几何形变实现种子源与新源之间的引力耦合。

Method: 以静态球对称各向异性流体作为种子源（seed source），再向该流体分布中插入新源并假定两者引力耦合；计算Einstein-Maxwell场方程后施加径向变换（radial transformation），得到两组方程，并在不同约束条件下分别求解；通过将内部几何与外部Reissner-Nordström时空在表面匹配确定积分常数。

Result: 获得两个新的各向异性内部解，其常数由表面匹配确定；利用恒星LMC X-4的已知质量与半径进行检验，两个相对论性恒星模型在指定解耦参数（decoupling parametric values）下满足全部物理要求。

Conclusion: 最小几何形变方法在f(R,T)理论中配合电磁场可有效生成物理上可接受的各向异性致密星模型，所构解通过了基于LMC X-4观测数据的自洽性检验。

Abstract: This work employs minimal geometric deformation to develop two new anisotropic solutions under the effects of an electromagnetic field in the context of $f(R,T)$ theory. Our investigation begins with a static spherical system with an anisotropic fluid as a seed source. After that, a new source is inserted to the existing fluid distribution and both the fluid distributions are assumed to be gravitationally coupled with each other. After computing the Einstein-Maxwell field equations, a radial transformation is applied which results in two different sets of equations. These systems are solved individually under different constraints, generating new solutions. To obtain the constants in our interior solutions, we match the internal geometry with the external Reissner-Nordström spacetime at the surface. We further validate our models by using the known mass and radius of a star LMC X-4. Our investigation confirms that both relativistic stellar models satisfy all the physical requirements for the specified decoupling parametric values.

</details>

Its sections are embeddings of the physical ray space whose images contain no exact solutions. The test-space inner product singles out one of them, and narrowing the seed profile gives Weyl sequences of approximate solutions.

The edge also separates the Page--Wootters state from the slice of the Klein--Gordon solution. An ideal clock shows none of these effects.

Abstract: In this paper, we probe the effect of induced frame dragging on the energy extraction process via magnetic reconnection in axially symmetric magnetized Reissner-Nordström black hole (MRNBH) spacetime and show the influence of BH charge $Q$ and the magnetic field $B$ parameters, respectively. We argue that the induced ergosphere, unlike the geometric ergosphere, is not a property of the spacetime metric but rather a particle-dependent negative energy region that changes as the charge on the particle varies. We find the region where magnetic reconnection can extract energy from the axially symmetric MRNBH and determine the conditions required for this process. We further examine the energies of the accelerated and decelerated plasma outflows and the corresponding magnetic reconnection efficiency. We show that within the parameter ranges considered, the extraction efficiency increases with the MRNBH charge $Q$ and the magnetic field parameter $B$. For sufficiently large $B$, it reaches the efficiency comparable to that of an extremal Kerr BH case. Our results therefore show that electromagnetically induced frame dragging can support energy extraction efficiencies similar to those associated with rotation parameter. To connect with observations, we evaluate the reconnection power directly from the decelerated-plasma energy at infinity and compare it with the observed power scales of M87* and Sgr~A*. For $Q=M$, reconnection extraction appears in our scan only for $|B|M\gtrsim0.2$ ($B\sim10^{9}\,\mathrm{G}$ for M87*, $\sim10^{12}\,\mathrm{G}$ for Sgr~A*), where $P_{\mathrm{ext}}\sim10^{53}$--$10^{57}\,\mathrm{erg\,s^{-1}}$ exceeds the observed power scales by many orders of magnitude.

Abstract: The improved sensitivity of upcoming gravitational-wave detectors will require correspondingly more accurate binary black hole (BBH) simulations. Given the high cost of such simulations, it is essential to understand their sources of error and use computational resources efficiently. We study sources of error in BBH simulations performed with the Spectral Einstein Code (SpEC) and seek to understand what limits their accuracy. In particular, we focus on the inspiral phase because it is the longest-running part of the simulation and accounts for the majority of the waveform error. We find that targeted changes to simulation parameters and resolution in different parts of the computational domain reduce constraint violations and gravitational waveform differences. We also discuss more fundamental limitations on our accuracy that become important at higher resolutions: floating-point errors and large differences in the scales of the fundamental variables. Addressing these limitations will require substantial changes to both the SpEC code and the formulation it uses to evolve Einstein's equations. Many of the improvements and changes discussed in this paper are likely to be relevant to other numerical relativity codes.

Abstract: We derive the linearised gravitational waves generated by a localised matter source in the Poincare patch of de Sitter spacetime at arbitrary multipole order. The linearised de Sitter problem is mapped exactly to an auxiliary flat-space problem in harmonic gauge, with a conserved source built from the physical stress tensor. This allows us to use the multipole expansion of Damour and Iyer verbatim, and yields the exterior metric at all orders in terms of two families of radiative multipole moments given as explicit integrals of the physical stress tensor. Abstract: Grey-body factors describe the frequency-dependent transmission of fields through the effective potential generated by a black hole or another compact object. The natural state, real at the turn, freezes the torus into a nearly rectangular box whose sides typically differ by $16$-$18\%$. The microwave background can read this fossil only if the shortest cycle lies between $0.985$ and about $1.04$ last-scattering diameters. A top-quark pole mass above $173.7$ GeV, within reach of FCC-ee, would exclude this beginning (Bayes factor $\lesssim0.03$).

At large maximum rapidity $η$, $N\sim(7α/\sqrt6)η$, while $E\propto e^{3η}$ at fixed passage timescale: the radiation chiefly hardens. Abstract: We apply gravitational path integral factorization to provide a semiclassical interpretation of generalized black hole free energy landscapes at fixed external temperature and angular velocities.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 引入无量纲变量使两个多项式均为四次，并借助Biermann-Weierstrass定理，以轨道上任意点（而非转折点）为参数给出径向与极角坐标的一对显式实值公式；时间坐标与两个方位角由求积（quadrature）得到，化归为第三类椭圆积分，仿射参数（affine parameter）亦同。

Conclusion: 该工作将五维Kerr-(A)dS测地线运动从逐例积分提升为闭式通解，并纠正了先前文献中的相关表达式。

Abstract: The Hamilton-Jacobi equation for geodesic motion in the five-dimensional Kerr-(A)dS black hole separates in Boyer-Lindquist coordinates, but the resulting first-order system has so far been integrated case by case, according to the real roots of the radial and the polar polynomial. We give the general solution in closed form. In dimensionless variables both polynomials are quartic, and a theorem of Biermann and Weierstrass then yields the radial and the polar coordinate as one pair of manifestly real formulas, parametrized by an arbitrary point of the orbit rather than by a turning point, and valid for timelike and null geodesics alike whatever the root structure. The time coordinate and the two azimuthal angles follow by quadratures that reduce to elliptic integrals of the third kind alone, and the affine parameter by one more of the same type. Along the way we correct the inverse metric and the radial potential as published.

</details>

Result: 类比几何中残余的声速因子会移动光环位置，并缩小光环对存在的正则化参数（regularization parameter）$\ell/M$ 范围；所推导的相互作用剖面可消除该位移。所得外部势与相互作用强度具闭式解，且 $M$ 与 $\ell$ 可通过势参数直接调节；结合准二维约束、愈合长度（healing length）分辨、三体寿命与定常流寿命等实验要求，给出三种可在现有 $^{39}\mathrm{K}$ 平台上实现的基准设计。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了从正规黑洞/模仿体度规到准二维 BEC 实验参数的完整映射，并证明现有 $^{39}\mathrm{K}$ 平台具备实现该类比方案的可行性。

Abstract: We show how static, spherically symmetric regular black holes and black-hole mimickers with radius-dependent mass function $m(r)$ can be emulated in a quasi-two-dimensional Bose--Einstein condensate, with the aim of probing inner-horizon and inner-light-ring instabilities in the laboratory. Matching the acoustic and gravitational line elements on an equatorial disc fixes the ratio of radial flow velocity to sound speed and reproduces both horizon locations and surface gravities exactly. The analogue geometry nevertheless contains a residual radially varying sound-speed factor in $g_{tt}$, which shifts the light-ring positions and reduces the range of the regularization parameter $\ell/M$ for which a light-ring pair exists. We derive the interaction profile that removes this shift. Combining the metric correspondence with the continuity and Bernoulli equations, we then obtain closed-form expressions for the external potential and interaction strength realizing the full target spacetime, with $M$ and $\ell$ directly tunable through parameters of the potential. Finally, we translate the construction into experimental requirements (quasi-two-dimensional confinement, healing-length resolution, three-body lifetime, and stationary-flow lifetime) and present three fiducial designs achievable with present-day $^{39}\mathrm{K}$ platforms.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 采用短脉冲数据（short pulse data）描述两个横向局域化、近乎平面波前的引力波，令二者碰撞；将两块光滑片段沿一条曲线拼接，使其未来指向的外零法向（outer null normals）连续匹配，从而构造外陷获面；随后改造 Penrose 原始论证以证明极大未来发展的不完备性。

Result: 在无对称性假设的光滑真空时空中严格构造出由引力波碰撞产生的外陷获面，并由此建立极大未来发展的不完备性（incompleteness）。

Conclusion: 该工作将 Penrose 基于 Aichelburg–Sexl 解的启发式论证推广为严格的、无需对称性假设的构造，为引力波碰撞导致奇异性形成的图景提供了严格基础。

Abstract: We give a new rigorous construction of outer trapped surface formation in smooth vacuum spacetimes without symmetry assumptions. The outer trapped surface is produced by the collision of two transversely localized, nearly plane-fronted gravitational waves described by short pulse data. The outer trapped surface is obtained by joining two smooth pieces along a curve with their future-directed outer null normals matching continuously. The incompleteness of the maximal future development is then established by adapting Penrose's original argument. This construction is inspired by Penrose's argument in constructing a marginally outer trapped surface in the collision of two ultra-relativistic particles modeled by the Aichelburg--Sexl solution.

</details>


### [72] [Primordial black holes in braneworld gravity: A gateway to hidden dimensions and the dark Universe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26482)
*Itzi Aldecoa-Tamayo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在Randall–Sundrum Type II（RS-II）膜世界（braneworld）框架下重新审视原初黑洞（primordial black holes, PBHs）的宇宙学演化，核心结论是高维效应显著增强早期吸积，并改变PBH质量谱与现今暗物质（dark matter）丰度之间的映射关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有PBH吸积与蒸发分析多基于四维广义相对论（general relativity），在高维膜世界情形下PBH的质量演化、初始质量谱（mass spectrum）与暗物质约束的对应关系可能被系统性误估，因此需要自洽的相对论性处理与观测约束再分析。

Method: 采用协变的Shiromizu–Maeda–Sasaki（SMS）形式体系，将Michel吸积理论推广至全相对论性框架以处理早期宇宙中的PBH吸积；并重新分析直接Hawking辐射、未分辨弥散伽马射线背景（diffuse gamma-ray background）、微引力透镜（microlensing）巡天与宇宙微波背景（cosmic microwave background, CMB）能量注入所给出的约束。

Result: 高维效应大幅增强轻质量PBH的早期吸积，本工作所给黑洞质量与先前估计的偏差随宇宙时间以t^0.33增长，在强吸积时期（strong-accretion epoch）结束时可达数个量级；结合修正的高维修正蒸发动力学与更新观测数据，界定了PBH可构成全部暗物质且满足现有观测上限的参数空间（parameter space），且这类PBH的初始质量可低于有效四维Planck尺度（Planck scale）。

Conclusion: RS-II膜世界中的PBH具有独特的演化历史与今日观测特征，可作为暗物质候选者；其初始质量谱与现今丰度之间的映射需按高维吸积与蒸发效应重标定，从而放宽对亚Planck质量PBH的限制。

Abstract: This thesis investigates the cosmological dynamics of primordial black holes (PBHs) within the Randall--Sundrum Type II braneworld scenario. The work comprises a preliminary discussion on gravitational collapse in the small-black-hole limit at early cosmological epochs, and two principal studies. The first study presents a fully relativistic treatment of PBH accretion in the early Universe, employing Michel accretion theory within the covariant Shiromizu--Maeda--Sasaki formalism. The analysis reveals that higher-dimensional effects substantially enhance early-time accretion onto light PBHs, with discrepancies between our black hole masses and previous estimates growing with cosmic time as $t^{0.33}$ --- a difference that reaches several orders of magnitude by the end of the strong-accretion epoch. This finding fundamentally alters the mapping between the initial PBH mass spectrum and present-day populations. The second study reassesses the observational and cosmological constraints on higher-dimensional PBHs in Randall--Sundrum cosmologies. Constraints from direct Hawking radiation, unresolved diffuse gamma-ray backgrounds, microlensing surveys, and energy injection into the cosmic microwave background are reanalysed in light of the modified higher-dimensional evaporation dynamics and updated observational surveys. Taken together, these results identify viable regions of parameter space in which PBHs can account for the entirety of dark matter while remaining consistent with current observational bounds. Notably, due to the enhanced accretion epoch, PBHs capable of constituting a significant fraction of dark matter may originate from masses below the effective four-dimensional Planck scale. This thesis thus puts forward a dark matter candidate with a distinct evolutionary history and observational signatures today.

</details>


### [73] [GPU-accelerated Bayesian inference for diffracted gravitational wave signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26619)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波探测率的持续增长使引力透镜化引力波（lensed gravitational wave）的观测概率上升；当透镜的史瓦西半径（Schwarzschild radius）与引力波波长相当时，波光学效应（wave-optics effects）会产生频率依赖的信号调制。基于CPU的参数估计计算开销大，限制了候选透镜事件显著性研究的规模。

Method: 在GPU加速的参数估计流程Jim中实现点质量透镜模型，构建Jim-lensing框架，从而在无需训练数据的前提下开展全波光学下的贝叶斯推断，包括贝叶斯因子（Bayes factors）的计算。

Result: 相对当前最先进的CPU流程，点质量透镜假设下的完整参数估计运行时间由10小时以上缩短至约10分钟量级；该实现稳定，并获得与最先进流程相当的结果。

Conclusion: 显著的加速为在更广泛的透镜模型下分析引力波数据铺平道路，并提升产生稳健频率派显著性估计（frequentist significance estimates）的能力。

Abstract: As the rate of gravitational wave detections continues to grow, the probability of observing a gravitationally lensed gravitational wave also increases. When the Schwarzschild radius of the lens is comparable in size to the gravitational wave wavelength, wave-optics effects induce a frequency- dependent modulation on the signal. CPU-based parameter estimation analyses for wave-optics lensing are computationally intensive, limiting the scale of studies to determine the significance of any potential lensing candidate. In this work, we implement a point-mass lens model in Jim, a high-performance GPU-based parameter estimation pipeline. This enables, for the first time, GPU-accelerated Bayesian inference in full-wave optics, including the computation of Bayes factors, without the need for training data. We call this framework Jim-lensing. Using this framework, we are able to speed up a full parameter estimation run under the point-mass lens hypothesis from more than 10 hours to the order of 10 minutes compared to the current state-of-the-art CPU-based pipeline. We show that our implementation is stable and obtains comparable results to this state-of- the-art pipeline. The substantial speed gain over existing CPU pipelines paves the way for analyzing gravitational wave data under a broader range of lensing models with greater capacity to produce robust frequentist significance estimates.

</details>


### [74] [Cosmological perturbations from localized sources: a gauge-invariant multipolar theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26623)
*Geoffrey Compère,Sk Jahanur Hoque*

Main category: gr-qc
