Abstract: We develop a first-principles description of the linearized gravitational field of localized sources in spatially flat Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker universes with arbitrary smooth expansion histories. The construction determines the complete retarded metric and the induced perturbation of the surrounding cosmic fluid from prescribed source stresses and a cosmologically stationary past field. It includes all multipole orders and accounts for curvature tails, through which the field depends on the source's past history inside the light cone. Generalizing the Blanchet-Damour multipolar formalism, we identify gauge-invariant mass and current moments together with a canonical cosmic fluid moment that determines an independent scalar response. This response vanishes in Minkowski and pure de Sitter spacetime, although curvature tails persist in de Sitter. The gauge-invariant multipoles are obtained directly in position space. This gives an exterior description without the nonlocal spatial projection through the source interior required by the usual scalar-vector-tensor decomposition. We also relate the canonical multipole metric to the standard variables of cosmological perturbation theory. The retarded construction requires neither a slow-motion nor a geometric-optics approximation. We define effective multipolar sources by truncating the moments of slowly varying sub-Hubble extended sources at a prescribed Cartesian multipole order, recovering the earlier quadrupolar and octupolar models in de Sitter spacetime. We recover the known Minkowski and de Sitter multipolar fields and obtain an analytical representation for gravitational propagation in the $Λ$CDM model including radiation.

</details>


### [75] [A new class of rotating black hole solutions endowed with a scalar hair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26678)

Conclusion: 研究表明，在该Horndeski子类中存在带原初毛发的稳态轴对称黑洞，其几何与热力学性质均偏离Kerr图像，提示黑洞热力学定律在带毛解中可能不再普遍成立。

Abstract: We present new hairy stationary and axisymmetric black hole solutions. These solutions belong to a subclass of Horndeski scalar-tensor theories, in which there is no non-minimal coupling to curvature. The full rotating solutions are obtained numerically using the Kadath library based on spectral methods. The metric does not rely on an explicit ansatz but rather on the use of a set of numerical coordinates. Black holes obtained differ from Kerr in general, and depend on a new independent parameter, a primary hair, coming from a linear time dependence in the scalar field. These solutions admit a Killing horizon like the Kerr black hole, but are non-circular, and break the zeroth law of black hole thermodynamics.

</details>


### [76] [Gravitational fingerprints of a black hole solution in Palatini Kalb-Ramond gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26692)
*Carlos F. S. Pereira,Araújo Filho,Marcos V. de S. Silva,N. Heidari*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在零宇宙学常数的Palatini Kalb–Ramond引力中，围绕静态黑洞研究粒子运动、光学观测量与线性波传播；在固定轨道质量时几何观测量多退化为Schwarzschild值，而潮汐、时间与轨道角等量依赖径向形变σ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清Palatini Kalb–Ramond引力中黑洞时空的径向形变对可观测量的影响，并区分守恒质量与轨道质量以避免参数耦合带来的简并。

Method: 采用解析方法分析光子球、临界碰撞参数、最内稳定圆轨道与径向频率比，并计算光偏折（弱场与强场，含锥形渐近）；对标量、电磁与无质量Dirac扰动及δB_{μν}=0的轴向引力扇区进行谱分析，辅以三阶WKB与时间演化数值对比。

Result: 固定轨道质量时，光子球、临界碰撞参数与最内稳定圆轨道保持Schwarzschild值，径向频率比亦不变；固有时距、下落时间与累积轨道角依赖σ。径向下落潮汐本征值获σ^{-2}因子，光子轨道不稳定指数按σ^{-1}标度。轴向引力扇区可精确映射为Schwarzschild谱，振荡与衰减率按σ^{-1}标度；三阶WKB基频与时间演化结果偏差在0.7%以内。与Sgr A*和M87*的阴影比较显示耦合简并。

Conclusion: 结果表明该理论的可观测量对径向形变σ的敏感性因观测量而异，且阴影观测在给定轨道质量与面积距离下存在参数简并，需其他观测量辅助破简并。

Abstract: We investigate particle motion, optical observables, and linear wave propagation around a static black hole in Palatini Kalb Ramond gravity with a vanishing cosmological constant. The analysis distinguishes the conserved mass $M$ from the orbital mass $\bar{M}=M/ρ$ and isolates the radial deformation $σ^2=1-q/2$, where $q=\ell_2/(1-\ell_1)$. At fixed $\bar{M}$, the photon sphere, critical impact parameter, and innermost stable circular orbit retain their Schwarzschild values. Radial frequency ratios also preserve their Schwarzschild profiles for the same areal release and particle radii, while proper distances, infall times, and accumulated orbital angles depend on $σ$. For radial fall, the tidal eigenvalues acquire a factor $σ^{-2}$, and the instability exponent of the photon orbit scales as $σ^{-1}$. In addition, we examine light deflection in the weak and strong regimes with an explicit account of the conical asymptotics. The shadow comparison with Sgr A* and M87* exhibits a coupling degeneracy when the orbital mass and areal distance are set. Scalar, electromagnetic, and massless Dirac perturbations probe the physical metric, whereas a homogeneous axial gravitational sector with $δB_{μν}=0$ admits an exact Schwarzschild spectral map. In this sector, both oscillation and attenuation rates scale as $σ^{-1}$ at fixed $\bar{M}$. Third order WKB estimates agree with frequencies extracted from time evolution to within $0.7\%$ for the reported fundamental modes.

</details>


### [77] [Spectroscopy as a Probe of Spacetime Foam](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26766)
*A. G. Kim,P. E. Nugent,L. Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出利用远处光源到达时间的无彩色随机抖动（achromatic stochastic arrival-time jitter）对一阶时间相干度 $g^{(1)}(\tau)$ 及观测光谱的修正，作为探测随时间变化的量子引力时空泡沫（quantum-gravitational spacetime foam）的光谱学探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间中变化的时空泡沫可通过横向相位退相干（transverse phase decoherence）由成像通道约束，而时间变化的泡沫缺乏对应的互补探针；本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在动力学泡沫极限（dynamic-foam limit，泡沫在光相干时间内去相关）下推导光谱分裂为被 $e^{-\sigma_\phi^2}$ 压低的相干线核与无相干基座（incoherent pedestal），据此构造三种探针：宽带通量存活、谱线分数宽度、宽带连续谱的色差亏损（chromatic deficit）；并应用于 GRB 221009A 的 MeV 发射线与 COBE/FIRAS 单极谱。

Result: GRB 221009A 给出 $\alpha \gtrsim 0.63$（弱、以杠杆臂为主的底线）；CMB 拟合达 $\alpha \gtrsim 0.52$（95% CL），下一代光谱仪预报仅 $\alpha \gtrsim 0.54$–$0.55$，受泡沫–温度简并（foam–temperature degeneracy）限制。

Conclusion: 与基座无关的线宽与 CMB 下限均低于全息值 $\alpha = 2/3$，与 GRB 221009A 成像窗口相容且不排除其任何部分；仅条件性 TeV 存活下限（$\alpha \gtrsim 0.74$，仅适用于近普朗克刷新泡沫 $\tau_{\rm foam}\lesssim 4\times10^{-28}$ s）会排除该窗口。

Abstract: Quantum-gravitational spacetime foam varying in space and in time imprints, respectively, a transverse phase decoherence and an achromatic, stochastic arrival-time jitter on light from distant sources. We show that the jitter modifies the first-order temporal coherence $g^{(1)}(τ)$ and hence the observed spectrum, providing a spectroscopic probe of the temporal variation, complementary to existing imaging-channel constraints on the spatial variation. In the dynamical-foam limit, where the foam decorrelates within the light's coherence time, the spectrum splits into a coherent line core suppressed by $e^{-σ_φ^2}$, with $σ_φ^2$ the variance of the foam-induced phase jitter, and an incoherent pedestal, yielding a spectral shape-distortion bound on the foam exponent $α$. Three probes follow: the outright survival of broadband flux, the fractional width of a spectral line, and a chromatic deficit across a broadband continuum. Applied to the MeV emission lines of GRB\,221009A we obtain $α\gtrsim 0.63$, a weak, lever-arm-dominated floor. For the broadband CMB the foam induces an $e^{-σ_φ^2(ν)}$ deficit ($σ_φ^2 \propto ν^2$); a fit to the COBE/FIRAS monopole spectrum reaches $α\gtrsim 0.52$ ($95\%$ CL), and next-generation spectrometers reach only $α\gtrsim 0.54$--$0.55$ (forecast), limited by the foam--temperature degeneracy. The pedestal-independent line-width and CMB bounds are floors below the holographic value $α= 2/3$, consistent with and excluding no part of the imaging window reported for GRB\,221009A; only the conditional TeV survival bound ($α\gtrsim0.74$), which holds only for near-Planck-refresh foam ($τ_{\rm foam}\lesssim4\times10^{-28}$\,s), would exclude it --- and, with it, the window.

</details>


### [78] [Spherically symmetric solutions with dust and radiation sources in Schouten-Codazzi Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26775)
*Sebastián Nájera,Roberto A. Sussman,Juan Carlos Hidalgo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文求解了Schouten-Codazzi引力（SCG）在广义Vaidya与Lemaître-Tolman-Bondi（LTB）球对称时空中的精确解，表明Codazzi张量贡献可修正Misner-Sharp质量并改变LTB演化方程，从而在无需暗能量的前提下实现加速膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SCG是在爱因斯坦场方程中加入Codazzi张量的二阶度规理论，其精确解尚待系统考察；本文旨在检验该理论在描述零辐射与 inhomogeneous dust 等球对称几何时的可解性与宇宙学潜力。

Method: 选取两类球对称几何（广义Vaidya时空与LTB时空），要求Codazzi张量满足Codazzi方程，由此对时空几何施加限制，并将解确定至两个积分函数，这两个函数自然生成有效“宇宙学常数”与一个额外自由度。

Result: 对Vaidya几何，Codazzi贡献修正Misner-Sharp质量；对LTB模型，演化方程变为系数依赖Codazzi积分函数的三次多项式，从而给出比广义相对论（General Relativity）更广的动力学类别，可出现加速膨胀与永远膨胀模型而无需引入“暗能量（dark energy）”。

Conclusion: 分析表明Schouten-Codazzi引力为宇宙学应用提供了一个有吸引力的框架。

Abstract: We examine in this paper exact solutions of Schouten-Codazzi gravity (SCG), a second order metric theory in which a Codazzi tensor is added to the Einstein field equations. We consider two spherically symmetric geometries that describe null radiation and inhomogeneous dust, corresponding to generalized Vaidya and Lemaître-Tolman-Bondi (LTB) spacetimes. The Codazzi tensor has to comply the Codazzi equations, this imposes restrictions on the geometry of spacetime that are determined up to two integration functions that naturally generate an effective ``cosmological constant'' and an additional degree of freedom. We show that for the Vaidya geometry, these Codazzi contributions modify the Misner-Sharp mass, while the LTB models have as an evolution equation a cubic polynomial whose coefficients depend on the Codazzi integration functions. This leads to a broader class of dynamics than those of General Relativity, and allows for an accelerated expansion and ever-expanding models without the introduction of ``dark energy''. Overall, our analysis suggests that Schouten-Codazzi gravity provides an appealing framework for applications in cosmology.

</details>

### [79] [Quantifying the Kinetic and Thermodynamic Origins of the Glass-Forming Ability in Metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24101)
*Weiwei Jin,Jinpeng Fan,Mark D. Shattuck,Corey S. O'Hern*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文通过经典形核理论（classical nucleation theory）与独立计算的晶体生长速率，定量评估了纯金属与合金的临界冷却速率（critical cooling rate）$R_c$ 与结晶速率 $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$，发现二者在十一个数量级范围内呈强正相关，且结晶速率由热力学贡献主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属液体的玻璃形成能力（glass-forming ability, GFA）可由临界冷却速率 $R_c$ 量化，而 $R_c$ 取决于冷却速率与结晶速率 $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$ 之间的竞争；因此需要厘清 $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$ 的物理来源及其与 $R_c$ 的关系。

Method: 针对具有 BCC/B2 与 FCC 平衡晶体结构的模型纯金属与合金，采用经典形核理论（classical nucleation theory）并结合独立计算的晶体生长速率，在 $T=0.8T_m$ 处计算 $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}=(IU^3)^{1/4}$（$I$、$U$ 分别为形核率与生长速率）；并将 $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$ 分解为动力学贡献（涉及原子团簇附着与扩散）与热力学贡献。

Result: $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$ 与 $R_c$ 在十一个数量级范围内呈强正相关；在考察的冷却速率范围内，热力学贡献对 $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$ 起主导作用。形核势垒 $\Delta G_c \sim \tilde\gamma^3/\Delta\tilde\mu^2$（$\tilde\gamma$ 为约化有效液–晶界面自由能，$\Delta\tilde\mu$ 为约化体驱动力）决定热力学贡献的大小，且 $\tilde\gamma$ 与 $\Delta\tilde\mu$ 的相对重要性依材料而异。

Conclusion: 结构与化学序分析表明，玻璃形成能力更强的体系具有更强的结构阻挫（structural frustration）、更多二十面体样（icosahedral-like）而更少晶体样（crystal-like）的局域环境，以及过冷液体与晶相之间更大的化学序失配（chemical-order mismatch）。

Abstract: The glass-forming ability (GFA) of a metallic liquid can be quantified by the critical cooling rate $R_c$, below which it crystallizes upon cooling. $R_c$ is determined by the competition between $R$ and the crystallization rate $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$. Here, we calculate $R_c$ and $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$ in model pure metals and alloys with BCC/B2 and FCC equilibrium crystal structures. Using classical nucleation theory and independently calculated crystal growth rates, we evaluate $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}=(IU^3)^{1/4}$ at $T=0.8T_m$, where $I$ and $U$ are the nucleation and growth rates and $T_m$ is the melting temperature. We find a strong positive correlation between $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$ and $R_c$ over eleven orders of magnitude. We decompose $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$ into a product of a kinetic contribution, involving atomic cluster attachment and diffusion, and a thermodynamic contribution. We show that the thermodynamic contribution to $\mathcal{K}_{\rm X}$ dominates the kinetic contribution over the range of cooling rates that we consider. The nucleation barrier $ΔG_c \sim \tildeγ^3/Δ\tildeμ^2$, where $\tildeγ$ is the reduced effective liquid--crystal interfacial free energy and $Δ\tildeμ$ is the reduced bulk driving force, determines the magnitude of the thermodynamic contribution. The relative importance of $\tildeγ$ and $Δ\tildeμ$ depends on the material: a large interfacial free energy can suppress nucleation in some systems, whereas a weak bulk driving force can inhibit crystallization in others. Analyses of the structural and chemical order show that better glass formers have enhanced structural frustration, more icosahedral-like and less crystal-like local environments, and a larger chemical-order mismatch between the supercooled liquid and crystal phases.

</details>


### [80] [Signed FK percolation in Ising spin glasses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24629)
*Alberto Gandolfi,Charles Newman,Danile Stein*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 该文提出“带符号（signed）Fortuin-Kasteleyn（FK）渗流”以在单一无限体积ES/FK表示框架下刻画非零均匀磁化密度与双副本重叠（two-replica overlap）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通FK连通性无法区分耦合符号，难以在同一渗流语言中同时表达磁性序与副本重叠，因此需要保留开路径上耦合乘积奇偶性的精细化构造。

Method: 在至多一个无限FK团簇（infinite FK cluster）几乎必然成立的Gibbs分布类中，利用无限体积Edwards-Sokal（ES）表示及其DLR方程研究伴随对象（companions）与相容自旋赋值；单副本以正宇称FK连接定义companions，双副本以宇称一致的连接定义companions。

Result: 对满足无限团簇假设及沿所选van Hove序列companion密度几乎必然存在的极端态，非零极限期望磁化密度等价于无限FK团簇companions的密度可区分性（density distinguishability）；对两副本极端态乘积，密度可区分性等价于非零极限期望相互重叠，同态取样时特化为Edwards-Anderson自重叠（self-overlap）。

Conclusion: 带符号FK渗流为±J随机相互作用Ising模型提供了统一的几何刻画，将磁化与重叠的判据归结为companions的密度可区分性。

Abstract: We introduce signed Fortuin-Kasteleyn (FK) percolation for the +/-J random-interaction Ising model to characterize nonzero uniform magnetization density and two-replica overlap within the class of Gibbs distributions whose infinite-volume Edwards-Sokal (ES)/FK representation has at most one infinite FK cluster almost surely. The construction refines ordinary FK connectivity by retaining the parity of coupling products along open paths. Using the infinite-volume ES representation and its DLR equations, we study pairs of companion objects and the compatible spin assignments on them. In one replica, companions are defined by positive-parity FK connections. For extremal states satisfying the infinite-cluster assumption and almost-sure existence of companion densities along the chosen van Hove sequence, nonzero limiting expected magnetization density is equivalent to density distinguishability of the companions of the infinite FK cluster. For two independent replicas, companions are defined by FK connections coherent in parity. For products of extremal one-replica states satisfying the infinite-cluster assumption and almost-sure existence of the companion densities along the chosen sequence, density distinguishability of the companions is equivalent to nonzero limiting expected mutual overlap. For two independent replicas drawn from the same extremal state, this specializes to the Edwards-Anderson self-overlap.

</details>


### [81] [Synchronous and asynchronous Monte Carlo dynamics of mean-field spin glasses with rotationally invariant couplings: what they share and where they differ](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24942)
*Yoshiyuki Kabashima*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文比较了旋转不变耦合的 Ising 自旋玻璃（Ising spin glass）两种马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛动力学：同步蒙特卡洛（SyncMC）与异步 Metropolis-Hastings，并通过 Guionnet-Maïda 公式导出二者的动力平均场理论（dynamical mean-field theory）。结果表明两者在慢时间尺度上的方程（含涨落-耗散比与 Parisi 方程）完全一致，仅快时间尺度项及收敛所需更新次数不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同步与异步两种蒙特卡洛更新在自旋玻璃模拟中均被广泛使用，但其理论层面的等价性与差异缺乏系统刻画，尤其缺乏对旋转不变耦合情形的动力学平均场分析。

Method: 利用 Guionnet-Maïda 公式，以耦合谱的 R 变换（R-transform）为工具，导出 SyncMC（借助高斯辅助场并行更新全部自旋）与异步 Metropolis-Hastings（逐次更新单个自旋）两种动力学的动力平均场理论，得到各自的有效单自旋过程。

Result: 两种有效单自旋过程均含延迟自相互作用（retarded self-interaction）与高斯噪声；SyncMC 额外具有瞬时自耦合（instantaneous self-coupling）和仅依赖响应函数的噪声。这些额外项仅作用于最快时间尺度，故慢时间尺度方程——包括涨落-耗散比（fluctuation-dissipation ratio）及 Sherrington-Kirkpatrick 模型的 Parisi 方程——对两种动力学相同。

Conclusion: 随机正交模型与 Sherrington-Kirkpatrick 模型的模拟证实，两种动力学收敛到相同的涨落-耗散关系，差别仅在于达到该关系所需的更新次数。

Abstract: We compare two Markov chain Monte Carlo dynamics for Ising spin glasses with rotationally invariant couplings: the synchronous Monte Carlo method (SyncMC), which updates all spins in parallel with the help of Gaussian auxiliary fields, and asynchronous Metropolis-Hastings dynamics, which updates one spin at a time. Using the Guionnet-Maïda formula, we derive the dynamical mean-field theory of both dynamics in terms of the R-transform of the coupling spectrum. The two effective single-spin processes share a retarded self-interaction and a Gaussian noise; SyncMC has, in addition, an instantaneous self-coupling and a noise that depends only on the response function. These additional terms act only on the fastest time scale, so that the equations for slow time scales, including the fluctuation-dissipation ratio and, for the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick model, the Parisi equation, are the same for the two dynamics. Simulations of the random orthogonal and Sherrington-Kirkpatrick models confirm that both dynamics approach the same fluctuation-dissipation relations, differing only in how many updates they need to do so.

</details>


### [82] [Random spin-3/2 antiferromagnetic Heisenberg chains revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24994)
*Yen-Tung Lin,Pochung Chen,Yu-Cheng Lin*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文利用张量网络强无序重整化群（tensor-network strong-disorder renormalization group, tSDRG）重新审视随机自旋-3/2反铁磁海森堡链（random spin-3/2 antiferromagnetic Heisenberg chain）中的自旋约减转变（spin-reduction transition）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该转变据预测分隔两个随机单态相（random-singlet, RS phases）：弱无序下由有效自旋-1/2矩构成的一相，以及强无序下由完整自旋-3/2矩构成的另一相；在 Damle–Huse 分类框架下其为 P2 临界线上涌现的置换对称 P4 多临界点（multicritical point），其精确定位与临界行为尚需重新检验。

Method: 首先在 Damle–Huse 框架内推导 Pn 普适类（universality classes）中无序平均的体关联函数（bulk correlation function）与端到端关联函数（end-to-end correlation function）所遵循的临界指数；随后结合基于能隙分布（energy-gap distribution）均值与方差构造的诊断量，用 tSDRG 数值定位转变点。

Result: 借助上述理论预测与能隙分布诊断量，识别出 P4 转变点，并观测到 RS 相的回返行为（reentrant behavior）。

Conclusion: 结果为 Damle–Huse 分类下 P4 多临界点的存在及其与 P2 临界线的关系提供了支持，并表明关联函数临界指数与能隙分布统计可作为定位自旋约减转变的有效判据。

Abstract: We revisit the spin-reduction transition in the random spin-$3/2$ antiferromagnetic Heisenberg chain using the tensor-network strong-disorder renormalization group (tSDRG). The predicted transition separates two random-singlet (RS) phases: one formed by effective spin-$1/2$ moments at weaker disorder and the other by full spin-$3/2$ moments at stronger disorder. Within the Damle--Huse classification, this transition corresponds to an emergent permutation-symmetric $\mathcal{P}_4$ multicritical point on a $\mathcal{P}_2$ critical line associated with the RS phases. We first derive the critical exponents governing the disorder-averaged bulk and end-to-end correlation functions in the $\mathcal{P}_n$ universality classes within the Damle--Huse framework. Using these theoretical predictions, together with a diagnostic constructed from the mean and variance of the energy-gap distribution, we identify the $\mathcal{P}_4$ transition point and the reentrant behavior of the RS phase.

</details>


### [83] [Exact cost of the Euclidean Maximum Traveling Salesman Problem in one dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26536)
*Sergio Caracciolo,Carlo Vanoni*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 研究一维直线上随机欧氏最大旅行商问题（random Euclidean Maximum Traveling Salesman Problem），刻画不同凸凹性与单调性成本函数下的最大代价哈密顿圈（Hamiltonian cycle），并针对单位区间均匀采样点给出任意 N 的平均最大成本显式表达及幂律边成本下的渐近展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注以最大化总成本为目标的欧氏旅行商变体，但已有认识主要集中于一般度量或最小化情形；对一维直线上按边欧氏长度决定边成本时，最大化解的结构与平均最优值尚缺乏系统刻画，因而需要针对不同成本函数类型给出分类与定量分析。

Method: 设定 N 个城市位于直线上，边成本为边欧氏长度的函数；分别在凸递增（convex increasing）、凹递增（concave increasing）与凸递减（convex decreasing）成本函数下刻画使总成本最大的哈密顿圈；并假设点独立均匀采样自单位区间（unit interval），推导平均最大成本（average maximal cost）的解析结果。

Result: 给出三类成本函数下最大化循环的结构性刻画；对单位区间独立均匀采样，得到任意 N 下平均最大成本的显式表达式（explicit expressions），以及幂律边成本（power-law edge costs）下的渐近展开（asymptotic expansions）。

Conclusion: 一维随机欧氏最大旅行商问题的最优圈结构与平均最优成本可由成本函数的凸性与单调性分类刻画，并在均匀采样情形下获得显式与渐近解析结果，为幂律成本下的规模化行为提供定量描述。

Abstract: We study the random Euclidean Maximum Traveling Salesman Problem: finding a Hamiltonian cycle of maximal total cost through any set of $N$ cities on a line, where each edge cost is a function of its Euclidean length. We characterize the maximizing cycles for convex increasing, concave increasing, and convex decreasing cost functions. For points sampled independently and uniformly from the unit interval, we derive explicit expressions for the average maximal cost for arbitrary $N$ and asymptotic expansions with power-law edge costs.

</details>


### [84] [Higher-order disorder correlations control reflected phonon counting statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25153)
*Ara Sedrakyan*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 在一维声学堆栈中，超越功率谱（power spectrum）的结构关联（structural correlations）决定声子计数统计：相同两点关联函数的不同集合可给出 g^(2)=3/4 与 9/8，分居泊松值两侧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清除功率谱之外的高阶结构关联如何影响无序声学堆栈中的声子计数（phonon counting）与反射脉冲统计，并检验端点公式是否可作为大 N 极限。

Method: 采用整数厚度构造（integer-thickness construction）消去主导的背散射项（backscattering term），使有限链反射振幅由端点创新（endpoint innovations）决定；结合条件张量平均（conditional tensor averaging）与精确枚举（exact enumeration）进行验证。

Result: 对称独立创新下归一化反射功率二阶矩趋于 (μ_4+5)/4；真空环境下双声子 Fock 输入的集合平均 g^(2)=(μ_4+5)/8。同均值、同两点关联的两组集合分别给出 3/4 与 9/8。固定失谐增大 N 时出现由累积高阶体散射（bulk scattering）控制的渡越，而非收敛至端点公式。

Conclusion: 结果仅涉及无序平均的脉冲统计，既不能由相干输入产生非经典性（nonclassicality），也不改变单个已分辨被动样品的条件阶乘关联；另给出任意阻抗对比下的四阶电阻增长系数及未被最低阶谱移动恢复的正贡献。

Abstract: We investigate how structural correlations beyond the power spectrum affect phonon counting in a one-dimensional acoustic stack. An integer-thickness construction cancels the extensive leading backscattering term and leaves a finite-chain reflection amplitude determined by endpoint innovations. For symmetric independent innovations with unit variance and fourth moment $μ_4$, the normalized reflected-power second moment tends to $(μ_4+5)/4$. With vacuum environmental inputs, a two-phonon Fock input therefore gives an ensemble-averaged reflected factorial correlation $g^{(2)}=(μ_4+5)/8$. Two explicitly constructed ensembles with identical mean and complete two-point correlation function yield $g^{(2)}=3/4$ and $9/8$, placing their counting variances on opposite sides of the Poisson value. Conditional tensor averaging also gives a general fourth-order resistance-growth coefficient for arbitrary impedance contrast, including a positive higher-scattering contribution not recovered by shifting the lowest-order spectrum. Exact enumeration confirms the fixed-$N$, small-detuning limit and shows that increasing $N$ at fixed detuning produces a crossover governed by accumulated higher-order bulk scattering rather than convergence to the endpoint formula. Further calculations test bandwidth, thickness error, propagation loss and thermal background. The result concerns disorder-averaged pulse statistics; it neither creates nonclassicality from coherent input nor changes the conditional factorial correlation of an individually resolved passive sample.

</details>


### [85] [Signatures of slow particle transport in the interacting Aubry-André chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25557)
*S. Sharifan,M. Kiefer-Emmanouilidis,J. Sirker*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 在相互作用 Aubry-André 链中数值研究发现，纠缠熵与数熵按 S(t)~μ ln t、S_N(t)~ν ln ln t 缓慢增长，且 Hartley 数熵饱和值随系统尺寸以 ln L 持续增长；该行为与随机无序情形高度相似，表明慢离域化（slow delocalization）源于不依赖稀有区域雪崩的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强无序多体量子链中的慢离域化可能源自两种机制：基于能量统计的稀有共振（rare resonances）与雪崩（avalanches），或算符空间中与能量无关的组合路径熵（path-entropy）阻碍。准周期势（quasiperiodic potential）可消除稀有区域并改变共振统计，从而为区分二者提供判据。

Method: 对相互作用 Aubry-André 链（interacting Aubry-André chain）施加量子淬火（quantum quench），在无序强度 D 下数值计算纠缠熵 S(t)、数熵 S_N(t) 与 Hartley 数熵 S_H(t) 的时间演化及饱和行为。

Result: 拟合得到 S(t)~μ ln t、S_N(t)~ν ln ln t，增长率 μ~ν~D^{-γ} 且 γ≈3，与随机无序情形一致；饱和值 S_H(t→∞) 随系统尺寸 L 以 ln L 增长，且在 D≳8（此前认为 MBL 稳定的区域）仍持续存在。

Conclusion: 准周期与随机无序情形的相似性指向慢粒子输运（slow particle transport）的共同起源，该机制不依赖稀有区域雪崩，并在共振统计被显著改变后依然存在，支持基于算符空间路径熵阻碍的解释。

Abstract: Slow delocalization in strongly disordered many-body quantum chains can originate from two distinct mechanisms: rare resonances and avalanches, rooted in the energy statistics, or an energy-independent combinatorial path-entropy obstruction in operator space. Quasiperiodic potentials eliminate rare regions with similar potentials that are thought to be responsible for avalanche instabilities and modify the statistics of resonances, thus allowing us to delineate the two mechanisms. To do so, we numerically study the entanglement entropy $S(t)$, number entropy $S_N(t)$, and Hartley number entropy $S_H(t)$ following a quantum quench in the interacting Aubry-André chain with disorder strength $D$. We find $S(t)\simμ\ln t$, $S_N(t)\simν\ln\ln t$ with growth rates $μ\simν\sim D^{-γ}$ and $γ\approx 3$, the same behavior that was previously found in the case of quenched disorder. Furthermore, the saturation values $S_H(t\to\infty)$ continue to grow with system size $L$ as $\ln L$, a growth that persists for $D\gtrsim 8$ where MBL was argued to be stable. The similarity of the quasi-periodic and quenched disorder cases points to a common origin of the slow particle transport that does not rely on rare-region avalanches and persists despite strongly modified resonance statistics.

</details>

### [86] [Yielding-dependent Taylor dispersion in electrokinetic-magnetohydrodynamic Casson microflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25433)
*Nanda Poddar,Debabrata Das,Suman Chakraborty*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 研究压力辅助动电与磁流体动力学驱动下，Casson 流体在狭缝微通道中的纵向色散（longitudinal dispersion）；用 Mei 多尺度均匀化方法导出有效扩散系数，并揭示屈服参数改变时磁哈特曼数最优点与扩散增强的反转趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 屈服流体（yielding fluid）的纵向色散取决于速度分布及流动维持方式，而屈服应力与外加电磁场耦合下的色散规律尚不清晰，需要区分定压与定流量两种控制条件下示踪剂的扩散行为。

Method: 建立非线性应力与速度方程以确定屈服层（yielded layers）、未屈服核心（unyielded core）与轴向流动；对对流-扩散方程采用 Mei 多尺度均匀化（multiple-scale homogenization），分离横向弛豫、平均漂移与纵向扩展，得到 Taylor-Aris 辅助问题（auxiliary problem）与有效扩散系数；并以解析极限解、直接浓度计算、数值收敛研究及耦合 Casson 剪切测试进行验证。

Result: 精确压力驱动解显示，临近流动停滞时平均速度呈三次方衰减、色散增强呈八次方衰减。定压与定电场强迫下，屈服参数由 Y=0 增至 0.7 使最优哈特曼数由 Ha_p=0.696 移至 2.634，归一化扩散系数由 D_T^C=1.454 降至 1.015；而在定平均流量与 Ha_p=1 时趋势反转，D_T^C 由 1.000433 升至 1.002311。

Conclusion: 均匀化能量恒等式将上述差异归因于速度相对截面均值的偏离（含未屈服核心内的偏离）。基于电解质的量纲评估表明磁最优点为代表水性微通道中的理论强迫状态；结果可用于预测压力或流量控制下含屈服应力流体微通道中的纵向示踪剂扩散。

Abstract: Longitudinal dispersion in a yielding fluid depends on the velocity distribution and on how the flow is maintained. We study a neutral tracer in pressure-assisted electrokinetic and magnetohydrodynamic flow of a Casson fluid through a slit microchannel. The nonlinear stress and velocity equations determine the yielded layers, the unyielded core and the axial flow. Mei's multiple-scale homogenization of the advection-diffusion equation then separates transverse relaxation, mean drift and longitudinal spreading, yielding the Taylor-Aris auxiliary problem and effective diffusivity. An exact pressure-driven solution gives cubic decay of the mean velocity and eighth-power decay of the dispersion enhancement near flow arrest. Under fixed pressure and electrical forcing, increasing the yield parameter from $Y=0$ to $0.7$ moves the optimum Hartmann number from $Ha_p=0.696$ to $2.634$, while the corresponding normalized diffusivity decreases from $D_T^{\mathrm C}=1.454$ to $1.015$. At fixed mean throughput and $Ha_p=1$, the trend reverses: $D_T^{\mathrm C}$ increases from $1.000433$ to $1.002311$. The homogenized energy identity traces this difference to the velocity departure from the sectional mean, including that within the unyielded core. Analytical limiting solutions, direct concentration calculations, numerical convergence studies and a coupled Casson shear test support the reduced-flow predictions. An electrolyte-based dimensional assessment identifies the magnetic optima as theoretical forcing states for representative aqueous microchannels. These results are relevant to predicting longitudinal tracer spreading in microchannels carrying fluids with a yield stress, whether pressure or throughput is controlled.

</details>

### [87] [T-embeddings as stationary measures on T-graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24180)
*Mikhail Basok,Benoit Laslier,Marianna Russkikh*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究同一加权平面二部图（weighted planar bipartite graph）所对应的 t-嵌入（t-embedding）与 T-图（T-graph）之间的关系，证明 t-嵌入可由 T-图上的正平稳测度（positive stationary measure）分类，并给出重构程序与构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注 t-嵌入与 T-图这两个与同一加权平面二部图相关联的对象之间缺乏明确刻画的问题，希望厘清二者的对应关系，并为构造 t-嵌入提供可行途径。

Method: 在给定 T-图的条件下，证明相应的 t-嵌入由该 T-图上的正平稳测度所分类，并描述由这类测度出发重构 t-嵌入的具体程序。

Result: 建立了 t-嵌入与 T-图上正平稳测度之间的分类对应，并给出相应的重构步骤；作为应用，发展出针对任意充分非退化（sufficiently non-degenerate）平面二部图构造 t-嵌入的方法。

Conclusion: t-嵌入与 T-图通过正平稳测度建立了明确联系，该联系不仅给出分类与重构结果，也为一般非退化平面二部图的 t-嵌入构造提供了系统途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we investigate the relation between t-embeddings and T-graphs associated with the same weighted planar bipartite graph. We prove that, given such a T-graph, the corresponding t-embeddings are classified by positive stationary measures on this T-graph and describe a reconstruction procedure of the t-embedding given such a measure. As an application, we develop an approach for constructing t-embeddings of arbitrary sufficiently non-degenerate planar bipartite graphs.

</details>


### [88] [An Accurate Zeroth-Order Perturbation Theory for Solving Power-Law Potentials within the Framework of the Asymptotic Iteration Method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24805)
*A. J. Sous,Ibsal Assi,Nasser Saad*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在渐近迭代法（asymptotic iteration method, AIM）框架下提出一种零阶微扰理论（zeroth-order perturbation theory），用于求解球对称奇异势（spherically symmetric singular potentials）下的薛定谔方程（Schrödinger equation），并通过对四次与六次奇异势的算例验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 球对称奇异势在分子物理与原子物理等领域应用广泛，但直接用渐近迭代法求解此类问题会出现振荡行为，影响解的质量，因此需要一种能够规避该振荡的求解方案。

Method: 采用指数型与双曲型势试探替换（potential ansatz substitution），将原始势与新势之差视为小扰动；每个试探势含有一个可选取足够小的自由参数，以保证零阶微扰波动方程解具有足够精度，从而避免AIM解的振荡。

Result: 将该方法应用于若干算例，特别是含四次（quartic）与六次（sextic）奇异性的势，所得结果与其他方法的结果进行对比，表现出良好的一致性。

Conclusion: 所提出的零阶微扰AIM方案可有效求解球对称奇异势的薛定谔方程，并规避直接使用AIM时的振荡问题；与其他方法结果的吻合支持该方法的可靠性。

Abstract: Spherically symmetric singular potentials have a wide range of applications, such as molecular and atomic physics. In this work, we present a zeroth-order perturbation theory for solving the Schrödinger equation within the framework of the asymptotic iteration method (AIM). We rely on exponential and hyperbolic potential ansatz substitutions, where the difference between the original and new potential is treated as a small perturbation. Each potential ansatz has a free parameter chosen small enough to give an accurate zeroth-order perturbative wave equation solution. This method avoids the oscillatory behavior observed in the asymptotic iteration solutions when applying the AIM to such problems. Finally, we illustrate our approach by solving a few problems, particularly potentials with quartic and sextic singularities. We compare our results with those obtained via other methods and find good agreement.

</details>


### [89] [Eynard-Orantin Topological Recursion, Moduli-Space Virasoro Constraints, and Semisimple CohFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24980)
*Dingkang Xiong,Jian Zhou*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文利用 Virasoro 约束（Virasoro constraints）证明了多分歧点情形下 Eynard-Orantin 拓扑递归（topological recursion）的交数公式，并给出 DOSS 对应关系的 Virasoro 理论证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Eynard 针对含多个简单分歧点（simple ramification points）的 Eynard-Orantin 拓扑递归提出了交数公式（intersection-number formula），而 DOSS 关于拓扑递归与 Givental 公式在半单上同调场论（semisimple cohomological field theories）中的等同亦有待 Virasoro 层面的论证。

Method: 核心构造是通过由 Bergman 核（Bergman kernel）决定的二次微分算子（quadratic differential operator）耦合各分歧点对应的高阶 Weil-Petersson 配分函数（higher Weil-Petersson partition functions）；并将该算子等同于 Givental 量子化 R-矩阵（quantized R-matrix）的二次部分。此外在固定 Bergman 核/R-矩阵的前提下允许 y-函数（y-function）变化。

Result: 借助 Virasoro 约束证明了 Eynard 的交数公式，并识别出耦合算子与 Givental 量子化 R-矩阵二次部分的同一性，从而获得 DOSS 等同关系的 Virasoro 理论证明；同时表明保持 Bergman 核固定而改变 y 等价于移动相应祖先势（ancestor potential）的赋值点。

Conclusion: 研究为拓扑递归与 Givental 形式化之间的 DOSS 对应提供了 Virasoro 理论视角的证明，并揭示了 y-函数自由度对应于祖先势赋值点的平移。

Abstract: We use Virasoro constraints to prove Eynard's intersection-number formula for Eynard-Orantin topological recursion with several simple ramification points. The key construction couples the higher Weil-Petersson partition functions associated with the individual ramification points through a quadratic differential operator determined by the Bergman kernel. We identify this operator with the quadratic part of Givental's quantized $R$-matrix, thereby obtaining a Virasoro-theoretic proof of the DOSS identification of topological recursion with Givental's formula for semisimple cohomological field theories. Furthermore, while the DOSS identification fixes the distinguished $y$-function, we keep the Bergman kernel/$R$-matrix fixed and allow $y$ to vary, showing that this amounts to shifting the evaluation point of the corresponding ancestor potential.

</details>


### [90] [Spectral Curves for Semisimple Conformal Frobenius Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24985)
*Dingkang Xiong,Jian Zhou*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文为带平坦单位（flat unit）的半单共形Frobenius流形（semisimple conformal Frobenius manifolds）构造连通谱曲线（connected spectral curves），并证明其上Eynard-Orantin拓扑递归（topological recursion）可重现Givental-Teleman重构的稳定祖先关联子（stable ancestor correlators）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将Givental-Teleman重构所给出的稳定祖先关联子，与谱曲线上的Eynard-Orantin拓扑递归框架建立联系，从而为Frobenius流形的关联子计算提供谱曲线语言下的统一描述。

Method: 利用第二结构方程（second structure equation）的周期（periods）构造谱曲线，曲线定义在复平面的单连通区域（simply connected domains）上；并将该构造应用于 $\mathbb P^n$（$n\ge2$）某点处的量子上同调（quantum cohomology）。

Result: 拓扑递归复现了稳定祖先关联子；对 $\mathbb P^n$ 得到以Hecke三角群（Hecke triangle groups）与超几何函数（hypergeometric functions）表示的显式谱曲线数据；对 $\mathbb P^2$ 与 $\mathbb P^3$，给出包括双微分（bidifferentials）在内的完整谱曲线数据，并以经典模形式（modular forms）与拟模形式（quasimodular forms）表达。

Conclusion: 该构造为半单共形Frobenius流形提供了以谱曲线与拓扑递归为核心的描述框架，并在射影空间的量子上同调情形下给出可由模形式显式刻画的谱曲线数据。

Abstract: We construct connected spectral curves for semisimple conformal Frobenius manifolds with flat unit, using the periods of the second structure equation. Eynard-Orantin topological recursion on these curves reproduces the stable ancestor correlators of Givental-Teleman reconstruction. The spectral curves are defined on simply connected domains in the complex plane. We apply the construction to the quantum cohomology of $\mathbb P^n$ for $n\ge2$ at a point, obtaining explicit spectral curve data in terms of Hecke triangle groups and hypergeometric functions. Finally, for $\mathbb P^2$ and $\mathbb P^3$, we express the complete spectral curve data, including the bidifferentials, in classical modular and quasimodular forms.

</details>


### [91] [Thoughts on Quantum Events, Quantizing Gravity and von Neumann Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25010)
*Stephen Bruce Sontz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文为简短意见性文档，不含新结果，提出以"量子事件（quantum events）"为核心的量子理论新进路，并设想借此通向引力（gravity）的量子化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准量子理论文献大多未定义与物理事件相对应的数学对象，作者认为这一缺失值得弥补，并希望借此为广义相对论（general relativity）即爱因斯坦方程（Einstein's equation）的量子化提供路径。

Method: 作者先论证并详加讨论"量子事件"的定义，主张包含足够多量子事件、足以描述量子系统的数学对象是冯·诺依曼代数（von Neumann algebra）；随后提出将狭义相对论（special relativity）的运动学（kinematics），即平直闵可夫斯基空间（flat Minkowski space），作为量子化的第一步，再推进至广义相对论。

Result: 摘要未给出新的计算结果或定量结论；其主张是：狭义相对论与广义相对论中的事件均被归入运动学（kinematics）而非动力学（dynamics），量子事件同样被视为量子理论运动学的一部分，尽管这一看法被认为有违直觉。

Conclusion: 作者认为该框架可自然解释波函数"坍缩"（collapse），并在附录中作了说明；整体为概念性构想，尚未构成完整的引力量子化方案。

Abstract: This short document presents no new results. It is a collection of related ideas that may lead to a way of quantizing gravity. To achieve this it may be relevant to use my recent new approach to quantum theory, which is based on quantum events. Given that most standard quantum texts do not define a mathematical object which corresponds to a physical event, the definition of a quantum event is motivated and discussed in detail. This in turn leads to the idea that the mathematical object that contains enough quantum events for describing a quantum system is a von Neumann algebra. The next idea is that the kinematics of special relativity (as embodied in flat Minkowski space) should be quantized as a first step in the program of the quantization of general relativity, that is, quantizing Einstein's equation. It must be emphasized that the events of special relativity are viewed as part of the kinematics, rather than of the dynamics of that theory. The same holds for general relativity. Similarly, I am viewing quantum events as part of the kinematics of quantum theory, even though some find this to be counterintuitive. An Appendix shows how ``collapse'' is easily understood in this new approach to quantum theory.

</details>


### [92] [Complementary Cauchy Elasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25218)
*Arash Yavari*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立互补柯西弹性（complementary Cauchy elasticity）的非线性理论，以右柯西-格林应变（right Cauchy-Green strain）为第二皮奥拉-基尔霍夫应力（second Piola-Kirchhoff stress）的函数，不预设本构可逆性或互补应变能（complementary strain-energy function）存在性，并借助微分几何与Darboux分类给出各向异性与各向同性固体的局部标准表示及协变热力学表述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统柯西弹性（Cauchy elasticity）以应变表示为应力的函数，但一般非线性固体的本构关系未必可逆，也未必存在应变能函数；因此需要一个不依赖本构可逆性与超弹性（hyperelasticity）假设的互补型非线性弹性理论框架。

Method: 引入互补应力功1-形式（complementary stress-work 1-form），以切向柔度张量（tangent compliance tensor）的主对称性（major symmetry）失效刻画互补超弹性的障碍；对一般隐式弹性，用本构流形（constitutive manifold）的拉格朗日结构（Lagrangian structure）刻画两类应力功1-形式的同步封闭性，并利用Darboux分类得到局部标准表示。

Result: 给出各向异性与各向同性互补柯西弹性固体的局部标准表示，并包含显式的非超弹性（non-hyperelastic）例子；分析运动学与应力约束（含五模固体（pentamode solids）应用），表明内部约束本构关系可自然定义在应力模去反作用应力（reactive stresses）的等价类上；协变表述下能量平衡的协变性可直接导出角动量平衡（balance of angular momentum），这与传统柯西弹性不同。

Conclusion: 对隐式、柯西与互补柯西弹性在有限变形与预应力构型附近做线性化，表明非线性非超弹性固体可以具有超弹性的线性化响应（hyperelastic linearized responses）。

Abstract: We develop a nonlinear theory of complementary Cauchy elasticity in which the right Cauchy-Green strain is prescribed as a function of the second Piola-Kirchhoff stress, without assuming constitutive invertibility or the existence of a complementary strain-energy function. We introduce the complementary stress-work 1-form and characterize the obstruction to complementary hyperelasticity through the failure of major symmetry of the tangent compliance tensor. For general implicit elasticity, we show that the simultaneous closure of the stress-work and complementary stress-work 1-forms is characterized by the Lagrangian structure of the constitutive manifold. Using the Darboux classification, we obtain local canonical representations of anisotropic and isotropic complementary Cauchy elastic solids, including explicit non-hyperelastic examples. We investigate kinematic and stress constraints, including applications to pentamode solids, and show that internally constrained constitutive relations are naturally formulated on equivalence classes of stresses modulo reactive stresses. We formulate the balance laws and thermodynamics covariantly and establish that covariance of the energy balance directly yields the balance of angular momentum in complementary Cauchy elasticity, unlike in conventional Cauchy elasticity. Finally, we linearize implicit, Cauchy, and complementary Cauchy elasticity about finitely deformed and prestressed configurations and show that nonlinear non-hyperelastic solids may possess hyperelastic linearized responses.

</details>


### [93] [Response theory at low temperature for weakly interacting quantum spin systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25249)
*Joscha Henheik,Marius Lemm,Tom Wessel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文将正温度绝热理论（adiabatic theory）从弱相互作用晶格费米子（lattice fermions）推广至弱相互作用量子自旋系统（quantum spin systems），首次覆盖非费米型系统，并借助局部不可区分性（local indistinguishability）的局域性完成低温绝热展开与响应理论（response theory）推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Greenblatt、Lange、Marcelli 与 Porta（Commun. Math. Phys. 2024）首次为正温度下相互作用量子系统的绝热理论给出严格论证，但其分析局限于弱相互作用晶格费米子。本文旨在突破费米子框架，为非费米型系统建立相应的正温度响应理论。

Method: 研究对象为弱相互作用量子自旋系统；证明中识别并利用局部不可区分性的局域性质作为低温绝热展开的关键要素。该性质此前与 Adhikari 合作（arXiv:2508.15907）通过团簇展开（cluster expansion）方法结合概率交换技巧（probabilistic swapping trick）建立。

Result: 对一类弱相互作用量子自旋系统导出正温度下的响应理论，从而首次将正温度绝热与响应分析扩展至非费米系统；并表明局部不可区分性的局域性是实施低温绝热展开的有效工具。

Conclusion: 正温度绝热理论可超越费米子设定，推广至量子自旋系统；局部不可区分性的局域性构成该类展开的核心机制，为后续非费米系统的严格绝热与响应分析提供路径。

Abstract: Recent work by Greenblatt, Lange, Marcelli, and Porta (Commun. Math. Phys. 2024) provides the first rigorous justification of adiabatic theory for interacting quantum systems at positive temperature. Their analysis treats weakly interacting lattice fermions and combines a rigorous Wick rotation with fermionic cluster expansion techniques. Here, we study a class of weakly interacting quantum spin systems and derive response theory at positive temperature, thereby covering non-Fermi systems for the first time. Our proof identifies and exploits the locality property of local indistinguishability as a key ingredient in carrying out low-temperature adiabatic expansions. The fact that these systems satisfy local indistinguishability was recently established jointly with Adhikari (arXiv:2508.15907) using cluster expansion methods together with a probabilistic swapping trick.

</details>


### [94] [Upper Bound on the Correlation Energy of a Dense Fermi Gas in the Thermodynamic Limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25262)
*Niels Benedikter,Sascha Lill,Marcello Porta,Benjamin Schlein*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明随机相近似（random phase approximation, RPA）在高密度弱相互作用三维费米气体（Fermi gas）热力学极限下给出关联能（correlation energy）的上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为随机相近似作为关联能上界提供严格数学依据，此前缺乏在热力学极限下的严格证明。

Method: 在平均场标度（mean-field scaling）下（动能与相互作用分别按 ρ^{-2/3} 与 ρ^{-1} 缩放），通过费米面上各小块（patches）对应的集体粒子—空穴激发（particle–hole excitations）的准玻色型 Bogoliubov 变换构造关联试探态，并控制激发的位形空间局域化，结合（局域）粒子数算符期望值的迭代 Duhamel 展开获得广延估计。

Result: 得到广延性估计，从而论证约化为有效二次哈密顿量（effective quadratic Hamiltonian）及其近似对角化，并由此给出 RPA 关联能的上界。

Conclusion: 随机相近似在高密度弱相互作用三维费米气体热力学极限下构成关联能的严格上界。

Abstract: We prove that the random phase approximation provides an upper bound on the correlation energy of a three-dimensional, weakly interacting, Fermi gas in the thermodynamic limit at high density. More precisely, we consider a mean-field scaling regime where the kinetic and interaction Hamiltonian are scaled by $ρ^{-2/3}$ and $ρ^{-1}$, respectively. We use a correlated trial state constructed through a quasi-bosonic Bogoliubov transformation of collective particle--hole excitations associated to patches on the Fermi surface. Key of the proof is to control the position-space localization of the collective excitations. Combined with an iterated Duhamel expansion of the expectation values of (localized) particle number operators we obtain extensive estimates, which justify the reduction to an effective quadratic Hamiltonian and its approximate diagonalization.

</details>


### [95] [De Finetti Structures in Networks of Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25280)
*Gavin Hull,Marco Merkli*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明在一般连通性假设下，图顶点上 N 个全同量子系统网络中固定子系统 S 的约化态在 N→∞ 时收敛为对称积态的凸组合（de Finetti 表示），并给出显式构造与收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准 de Finetti 表示推导依赖系统的可交换性（exchangeability），而量子网络通常不具备可交换性，因此需要为这类非可交换结构建立类似的表示与定量收敛结论。

Method: 将图顶点上的 N 个全同量子系统经成对边耦合（pairwise edge couplings）相互作用，指定 K 个顶点为关注系统 S、其余为储库 R；在 S 与 R 之间的一般连通性假设下，显式构造 de Finetti 表示并量化收敛速率，并应用于 Erdős–Rényi 随机图（random graphs）与量子比特网络。

Result: N→∞ 时 S 的约化态收敛为 K 个单顶点密度矩阵对称积态的凸组合；Erdős–Rényi 随机图中随储库增大，任意固定有限子图的约化态几乎必然收敛到显式确定的积态；并给出 S 的纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）的面积律（area law）、S–R 相互作用平均场与涨落标度分析，以及 von Neumann 熵、互信息（mutual information）与纠缠等信息的定量收敛估计。

Conclusion: de Finetti 型表示可推广至本质非可交换的量子网络，并为纠缠熵面积律及多种信息论量的收敛提供统一定量框架。

Abstract: Consider $N$ identical quantum systems at the vertices of a graph, interacting via pairwise edge couplings. We designate $K$ vertices as a system of interest $\mathrm S$ and the remaining $N-K$ as a reservoir $\mathrm R$. Under generic connectivity assumptions between $\mathrm S$ and $\mathrm R$, we show that for $N\to\infty$, the reduced state of $\mathrm S$ converges to a convex combination of symmetric product states of $K$ single-vertex density matrices -- called a de Finetti representation. Unlike in standard derivations for symmetric (exchangeable) systems, we establish de Finetti representations for quantum networks, which are inherently non-exchangeable. We construct the representations explicitly and quantify the convergence rate. We show that Erdős--Rényi random graphs exhibit the de Finetti structure: as the reservoir grows, the reduced state of any fixed finite subgraph converges almost surely to an explicit deterministic product state. As applications, we establish a general area law for the entanglement entropy of $\mathrm S$, analyze mean-field and fluctuation scalings of the $\mathrm S$-$\mathrm R$ interaction, and illustrate our results on qubit networks. We also derive quantitative convergence estimates for information-theoretic quantities, including von Neumann entropy, mutual information, and entanglement within $\mathrm S$ and between $\mathrm S$ and $\mathrm R$.

</details>


### [96] [Symmetry algebra of a charged harmonic oscillator in the field of the Dirac magnetic monopole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25408)
*Ian Marquette,Luc Vinet*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文求得35年前已识别但未获代数结构的守恒二阶张量（rank-2 tensor）T与角动量J的协变对易关系，证明经四极矩（quadrupole）平移T'=T+2(H-1)J^(2)后所有对易子成为J的多项式，并在固定能量与J_z扇区得到Heun–Racah型三次代数，进而抽象定义该对称代数并分类其无重数有限维表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电粒子在各项同性谐振子势（isotropic harmonic potential）与Dirac磁单极（Dirac magnetic monopole）束缚下，当离心项使轨道闭合（q为整数）时，能级存在超出旋转不变性（rotational invariance）的额外简并，可理解为去掉l<|q|态后的振子谱。解释这些简并的守恒二阶张量T虽在三十五年前已被识别，但其与角动量J生成的代数始终未得到，构成研究动机。

Method: 以升降算符（ladder operators）构造的守恒张量T为对象，推导其与J的协变形式对易关系；引入由J的四极矩（quadrupole）给出的唯一平移T'=T+2(H-1)J^(2)，使对易子多项式化；在固定能量与J_z的扇区中分析(J^2, T'_zz)生成的三次代数，并将其抽象定义为so(3)协变（covariant）的Heun–Racah代数扩展；随后对该代数的无重数（multiplicity-free）有限维表示进行分类。

Result: 所得代数虽为多项式但非二次型；四极矩平移是唯一能使全部对易子成为J多项式的平移。每个固定能量与J_z扇区中(J^2, T'_zz)生成Heun–Racah型三次代数（cubic algebra），其参数（至多差一个标量平移）对磁荷与磁量子数对称。q=0时T即为(so(3) Casimir, su(3)四极矩)对的代数Heun算符（algebraic Heun operator）。表示分类显示，除系统能级外，非零整数q还给出第二族表示，由磁单极所移除的态、即Newman–Penrose算符的核（kernel）承载。

Conclusion: 该研究给出了磁单极–谐振子系统中扩展对称代数的完整协变表述与抽象刻画，全部关系由升降算符的动力学代数（dynamical algebra）导出，且对整数与半整数电荷同样成立，从而将隐藏简并的代数解释提升为可分类的代数结构。

Abstract: A charged particle bound by an isotropic harmonic potential to a Dirac magnetic monopole, with the centrifugal term that makes the orbits close, has degeneracies beyond rotational invariance: for integer $q$, the product of the electric and magnetic charges, the levels are those of the oscillator with the states of angular momentum $l<|q|$ removed. The conserved rank-2 tensor $T$ built from the ladder operators that accounts for them was identified thirty-five years ago, but the algebra it generates with the angular momentum $\mathbf{J}$ was not obtained. We obtain its relations in covariant form and identify it. It is polynomial but not quadratic, and the shift $T'=T+2(H-1)J^{(2)}$ by the quadrupole of $\mathbf{J}$, and only this one, makes all commutators polynomials in $\mathbf{J}$. In each sector of fixed energy and $J_z$, $(\mathbf{J}^2,T'_{zz})$ generates a cubic algebra of Heun--Racah type whose parameters are symmetric, up to a scalar shift, in the magnetic charge and the magnetic quantum number. At $q=0$, $T$ is the algebraic Heun operator of the pair (Casimir of $\mathfrak{so}(3)$, quadrupole of $\mathfrak{su}(3)$): the symmetry algebra is the $\mathfrak{so}(3)$-covariant extension of a Heun--Racah algebra. We define it abstractly and classify its multiplicity-free finite-dimensional representations at the energies of the system: besides the levels, they include, for nonzero integer $q$, a second family carried by the states the monopole removes, the kernel of the Newman--Penrose operator. All relations are derived from the dynamical algebra of the ladder operators and hold for integer and half-integer charges alike.

</details>


### [97] [Upper bounds on the number of images produced by point-mass lenses at different distances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25594)
*J. An*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究多层点质量引力透镜（multi-plane point-mass lens）系统中孤立像数目（image counts）的上界问题，给出无剪切时不超过 $2\cdot 3^{n-1}$、含剪切时可达 $4^n$ 的界，并推导了 BKK 界的递推关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多平面透镜成像数目随透镜层数如何增长，缺乏系统性的严格上界刻画；已有 Perry 界与真实锐界（sharp bound）之间存在差距，需明确各类界之间的关系与紧致性。

Method: 采用代数几何中的 BKK 界（Bernstein–Kushnirenko–Khovanskii bound）与结式次数（resultant degree）分析，对 $n$ 个不同距离上的点质量透镜逐层成像进行计数，并推导多平面点质量系统的 BKK 界递推式（recurrence）。

Result: 一般源位置下孤立像数不超过 $2\cdot 3^{n-1}$；若每个透镜平面存在剪切（shear），像数可达 $4^n$；所推 BKK 界在 $n\ge 2$ 时严格小于 Perry 界，但每平面仍随透镜数呈二次增长，故可能高估真实锐界。

Conclusion: 真实的锐界预期随透镜数目线性增长，现有 BKK 界因二次依赖而偏松，指示后续需构造更紧的计数界。

Abstract: If a light source is sequentially lensed by $n$ intervening point-mass lenses at distinct distances, the number of isolated images for a generic source position cannot be greater than $2\cdot 3^{n-1}$. If lensing shear is present in each lens plane, the image counts could be as large as $4^n$, however. For a general multi-plane point-mass lens system, we also devise the recurrence for the BKK bound on the image counts, which is strictly less than Perry's bound due to the resultant degree for $n\ge 2$. None the less, this bound is still quadratic in the number of lenses on each plane, so is likely to overestimate the true sharp bound, which is expected to grow linearly with the number of lenses.

</details>


### [98] [Wave-Particle Complementarity in Quantum Walk from a Quantum Mechanical Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26204)
*Norio Konno,Shigeki Matsutani,Etsuo Segawa,Tomoyuki Terada*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文从量子力学视角重新审视量子行走（quantum walk），将其类比为费曼（Feynman）路径积分中的玩具模型，并通过对两个位点支撑初始态的Hadamard行走弱极限（weak limit）推导，表明该模型同时呈现粒子性与波动性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子行走已从数学与物理角度研究约二十年，但本文意在回到量子力学本源，借费曼路径积分（path integral）的玩具模型视角重新评估该模型，凸显其波粒二象性（wave-particle duality）的刻画能力。

Method: 以反射与部分反射（partial reflection）体现粒子性，以干涉（interference）体现波动性；针对初始态支撑于两个位点 ±ℓ 的情形，显式推导 Hadamard 行走的弱极限分布，并分析其干涉因子。

Result: 推导得到该初始态下的弱极限分布：Konno 分布被干涉因子 2cos²(ℓk(x)) 所调制，所得干涉图样类似远场（Fraunhofer 型）衍射。

Conclusion: 量子行走模型能够统一地展示粒子行为（反射、部分反射）与波动行为（干涉），其结果与远场衍射（Fraunhofer diffraction）形式相似，说明量子行走是研究波粒二象性的有效理论框架。

Abstract: A quantum walk is a mathematical model and a quantum version of a random walk. For the past two decades, it has been studied from mathematical and physical viewpoints. It has many desirable properties. This paper reevaluates quantum walk from a quantum mechanical perspective, as Feynman essentially proposed it as a toy model in his path integral method. Then we demonstrate that the quantum walk model exhibits both particle-like and wave-like properties by showing reflection and partial reflection as a particle and interference as a wave. In particular, for an initial state supported at two sites $\pm\ell$, we explicitly derive the weak limit distribution of the Hadamard walk and show that the Konno distribution is modulated by the interference factor $2\cos^2(\ell k(x))$. The resulting interference profile is reminiscent of far-field (Fraunhofer-type) diffraction.

</details>


### [99] [Two-Dimensional Euclidean Free Fermi Fields from Grassmannian Stochastic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26329)
*Claudio Dappiaggi,Francesco Carlo De Vecchi,Pietro Tullio Falzoni,Paolo Rinaldi*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该工作借鉴 Nelson 的欧氏马尔可夫场（Euclidean Markov field）构造，在非交换概率空间（non-commutative probability space）上建立了有质量二维自由狄拉克场（massive two-dimensional free Dirac field）的欧氏对应构造，并给出了其时间零点场与 Wick 多项式的相容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nelson 的欧氏马尔可夫场构造为玻色场提供了概率论刻画，但费米场（Fermi field）缺乏对应的非交换概率构造，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 以非交换概率空间上取值于 Grassmann 代数（Grassmann algebra）的高斯白噪声（Gaussian white noise）为起点，构造布朗运动与平稳 Ornstein–Uhlenbeck 场，并将其实现为时空检验函数上的缓增分布（tempered distribution）。借助对过去滤波的条件期望（conditional expectation）得到时间零点场，并研究其与 Wick 序（Wick ordering）的相容性及时间平移半群。

Result: 所得欧氏费米场的混合两点函数（mixed two-point functions）重现了欧氏狄拉克基本解（Euclidean Dirac fundamental solution）；时间零点场的协方差为反射欧氏狄拉克核（reflected Euclidean Dirac kernel）。条件期望与 Wick 序相容，并把欧氏 Wick 多项式映为对应的时间零点 Wick 多项式；时间平移半群的生成元为一粒子哈密顿量（one-particle Hamiltonian）及其第二量子化（second quantization）。

Conclusion: 该构造在非交换概率框架下实现了 Nelson 型欧氏费米场，并建立了时间零点场、Wick 多项式与时间平移半群之间的系统对应关系。

Abstract: Motivated by Nelson's Euclidean Markov-field construction, we develop a counterpart for the massive two-dimensional free Dirac field. Starting from a Gaussian white noise with values in a Grassmann algebra on a non-commutative probability space, we construct the associated Brownian motion and a stationary Ornstein--Uhlenbeck field. The resulting Euclidean Fermi field is realized as a tempered distribution on spacetime test functions, with values in the underlying algebra. Its mixed two-point functions reproduce the Euclidean Dirac fundamental solution. Time-zero fields are obtained by conditional expectation onto the past filtration generated by the noise, while their covariance is the reflected Euclidean Dirac kernel. We show that conditional expectation is compatible with Wick ordering and maps Euclidean Wick polynomials to the corresponding time-zero Wick polynomials. The Euclidean time-translation semigroup is then constructed on time-zero fields and Wick sectors, with generators given by the one-particle Hamiltonian and by its second quantization.

</details>


### [100] [Average lower bound on magnetic double-well tunneling with asymptotically large well separation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26637)
*Charles L. Fefferman,Say-Yeon Kwon,Jacob Shapiro,Michael I. Weinstein*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 研究了恒垂直磁场中两个相同、光滑、紧支撑势阱之间的隧穿（tunneling），在耦合与磁场固定、阱间距趋于无穷的极限下，证明跳跃系数（hopping coefficient）及最低两个特征值分裂（splitting）在密度为零的间距例外集之外的下界，形式为 e^{-bλd₁²-Cd₁^{1+δ}}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典隧穿分裂的渐近估计多依赖单阱势的径向或反射对称性，本文旨在去掉这些对称性假设，并在磁场存在时给出对阱间距一致成立的下界。

Method: 将显式的朗道预解核（Landau resolvent kernel）与对跳跃系数对数平均（logarithmic averages）的复分析估计相结合，用于处理密度为零的例外间距集。

Result: 对足够小的正磁场，在除密度为零的间距集之外的所有间距上，得到跳跃系数与最低两特征值分裂的下界 e^{-bλd₁²-Cd₁^{1+δ}}，其中 δ∈(0,1) 固定；且不要求单阱势具有径向或反射对称性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在弱磁场与无对称性假设下，双阱间的隧穿耦合与谱分裂仍具有可量化的指数型下界，例外间距集在密度意义下可忽略。

Abstract: We study tunneling between two identical, smooth, compactly supported potential wells in a constant perpendicular magnetic field, with the coupling and magnetic field fixed and the well separation tending to infinity. For a sufficiently small positive magnetic field, we prove lower bounds on the hopping coefficient and on the splitting of the two lowest eigenvalues outside a set of separations of density zero. If the well centers are separated by $2d_1$ and the magnetic field strength is $bλ$, the lower bounds have the form $e^{-bλd_1^2-Cd_1^{1+δ}}$ for every fixed $δ\in(0,1)$. No radial or reflection symmetry of the single-well potential is assumed. The proof combines the explicit Landau resolvent kernel with a complex analytic estimate for logarithmic averages of the hopping coefficient.

</details>

### [101] [A new class of pyramidal central configurations for spatial Newtonian 6-body problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24445)
*Liang Ding,Jin Wang,Jinrong Wang,Zhifu Xie*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该论文在空间牛顿六体问题中构造了一类以非共圆四边形（non-concyclic quadrilateral）为底面的新型金字塔型中心构型（pyramidal central configuration），并给出其基本性质、数值算例以及在三维空间中构造反常解（perverse solutions）的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面牛顿四体问题中非共圆菱形（non-concyclic rhombus）可支撑中心构型，但这种构型无法作为空间五体问题中金字塔型中心构型的底面，这一限制促使作者探寻更高体数情形下是否存在以非共圆四边形为底的新构型。

Method: 研究在空间牛顿六体问题框架下建立以非共圆四边形为底面的金字塔型中心构型的存在性，并分析其若干基本性质，同时针对每种情形提供数值算例以验证理论结果。

Result: 证明了空间牛顿六体问题中一类新的金字塔型中心构型的存在性，其非共圆四边形底面使其区别于已知的空间五体问题金字塔型中心构型；数值算例验证了各项理论结论。

Conclusion: 该类新构型可用于在三维空间中构造反常解（perverse solutions），揭示了中心构型几何与牛顿 N 体问题动力学之间的又一联系。

Abstract: While a non-concyclic rhombus can support a central configuration in the planar Newtonian four-body problem, such a configuration cannot serve as the base of a pyramidal central configuration in the spatial five-body problem. In this paper, we establish the existence of a new class of pyramidal central configurations in the spatial Newtonian six-body problem, characterized by a non-concyclic quadrilateral base. This geometric structure distinguishes the new configurations from the known pyramidal central configurations of the spatial five-body problem. We investigate several fundamental properties of these configurations and provide numerical examples illustrating the theoretical results in each case. Furthermore, we demonstrate that this new class of central configurations can be used to construct perverse solutions in three-dimensional space, revealing an additional connection between the geometry of central configurations and the dynamics of the Newtonian $N$-body problem.

</details>


### [102] [Uniformly hyperbolic strictly convex billiards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26441)
*Wentao Fan,Boris Hasselblatt*

Main category: math.DS

TL;DR: 该文研究严格凸台球（strictly convex billiard）能否为遍历（ergodic）的问题，在两圆适当交集构成的台球中，证明了沿选定截面回归映射下不变锥族（invariant cone family）内向量的一致扩张（uniform expansion）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是回答"严格凸台球是否可遍历"这一长期问题，并为 Chen–Mohr–Zhang–Zhang 猜想及统计性质（statistical properties）的后续证明奠定首个关键步骤。

Method: 方法是取两圆（two circles）的合适交集作为台球域，选取恰当截面（section），在其回归映射下建立不变锥族中向量的一致扩张性。

Result: 获得该截面回归映射下不变锥族的一致扩张，作者称这是通向遍历性证明"首个且最繁重"的步骤；摘要并指出这似为首次显示严格凸台球具有遍历性。

Conclusion: 该结果构成 Chen–Mohr–Zhang–Zhang 猜想证明的第一步，并据摘要表述似为严格凸台球遍历性的首个例证，后续将延伸到遍历性与统计性质的完整证明。

Abstract: Can a strictly convex billiard be ergodic? For the billiard in a suitable intersection of two circles, we establish \emph{uniform} expansion of vectors in an invariant cone family under the return to a well-chosen section. This is the first and most onerous step towards a forthcoming proof of ergodicity (the Chen--Mohr--Zhang--Zhang conjecture) and statistical properties. Together, this appears to be the first time that a strictly convex billiard is shown to be ergodic.

</details>
