### [103] [Two quantum spin liquid states coexisting in the kagome antiferromagnet herbertsmithite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24065)
*Arthur C. Campello,Shengtao Jiang,Wei He,Jiajia Wen,Daniel M. Pajerowski,Steven A. Kivelson,Hong-Chen Jiang,Young S. Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过高分辨率非弹性中子散射与大规模DMRG模拟对比，确定herbertsmithite的Heisenberg哈密顿量能量密度与模拟吻合，并发现两种近简并的能隙量子自旋液体态共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Herbertsmithite作为研究最广泛的量子自旋液体（quantum spin liquid, QSL）候选材料之一，其基态的确切性质仍未解决，需要定量检验自旋哈密顿量并揭示基态基本属性。

Method: 对单晶herbertsmithite（Zn_xCu_{4-x}(OD)_6Cl_2，x≈0.85）进行高分辨率非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering）测量，并与大规模密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group, DMRG）及动力学DMRG模拟进行对比。

Result: 实测自旋关联给出与模拟高度吻合的Heisenberg模型能量密度；DMRG模拟揭示两个近简并的QSL态：二者均保持平移对称性，其中一个保持晶格点群（point-group）对称性，另一个则产生破坏点群对称性的各向异性自旋关联，两者具有不同的自旋结构因子（spin structure factor）；两种态的混合与中子谱符合良好。

Conclusion: 中子散射与模拟之间建立了定量对应关系，表明herbertsmithite处于两种近邻能隙QSL态共存的区域。

Abstract: Herbertsmithite (ZnCu$_3$(OH)$_6$Cl$_2$) is one of the most extensively studied quantum spin liquid (QSL) candidates, yet the precise nature of its ground state remains unresolved. Here, we compare the results of high-resolution inelastic neutron scattering on single crystals of herbertsmithite (Zn$_\mathrm{x}$Cu$_\mathrm{4-x}$(OD)$_6$Cl$_2$, $x\simeq 0.85$) with large-scale density-matrix renormalization group (DMRG) and dynamical DMRG simulations to benchmark the spin Hamiltonian and uncover fundamental properties of the ground state. The measured spin correlations determine an energy density for the Heisenberg model that closely matches simulations, an essential verification of the Hamiltonian. In parallel, DMRG simulations reveal two nearly degenerate QSL states. Both preserve translational symmetry, but one retains the lattice point-group symmetries whereas the other develops anisotropic spin correlations that break point-group symmetry. These two QSLs have distinct spin structure factors, and, remarkably, close agreement with the neutron spectra is achieved with an admixture of the two states. Our results establish a quantitative correspondence between neutron scattering and simulations, and suggest that herbertsmithite realizes a regime in which two proximate gapped QSL states coexist.

</details>


### [104] [Monolayer van der Waals kagome lattice for high-temperature flat-band physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24071)
*Hyungkun Bong,Choongjae Won,Seongyong Lee,Soomin Oh,Phuong X. Nguyen,Raghav Chaturvedi,Sehyun Oh,Jiyong Han,Paul Malinowski,Chad Mowers,Jimin Kim,Sae Hee Ryu,Hyunjong Lee,Jinhyeok Choi,Sangsu Yer,Chris Jozwiak,Aaron Bostwick,Eli Rotenberg,Craig M. Polley,Matthew D. Watson,Timur K. Kim,Heejun Yang,Jieun Lee,Kin Fai Mak,Jie Shan,Jae-Hoon Park,Gil Young Cho,Kyle M. Shen,Mingu Kang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作首次实现了理想二维单层kagome晶格（A2T3X4家族），通过nano-ARPES直接观测到费米能级下方孤立的kagome平带，并结合栅极调控与多体计算展示其作为高电子密度平带平台的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既往kagome研究多局限于三维块体晶体，真正二维kagome体系的缺失限制了拓扑、关联与阻挫（frustration）交织的新物理探索，尤其难以获得可在实验上直接访问的平带（flat band）物理。

Method: 利用新型范德华（van der Waals, vdW）kagome材料家族A2T3X4（A = K, Rb, Cs; T = Ni, Pd, Pt; X = S, Se）制备至单层极限，采用纳米聚焦角分辨光电子能谱（nano-focused angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, nano-ARPES）表征电子结构，并通过栅极工程（gate-engineering）调节平带位置，辅以多体计算（many-body calculations）支持。

Result: nano-ARPES在单层极限下直接观测到费米能级下方孤立的kagome衍生平带；栅极调控可将该平带移至费米能级，实现部分填充平带物理的直接访问。相较基于莫尔（moire）的人工平带体系，其本征平带电子密度高出220倍以上。

Conclusion: 二维vdW kagome本征平带体系开辟了新的密度与能量区间，为将分数量子反常霍尔态（fractional quantum anomalous Hall states）等演生平带现象推广至更高温度提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Kagome lattice materials host a remarkably rich landscape of novel quantum phenomena, all arising from the intrinsic interplay of topology, correlation, and frustration. While previous studies have been mostly limited to bulk three-dimensional (3D) kagome crystals, the realization of a true two-dimensional (2D) kagome system has remained a long-standing goal, to unlock fundamentally new physics and engineering pathways inaccessible in 3D systems. In this work, we establish the first ideal 2D monolayer kagome lattices by leveraging a novel and versatile van der Waals (vdW) kagome family, A2T3X4 (A = K, Rb, Cs; T = Ni, Pd, Pt; X = S, Se). Our nano-focused angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (nano-ARPES) directly characterizes the electronic structure of A2T3X4 down to the monolayer limit, notably revealing an isolated kagome-derived flat band just below the Fermi level. Via gate-engineering of 2D A2T3X4, the kagome flat band can be further tuned to the Fermi level, allowing direct access to the partially filled flat band physics and establishing a qualitatively new 2D flat band platform. Compared to existing moire-based artificial flat band systems, this vdW kagome-based intrinsic flat band system realizes more than 220 times higher flat band electron density, opening an entirely new density and energy regime for exploring 2D flat band physics. Harnessing this high-electron-density flat band in 2D vdW kagome system provides a clear pathway to scale emergent flat band phenomena - such as fractional quantum anomalous Hall states - to far higher temperatures than currently achievable, as long-envisioned in theory and further supported by our many-body calculations.

</details>


### [105] [Kohler's Rule Holds in the Strange Metallic Hubbard Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24208)
*Rebekah M. Jin,Emily Z. Zhang,Jixun K. Ding,Brian Moritz,Thomas P. Devereaux*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用行列式量子蒙特卡洛（determinant quantum Monte Carlo）计算空穴掺杂 Hubbard-Hofstadter 模型的磁阻，发现尽管存在超越 Mott-Ioffe-Regel 极限的 T-线性电阻率且无良定义准粒子，Kohler 规则（Kohler's rule）在较宽参数范围内仍成立，且其出现与局域单重态关联（local singlet correlations）的起始温度 T*≈t 吻合，表明强关联与奇异金属性本身并不必然产生违反 Kohler 规则的异常磁阻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要指出，许多强关联奇异金属（strange metals）中报道了违反 Kohler 规则的非常规磁输运，但其确切起源仍不清楚，本文旨在厘清这一违反现象的物理来源。

Method: 采用行列式量子蒙特卡洛方法计算空穴掺杂 Hubbard-Hofstadter 模型的磁阻，并在较宽的格点相互作用强度与空穴掺杂范围内检验 Kohler 标度（Kohler scaling）；结合双占据（double occupancy）与比热（specific heat）确定交叉温度 T*。

Result: 在电阻率呈 T-线性（T-linear resistivity）且超过 Mott-Ioffe-Regel 极限、名义上不存在良定义准粒子的条件下，Kohler 规则仍出人意料地在较宽参数范围内成立；Kohler 标度在由双占据与比热识别的交叉温度 T*≈t 以下出现，并与格点间局域单重态关联的起始相吻合。

Conclusion: 作者提出该区域内的 Kohler 标度反映了一种更普遍的多体量子相干性（many-body quantum coherence）；结果表明强关联与奇异金属性本身通常不会直接产生异常磁阻，Kohler 违反的起源需另寻解释。

Abstract: Unconventional magnetotransport violating Kohler's rule has been reported in many strongly correlated strange metals, but the exact origin of these violations is yet unknown. To investigate this question, we use determinant quantum Monte Carlo to compute the magnetoresistance of the hole-doped Hubbard-Hofstadter model. Despite $T$-linear resistivity exceeding the Mott-Ioffe-Regel limit and the nominal absence of well-defined quasiparticles, we find Kohler's rule unexpectedly holds across a wide range of on-site interaction strengths and hole dopings. In particular, Kohler emerges below a crossover temperature $T^\star\approx t$ identified from the double occupancy and specific heat, coinciding with the onset of local singlet correlations between lattice sites. We thus propose that Kohler scaling in this regime reflects a more general form of many-body quantum coherence. Our results show that strong correlations and strange metallicity do not themselves generically produce anomalous magnetoresistance.

</details>


### [106] [Theory of High-Temperature Fractional Chern Phases in Monolayer van der Waals Kagome Materials A$_2$T$_3$X$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24633)
*Soomin Oh,Jiyong Han,Jinhyeok Choi,Donghae Seo,Mingu Kang,Gil Young Cho*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文理论提出，新合成的范德华（van der Waals）kagome 家族单层 A₂T₃X₄ 是实现原子尺度分数量子陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator）的候选平台，兼具本征拓扑平带与强库仑相互作用，可望在更高能标下实现分数化（fractionalization）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分数量子陈绝缘体仅在莫尔材料（moiré materials）中实现，其超晶格周期长、削弱电子相互作用，使相关相仅存于极低温；早期设想的原子尺度拓扑平带平台此前缺乏合适材料。

Method: 基于密度泛函理论（density functional theory）导出模型并进行严格对角化（exact diagonalization），在允许至多一个带间电子-空穴激发的带混合条件下研究 A₂T₃X₄ 单层的多体相图。

Result: 计算显示宽填充范围内存在铁磁性，支持多种分数量子陈绝缘体、反常复合费米液体（anomalous composite Fermi liquid）与电荷有序相；在 Cs₂Pd₃S₄ 中分数量子陈绝缘体的多体能隙（many-body gap）约 26 meV。反平行磁化的双层结构可在实际层间距下承载分数量子自旋霍尔绝缘体（fractional quantum spin Hall insulator）。

Conclusion: A₂T₃X₄ 家族为实验可及的材料平台，化学组成、载流子密度与堆叠方式提供互补调控途径，有望在更高能标下实现分数化并调控拓扑序（topological order）。

Abstract: Fractional Chern insulators bring the fractional quantum Hall effect, with its anyonic excitations, to zero magnetic field. So far they have been realized in moiré materials, where the long superlattice period weakens electronic interactions and confines these phases to cryogenic temperatures. Early proposals instead envisioned topological flat bands on atomic-scale lattices, where fractionalization could persist to far higher temperatures, but a suitable material has been lacking. Here we theoretically identify monolayers of the recently synthesized van der Waals kagome family $\mathrm{A}_2\mathrm{T}_3\mathrm{X}_4$ as such a platform, combining intrinsic topological flat bands and strong Coulomb interactions with gate-tunable carrier density and layer assembly. Exact diagonalization of models derived from density functional theory reveals ferromagnetism over broad filling ranges, supporting diverse fractional Chern insulators, anomalous composite Fermi liquids, and charge-ordered phases. In particular, in $\mathrm{Cs}_2\mathrm{Pd}_3\mathrm{S}_4$, the fractional Chern insulator exhibits a many-body gap of approximately 26 meV in the largest system accessible with band mixing allowing at most one interband electron-hole excitation. Layer assembly further provides a natural route to fractional quantum spin Hall insulators: bilayers with antiparallel magnetizations support this phase at realistic separations. These results identify an experimentally accessible material family in which chemical composition, carrier density, and stacking offer complementary routes to fractionalization at enhanced energy scales and control over topological order.

</details>


### [107] [Microwave probing of magnetic phases in a dipolar-exchange magnet LiGdF$_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24734)
*S. S. Sosin,A. I. Smirnov,T. A. Soldatov,V. S. Edelman,O. A. Morozov,S. L. Korableva,I. V. Romanova*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 对弱各向异性偶极-交换反铁磁体（dipolar-exchange antiferromagnet）LiGdF$_4$ 单晶开展低温磁共振测量，观察到有序相形成导致的谱线转变、沿四重轴磁场下的系列场致相变，以及低场相与饱和场以上的清晰共振谱，并用交换与偶极相互作用竞争解释其延迟有序与复杂相图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究弱各向异性偶极-交换反铁磁体中的磁阻挫（magnetic frustration）如何影响有序行为与磁相图，LiGdF$_4$ 作为代表性体系可提供实验依据。

Method: 对 LiGdF$_4$ 单晶样品进行低温磁共振测量（magnetic resonance），并在沿晶体四重轴（fourfold axis）施加外磁场条件下探测共振谱随温度和磁场的变化。

Result: 在 $T_N=0.19(1)$ K 以下观察到有序相形成引起的共振谱转变；沿四重轴加场时探测到一系列场致相变（field-induced phase transitions）；在其中一个低场磁相以及饱和场（saturation field）以上均观测到分辨良好的磁共振谱。

Conclusion: 交换与偶极相互作用（exchange and dipolar interactions）的竞争作为一种特殊类型的磁阻挫，被认为是该体系延迟有序（delayed ordering）与复杂磁相图（magnetic phase diagram）的可能原因。

Abstract: We report the results of low-temperature magnetic resonance measurements performed on a single-crystal sample of a weakly anisotropic dipolar-exchange antiferromagnet LiGdF$_4$. The transformation of a resonance spectrum due to the formation of the ordered phase is observed at temperatures below $T_N=0.19(1)$ K. A sequence of field-induced phase transitions is detected for the external magnetic field applied along the fourfold axis of the crystal. A well-resolved magnetic resonance spectrum is observed in one of the low-field magnetic phases as well as above the saturation field. Competing exchange and dipolar interactions, as a special type of magnetic frustration, are suggested to account for the delayed ordering and complex magnetic phase diagram of the system.

</details>


### [108] [Interaction-driven competing instabilities in two-dimensional checkerboard quadratic band crossing semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25046)
*Jia-Hao Wen,Wen-Hao Bian,Jing Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了自旋-1/2棋盘晶格二维二次能带交叉点（QBCP）半金属中全部16种边缘短程费米子-费米子相互作用驱动的低能物理，通过动量壳重整化群（RG）给出耦合流方程，识别出由固定点控制的多类不稳定性及主导有序相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维QBCP半金属是相互作用驱动量子临界性（quantum criticality）的重要平台，但完整的费米子-费米子相互作用所扮演的角色尚未被充分探索，此前工作多基于简化模型。

Method: 考虑自旋-1/2棋盘晶格（checkerboard-lattice）QBCP半金属中全部16种边缘短程（marginally short-range）费米子-费米子相互作用，采用动量壳重整化群（momentum-shell renormalization group）推导耦合流方程；并通过引入源项、计算磁化率（susceptibility）来判定主导与次主导不稳定性。

Result: 低能物理由一系列固定点支配：依初始条件可出现单个稳定固定点、若干离散固定点，或由少数变量参数化的三组连续固定点。主导相包括向列自旋向列（nematic spin-nematic, NSN）、量子反常霍尔（quantum anomalous Hall, QAH）、手征超导（chiral superconducting, SC₁）与量子自旋霍尔（quantum spin Hall, QSH）序。

Conclusion: 非对角相互作用（off-diagonal interactions）产生可促进简化模型中缺失有序相的项；连续固定点群允许对主导不稳定性进行系统调节；并出现轨迹依赖（trajectory-dependent）的不稳定性，即主导态由完整RG流而非仅固定点决定。结果为二维QBCP半金属及相关电子体系的低能量子临界行为与相不稳定性提供了较完整的理论认识。

Abstract: Two-dimensional quadratic band crossing point (QBCP) semimetals serve as a fertile platform for interaction-driven quantum criticality, nevertheless, the role of the complete fermion-fermion interactions remains largely unexplored. To address this, we systematically investigate the low-energy physics of a spin-1/2 checkerboard-lattice QBCP semimetal by considering all 16 marginally short-range fermion-fermion interactions. Using the momentum-shell renormalization group, we derive the coupled flow equations, which reveal that the low-energy physics is governed by a series of fixed points. Depending on the initial conditions, the system may exhibit a single stable fixed point, several discrete fixed points, or three continuous groups of fixed points parameterized by a few variables. By introducing source terms and evaluating susceptibilities, we identify the dominant and subdominant instabilities. The leading phases include nematic spin-nematic (NSN), quantum anomalous Hall (QAH), chiral superconducting ($\mathrm{SC}_1$), and quantum spin Hall (QSH) orders. Moreover, several features distinguish our findings from previous simplified models. Off-diagonal interactions generate terms that promote orders absent in such models, the continuous groups allow systematic tuning of the leading instability, and trajectory-dependent instabilities emerge where the full RG flow, rather than the fixed point alone, determines the dominant state. These results offer a comprehensive theoretical understanding of low-energy quantum critical behaviors and phase instabilities in two-dimensional QBCP semimetals and related electron systems.

</details>


### [109] [Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions and deconfined criticality in SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25081)
*Yasir Iqbal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在SU(2)规范理论中加入Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya（DM）相互作用后，时间反演与自旋-轨道锁定的滑移对称性禁止任何规范单态自旋子质量，故SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$仍是去禁闭量子临界（deconfined criticality）的候选平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$是去禁闭临界性的主要候选材料，但既有临界区理论忽略了会使kagome狄拉克自旋液体（Dirac spin liquid）产生能隙的DM相互作用，该缺失需被补足以判断其临界性是否成立。

Method: 将DM相互作用加入SU(2)规范理论，分析时间反演与自旋-轨道锁定滑移对称性对规范单态自旋子质量的约束，并分别考察面内与面外DM的作用及SO(5)各向异性的微扰阶数。

Result: 时间反演与自旋-轨道锁定滑移在所有阶上禁止规范单态自旋子质量；面内DM仅使自旋子发生扭曲，在0.03–0.17 K以下重构节点；面外DM为无关（irrelevant）微扰，SO(5)各向异性仅在二阶出现。

Conclusion: 该材料仍可作为去禁闭临界性的研究平台，并可由核磁共振（NMR）检验：沿[100]方向施加磁场会诱导Néel矩，而沿[001]方向施加磁场则不会。

Abstract: SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$ is the leading candidate for deconfined criticality, yet theories of its critical regime omit the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) interactions, which gap the kagome Dirac spin liquid. Adding them to the SU(2) gauge theory, we show that time reversal and a spin-orbit-locked glide forbid any gauge-singlet spinon mass to all orders. In-plane DM only twists the spinons, reconstructing the nodes below $0.03$-$0.17$ K, while out-of-plane DM is irrelevant and SO(5) anisotropy arises only at second order. The material thus remains a platform for deconfined criticality, which NMR can test, since a $[100]$ field induces a Néel moment and a $[001]$ field does not.

</details>


### [110] [Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interactions in the SU(2) gauge theory of the Shastry-Sutherland Dirac spin liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25082)
*Yasir Iqbal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文在自旋-轨道锁定的空间群下对 Shastry-Sutherland Dirac 自旋液体（Dirac spin liquid）的 SU(2) 规范理论中费米双线性项（fermion bilinears）进行分类，并按 1/N 领头阶与单圈（one-loop）处理 SrCu₂(BO₃)₂ 的 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya（DM）相互作用，给出 DM、无序与磁场对赝临界窗口和低能物态选择的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的是判明 DM 相互作用能否打开自旋子（spinon）能隙或破坏 SO(5) 临界点，以及在零场、有限场和临界压力下确定该材料的低能有序态与可观测判据。

Method: 方法包括：在自旋-轨道锁定空间群下对费米双线性项做对称性分类；在 1/N 领头阶与单圈近似处理 DM 相互作用和规范流；分析单重态双线性算符的维度、Yukawa 耦合以及带接触（band touching）对规范场的屏蔽。

Result: 线性阶 DM 既不能产生自旋子能隙，也不能在 T* 以上破坏 SO(5) 点；对称性禁止任意阶的局域规范单重态费米质量。面内耦合仅给出维度恰为 2 的守恒自旋流，设定 T*=4λ≈0.03–0.17 K，面外耦合仅给出不可约化的味自旋-2流；DM 诱导的 Yukawa 耦合小于约束赝临界窗口的耦合的 2%。

Conclusion: 在 T* 以下、临界压力的二维样品中，Dirac 锥重构为手征保护的 d 波二次带接触；单圈流在 T**≈(0.03–0.1)λ 终止，对材料交换和多数随机耦合落在含价键序（valence-bond order）的射线上，否则为有隙手征 ℤ₂ 自旋液体。DM 对两种 plaquette 图案的偏置至多约 10⁻⁴ 且方向在 O(1/N) 未定；非磁淬火无序仅对 plaquette 双重态耦合相关随机场。沿 [100] 的磁场诱导线性 (π,π) Néel 矩，沿 [001] 则不诱导，该选择定则可由压力下 ¹¹B NMR 检验。

Abstract: We classify the fermion bilinears of the SU(2) gauge theory of the Shastry-Sutherland Dirac spin liquids under the spin-orbit-locked space group and treat the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) interactions of SrCu$_2$(BO$_3$)$_2$ at leading order in $1/N$ and at one loop. At linear order DM can neither gap the spinons nor destabilize the SO(5) point above a scale $T^*$. Symmetry forbids every local gauge-singlet fermion mass at any order. Among the singlet bilinears, the in-plane couplings induce only a conserved spin current of dimension exactly 2, which sets $T^*=4λ\simeq0.03$-$0.17$ K, and the out-of-plane coupling only a flavored spin-2 current, irrelevant at a unitary critical point. DM-induced Yukawa couplings start below $2\%$ of the one that bounds the pseudocritical window. In zero field, measured samples order above $1$ K, at or above $T^*$. Below $T^*$, in a two-dimensional sample at the critical pressure, the Dirac cones reconstruct into a chirally protected $d$-wave quadratic band touching whose only gauge-singlet gap channels are the two valence-bond components and the scalar chirality. A one-loop flow, with the gauge field screened by the band touching, ends at $T^{**}\sim(0.03$-$0.1)λ$ on a ray containing valence-bond order for the material's exchange and for most random couplings, and otherwise on a gapped chiral $\mathbb{Z}_2$ spin liquid. The DM bias between the two plaquette patterns is at most of order $10^{-4}$, with a direction not fixed at $O(1/N)$, whereas non-magnetic quenched disorder couples a relevant random field to the plaquette doublet and none to the chirality. A field along $[100]$ induces a $(π,π)$ Néel moment linear in the field; a field along $[001]$ does not. This selection rule also holds in the dimer phase and is testable by $^{11}$B NMR under pressure.

</details>


### [111] [Large exchange bias from coupled antiferromagnetic order and glassy magnetic freezing in $Co_{0.36}NbS_{2}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25102)
*Suman Mondal,Himadri Roy Dakua,Raz Luger,Muntaser Naamneh,Eran Maniv*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 单晶（single-crystalline）Co_{0.36}NbS_{2}中观察到约26 K的反铁磁序（antiferromagnetic order）及低温下的玻璃态冻结（glassy freezing）行为，冻结的未补偿磁矩与反铁磁基体耦合产生显著交换偏置（exchange bias），最大|H_EB|约4.4 kOe（1.8 K）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探究非化学计量磁性NbS_{2}插层化合物（intercalates）中的磁有序与交换偏置现象，检验反铁磁-玻璃态磁矩耦合是否可作为获得交换偏置的普适途径。

Method: 采用磁化测量（magnetization measurements）手段，对单晶Co_{0.36}NbS_{2}进行场冷（field cooling）后的M(H)磁滞回线测量，并考察训练效应（training effect）、场扫掠与冷却场协议依赖性。

Result: 场冷后M(H)回线出现明显交换偏置偏移，70 kOe场冷扫掠协议下1.8 K时|H_EB|约4.4 kOe；偏置在阻塞温度（blocking temperature）T_B约12 K附近消失，显著低于T_N；训练效应与协议依赖性均被观测到。

Conclusion: 结果支持冻结未补偿磁矩与反铁磁基体交换耦合的解释，并提示反铁磁-玻璃态磁矩耦合可能为非化学计量磁性NbS_{2}插层物中交换偏置提供更广泛的实现途径。

Abstract: Magnetization measurements on single-crystalline $Co_{0.36}NbS_{2}$ reveal antiferromagnetic order near $T_{N} \simeq 26 K$ and a lower-temperature history-dependent magnetic response consistent with glassy freezing of a subset of magnetic moments. After field cooling, $M(H)$ loops exhibit a sizable exchange-bias shift, reaching $|H_{EB}| \simeq 4.4 kOe$ at $1.8 K$ for a $70 kOe$ field-cooling sweep protocol. The training effect, field-sweep and cooling-field protocol dependence, and disappearance of the bias near a blocking temperature $T_{B} \simeq 12 K$, well below $T_{N}$, support an interpretation in which frozen uncompensated moments are exchange-coupled to an antiferromagnetic matrix. These results suggest that antiferromagnetic-glassy-moment coupling may provide a broader route to exchange bias in off-stoichiometric magnetic $NbS_{2}$ intercalates.

</details>


### [112] [Frustration-Assisted Electrical Switching in $Co_xTaS_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25112)
*Matan Sterenberg,Bar Segura,Muntaser Naamneh,Eran Maniv*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作展示了近化学计量比 Co_xTaS_2（一种位于霍尔活性与霍尔非活性多 Q 磁区边界处的范德华反铁磁体）中的阻挫辅助电学翻转，揭示磁阻挫与玻璃态动力学是电重构的核心要素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反铁磁体电学翻转通常被描述为电流诱导力矩作用于刚性序参量，但阻挫磁体中竞争态可形成历史依赖的能量景观，使缓慢集体动力学参与电重构，这一机制此前未被充分认识。

Method: 在微结构器件中施加电流脉冲并测量各向异性电阻与非易失性；结合变温翻转响应分析、独立磁化老化（magnetization aging）与场后弛豫（post-field-relaxation）测量，考察历史依赖磁动力学。

Result: 晶体无分辨的自发霍尔响应，但保留费米能级附近的范霍夫奇点（van Hove singularities），并在 T_N ≈ 31.5 K 以下反铁磁有序；翻转响应分为两个温度依赖的动力学区间而非单一电流阈值，激活电流密度比霍尔活性低插层相约低一个数量级；弛豫强度与翻转幅度成反比。

Conclusion: 磁阻挫与玻璃态动力学（glassy dynamics）是 Co_xTaS_2 电重构的关键要素，确立了通过动力学柔软的反铁磁能量景观实现功能化翻转的途径。

Abstract: Electrical switching in antiferromagnets is commonly described as the action of current-induced torques on a rigid order parameter. In frustrated magnets, however, competing states can create a history-dependent energy landscape in which slow collective dynamics actively participate in electrical reconfiguration. Here, we demonstrate frustration-assisted switching in near-stoichiometric $Co_xTaS_2$, a van der Waals antiferromagnet located at the composition-sensitive boundary between Hall-active and Hall-inactive multi-Q-related magnetic regimes. Although the crystals exhibit no resolved spontaneous Hall response, they retain van Hove singularities near the Fermi level and order antiferromagnetically below $T_N \simeq 31.5 K$. Current pulses write nonvolatile anisotropic resistance states in microstructured devices, with an activation current density nearly an order of magnitude below that reported for the Hall-active lower-intercalation phase. The switching response separates into two temperature-dependent dynamical regimes rather than following a single current threshold. Independent magnetization-aging and post-field-relaxation measurements reveal slow, history-dependent magnetic dynamics, and the relaxation strength closely follows the inverse switching amplitude. These results identify magnetic frustration and glassy dynamics as central ingredients in the electrical reconfiguration of $Co_xTaS_2$, establishing a route to functional switching through a dynamically soft antiferromagnetic landscape.

</details>


### [113] [Categorical classification of non-Fermi liquids in 2+1 dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25713)
*Shubham Kukreja,Dawson M. Willerton,Sung-Sik Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文指出配对通道的四点函数（four-point function）可正面区分不同类型非费米液体（non-Fermi liquids），并借助标度依赖的普适配对相互作用与广义不动点概念，对2+1维非费米液体给出非微扰分类，得到七个"超普适类"（superuniversality classes）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非费米液体的定义性特征（费米面上无准粒子峰）在众多体系中定性相同，二点与三点顶点函数（vertex functions）亦难以区分，导致只能以"否定式"定义来刻画，缺乏正面的分类依据。

Method: 论证配对通道的四点函数包含可正面区分不同非费米液体的丰富信息；采用可刻画标度依赖普适配对相互作用的广义不动点（fixed points）概念，并在大费米动量极限、固定无量纲耦合下，利用运动学约束（kinematic constraints）进行非微扰（non-perturbative）分类。

Result: 与长波长临界涨落（critical fluctuations）耦合的金属被划分为七个超普适类，每一类在温度与裸四费米耦合构成的空间中呈现独特的相图（phase diagram）。

Conclusion: 该工作借助运动学约束，将先前的微扰分类（perturbative classification）推广至强相互作用的非费米液体，实现了以四点配对通道为基础的非微扰分类框架。

Abstract: A defining feature of non-Fermi liquids is a lack of the quasiparticle peak on the Fermi surface in the spectral function, which is usually realized by coupling a finite density of fermions with critical collective fluctuations. However, these features of the two- and three-point vertex functions are qualitatively the same across many non-Fermi liquids, which has made it difficult to classify them beyond the definition by negation. We point out that the four-point function in the pairing channel contains rich information that positively differentiates distinct types of non-Fermi liquids. Using the generalized notion of fixed points that captures scale-dependent universal pairing interaction, we provide a non-perturbative classification of 2+1-dimensional non-Fermi liquids. In the large Fermi momentum limit with fixed dimensionless couplings, metals coupled with long-wavelength critical fluctuations are classified into seven superuniversality classes. Each of them exhibits a unique phase diagram in the space of temperature and bare four-fermion coupling. The present work generalizes the earlier perturbative classification to strongly interacting non-Fermi liquids by leveraging kinematic constraints.

</details>


### [114] [Ferroelastic domain wall driven glassy dynamics and photoresponse in a SrTiO$_3$ (111) two-dimensional electron gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25943)
*Jyotirmay Maity,Manav Beniwal,Pranab Hazra,Sumit Tomar,Srimanta Middey*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究报道 AlO_x/SrTiO_3(111) 界面二维电子气（2DEG）中，极性铁弹畴壁（ferroelastic domain wall, DW）网络对输运与光电响应的调控作用，并证明该耦合可通过载流子密度调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SrTiO_3(111) 基异质结已成为二维电子气（two-dimensional electron gas, 2DEG）的重要平台，但 STO 本征极性铁弹畴壁网络对电荷输运的影响此前基本未被探索。

Method: 制备并对比两种载流子密度不同的 AlO_x/STO(111) 界面 2DEG，测量其低温输运特性（热滞回、电阻弛豫、降温速率依赖、记忆效应）与持续光电导（persistent photoconductivity），并与块体 STO 铁弹畴壁态的已知演化进行对照。

Result: 低载流子密度样品呈现玻璃态电子动力学（glasslike electron dynamics）；弛豫随温度非单调变化——约 80 K 以下增强、25 K 附近达峰、约 12 K 以下消失，其温度轨迹与块体 STO 铁弹畴壁态的演化一致，而非任何电子能量尺度；高载流子密度的金属性界面中这些特征消失。持续光电导在约 80 K 以下急剧上升，与畴壁极性的出现相吻合。

Conclusion: 极性铁弹畴壁构成 STO(111) 基 2DEG 中一种载流子密度可调的电子自由度（electronic degree of freedom），为通过拓扑缺陷（topological defects）调控 2DEG 性质提供了新途径。

Abstract: The (111) orientation of SrTiO$_3$ (STO)-based heterostructures has emerged as a versatile platform for two-dimensional electron gases (2DEGs), yet the influence of STO's polar ferroelastic domain-wall (DW) network on charge transport remains largely unexplored. Here we study AlO$_x$/STO (111) interfaces hosting 2DEGs of different carrier densities. In the lower-density 2DEG, low-temperature transport exhibits thermal hysteresis, slow resistance relaxation, cooling-rate dependence, and memory effects, signaling glasslike electron dynamics. The relaxation is strongly non-monotonic in temperature, increasing below $\sim$80 K, peaking near 25 K, and vanishing below $\sim$12 K, tracking the established evolution of the ferroelastic DW state of bulk STO rather than any electronic energy scale, which identifies the DW network as its origin. These signatures are absent in a second, more metallic interface with much higher carrier density, demonstrating that the electron-DW coupling can be tuned. We further show that polar DWs govern the photoresponse: the persistent photoconductivity rises sharply below $\sim$80 K, coinciding with the onset of DW polarity. Together, these results establish polar ferroelastic DWs as a carrier-density-tunable electronic degree of freedom in STO (111)-based 2DEGs, opening routes to manipulate 2DEG properties through topological defects.

</details>


### [115] [Methods of density functional theory in one dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26012)
*Thomas E. Baker,Kasey Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文讨论一维Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论的高效数值计算策略，并给出可公开获取的配套计算库；同时探讨求取精确性质（含时间依赖）的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密度泛函理论仅由存在性证明在形式上获得保证，因此发展计算方法成为理解完整理论的有用途径；摘要以此为出发点提出需要系统化的数值实现手段。

Method: 按摘要，工作综述一维密度泛函实现所需的表达式，讨论一维Kohn-Sham系统高效计算的数值策略，并给出基于梯度技术的一种替代论证，可应用于精确Kohn-Sham势的时间依赖演化。

Result: 摘要述及可公开获取的配套库，用于一维密度泛函理论计算，并涉及求取精确性质（包括时间依赖）的方法；具体定量结果摘要未给出。

Conclusion: 一维密度泛函的数值方法与公开库可服务于理论与精确性质（含含时情形）的探究，梯度技术的替代论证为含时精确Kohn-Sham势的演化提供了依据。

Abstract: Density functional theory is formally justified by an existence proof. As such, the need for computational methods to study the theory is a useful route to understanding the full theory. In this paper, we discuss the numerical strategies to efficiently evaluate computations for Kohn-Sham systems in one dimension and also methods for determining exact properties, including time-dependence. An accompanying library is also available for computing one-dimensional density functional theory and publicly available. The expressions used to implement the density functional in one-dimension are surveyed. An alternative justification for a gradient-based technique is also shown that can be applied to time-dependent evolution of the exact Kohn-Sham potential.

</details>


### [116] [Evidence of valence bond solid and quantum paramagnetism in the $J_1$--$J_2$--$J_3$ Heisenberg model from pseudofermion functional renormalization group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26135)
*Gün Günal,Erhai Zhao,Ahmet Keleş*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 采用赝费米子泛函重整化群（pseudofermion functional renormalization group, pf-fRG）数值研究方格晶格 $J_1$-$J_2$-$J_3$ 海森堡模型（Heisenberg model）零温相图，发现扩展的量子顺磁（quantum paramagnetic, QP）区可进一步分裂为价键固体（valence bond solid, VBS）区与量子自旋液体（quantum spin liquid, QSL）区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期张量网络（tensor network）研究报道量子顺磁区内可能存在量子自旋液体，本文据此对 $J_1$-$J_2$-$J_3$ 海森堡模型的零温相图进行数值检验。

Method: 使用赝费米子泛函重整化群（pf-fRG），借助改进的频率分辨率（frequency resolution）与更大的晶格截断（lattice truncation）探索更大参数空间，并通过测量二聚体响应（dimer response）对破坏点群对称性（point-group symmetry）的 $J_1$ 键各向异性（bond anisotropy）的敏感性来分辨 QP 区内的键序。

Result: 相比以往 pf-fRG 工作覆盖了显著更大的参数空间，并绘制出扩展 QP 区的相边界；二聚体磁化率（dimer susceptibility）的 fRG 流显示 QP 窗口分裂为两部分：一部分持续增长至最低能标，指示 VBS 序；另一部分在有限能标处达到峰值随后被抑制，指示 QSL。

Conclusion: QP 区并非单一相，而是至少分裂为 VBS 有序区与 QSL 区，为张量网络所报道的 QSL 提供了 pf-fRG 方面的支持性证据。

Abstract: Motivated by recent tensor network studies reporting a quantum spin liquid (QSL) within the quantum paramagnetic (QP) regime, we investigate numerically the zero-temperature phase diagram of the $J_1$--$J_2$--$J_3$ Heisenberg model on the square lattice using the pseudofermion functional renormalization group (pf-fRG). Thanks to improved frequency resolution and larger lattice truncations, we are able to explore a significantly larger parameter space than previous pf-fRG work to map out the phase boundary of an extended QP region. We further resolve possible bond order within the QP regime by measuring the dimer response to small deformations in $J_1$, i.e. bond anisotropy that breaks the point-group symmetry of the Hamiltonian. The functional renormalization group flow of the dimer susceptibility suggests that the QP window splits into two: a region where it grows continuously down to the lowest energy scale to signal valence bond solid (VBS) order, and another region where it reaches a peak at a finite scale and is subsequently suppressed to indicate a QSL.

</details>


### [117] [High-order resonances and emergent coherent dynamics in interacting Stark chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26328)
*Jiahe Chang,Hongzheng Zhao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文在相互作用 Stark 链（interacting Stark chain）中揭示了高阶相互作用—倾斜共振（interaction–tilt resonances）的系统性层级结构，共振阶数对应最小基本交换步数，表现为寿命长、时间尺度依赖阶数的相干振荡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性势梯度（linear potential gradient）可强烈抑制量子动力学，而相互作用能激活共振多体过程，但其高阶结构此前基本未被探索，故本文针对该空白展开研究。

Method: 通过隔离单个共振通道并构建微观有效描述（microscopic effective description），将动力学时间尺度与底层多体交换过程相联系。

Result: 观察到长寿命相干振荡，其时间尺度依赖于共振阶数；动力学时间尺度与初始态结构（initial-state structure）呈显著依赖关系。

Conclusion: 结果深化了对高阶多体共振的理解，并表明公度（commensurate）的相互作用—倾斜比值可产生参数化慢速（parametrically slow）的相干多体动力学层级。

Abstract: A linear potential gradient can strongly suppress quantum dynamics, while interactions can activate resonant many-body processes whose higher-order structure remains largely unexplored. Here we uncover a systematic hierarchy of high-order interaction--tilt resonances in an interacting Stark chain, where the resonance order counts the minimal number of elementary exchange steps involved. These resonances manifest as long-lived coherent oscillations with order-dependent timescales. By isolating individual resonant channels and developing a microscopic effective description, we connect these dynamical timescales to the underlying many-body exchange processes and reveal a pronounced dependence on the initial-state structure. Our results provide crucial insights into high-order many-body resonances and show how commensurate interaction-to-tilt ratios generate a hierarchy of parametrically slow coherent many-body dynamics.

</details>


### [118] [Variational Block Correlated Coupled Cluster Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26646)
*Hunter Brodie,Alexander Shilcusky,Adrian E. Feiguin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种适用于量子蒙特卡洛（quantum Monte Carlo）模拟的变分耦合簇（variational coupled cluster）实现，并借助杨表（Young tableaux）表示波函数系数及其导数以降低变分优化的计算复杂度。在一维与二维海森堡模型（Heisenberg model）上以含双激发与三激发的块相关耦合簇（block correlated coupled cluster, BCCC）拟设验证，精度具竞争力且框架可扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受量子神经态（quantum neural states）近期成功的启发，作者希望将变分耦合簇方法嵌入量子蒙特卡洛框架，以结合两者的优势。

Method: 引入波函数系数及其导数的杨表（Young tableaux）表示，显著降低变分优化的计算复杂度；算法具有通用性，实例化时采用含双激发与三激发的块相关耦合簇（BCCC）拟设，并结合基于虚拟温度（fictitious temperature）的重要性采样（importance sampling）改进收敛。

Result: 在一维与二维海森堡模型（Heisenberg model）上，基于虚拟温度的重要性采样显著改善了收敛性，所得方法达到具有竞争力的精度（competitive accuracy）。

Conclusion: 该方法为变分耦合簇计算提供了一个可扩展（scalable）的框架，并展示出与现有方法相当的精度的潜力。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent success of quantum neural states, we present a variational implementation of the coupled cluster method suitable for quantum Monte Carlo simulations. We introduce a Young tableaux representation of the wave-function coefficients and their derivatives that substantially reduces the computational complexity of the variational optimization. While the algorithm is generic, we illustrate its implementation with an application to the Heisenberg model in one and two dimensions, using the block correlated coupled cluster ansatz (BCCC) with doubles and triples. Convergence is greatly improved by means of importance sampling based on a fictitious temperature. The resulting approach achieves competitive accuracy while providing a scalable framework for variational coupled-cluster calculations.

</details>


### [119] [Kondo Lattice model for field induced triplet pairing in CeRh$_2$As$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26858)
*Indra Gankhuyag,Piers Coleman*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作建立CeRh$_2$As$_2$的Kondo晶格模型（Kondo lattice model），指出Ce位点局域反演对称性破缺驱动的$d$-$f$局域杂化（local hybridization）产生交错反对称自旋轨道耦合（antisymmetric spin-orbit coupling, ASOC），并由此得到近简并的偶宇称与奇宇称超导态及场致宇称转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋三重态超导（spin triplet superconductivity）因拓扑性质与高磁场鲁棒性而受关注；CeRh$_2$As$_2$是兼具偶宇称相与候选奇宇称相、并可经场致相变切换库珀对宇称的独特重费米子超导体。但其超导的重费米子起源及ASOC的来源此前未被探讨。

Method: 构建针对CeRh$_2$As$_2$的Kondo晶格模型，考察Ce位点局域反演对称性破缺下Ce $d$轨道与$f$轨道的局域杂化如何生成ASOC，并在该模型框架内分析超导态的宇称性质与场致宇称转变。

Result: 所得到的Kondo晶格模型给出近简并（nearly degenerate）的偶宇称与奇宇称超导态，并呈现从偶宇称态到场致奇宇称赝自旋三重态（pseudospin triplet state）的宇称转变。

Conclusion: Ce位点局域杂化是ASOC的微观来源，可解释CeRh$_2$As$_2$中场致宇称转变的机制；作者进一步讨论了结果推广至CeSiI等其他重费米子体系（heavy fermion systems）的启示。

Abstract: Spin triplet superconductors are of interest because of their topological properties, and their robustness to high magnetic fields. CeRh$_2$As$_2$ is a unique heavy fermion superconductor with both even-parity and candidate odd-parity superconducting phases with a special feature enabling a field induced phase transition between the two. The field induced transition which switches the parity of the Cooper pairs is enabled by a staggered antisymmetric spin orbit coupling (ASOC) interaction. However, the heavy fermion origins of the superconductivity in CeRh$_2$As$_2$ and the origin of the antisymmetric spin-orbit coupling are currently unexplored. Here, we develop a Kondo lattice model for CeRh$_2$As$_2$ and demonstrate how local hybridization between Cerium $d$- and $f$-orbitals, driven by the locally broken inversion symmetry, at the Cerium sites generates the ASOC. The resulting Kondo lattice model leads to nearly degenerate even and odd parity superconducting states that display a field induced parity transition between an even-parity state and an odd-parity pseudospin triplet state. We discuss the wider implications of our results extended to other heavy fermion systems like CeSiI.

</details>


### [120] [On Higher-Form Fracton Hydrodynamics with Momentum Conservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26862)
*Ben Bobell,Rahul Nandkishore,Andrew Lucas*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用Martin-Siggia-Rose（MSR）有效场论形式，构建了一个表示论（representation theory）框架，用于分析具有奇异守恒律（exotic conservation laws）且平移不变、无自发对称性破缺的系统之流体力学（hydrodynamics），并证明动量守恒对此类理论施加了严格的表示论约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动量守恒（momentum conservation）在流体中普遍存在，但可能无法与具有奇异守恒律的理论（如fracton流体力学）的流体力学相容，除非对称性发生自发破缺。作者希望在无自发对称性破缺的前提下，理解平移不变系统中动量守恒与奇异守恒律共存的可能条件。

Method: 采用Martin-Siggia-Rose（MSR）有效场论形式，发展了一个表示论框架，并在对流体力学形式作温和假设的条件下，推导出两个表示论一致性条件（representation theoretic consistency conditions）和一个广义高斯定律约束（generalized Gauss's law constraint）；随后检验高阶形式对称性（higher-form symmetries）是否满足这些条件并推导其理想流体力学。

Result: 高阶形式对称性满足上述一致性条件，并可推导出相应的理想流体力学（ideal hydrodynamics）；在三维情形下，一形式（one-form）情形给出理想的非相对论磁流体力学（non-relativistic magnetohydrodynamics）；同时证明在SO(3)以及足够大奇维数d的SO(d)下，除高阶形式对称性外不存在其他理论能与动量守恒相容。

Conclusion: 动量守恒对奇异守恒律理论构成严格的分类性限制：在无自发对称性破缺时，仅高阶形式对称性可与动量守恒相容，其他情形（在SO(3)及足够大奇维数SO(d)下）均被排除，为奇异流体力学理论提供了分类判据。

Abstract: Momentum conservation is common in fluids, yet it may be incompatible with the hydrodynamics of theories with exotic conservation laws (such as fracton hydrodynamics) unless symmetries are spontaneously broken. Using the Martin-Siggia-Rose (MSR) effective field theory formalism, we develop a representation-theoretic framework for the hydrodynamics of translationally invariant systems with exotic conservation laws without spontaneous symmetry breaking. Under mild assumptions on the form of hydrodynamics, we show that such theories must satisfy two representation theoretic consistency conditions and a generalized Gauss's law constraint. Higher-form symmetries satisfy these conditions and we derive their ideal hydrodynamics; in three dimensions, the one-form case produces ideal non-relativistic magnetohydrodynamics. Finally, we prove that no other theories are compatible with momentum conservation in SO(3) nor SO($d$), for sufficiently large odd $d$.

</details>

### [121] [Analytic quantum invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24247)
*Rinat Kashaev,Vladimir Mangazeev,Marcos Marino*

Main category: math.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于变形Borel-Laplace重求和（deformed Borel-Laplace resummation）的框架，将量子不变量（quantum invariants）重构为解析不变量（analytic invariants）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子不变量通常以幂级数形式出现，缺乏解析结构；作者希望借助重求和手段赋予其解析意义，并厘清其与既有Borel重求和结果的关系。

Method: 以若干简单纽结的着色Jones多项式（coloured Jones polynomials）为例进行详细分析，导出以$(q,X)$-级数表示的显式解析不变量；并将方法用于假定的八字结Links-Gould多项式序列及三流形不变量中的$q$-级数。

Result: 所得解析不变量与通过Melvin-Morton-Rozansky展开的传统Borel重求和（Borel resummation）结果密切相关但一般不同；对$U_q(\mathfrak{gl}(n|1))$相关的八字结Links-Gould多项式序列构造出解析不变量，并在三流形不变量的$q$-级数中发现与mock模形式（mock modular forms）的联系。

Conclusion: 该框架为将量子不变量转化为解析不变量提供了可行途径，并揭示了其与模形式等解析对象之间的潜在联系。

Abstract: Using a deformed Borel-Laplace resummation, we propose a framework for repackaging quantum invariants as analytic invariants. We illustrate our approach through a detailed analysis of the coloured Jones polynomials of several simple knots, deriving explicit expressions for the resulting analytic invariants in terms of $(q,X)$-series. These invariants are closely related to, but generally distinct from, those obtained via the conventional Borel resummation of the Melvin-Morton-Rozansky expansion. As a further example, we construct an analytic invariant from a sequence of two-variable polynomials conjectured to be the Links-Gould polynomials of the figure-eight knot, associated with the quantum superalgebras $U_q(\mathfrak{gl}(n|1))$. Finally, we apply our technique to $q$-series arising in the study of three-manifold invariants, revealing connections with mock modular forms.

</details>


### [122] [Non-planar contact structures with Calabi-Yau Fillings and topological quantum computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25878)
*Atsuhide Mori*

Main category: math.GT

TL;DR: 本文是作者此前关于平面接触结构（planar contact structures）与拓扑量子计算（topological quantum computation）工作的非平面（non-planar）推广：将页（page）从平面推广为亏格 g、连通绑定（connected binding）的曲面，并研究可提升弧系统、半扭转（half-twist）立方与 Calabi-Yau（CY）Stein 填充的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作局限于平面页、分支点由不相交可提升弧（liftable arcs）配对的情形；本文旨在考察页具亏格 g 且绑定连通时的相应结构，检验 Ising 表示、CY Stein 填充与辫群作用之间的对应是否仍成立。

Method: 将页表述为圆盘的三次简单覆盖（simple degree-three cover），分析极大不相交可提升弧系统（恰含 g 条弧、余下两个分支点）；利用交换两点的半扭转（half-twist）不可提升、但其立方可提升且提升为恒等的事实；并以积分留数判据（integral residue criterion）作为 CY 检验。

Result: 局部化于接触连通和（contact connected sum）的分解自动为 CY；与平面情形不同，CY 分解不必是最短的。固定 g 对融合通道（fusion channels）后，Ising 融合决定剩余一对的通道且编织（braiding）仅以标量作用；Fibonacci 融合则可使其通道不确定，此时立方的幂在单一联合本征空间上以十个不同的相对相位作用，而 CY Stein 填充保持不变。

Conclusion: 非平面情形在若干方面偏离平面结论：CY 分解不再等价于最短分解，且 Fibonacci 融合下立方幂可产生丰富的相对相位而 CY Stein 填充固定，提示拓扑量子计算编码性质与几何填充条件之间存在更弱或更精细的对应。

Abstract: This paper is a non-planar counterpart of the author's earlier work on planar contact structures and topological quantum computation. There the page is planar and all branch points are paired by disjoint liftable arcs, compatibly with the joint eigenlines of the Ising representation and with Calabi-Yau (CY) Stein fillings, those with vanishing first Chern class. Here the page has genus g and connected binding, presented as a simple degree-three cover of the disk. Every maximal system of disjoint liftable arcs has exactly g members and leaves two branch points unused. The half-twist interchanging the two points is not liftable, but its cube is, and lifts to the identity. An integral residue criterion supplies the CY test. A factorization localized on a contact connected sum is automatically CY. Unlike in the planar case, a CY factorization need not be the shortest. Once the fusion channels of the g pairs are fixed, Ising fusion determines the channel of the remaining pair, and braiding acts there only by scalars. Fibonacci fusion can leave that channel undetermined, and the powers of the cube then act by ten distinct relative phases on a single joint eigenspace, while the CY Stein filling stays fixed

</details>


### [123] [Negative-Definite Plumbings and New Unknotting Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25430)
*Seong-Jin Lee*

Main category: math.GT

TL;DR: 该论文利用 Neumann 标准形（Neumann's normal form）判定纽结的双重分支覆盖（double branched cover）在任一取向下是否界定负定管道树（negative-definite plumbing tree），并据此计算 Heegaard Floer 同调，确定 112 个新的解结数（unknotting number）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对至多十三个交叉的许多非交错纽结（non-alternating knots），其解结数此前未知；需要一种可判定的方法来判断覆盖空间能否由管道树计算 Heegaard Floer 同调，从而获得下界。

Method: 从覆盖空间已知的 Seifert 或图流形（graph-manifold）结构出发，使用 Neumann 标准形判定双重分支覆盖是否（在任一取向下）界定负定管道树；对可界定者用管道树计算 Heegaard Floer 同调，对多数不可界定者则证明其无法由此计算。

Result: 确定了 112 个新的解结数；其中 43 个的下界来自管道树计算的 Heegaard Floer 同调，另 69 个来自环绕配对（linking pairings）以及 McCoy 与 Park 关于特殊交错纽结（special alternating knots）的定理。此外证明纽结 13n1587 是继 13n3370 之后 Bernhard–Jablan 猜想（Bernhard–Jablan conjecture）的又一显式反例。

Conclusion: Neumann 标准形为判定管道树可界定性及可计算性提供了有效判据，据此既扩展了解结数的确定范围，也给出了 Bernhard–Jablan 猜想的新反例。

Abstract: We use Neumann's normal form to decide whether the double branched cover of a knot bounds a negative-definite plumbing tree in either orientation, starting from the recognised Seifert or graph-manifold structure of the cover. For many non-alternating knots with at most thirteen crossings whose unknotting numbers were unknown, this identifies the cases in which the Heegaard Floer homology of the cover can be computed from such a plumbing, and shows for most of the others that it cannot. We determine 112 new unknotting numbers. For 43 of them, the lower bound rests on Heegaard Floer homology computed from the plumbing; for the other 69, it comes from linking pairings and from a theorem of McCoy and Park on special alternating knots. Finally, we show that the knot 13n1587 is a further explicit counterexample to the Bernhard--Jablan conjecture, after 13n3370.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [A general mechanism for collective foraging: percolating recruitment cascades coupled to a maximum-entropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24865)
*Galen J. Wilkerson*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出觅食招募（foraging recruitment）本质上是一种“传染”：智能体发现资源后触发的线性阈值级联（linear-threshold cascade）嵌于物理空间中，且级联所在的通信网络与物理空间构成耦合系统；这种反馈使协调自限制，无需参数调优即可同时涌现招募、边际值离开斑块（marginal-value patch-leaving）与Lévy搜索等行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 觅食生物学中记录的招募、斑块离开与Lévy搜索等行为通常被分别研究，缺少统一机制解释；本文旨在说明这些行为可由单一“最少承诺”（least-committal）原则——最大熵（maximum entropy）——在同一系统中共同产生，而非分别调参拟合。

Method: 将线性阈值级联模型嵌入空间：激活按度归一化（degree-normalized），使同一规则在邻域拥挤时产生密度依赖阻塞（density-dependent blocking）；级联在通信网络上传播，同时智能体的移动改变物理位置并重连网络，形成网络—空间耦合反馈。并借助渗流（percolation）与自组织临界（self-organized criticality，沙堆式驱动—雪崩循环）框架进行分析。

Result: 超过临界连通度后，群体自发维持在“级联窗口（cascade window）”内——局部近临界而全局亚临界；招募链沿图而非仅邻近传播，决定发现是局部还是全局扩散。在资源稀疏且成簇时收获可达单独搜索的7.6倍，搜索容易时增益消失。

Conclusion: 渗流级联以极少通信获得大回报；稀疏成簇的资源场是普遍情形（资源趋于聚集，最大熵使其成为最少承诺的分布），因此该无需调优的规则预期广泛重现，并说明主动物质（active matter）有序与最优觅食（optimal foraging）实为同一系统在两个域中的表现。

Abstract: Foraging recruitment is a contagion: an agent finding a resource triggers a linear-threshold cascade -- the model for rumour and adoption spread on social networks -- embedded in space. Because activation is degree-normalized, the same rule that spreads the cascade also stifles it as neighbourhoods crowd (density-dependent blocking), so the swarm aggregates on a resource, then releases to explore again. The cascade runs on the communication network, but acting on it moves agents in physical space, and that motion rewires the network: network and physical space are one coupled system, and this feedback -- not tuning -- makes the coordination self-limiting. Because recruitment chains along the graph rather than near neighbours alone, whether a discovery stays local or spans the swarm is a matter of percolation, and past a critical connectivity the swarm holds itself in a cascade window -- locally near-critical though globally subcritical, a self-organized, sandpile-like drive-avalanche cycle. Nothing is tuned, yet behaviours foraging biology documents separately emerge together -- recruitment, marginal-value patch-leaving, and Levy search with a switch to local exploitation. These are not coincidences but one least-committal principle: the resource field and the search both follow from maximum entropy -- Levy search matched to the field's sparsity, recruitment to its clustering, so active-matter order and optimal foraging are one system in two domains. The gains are real but conditional: up to 7.6x the harvest of solitary search when resources are sparse and clustered, shrinking to nothing when search is already easy. Percolating cascades yield a large return on minimal communication. And that sparse, clustered field is the generic one -- resources almost inevitably aggregate, and maximum entropy makes it the least-committal one once they do -- so one untuned rule should recur widely.

</details>


### [125] [Systemic collapse emergence from climate-induced perturbations in transportation networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26428)
*Mauro Faccin,Tomas Scagliarini,Manlio De Domenico*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出将交通系统动力学与外部事件场动力学相耦合的模型，用以刻画基础设施的级联失效（cascading failures）临界行为及外部事件影响强度；以降雨对真实铁路网络的影响为案例，发现降雨强度临界值附近的局部性与全网性中断现象，并据此评估系统扰性（perturbability）与社会风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类基础设施与外部事件的相互作用会通过级联失效的"雪球效应"导致功能丧失甚至系统崩溃，其复杂耦合关系使预防工作面临挑战。气象事件虽不可预防，却对依赖基础设施的人口产生重大影响，因此理解网络对外部扰动的响应方式对预测与缓解破坏性事件至关重要。

Method: 构建将交通系统动力学与作为场（fields）的外部事件动力学相耦合的模型，同时捕捉前者的临界行为（critical behavior）与后者的影响水平；并以降雨作用于真实铁路网络（real-world railway networks）作为案例研究进行验证。

Result: 降雨强度存在一个临界值（critical value of the rainfall strength），在其附近同时出现局部性（endemic）中断与系统级（system-wide）中断；对更大网络中局部扰动的分析可揭示整体基础设施的局部薄弱环节，并以系统扰性和相应社会风险（societal risk）加以评估。

Conclusion: 该框架为定量分析关键人类基础设施中由脆弱性引发的临界性（criticalities）开辟了途径，且这些脆弱性因气候变化（climate change）而预计将进一步加剧。

Abstract: The interaction between human infrastructures and external events results in loss of functionality or even complete system disruption through a snowball effect of cascading-failures. Their complex interplay challenges their prevention, affecting the human population that rely on their functionality. Understanding how these networks respond to external perturbations is crucial for predicting and mitigating the impacts of disruptive events. In particular, meteorological events, while not preventable, can have significant impacts on human infrastructure. Here, we propose a model coupling the dynamics of transportation systems to the dynamics of external events considered as fields, to capture at the same time the critical behavior of the former and the level of influence of the latter. The impact of rainfalls on real-world railway networks provides a compelling case study, showcasing local endemic and system-wide disruptions around a critical value of the rainfall strength. Remarkably, localized perturbations on the wider network reveals the local weaknesses of the global infrastructure, and we assess them in terms of system perturbability and corresponding societal risk. Our framework opens the door to the quantitative analysis of criticalities in essential human infrastructure imputable to vulnerabilities that are set to intensify due to climate change.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [126] [Generative marginal learning for finite-size molecular simulation with application to stress calculation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25105)
*Cheng Yuan*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 提出生成边际学习（generative marginal learning, GML）框架，通过在大体系模拟上训练条件扩散模型（conditional diffusion model）学习保留区域的边际分布，实现无需显式模拟环境或周期边界条件的平衡采样与局部可观测量估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小体系周期分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟存在有限尺寸效应（finite-size effects），难以准确计算局部分子可观测量，而显式模拟周围环境代价高昂。

Method: 训练条件扩散模型以学习保留区域的边际分布（marginal distribution），并推导边际有效势（marginal effective potential）及相应的条件平均力恒等式（conditional mean-force identity），作为去噪与力对齐训练的基础。

Result: 以空位缺陷附近的空间平均欧文—柯克伍德应力（Irving–Kirkwood stress）为应用对象，数值结果表明 GML 相比小体系周期 MD 显著提升对结构（pair structure）与局部应力的精度。

Conclusion: GML 能够缓解计算局部分子可观测量时的有限尺寸效应，为平衡采样与局部量估计提供有效途径。

Abstract: We propose generative marginal learning (GML), a framework for equilibrium sampling and local observable estimation in molecular simulations. By training a conditional diffusion model on large-system simulations, GML learns the marginal distribution of a retained region, enabling configuration generation without explicitly simulating the surrounding environment or imposing periodic boundary conditions on the retained region. We derive a marginal effective potential and the associated conditional mean-force identity, which provide the basis for both denoising and force-alignment training. As an application, we estimate the spatially averaged Irving--Kirkwood stress near a vacancy defect. Numerical results demonstrate that GML substantially improves pair structure and local stress accuracy compared with small periodic molecular dynamics simulations, mitigating finite-size effects in calculating local molecular observables.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [127] [Encoding Hyperuniformity in Geometric Networks: Structural Signatures and Diffusion Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24317)
*Yongyi Wang,Eli Newby,Yang Jiao,Salvatore Torquato,Réka Albert*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究几何网络（geometric networks）对无序超均匀性（disordered hyperuniformity）的刻划问题，发现几何网络比 Delaunay 三角剖分保留更多长波密度信息，并以网络结构可观测量与扩散弛豫行为区分超均匀与非超均匀网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无序超均匀性是在长波极限下密度涨落被异常抑制的奇异状态，但其向网络系统的推广仍是重要开放问题；同时，网络超均匀性领域存在难以区分“近超均匀（near-hyperuniform）”与“强局域无序的超均匀”体系这一核心挑战。

Method: 在多种系统中比较几何网络与 Delaunay 三角剖分对底层点构型（point configurations）长波密度信息的保留程度；以接近中心性（closeness centrality）、顶点强度（vertex strength）与特征向量中心性（eigenvector centrality）等网络结构可观测量刻画顶点间涨落；并引入基于扩散（diffusion）的弛豫时间中心性（relaxation-time centrality）连接结构与动力学。

Result: 几何网络在长波长上保留的密度信息显著多于 Delaunay 三角剖分；超均匀性表现为上述多项网络结构观测量的顶点间涨落被抑制，这些量既能区分超均匀与非超均匀网络，也能区分近超均匀构型与强局域无序的超均匀体系；超均匀几何网络表现出比非超均匀几何网络更均匀的弛豫行为。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了大尺度密度涨落抑制如何在网络结构与扩散动力学两个层面同时体现，为网络超均匀性的刻划与判别提供了结构—动力学统一的视角。

Abstract: Disordered hyperuniformity is a recently discovered exotic state exhibiting anomalously suppressed density fluctuations at long wavelengths, and its generalization to network systems remains an important open question. Here, we show that, across a wide range of systems, geometric networks retain substantially more long-wavelength density information from the underlying point configurations than Delaunay triangulations. Within geometric networks, hyperuniformity manifests as suppressed vertex-to-vertex fluctuations in a range of network structural observables, including closeness centrality, vertex strength, and eigenvector centrality. These measures not only distinguish hyperuniform networks from non-hyperuniform ones, but also separate near-hyperuniform configurations from hyperuniform systems with strong local disorder, solving a central challenge in network hyperuniformity. We connect these structural signatures to network dynamics by introducing a relaxation-time centrality based on diffusion and demonstrate that hyperuniform geometric networks exhibit more homogeneous relaxation behavior than non-hyperuniform geometric networks. These results reveal how the suppression of large-scale density fluctuations manifests in both network structure and diffusion dynamics.

</details>


### [128] [Maximum-Temperature Theorem for a Nanothermometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24340)
*Taras Plakhotnik*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文推导出温度传感器准稳态温升的一个尖锐且与形状无关的上界，该上界仅依赖总热功率、热导率下界与传感器测温体积，从而说明点热源附近的稳态导热无法解释开尔文量级的温度读数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理想点热源（ideal point heat source）在传统热传导理论中预测的稳态温度发散，但任何物理温度计都无法测得点值；由此需要针对有限测温体积的传感器给出有物理意义的温度上界。

Method: 在由等温参考面（isothermal reference surface）包围的区域内，对测温体积为 $V_\mathrm{s}$ 的传感器推导准稳态温升（quasi-steady temperature rise）的严格上界，其仅需区域总热功率 $\dot{Q}$、热导率（thermal conductivity）下界 $κ_\mathrm{m}$ 与 $V_\mathrm{s}$ 三个量。

Result: 该上界与热源分布、区域形状及源—传感器距离无关；在 $\dot{Q}=50~\mathrm{pW}$、$κ_\mathrm{m}=0.1~\mathrm{W\,m^{-1}K^{-1}}$、$V_\mathrm{s}=10^{-22}\,\mathrm{m^3}$（约合直径 60 nm 球体体积）的取值下，定理允许的最大温升约为参考水平以上 $2~\mathrm{mK}$。

Conclusion: 结果可直接应用于纳米金刚石（nanodiamonds）、上转换纳米颗粒（upconversion nanoparticles）、荧光聚合物颗粒与核壳量子点（core-shell quantum dots）；并表明在稳态热传导下，热源定位或源—传感器间距不确定均无法解释开尔文量级的读数。

Abstract: The steady-state temperature predicted for an ideal point heat source diverges in conventional heat-conduction theory, but no physical thermometer measures a point value. We derive a sharp, shape-independent upper bound on the quasi-steady temperature rise of a sensor with thermometric volume $V_\mathrm{s}$, placed anywhere inside a region enclosed by an isothermal reference surface. The bound requires only the total heat power generated in the region $\dot{Q}$, a lower bound on its thermal conductivity $κ_\mathrm{m}$, and the value of $V_\mathrm{s}$. It is otherwise independent of the heat-source distribution, domain shape, and source--sensor distance. For $\dot{Q}=50~\mathrm{pW}$, $κ_\mathrm{m} = 0.1~\mathrm{W\,m^{-1}K^{-1}}$, and $V_\mathrm{s}=10^{-22}\mathrm{\,m^3}$, approximately the volume of a $60~\mathrm{nm}$-diameter sphere, the theorem permits a maximum temperature rise of approximately $2~\mathrm{mK}$ above the reference level. The result applies directly to nanodiamonds, upconversion nanoparticles, fluorescent polymer particles, and core-shell quantum dots. It shows that neither source localization nor uncertain source-sensor separation can account for kelvin-scale readings under steady-state heat conduction.

</details>


### [129] [Beyond low-dissipation regime: general condition for Curzon-Ahlborn efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24434)
*Yuki Izumida*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将低耗散卡诺热机模型推广至逆操作时间的非线性响应，推导出在二阶（温度差二次阶）恢复Curzon-Ahlborn（CA）效率的一般条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CA效率在线性响应（linear response）下的一阶普适性已确立，但二阶普适性通常丧失；标准低耗散模型所需的对称耗散条件（symmetric dissipation condition）并非必要条件，因采用内可逆性（endoreversibility）的原始CA模型并不要求该对称性。

Method: 将低耗散模型（low-dissipation model）扩展至逆操作时间的非线性响应（nonlinear response in the inverse operation time），据此推导CA效率在二次阶得以恢复的一般条件。

Result: 广义对称耗散条件与基于牛顿冷却定律（Newton's law of cooling）的CA模型均为该一般条件的特例；借助所得条件，进一步阐明具有一般传热定律（heat-transfer law）的内可逆模型呈现CA效率的条件。

Conclusion: CA效率在二阶的恢复可由一个涵盖对称耗散与内可逆情形的统一条件刻画，从而统一并澄清了低耗散框架与内可逆框架下CA效率普适性的适用边界。

Abstract: In recent years, physicists have extensively investigated the universality of Curzon-Ahlborn (CA) efficiency as the efficiency at maximum power of heat engines, which, like Carnot efficiency, is determined only by heat-bath temperatures. While the universality of the CA efficiency to the first order in the temperature difference is established within linear response theory in the inverse operation time, it is generally lost at quadratic order. In the low-dissipation Carnot heat engine model known as the standard framework, the symmetric dissipation condition is necessary. Yet, it is not a necessary condition as the original CA model that adopts endoreversibility does not require such symmetry. In this study, we extend the low-dissipation model to include nonlinear response in the inverse operation time and derive a general condition under which the CA efficiency is recovered in the quadratic order. The generalized symmetric dissipation condition and the CA model with Newton's law of cooling are shown to be special cases of this condition. By applying the obtained condition, we also clarify the condition under which an endoreversible model with a general heat-transfer law exhibits the CA efficiency.

</details>


### [130] [Energy-optimal scheduling for clocked reversible computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24435)
*Michael C. Thumann,David Soloveichik*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文在有限内存与有限时间约束下，形式化计算中的能量—内存—时间权衡（energy–memory–time tradeoff），针对依赖图为线性的顺序计算推导精确分隔递推，并证明渐近最优调度为“严格可逆块+一次局部擦除”的重复结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原则上，信息擦除（information erasure）与有限时间操作引起的耗散可通过逻辑可逆计算（logically reversible computation）与慢速绝热协议（adiabatic protocol）消除，但在内存有限、时间有限时二者无法同时规避。

Method: 在外部时钟驱动、热耦合计算机的固定工作点上，为可逆检查点移动（checkpoint moves）与局部不可逆擦除设定有效价格 $ε_R$ 与 $ε_I$，以其比值 $λ=ε_I/ε_R$ 在检查点内存约束下选择能量最优的半可逆调度（semi-reversible schedule）；对线性依赖图推导精确分隔递推并给出渐近最优调度定理，再结合随机热力学本构模型。

Result: 证明渐近最优调度为严格可逆块后接一次局部擦除的重复模式；结合随机热力学（stochastic thermodynamics）本构模型，表明温度、基本操作时长、内存与器件相关操作成本共同决定最优调度采用可逆清理、择机擦除还是每次处置均擦除。

Conclusion: 可逆清理相比擦除需额外耗散操作，从而形成半可逆的能量—内存—时间权衡；该权衡可由价格比 $λ$ 与器件参数刻画，并给出可证明最优的调度结构。

Abstract: Computational dissipation may arise from both information erasure and finite-time operation, although both contributions can in principle be avoided in the absence of space and time constraints. Logically reversible computation can replace erasure with ancilla cleanup, and slow adiabatic protocols can eliminate any excess finite-time dissipation. Under limited memory and in finite time, however, choosing reversible cleanup over erasure requires additional dissipative operations, creating a semi-reversible energy--memory--time tradeoff. We formulate this tradeoff at a fixed operating point of an externally clocked thermally coupled computer, where reversible checkpoint moves and local irreversible erasures have specified effective prices $ε_R$ and $ε_I$. Their ratio $λ=ε_I/ε_R$ selects an energy-optimal semi-reversible schedule under a checkpoint-memory bound by trading reversible moves against erasures. For a sequential computation whose dependency graph is a line, we derive an exact separator recurrence and prove that an asymptotically optimal schedule repeats a strictly reversible block followed by one local erasure. Coupling this scheduling theorem to a stochastic-thermodynamic constitutive model shows how temperature, primitive duration, memory, and device-dependent operation costs determine whether the optimal schedule uses reversible cleanup, elective erasures, or erasure at every disposal.

</details>


### [131] [Autonomous Thermal Switching Outperforms the Frozen Optimum with Vanishing Correlations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24589)
*Siyi Deng,Longxing Xu,Zhuolin Ye,Xian He,Jianhui Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文在固定降低预算（lowering budget）与可见平衡态的约束下证明：保持联合细致平衡（joint detailed balance）时，最优快速切换（fast switching）可将预算集中于单条边来混合不同轮廓，并在有限可逆网络中获得优于最佳冻结势垒分配（frozen barrier allocation）的松弛优势，同时使系统—辅助关联（system–auxiliary correlations）在松弛过程中消失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心动机是检验自主热切换（autonomous thermal switching）能否在系统—辅助关联任意小、且不破坏热力学一致性的前提下，超越最优的冻结势垒分配策略。

Method: 在固定降低预算与可见平衡态的设定下，保持联合细致平衡；在有限可逆网络（finite reversible networks）上求解最优快速切换方案，并以三态模型（three-state model）给出解析性刻画，辅以集体模型与连续模型。

Result: 在有限可逆网络上，最优快速切换混合将预算集中于单条边的轮廓；三态模型给出严格增益（strict gain）及其有限速率阈值（finite-rate threshold）；更快切换在保持增益的同时使关联在整个松弛过程中趋于零。

Conclusion: 在给定预算与热力学一致性条件下，自主热切换确实可实现优于最佳冻结势垒分配的松弛优势，且不以残留关联为代价；集体与连续模型的推广提示该效应可通过胶体（colloidal）实验检验。

Abstract: Can autonomous thermal switching outperform the best frozen barrier allocation while keeping system--auxiliary correlations arbitrarily small? We establish this relaxation advantage at fixed lowering budget and visible equilibrium, preserving joint detailed balance. On finite reversible networks, optimal fast switching mixes profiles concentrating the budget on single edges. A three-state model gives a strict gain and its finite-rate threshold. Faster switching preserves the gain while correlations vanish throughout relaxation. Collective and continuous models suggest a colloidal test.

</details>


### [132] [Large Deviations in Switching Diffusion: Dynamical Condensation and Diffusivity BBP Transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25072)
*Mathis Guéneau,Timo Schorlepp,Satya N. Majumdar,Gregory Schehr*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究扩散系数按泊松切换、取值服从有界分布 W(D) 的布朗粒子的大偏差行为，刻画其动力学相变与轨迹凝聚，并揭示矩生成函数（moment-generating function）的转变受与 BBP 转变相同的边缘机制支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前作（Guéneau et al., PRL 135, 067102 (2025)）已用更新方法（renewal approach）得到位移速率函数（rate function）并发现多处奇异性，但缺乏对相应动力学相变（dynamical phase transitions）与轨迹凝聚（trajectory condensation）的细致分析。

Method: 在长时间大偏差区间 x(t)≃yt（y=O(1)）内，假设 W(D)∼C(D_max−D)^ν 于上边缘附近，分析切换间隔内位移增量的分配，并通过矩生成函数研究扩散系数选择；数值上采用稀有事件采样（rare event sampling）进行验证。

Result: 中等 y 时位移由 O(t) 个增量分担；超过第一阈值 y_- 后出现 O(t) 的宏观增量，即动力学凝聚；ν>1 时存在第二阈值 y_+>y_-，此时宏观增量渐近携带全部位移，而 0<ν≤1 时两阈值合并（y_-=y_+）。矩生成函数则发生从多扩散系数集体贡献向 D_max 附近状态选择的互补转变。

Conclusion: 该转变由与 Baik-Ben Arous-Péché（BBP）转变相同的边缘机制控制，表明扩散系数选择与轨迹凝聚是同一现象的两种表现；稀有事件采样数值结果与理论预测一致。

Abstract: We consider a Brownian particle with position $x(t)$ whose diffusivity changes at Poissonian switching times, with each new value drawn independently from a bounded distribution $W(D)$. Assuming $W(D)\sim C(D_{\max}-D)^ν$ near its upper edge, we study the large-deviation regime $x(t)\simeq yt$ with $y = O(1)$ at large times, far beyond the typical diffusive scale. In a recent work Guéneau et al., Phys. Rev. Lett. 135, 067102 (2025), the rate function of the displacement was obtained through a renewal approach and it was shown to display several singularities. In this paper, we carry out a detailed study of the corresponding dynamical phase transitions and trajectory condensation. For moderate $y$, the imposed displacement is shared among $O(t)$ displacement increments accumulated during successive intervals between switches. Beyond a first threshold $y_-$, one interval produces an increment of order $t$ carrying a finite fraction of the total displacement, signaling dynamical condensation. For $ν>1$, a second threshold $y_+>y_-$ is reached when this macroscopic increment carries asymptotically the entire imposed displacement, whereas for $0<ν\leq1$ the two thresholds merge, $y_-=y_+$. In turn, the moment-generating function of $x(t)$ displays a complementary transition from collective contributions of many diffusivities to selection of states near $D_{\max}$. We show that this transition is governed by the same edge mechanism as the Baik-Ben Arous-Péché (BBP) transition, indicating that diffusivity selection and trajectory condensation are two manifestations of the same phenomenon. We validate our predictions for the dynamical condensation against numerical results from rare event sampling.

</details>


### [133] [Spontaneous particle-hole symmetry breaking in a disordered two-site quantum breakdown model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24669)
*Kinya Guan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究无序双格点量子击穿模型中的自发粒子-空穴对称性破缺，在大模式数极限下解析求得自由能并发现有限温相变，其阶数随每次转化产生的费米子数增加由连续转为一级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究无序量子多体模型中自发粒子-空穴对称性破缺（particle-hole symmetry breaking）是否以及如何发生，特别是其在有限温度下的相变性质与普适行为。

Method: 采用可解的双格点量子击穿模型（quantum breakdown model），在每个格点费米子模式数（fermionic modes）很大的极限下解析推导自由能（free energy）；对两格点模式数之比较小的情形给出解析结果，并结合数值计算推广到更广参数区域。

Result: 解析得到有限的对称性破缺相变温度；随每次转化（conversion）产生的费米子数增加，相变由连续（continuous）变为一级（first order）。数值结果显示两类相变在该解析区域之外依然存在，连续相变的序参量指数（order-parameter exponent）接近 1/2。

Conclusion: 无序双格点量子击穿模型中存在稳健的自发粒子-空穴对称性破缺有限温相变，其阶数受转化过程产生的费米子数调控，且连续相变的临界指数近似符合平均场式预期。

Abstract: We study spontaneous particle-hole symmetry breaking in a disordered two-site quantum breakdown model. The model is solvable in the limit of a large number of fermionic modes on each site. For a small ratio of the numbers of modes on the two sites, we derive the free energy analytically and find a finite-temperature symmetry-breaking transition. This transition changes from continuous to first order as the number of fermions produced in each conversion increases. Numerical results show that both types of transition persist beyond this regime, with an order-parameter exponent close to $1/2$ for continuous transitions.

</details>


### [134] [Frustration and topology in the phase behavior of solute-frustrated $q$-clock models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24923)
*Hari Rajesh,Richard C. Remsing*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 论文提出并研究了“溶质阻挫 q-clock（solute-frustrated q-clock, SFqC）模型”，将溶质位点引入 q-clock 模型，通过蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）与理论分析考察 q=4 与 q=6 时的相行为，发现其相图依赖于参数选择，并具有丰富的低温相，包括强阻挫畴（strongly frustrated domains）、无长程序相以及拓扑缺陷（topological defects）驱动的结晶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阻挫（frustration）可增强涨落并产生新的相行为，与拓扑缺陷结合时还能通过拓扑转变（topological transitions）与阻挫拓扑转变增加相图复杂度；为理解阻挫与拓扑在相行为上的相互作用，需要构建可控的理论模型。

Method: 在 q-clock 模型（每个自旋可取 q 种取向的格点模型）中加入溶质位点，构成 SFqC 模型；溶质与自旋的相互作用本质上为系统中正拓扑缺陷或涡旋（vortices）浓度设定了一个固定的下界。针对 q=4 与 q=6 的情形，采用蒙特卡洛模拟并建立理论描述。

Result: SFqC 模型呈现依赖于参数选择的丰富相图，具有多样的低温相，包括存在强阻挫畴以及缺乏长程有序（long-range order）的情形；即使在低温下也观察到拓扑效应，例如拓扑缺陷可驱动结晶（crystallization）。

Conclusion: 阻挫与拓扑的耦合可显著调控系统的相行为与涨落；作者预期这些结果可为开发利用阻挫与拓扑来调节相行为和涨落的材料提供指导。

Abstract: Frustration can enhance fluctuations and create new and interesting phase behavior. When combined with the ability to host topological defects, frustration can increase the complexity of a system's phase diagram through topological and frustrated topological transitions. To understand the interplay of frustration and topology on phase behavior, we introduce and study solute-frustrated $q$-clock (SF$q$C) models, in which solute sites are added to the $q$-clock model, a lattice model where each spin can take one of $q$ orientations. The $q$-clock model can exhibit spontaneous symmetry breaking and topological transitions, and the interaction between the spins and a solute frustrates the system, in essence by creating a fixed lower bound on the concentration of positive topological defects or vortices. We perform Monte Carlo simulations and develop theoretical descriptions of SF$q$C models with $q=4$ and $q=6$ to understand their phase behavior. We find that these systems exhibit rich phase diagrams that depend on the choice of parameters. In particular, SF$q$C models have a wide range of interesting low-temperature phases, including the presence of strongly frustrated domains and the absence of long-range order. We also find topological effects even at low temperatures; for instance, we find that topological defects can drive crystallization. We anticipate these results to guide the development of materials that take advantage of frustration and topology to tune phase behavior and fluctuations.

</details>


### [135] [Scaling Dimensions and Two-Point Amplitudes from Operator Covariance Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24943)
*You-Chiuan Chen,Ribhu K. Kaul*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作深入分析利用有限格点算符协方差矩阵的本征分解来提取临界格点模型两点函数通用信息的方案，证明在标度不变假设下前导本征值衰减由最小的 N_ℓ 个标度维数控制（即使计入无关微扰），并提出由本征矢构造连续谱两点函数振幅的新方法，从而完整确定其结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临界格点统计力学模型（critical lattice statistical mechanics models）两点函数的长距离行为依赖底层量子场论（quantum field theory）的细节信息；但一般格点算符会与无穷多个连续谱标度算符（scaling operators）以未知系数耦合，使这些通用性质难以提取。已有方案仅初步解耦部分信息，缺乏系统论证。

Method: 基于 N_ℓ 个格点算符交叉关联构造算符协方差矩阵（operator covariance matrix）并作本征分解（eigen-decomposition）；给出在标度不变性（scale invariance）假设下的显式证明，分析本征值前导衰减与标度维数（scaling dimensions）的关系，并利用本征矢（eigenvectors）构造连续谱两点关联函数的振幅。

Result: 显式证明在计入无关微扰（irrelevant perturbations）后，本征值的前导衰减仍由最小的 N_ℓ 个标度维数控制；据此可由格点协方差矩阵完整确定连续谱两点函数的结构，并在合成数据集（依据标度假设生成）与二维伊辛模型（two dimensional Ising model）的数值精确数据上得到验证。

Conclusion: 协方差矩阵本征分解为从格点数据中系统提取连续谱两点函数信息提供了有理论保证的框架，既能识别主导标度维数，也能重构振幅，从而完整刻画两点函数结构。

Abstract: The long distance behavior of two-point functions of critical lattice statistical mechanics models depend on detailed information of the underlying quantum field theory. Yet these universal properties can be difficult to extract because generic lattice operators couple to the infinite hierarchy of continuum scaling operators with unknown coefficients. Recently in Physical Review E 114, 024146 (2026), one approach to disentangle some of this information was proposed using the eigen-decomposition of the operator covariance matrix constructed from cross correlations of a finite set of $N_\ell$ lattice operators. In this work we analyze this method in detail. We provide an explicit proof that under the assumption of scale invariance the leading decay of the eigenvalues are controlled by the smallest $N_\ell$ scaling dimensions, even after taking irrelevant perturbations into account. Based on this understanding, we propose a new method by which the eigenvectors of the covariance matrix can be used to construct the amplitudes of the continuum two-point correlation functions. This allows for a complete determination of the structure of the continuum two-point functions from the lattice covariance matrix. We demonstrate our ideas both on synthetic data sets (drawn from the scaling hypothesis) and numerically exact data for the two dimensional Ising model.

</details>


### [136] [Run-and-tumble particles in a harmonic external field: constructing the solution of the Fokker-Planck equation from the equations of motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24961)
*Aoran Sun,Fangfu Ye,Jeff Z. Y. Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种直接对run-and-tumble粒子（run-and-tumble particles）的Langevin运动方程进行积分的方法，用以计算其位置与主动速度的联合分布（joint distribution），并在一维谐振外势（harmonic external potential）下给出精确稳态解，验证其与相应Fokker-Planck方程（Fokker-Planck equations）精确解的等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多依赖Fokker-Planck方程描述run-and-tumble粒子的分布演化，而直接从Langevin方程（Langevin equations）出发计算位置与活性速度联合分布的方法尚不充分，本文旨在填补这一方法学空白。

Method: 核心步骤是构造分布的矩（moments）与关联函数（correlation functions），并以粒子在末时刻处于特定状态为条件；分别在无外场与有谐振外力场情形下对Langevin方程积分，并针对一维情形推导tumble态及在指定速度方向条件下的run态的精确稳态分布。

Result: 获得了一维谐振外势中tumble态的精确稳态分布，以及给定速度方向下run态的条件稳态分布；并证明这些结果与对应Fokker-Planck方程的精确解等价。

Conclusion: 所提出的Langevin方程积分方法可有效计算run-and-tumble粒子的联合分布，并与Fokker-Planck框架的精确解相互印证，为处理含外势的活性粒子（active particles）问题提供了可替代的解析途径。

Abstract: A method for integrating the Langevin equations of motion for run-and-tumble particles is introduced to compute the joint distribution of their position and active velocity, with and without a harmonic external force field. The key step is the construction of the moments of the distribution and the correlation functions, conditional on the particles being in a specific state at the final time. For one-dimensional run-and-tumble particles in a harmonic external potential, the exact steady-state distributions for the tumble state, as well as for the run state conditional on a specified velocity direction, are obtained. This enables us to demonstrate their equivalence to the exact solutions of the corresponding Fokker-Planck equations.

</details>


### [137] [The Ericson Transition in Time-Reversal Invariant Systems: Orthogonally Invariant Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25103)
*Simon Köhnes,Thomas Guhr*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究时间反演与旋转不变系统在Ericson区域内非对角散射矩阵元与截面的分布，通过渐近展开证明正交情形下分布的主导项为高斯与指数行为，并给出次主导项与数值模拟对比，最后比较三类Wigner-Dyson对称类的Ericson转变异同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前工作已用渐近展开方法分析幺正类与辛类的Ericson转变，但正交类——即实验分析中最常涉及的情形——尚缺相应结果，故需补齐并统一比较三类对称类。

Method: 采用渐近展开（asymptotic expansion）技术，针对时间反演与旋转不变（正交类）系统推导非对角散射矩阵元及截面分布的领头阶项，并进一步导出次领头阶项，与数值模拟（numerical simulations）结果进行对比。

Result: 在正交情形下，分布展开的领头阶表现为高斯（Gaussian）与指数（exponential）行为，与幺正类、辛类结论一致；次领头阶项被显式给出并与数值模拟相符。

Conclusion: 正交类的Ericson转变可由渐近展开解析刻画，从而完成三类Wigner-Dyson对称类的统一描述，并揭示其Ericson转变的共性与差异。

Abstract: We investigate the distributions of off-diagonal scattering matrix elements and cross-sections in the Ericson regime for systems that are time-reversal and rotationally invariant. In previous works, we developed techniques to analyze the Ericson transition by means of asymptotic expansions in the cases of unitarily and symplectically invariant Hamiltonians. Here, we prove that Gaussian and exponential behavior is found as the leading-order term of an asymptotic expansion of those distributions also in the orthogonal case, which is often the relevant one for analyzing experiments. Moreover, we derive the subleading-order term and compare with numerical simulations. Finally, we discuss similarities and differences of the Ericson transition for all three Wigner-Dyson classes.

</details>


### [138] [Annealing Dynamics of the Two-Dimensional Fully Frustrated Ising Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25109)
*Kai-Hsin Wu,Anders W. Sandvik,Ying-Jer Kao*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过模拟退火（simulated annealing）研究二维完全阻挫伊辛模型（fully frustrated Ising model）的动力学标度性质，将Kibble-Zurek机制推广至关联长度指数发散（T→Tc=0）的情形，并测得动力学临界指数（dynamic exponent）z≈2。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该模型具有高度简并的临界基态（critical ground state）及已知的平衡性质，但其非平衡（out-of-equilibrium）动力学行为尚待刻画；常规Kibble-Zurek机制不直接适用于关联长度在Tc=0极限下指数发散的情况，因此需要发展相应的退火方案与标度理论。

Method: 采用模拟退火（simulated annealing）方法，设计一种适用于关联长度指数发散的退火协议，从而将Kibble-Zurek机制推广到该情形，并推导相应的标度形式（scaling forms）；随后对磁化率（susceptibility）、过剩能量（excess energy）与Edwards-Anderson序参量进行拟合以提取动力学指数z。

Result: 模拟结果与推导的标度形式高度吻合，并出现常规Kibble-Zurek机制中不存在的初始温度依赖性。各物理量给出的z值一致：磁化率z=1.99(2)、过剩能量z=2.072(8)、Edwards-Anderson序参量z=2.09(3)，均接近z=2。

Conclusion: 测得的动力学指数与瞬时淬火（instantaneous quench）后弛豫及平衡态时间关联（time dependent correlations）的既有研究相符，亦与平衡自相关函数（autocorrelation functions）给出的估计z=2.004(9)一致，表明该模型动力学标度具有普适一致性。

Abstract: We study the dynamical scaling properties of the two-dimensional fully frustrated Ising model by simulated annealing. The model has a highly degenerate, critical ground state with well known equilibrium properties. To study its out-of-equilibrium behavior, we develop an annealing protocol that is convenient for extending the Kibble-Zurek mechanism to the case of an exponentially divergent correlation length in the limit of the temperature $T \to T_c = 0$. We find excellent agreement with the scaling forms derived, including a dependence on the initial temperature that is not present in the conventional Kibble-Zurek mechanism. The dynamic exponent $z$ is consistent among all quantities studied; fits of its dependence on the initial temperature give $z = 1.99(2)$ (susceptibility), $z = 2.072(8)$ (excess energy), and $z = 2.09(3)$ (Edwards-Anderson order parameter). These values are close to $z=2$, which is consistent with previous studies of relaxation after instantaneous quenches and of time dependent correlations in equilibrium, including the estimate $z = 2.004(9)$ from equilibrium autocorrelation functions.

</details>


### [139] [Boundary-Sensitive Quantum Order on Non-Amenable Lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26859)
*Xingzhi Wang,Zohar Nussinov,Gerardo Ortiz*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究非顺从晶格（non-amenable lattice）上的量子系统，发现在配位数为3的无限Cayley树上，p<5态clock模型存在一种由任意弱边界场诱导的涌现中间相，其体序（bulk order）机制超出Landau自发对称破缺范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非顺从晶格的边界占系统体积的有限比例（extensive fraction），可能承载无欧几里得对应的新型体有序形式，因此需要考察边界对体相行为的影响。

Method: 结合保留边界的无限密度矩阵重整化群（boundary-retaining iDMRG）与精确的对称性固定对偶（exact symmetry-fixing duality），研究配位数3的无限Cayley树上p<5的态clock模型。

Result: 在有序相与无序相之间存在一个涌现的中间相（emergent intermediate phase），其体序由任意弱的边界场诱导，而非传统自发对称破缺；该相中非极化边界选择特定的有序体态，实现体-边界对应（bulk-boundary correspondence）。

Conclusion: 边界敏感的序（boundary-sensitive order）构成一种独立的量子有序机制，突破了Landau自发对称破缺范式的描述范围。

Abstract: We show that quantum systems on non-amenable lattices, whose boundaries constitute an extensive fraction of the system, host novel forms of bulk ordering with no Euclidean counterpart. Combining boundary-retaining iDMRG with an exact symmetry-fixing duality, we investigate $p<5$ state clock models on the coordination-3 infinite Cayley tree. We find that between the ordered and disordered phases lies an emergent intermediate phase with bulk order induced by an arbitrarily weak boundary field rather than by conventional spontaneous symmetry-breaking. This establishes boundary-sensitive order as a distinct quantum ordering mechanism defying Landau's spontaneous symmetry-breaking paradigm. The intermediate phase realizes a bulk-boundary correspondence in which the unpolarized boundary selects a specific ordered bulk state.

</details>


### [140] [Tensor network Monte Carlo study of the J1-J2 Ising model on the square lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25270)
*Wanying Zheng,Tao Chen,Youjin Deng,Wanzhou Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作提出张量网络蒙特卡洛（tensor-network Monte Carlo, TNMC）算法，为周期性边界下阻挫方阵 J1-J2 Ising 模型提供高效、近无偏的大规模模拟途径，并完成有限尺寸标定分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阻挫方阵 J1-J2 Ising 模型相图丰富，但相变难以刻画：传统蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）受临界慢化（critical slowing down）限制，张量重整化群（tensor renormalization group）方法则存在截断误差（truncation errors）。

Method: 开发支持开放与周期性边界的 TNMC 算法；对周期格点采用扫描式块更新（sweeping block-update）策略，在固定周围自旋的同时更新自旋块；并将采样器与直方图重加权（histogram reweighting）耦合，且对各项技术单独测试以验证其独立优化效果。

Result: 在 g=0.55 与 g=1.0 处开展大规模有限尺寸标度（finite-size scaling）分析，由 Binder 累积量（Binder cumulant）数据提取表观临界温度（apparent critical temperatures）与有效热指数（effective thermal exponents）。

Conclusion: 该方法为周期性边界下 J1-J2 类阻挫磁体的大规模模拟提供了高效、近无偏的路径，并可方便推广至其他阻挫自旋格点模型。
