Abstract: The frustrated square-lattice J1-J2 Ising model exhibits rich magnetic phases, yet its phase transitions remain difficult to characterize due to critical slowing down in conventional Monte Carlo and truncation errors inherent to tensor renormalization group methods.We develop a tensor-network Monte Carlo (TNMC) algorithm for this frustrated model, which supports both open and periodic boundary conditions. For periodic lattices, we use a sweeping block-update strategy that updates spin blocks while fixing surrounding spins.To boost efficiency, we couple our sampler with histogram reweighting, and test each technique standalone to validate these independent optimizations.We perform extensive finite-size scaling analyses at g=0.55 and g=1.0, extracting apparent critical temperatures and effective thermal exponents from Binder-cumulant data.Our method offers an efficient, nearly bias-free path for large-scale simulations of J1-J2-type frustrated magnets under periodic boundaries and generalizes readily to other frustrated spin lattices.

</details>


### [141] [Generation of $N$-particle kappa-distributed velocities via superstatistical Gibbs sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25487)
*Leonardo Herrera_Fuenzalida,Sergio Davis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文指出，在粒子网格（particle-in-cell, PIC）模拟中正确使用kappa分布，不能仅对单粒子边缘分布采样，因为N粒子kappa分布本身蕴含粒子动能间的非平凡关联。作者基于超统计（superstatistics）结构实现Gibbs采样器，使速度与参数β的联合分布达到平稳，从而按构造复现N粒子kappa分布及其能量相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kappa分布广泛用于刻画空间与实验室等离子体中的重尾、非平衡速度分布。但PIC模拟中若仅采样单粒子边缘分布，将无法体现N粒子kappa分布所预测的粒子间动能相关结构，这一采样缺陷构成研究动机。

Method: 利用kappa分布的超统计（superstatistics）表述，构造一个Gibbs采样器，其平稳分布为速度与β的联合分布。

Result: 采样得到的速度分布遵循N粒子kappa分布，并按构造（by construction）自动复现粒子动能之间由该分布预测的关联。

Conclusion: 基于超统计结构的Gibbs采样为PIC模拟提供了一种正确生成N粒子kappa分布的方法，弥补了单粒子边缘采样无法再现多粒子关联的不足。

Abstract: The \emph{kappa} distribution is widely used to model heavy-tailed, non-equilibrium velocity distributions in space and laboratory plasmas. Its correct use in particle-in-cell (PIC) simulations requires more than sampling the single-particle marginal: the $N$-particle \emph{kappa} distribution predicts non-trivial correlations between the kinetic energies of particles. Building on the superstatistical structure, we implement a Gibbs sampler whose stationary distribution is the joint distribution of the velocities and $β$, so that the sampled velocities follow the $N$-particle \emph{kappa} distribution and reproduce these correlations by construction.

</details>


### [142] [Phase transitions of the antiferromagnetic $J_1$-$J_2$-$J_3$ Ising model on the cubic lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25884)
*Kai-Wei Huang,Chien-Ting Chen,Shang-Wei Li,Fu-Jiun Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究通过蒙特卡洛模拟与神经网络方法，在更大晶格尺寸下确认三维反铁磁 $J_1$-$J_2$-$J_3$ 伊辛模型在 $J_1=J_2=J_3=1.0$ 处的相变为二级相变，从而化解了既有文献中的争议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究对立方晶格上三维反铁磁（antiferromagnetic）$J_1$-$J_2$-$J_3$ 伊辛模型（Ising model）在 $J_1=J_2=J_3=1.0$ 时相变性质（phase transition）的结论相互矛盾，故需进一步厘清。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）与神经网络（neural networks, NN）相结合的方式，使用远大于以往研究的晶格尺寸进行分析；并采用专门的训练与测试策略提升 NN 计算效率、降低存储需求。

Result: 在 $J_1=J_2=J_3=1.0$ 处相变明确为二级相变（second order），无任何歧义；此外还研究了 $J_2/J_1=4/7$ 且 $J_3=J_1=1.0$ 情形下的临界现象（critical phenomenon）。

Conclusion: 该工作解决了相关争议，并提出一种极为节省资源（resource-efficient）的途径，使 NN 技术可用于研究高维系统的临界行为（critical behaviors）。

Abstract: Motivated by the conflicting findings in prior studies regarding the nature of the phase transition for the three-dimensional antiferromagnetic $J_1$-$J_2$-$J_3$ Ising model on the cubic lattice with $J_1 = J_2 = J_3 = 1.0$, in this study we conduct a detailed investigation of this model using both Monte Carlo simulations and neural networks (NN). Here $J_1$ and $J_2$ are the intralayer nearest-neighbor and next-nearest-neighbor couplings, respectively, and $J_3$ is the nearest-neighbor interlayer interaction. By investigating the data obtained from much larger lattices than those used in previous studies, we find that the phase transition for $J_1 = J_2 = J_3 = 1.0$ is second order without any ambiguity. Hence we bring a resolution to this debate. Apart from $J_1 = J_2 = J_3 = 1.0$, we study the critical phenomenon associated with $J_2/J_1 = 4/7$ and $J_3 = J_1 = 1.0$ as well. In addition, by employing a dedicated training and testing strategy, our NN computation not only is much more efficient than those utilizing the standard procedures commonly used in the literature, but also requires only a per-mille fraction of the storage capacity needed by a conventional NN calculation when a large linear system size $L$ is considered. The method employed here provides an extremely resource-efficient route for applying NN techniques to study the critical behaviors of higher-dimensional systems.

</details>


### [143] [Gini coefficient of scientists and universities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26053)
*Klaus M. Frahm,Dima L. Shepelyansky*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文将经济学中用于分析财富不平等的洛伦兹曲线（Lorenz curve）与帕累托曲线（Pareto curve）方法应用于Google Scholar引文分布，以基尼系数（Gini coefficient）刻画顶尖科学家与顶尖大学的引用集中度，并借用瑞利-金斯热化与凝聚（Rayleigh-Jeans thermalization and condensation）概念解释该分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引文分布（citation distribution）呈现高度不均衡，作者希望借助已广泛用于跨国财富不平等分析的经济学曲线工具，获得对世界顶尖科学家及大学引文分布的新洞见，并探索其与财富不平等的相似性。

Method: 基于Google Scholar公开的科学家与大学论文引用数据库，构建洛伦兹曲线与帕累托曲线，并据洛伦兹曲线计算基尼系数；对15位诺贝尔奖得主（含A. Einstein与R. Feynman）的文章引用以及世界领先大学所属科学家的引用分别进行测算。

Result: 15位诺贝尔奖得主的文章平均基尼系数 G=0.81，50%的文章仅占全部引用的2.7%，前10%（1%）的文章占71%（30%）的引用；对世界领先大学（如Princeton、Cambridge），约65%的大学引用由仅10%的科学家产生，而50%的科学家仅贡献3%的大学引用。引文分布可由瑞利-金斯热化与凝聚概念较好描述，并与世界财富不平等存在相似性。

Conclusion: 洛伦兹/帕累托曲线与基尼系数为刻画科学家与大学层面的引文不平等提供了有效且直观的度量；引文分布的高度集中与财富不平等类似，并可用瑞利-金斯热化与凝聚框架加以解释。

Abstract: Using the public database of Google Scholar article citations of scientists and universities we apply the algorithm for constructing Lorenz curves to the resulting citation distributions. Using Lorenz and Pareto curves, broadly used for economic analyses of wealth inequality across countries, we show that this approach gives a useful insight of citation distributions of world-leading scientists characterized by the Gini coefficient $G$ obtained from the Lorenz curves. For articles of 15 Nobel laureates (including A. Einstein and R. Feynman) we find the average $G=0.81$ with 50% of articles capturing only 2.7% of all citations and the top 10% (1%) of articles capturing 71% (30%) of all citations. Furthmore, we determine for world-leading universities the Gini coefficient via the citations of affiliated scientists. We show that about 65 percent of all citations belonging to a university (e.g. Princeton, Cambridge) are generated only by 10 percent of their university scientists while 50% of scientists produce only 3% of all university citations. The citation distributions are well described by the concept of Rayleigh-Jeans thermalization and condensation with similarities traced to the world wealth inequality.

</details>


### [144] [Generalised Fluctuating Dynamic Density-Functional Theory: Derivation and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26129)
*Toby Kay,Serafim Kalliadasis*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文从微观哈密顿动力学出发，借助粗粒化与 Zwanzig 投影算符方法，首次原理性地推导出适用于任意可观测场的一般化涨落动态密度泛函理论（fluctuating DDFT, FDDFT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典密度泛函理论（classical density-functional theory）及其动力学扩展 DDFT 多用于平衡或近平衡描述，对涨落显著的介观非平衡体系不足，因而需要涨落扩展；但既有 FDDFT 缺乏系统性微观推导基础。

Method: 以微观哈密顿动力学（Hamiltonian dynamics）为起点，采用粗粒化技术（coarse-graining），包括 Zwanzig 投影算符方法（projection operator method），推导含可逆、耗散与随机贡献的随机演化方程，并通过刘维尔算符（Liouville operator）、Green-Kubo 关系与涨落耗散定理（fluctuation-dissipation theorem）建立与微观动力学的联系。

Result: 所得 FDDFT 适用于任意可观测场，其动力学由以熵泛函（entropy functional）表示的热力学力驱动；进一步平均粗粒化可得到宏观尺度的广义 DDFT，从而区分 DDFT 与 FDDFT 的描述层级。应用于胶体体系可重新导出近期提出的胶体 FDDFT，应用于简单粘性流体则严格恢复非等温的 Landau-Lifshitz 涨落 Navier-Stokes 方程。

Conclusion: 该工作为涨落动态密度泛函理论提供了统一的微观基础，并澄清了著名唯象模型（非等温 Landau-Lifshitz 涨落 Navier-Stokes 方程）的基本原理，显示该形式体系在介观非平衡流体描述中的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: Classical density-functional theory is a powerful theoretical framework for studying the equilibrium density distribution of complex many-body systems. The core of the framework is to bridge microscopic and macroscopic descriptions of fluids through a variational principle that minimises the system's free energy. For dynamical settings, dynamic DFT (DDFT) extends the equilibrium framework to capture the time evolution of an order parameter, typically density. However, DDFT is often insufficient for mesoscopic systems far from equilibrium where fluctuations become significant, necessitating a stochastic extension: fluctuating DDFT. Here we derive a generalised FDDFT from first principles starting from the microscopic Hamiltonian dynamics using coarse-graining techniques, including Zwanzig's projection operator method. The resulting FDDFT applies to arbitrary observable fields, where the dynamics are driven by a thermodynamic force expressed in terms of an entropy functional. The dynamics consist of reversible, dissipative, and stochastic contributions, each directly linked to the underlying microscopic dynamics via the Liouville operator, Green-Kubo relations, and the fluctuation-dissipation theorem. To highlight the difference in the level of description between DDFT and FDDFT, we demonstrate how further coarse-graining via averaging leads to a generalised DDFT, valid at the macroscale. To showcase the effectiveness and utility of the generalised FDDFT, we apply it to two prototypical fluid systems: colloidal and simple viscous fluids. In the former case, we utilise the new formalism to re-derive the recently found colloidal FDDFT. In the latter case, we rigorously recover the non-isothermal extension of the Landau-Lifshitz fluctuating Navier-Stokes equations, providing fundamental derivation of this well-known phenomenological model, enabling to clarify its underlying principles.

</details>


### [145] [Motility-Induced Phase Separation of Run-and-Tumble Particles under Local Stochastic Resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26149)
*Pascal Grange*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 研究了局部随机重置（local stochastic resetting）下发生运动诱导相分离（MIPS）的一维活性气体，给出以Lambert函数表示的精确稳态密度分布，并揭示液滴尺寸对重置率的非单调依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全局重置（global resetting）会破坏大尺度空间自组织，而局部重置（local resetting）可在原点维持质量注入通量并保留大尺度结构，其与运动诱导相分离（motility-induced phase separation, MIPS）的耦合行为尚不明确，故对此展开研究。

Method: 针对由run-and-tumble粒子（RTPs）构成的一维活性气体，采用动力学演化方程（kinetic evolution equations）求解局部随机重置下的非平衡稳态，并进一步扩展至有限重置速度的模型。

Result: 当重置率超过临界值 \(r_c\) 时，系统转变为以原点为中心的高密度液滴与周围气相共存的稳态两相态；稳态密度分布可由Lambert函数精确表达，液滴尺寸随重置率 \(r\) 呈非单调变化并存在最大值；过饱和气相的核化液滴以 \(1/r\) 的弛豫时间尺度指数弛豫至稳态尺寸，且稳态液滴尺寸与重置速度无关。

Conclusion: 局部随机重置可诱导并调控活性气体中的液滴形成，其稳态性质（密度分布、液滴尺寸、弛豫动力学）可解析刻画，且对重置速度具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We investigate the non-equilibrium stationary states of a one-dimensional active gas composed of run-and-tumble particles (RTPs) undergoing motility-induced phase separation (MIPS) and subjected to local stochastic resetting at the origin. Unlike global resetting, local resetting maintains a mass-injection flux at the origin while preserving large-scale spatial self-organization. We demonstrate that above a critical resetting rate $r_c$, the active system undergoes a transition toward a stationary two-phase state featuring a dense liquid droplet centered at the origin coexisting with a surrounding gas phase. Using kinetic evolution equations, we derive the exact steady-state density profile expressed in terms of the Lambert function. The size of the droplet has a non-monotonic dependence on the resetting rate $r$, and attains its maximum. Furthermore, we analyze the dynamical stability of the supersaturated gas phase and show that a nucleated droplet relaxes exponentially toward its steady-state size with a relaxation timescale of $1/r$. We extend our framework to model a finite resetting velocity, proving that the stationary droplet size is invariant with respect to the resetting velocity.

</details>


### [146] [Poisson-shot-noise induced escape dynamics of a passive particle and a dimer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26316)
*Gemeit Goel,Rahul Marathe*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了布朗粒子（Brownian particle）与二聚体（dimer）在热浴与活性浴（active bath，泊松分布的随机冲击）共同作用下、处于双稳势（bistable potential）中的平均首次通过时间（mean first passage time, MFPT），并在小冲击方差近似下给出了弱噪声与强噪声两个标度区间的解析结果，其余情形用数值方法求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MFPT及其倒数逃逸率（escape rate）是刻画随机动力系统（stochastic dynamical systems）的关键量，而活性噪声（active noise）存在下被动粒子的MFPT动力学尚待澄清，且近期关于加速逃逸机制的研究提供了进一步动机。

Method: 构建两类系统——单个布朗粒子与由弹簧连接的二聚体——同时耦合热浴与产生泊松分布冲击（Poisson-distributed kicks）的活性浴，并置于双稳势中；解析处理限于小冲击方差近似（small kick variance approximation），一般情形采用数值求解；二聚体在弱键强下用顺序跳跃机制（sequential-hopping mechanism）、在强键强下用绝热消除方法（adiabatic elimination approach）处理，并考察中间势垒（intermediate barrier）的影响。

Result: 在小冲击方差近似下，MFPT呈现两个标度区间：弱噪声区遵循经典的阿伦尼乌斯标度（Arrhenius scaling）逃逸率，强噪声区MFPT随有效噪声强度呈幂律（power law）标度；数值结果表明，通过选择合适的中间势剖面（intermediate potential profile），MFPT可被增强并进一步优化。

Conclusion: 活性浴环境下粒子与二聚体的逃逸动力学可通过噪声强度区间与键强区分出不同的标度行为，且中间势垒的调控为优化（加速或调节）逃逸过程提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Mean First Passage Time (MFPT) is a crucial quantity that characterizes stochastic processes in various stochastic dynamical systems across different disciplines. The inverse of the MFPT, known as the escape rate, is helpful in the context of Brownian motion and chemical processes. The MFPT dynamics of a passive particle in the presence of active noises presents a fascinating avenue. In this work, we study the dynamics of two different systems: first, a single Brownian particle and second, a dimer (two Brownian particles connected by a spring). In both cases, the systems are kept in contact with a thermal bath and an active bath, and are trapped in a bistable potential. The active bath produces kicks that follow the Poisson distribution. The MFPT of the particle and the dimer is investigated in various scenarios. Analytical results are presented for the small kick variance approximation only. In this approximation, we formulate the MFPT in two scaling regimes: a weak-noise regime characterized by classical Arrhenius scaling for escape rate and a strong-noise regime in which the MFPT scales as a power law in the effective noise strength. For the general case, the problem is solved numerically. Next, under the same approximation, the MFPT of a dimer in the small-bond-strength regime is studied using a sequential-hopping mechanism, and in the large-bond-strength regime, the MFPT is formulated using the adiabatic elimination approach. Motivated by recent studies on speeding up the escape mechanism, the MFPT of the particle and the dimer in the presence of an intermediate barrier is also examined. We deduced from the numerical results that the MFPT can be enhanced and further optimized by selecting a suitable intermediate potential profile.

</details>


### [147] [Origins of Faceted Anomalous Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26356)
*Maximilien Bernard,José Luis Iguain,Alejandro Kolton,Alberto Rosso*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出“面状异常标度（faceted anomalous scaling）”的统计起源在于斜率场（slope field）的空间统计：斜率增量分裂为典型涨落与稀有大幅跳跃。据此可解析导出广义非谐振 Larkin 模型与柱状 KPZ 方程的全局、谱与局域粗糙度指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面状异常标度以 α_s>1、α_s≠α、α_loc=1 为特征，其名称源于界面倾向于形成近乎线性的长“面（facets）”，但其统计起源以及是否必须具备面结构长期不明。本文旨在澄清这一指数结构的物理机制。

Method: 从斜率场（slope field）的空间统计出发，将斜率增量（slope increments）分解为典型涨落与稀有的大幅跳跃两类成分；并在广义非谐振 Larkin 模型（generalized anharmonic Larkin model）与柱状 Kardar–Parisi–Zhang 方程（columnar KPZ equation）中对该框架进行解析构建。

Result: 异常标度可由两种机制产生：典型斜率增量呈现真实的系统尺寸依赖异常标度，或稀有跳跃主导二阶矩；二者可共存，其竞争导致斜率的双分形标度（bifractal scaling）。柱状 KPZ 属跳跃主导情形，给出 α_s=3/2；两类模型中全局、谱与局域粗糙度指数均被精确导出。

Conclusion: 面并非异常标度的必要条件：Larkin 模型同时实现两种机制，可产生有面或无面的异常界面，表明该指数结构由斜率场的空间统计决定，而非由几何面结构决定。

Abstract: Fluctuating interfaces are typically characterized by their global, spectral, and local roughness exponents. Faceted anomalous scaling, characterized by $α_s>1$, $α_s\neqα$, and $α_{\mathrm{loc}}=1$, owes its name to the tendency of such interfaces to develop long, nearly linear segments called facets. Its statistical origin, and whether facets are required, have remained unclear. We show that this exponent structure is determined by the spatial statistics of the slope field. Slope increments split into typical fluctuations and rare, larger jumps. This anomalous scaling arises either when the typical slope increment displays genuine system-size-dependent anomalous scaling or when the rare jumps dominate the second moment. The two contributions can coexist, and their competition leads to bifractal scaling of the slopes. We establish this framework analytically in the generalized anharmonic Larkin model and the columnar Kardar--Parisi--Zhang equation, and we derive exactly the global, spectral, and local roughness exponents in both cases. The Larkin model realizes both mechanisms and produces anomalous interfaces with or without facets, whereas columnar KPZ belongs to the jump-dominated case and yields $α_s=3/2$.

</details>


### [148] [An infinite sequence of higher order Ehrenfest transitions in a one-dimensional crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26371)
*Sakineh Mizani,Szabolcs Varga,Martin Oettel,Péter Gurin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出一个一维可精确求解模型，可展现任意 n≥2 阶的埃伦费斯特相变序列；这些相变无对称破缺，二阶相变也缺乏常规临界行为，但关联长度的密度导数发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶埃伦费斯特相变（Ehrenfest phase transitions）极为罕见且理解不足，本文旨在提供可严格分析的实例以澄清其性质与埃伦费斯特分类（Ehrenfest classification）的适用范围。

Method: 构造一个一维精确可解（exactly solvable）模型，该模型展示任意 n≥2 阶的埃伦费斯特相变序列。

Result: 这些相变不伴随对称破缺（symmetry breaking）；即使二阶相变也缺乏常规临界行为（critical behavior），但关联长度（correlation length）对密度的导数发散。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了一类独特的非临界相变（noncritical phase transitions），并有助于厘清埃伦费斯特分类的适用范围。

Abstract: Higher-order Ehrenfest phase transitions are exceptionally rare and poorly understood. We present an exactly solvable one-dimensional model exhibiting a sequence of Ehrenfest transitions of arbitrary $n\geq2$ order. These transitions occur without symmetry breaking and even the second-order transition lacks conventional critical behavior, although the density derivative of the correlation length diverges. Our results reveal a distinct class of noncritical phase transitions and help clarify the scope of the Ehrenfest classification.

</details>


### [149] [Coexistence phase of the two-dimensional Vicsek model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26561)
*Yu Duan,Hugues Chaté,Benoît Mahault,Xia-qing Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文考察Vicsek模型共存相中的带状图案（band patterns）多样性，指出多数图案在无穷尺寸极限下消失，但整体图像与此前预期不同；并证明局域扰动即可破坏这些规则有序的带状结构，表明共存相至少部分亚稳，渐近上应为由混沌相互作用行波带构成的无序相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vicsek模型在活性物质（active matter）统计物理研究中居于核心地位，近期出现如Toner-Tu群集相（flocking phase）部分亚稳性等重大意外结果，促使重新审视共存相的结构与稳定性。

Method: 基于Vicsek模型共存相的数值/理论分析，系统考察带状图案的多样性并对既往未见的图案进行报道；通过有限尺寸与无穷尺寸极限的比较，以及施加局域扰动（localized perturbation）来检验图案稳定性。

Result: 共存相中观察到多样的带状图案，其中部分为首次报道；多数图案在无穷尺寸极限下消失，但由此得到的整体图像仍与先前预期不同。规则的全局有序带状构型可被局域扰动破坏，说明共存相至少部分亚稳（partially metastable）。

Conclusion: Vicsek模型共存相渐近上应是无序相（disordered phase），由多种混沌相互作用（chaotically interacting）的行波带组成；这修正了对共存相有序性的既有认识。

Abstract: The Vicsek model continues to be central in statistical physics studies of active matter, with major recent surprises such as the partial metastability of the famous Toner-Tu flocking phase. Here we report on the diversity of band patterns observed in its coexistence phase --some of them never observed before--, but we also argue that most disappear in the infinite-size limit, leaving nevertheless a different picture than anticipated previously. We finally show that these rather regular, globally ordered band configurations can be destroyed by a localized perturbation: the coexistence phase is also at least partially metastable, and thus must be, asymptotically, a disordered phase made of a variety of chaotically interacting traveling bands.

</details>


### [150] [Phase Diagram of the Two-Dimensional Vicsek Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26569)
*Yu Duan,Hugues Chaté,Benoît Mahault,Xia-qing Shi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文对二维 Vicsek 模型（Vicsek model）相图进行大幅更新，基于超长时空尺度的数值模拟表明存在多种相分离（phase separation）机制，且共存相（coexistence phase）细分为两个子区，并给出均匀 Toner-Tu 液相（Toner-Tu liquid phase）的亚稳区（metastability region）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于当前对 Vicsek 模型认识的不足（gaps in current knowledge），尤其是既有相图（phase diagram）描述可能不完整，需在更大尺度的数值模拟基础上重新检验。

Method: 方法为二维空间中的大规模数值模拟（comprehensive numerical simulations），其长度与时间尺度（length- and timescales）均超出此前研究范围。

Result: 结果显示：存在多种相分离（phase separation）情形，而非此前文献（Phys. Rev. Lett. 114, 068101 (2015)）提出的单一微观相分离（microphase separation）；共存相（coexistence phase）被细分为两个部分；并给出了均匀 Toner-Tu 液相（Toner-Tu liquid phase）的亚稳（metastability）区域范围。

Conclusion: 结论指出二维 Vicsek 模型相图需实质性修正：相分离机制具多重性、共存区具内部结构，同时均匀 Toner-Tu 液相存在可刻画的亚稳区，这为后续理论分析提供更新的基准相图。

Abstract: Motivated by gaps in our current knowledge of the Vicsek model, we present a major update of its phase diagram in two space dimensions, summarizing comprehensive numerical simulations at length- and timescales never explored before. Our main conclusions are that multiple phase separation scenarios are at play, not just the simple microphase separation put forward in [Phys. Rev. Lett. 114, 068101 (2015)], and that the coexistence phase is subdivided in two. We also map out the region of metastability of the homogeneous Toner-Tu liquid phase.

</details>


### [151] [Avalanches and Aging Driven by Parametric Amplification in Kerr Oscillator Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26573)
*Alejandro S. Gómez,Javier del Pino*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究表明，具有非线性耦合的Kerr参量振荡器（KPO）网络在瞬态动力学中会出现类似玻璃态系统（glassy systems）弛豫雪崩的放大级联，且网络表现出老化（aging）行为：激活间隔按几何级数增长、激活事件随时间仅对数式累积，加性噪声会先缩短最长驻留时间并使网络脱困，而激活序列在强噪声下仍能保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KPO网络利用驱动耗散系统相空间景观（phase-space landscape）的稳态实现伊辛机（Ising machines）、玻色纠错码与传感，但从给定初始条件如何到达这些稳态的瞬态过程尚缺乏研究。

Method: 基于含增益、损耗与非线性耦合的KPO网络瞬态动力学分析，追踪参量增益（parametric gain）与非线性频移（nonlinear frequency shift）如何逐个激活模式，并结合加性噪声（additive noise）考察其对激活序列的影响；示例为20模式网络。

Result: 参量增益先激活失谐（detuning）可被克服的模式，其非线性频移再把休眠模式逐个拉入增益窗口；休眠模式等待期间持续衰减，故每次激活的种子更小、间隔按几何增长，激活仅随时间对数累积。20模式网络中每次驻留约为前一次的2倍。加性噪声为种子设下限并使网络脱困，优先缩短最长驻留，而激活序列在噪声强数个量级时仍存活。

Conclusion: KPO网络的瞬态动力学呈现类玻璃态的老化与雪崩特征，老化会延迟向稳态的趋近，而噪声虽加速脱困但不破坏激活序列，提示瞬态结构对噪声具有稳健性。

Abstract: Gain, loss, and nonlinearity shape the phase-space landscape of driven-dissipative systems, such as Kerr parametric oscillator (KPO) networks realized in superconducting, nanomechanical, and photonic arrays. Networks of KPOs exploit the stationary states of this landscape for Ising machines, bosonic error-correcting codes, and sensing. Yet how transients reach these states from a given initial condition remains largely unexplored. Here we show that KPO networks with nonlinear couplings can develop avalanches in their transient dynamics: rapid cascades of amplification between modes reminiscent of the relaxation avalanches of glassy systems. The mechanism is that parametric gain first activates the modes whose detuning it can overcome, and their nonlinear frequency shifts then pull dormant modes into the gain window one at a time. Dormant modes keep decaying while they wait for their turn, so every activation starts from a smaller seed and the intervals between activations grow geometrically. The network thus ages: the longer it has evolved, the slower it changes, and activations accumulate only logarithmically in time. In a 20-mode network, each dwell lasts about twice as long as the previous one. While aging delays the approach to the stationary state, additive noise sets a floor on the seeds and unsticks the network, shortening the longest dwells first while the activation sequence survives to noise several orders of magnitude stronger.

</details>


### [152] [From particles to continuum theories in effective models of self-propelled rods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26593)
*Elia Bronzo,Hugues Chaté,Benoît Mahault,Alexandre Solon*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究碰撞自驱动杆（self-propelled rods）的Vicsek型模型，通过将杆的几何效应有效实现为速度的向列排列（nematic alignment）与两类排斥，推导出与粒子层面定性一致的流体力学方程（hydrodynamic equations）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碰撞自驱动杆模型可抽象滑行细菌、被分子马达驱动的生物丝等真实系统，但此前多限于二维研究，参数繁多、相图复杂，且对显式杆模型做粗粒化（coarse-graining）极为困难，缺乏忠实于该现象的连续介质理论。

Method: 构建Vicsek型粒子模型，以速度的向列排列与两类排斥有效纳入杆的几何效应，并使模型足够简单以作为系统粗粒化的起点；据此推导流体力学方程。

Result: 所推导的流体力学方程的解在定性上与粒子层面的原始模型相符。

Conclusion: 该Vicsek型模型可作为系统粗粒化的出发点，所得连续介质理论能够定性再现粒子层面行为，为碰撞自驱动杆的宏观描述提供可行路径。

Abstract: Models of colliding self-propelled rods are abstractions of many real systems such as gliding bacteria and biofilaments displaced by molecular motors. They have been studied mostly in two space dimensions and shown to display a wealth of different collective regimes with many parameters involved in the ensuing phase diagrams. Continuum-level theories faithful to this phenomenology are largely lacking since coarse-graining models of explicit rods is notoriously difficult. Here we study collections of particles in which the rods geometry is taken into account effectively, by implementing the nematic alignment of their velocities and two types of repulsion. These Vicsek-style models are simple enough to be starting points for systematic coarse-graining. We thus derive hydrodynamic equations that we show to have solutions qualitatively faithful to their particle-level origin.

</details>


### [153] [Statistical decision theory in thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26617)
*Yoshihiko Hasegawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文将统计决策理论（statistical decision theory）引入随机热力学（stochastic thermodynamics），以极小极大风险（minimax risk）为统一量，借助 Le Cam 两点法（Le Cam's two-point method）导出下界，并在马尔可夫过程（Markov process）中证明该下界由动力学活性（dynamical activity）给出，特殊情形下退化为含动力学活性或熵产（entropy production）的不确定性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估计、假设检验与分类等统计任务此前在随机热力学中多为分散研究，缺乏统一框架；作者希望以统计决策理论统一感知、分类与不确定性关系，从而刻画随机热力学系统在最优估计下仍无法避免的损失。

Method: 引入统计决策理论，聚焦最坏参数选择下最优估计器仍能达到的最小期望损失即极小极大风险（minimax risk），并利用 Le Cam 两点法（Le Cam's two-point method）推导该风险的下界；随后在马尔可夫过程中考察分类与分布估计两类问题以作示例。

Result: 所导出的关系可适用于更广泛的热力学场景；在马尔可夫过程的分类与分布估计问题中，极小极大风险的下界由动力学活性（dynamical activity）给出；对特定动力学进一步分析表明，所得界可化简为涉及动力学活性或熵产（entropy production）的不确定性关系。

Conclusion: 统计决策理论可作为统一随机热力学中感知、分类与不确定性关系的框架，极小极大风险是将统计任务与热力学代价（动力学活性、熵产）联系起来的关键桥梁；所得下界具有跨场景的适用性。

Abstract: We introduce statistical decision theory in stochastic thermodynamic systems. Statistical decision theory integrates several statistical tasks, such as estimation, hypothesis testing, and classification. In this study, we consider statistical decision theory in stochastic thermodynamics, which unifies sensing, classification, and uncertainty relations. In particular, we focus on the minimax risk, the smallest achievable expected loss under the worst-case parameter choice, even with an optimal estimator. We derive lower bounds on the minimax risk using Le Cam's two-point method. The derived relationships can be used across a broader range of thermodynamic scenarios. To illustrate these relations, we study classification and distribution estimation problems in a Markov process. We demonstrate that the minimax risk provides lower bounds given by the dynamical activity in these problems. Furthermore, by examining particular dynamics, we establish that the resulting bounds simplify to uncertainty relations involving dynamical activity or entropy production.

</details>


### [154] [Nucleation-type criticality with infinite number of free-energy minima beyond mean-field theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26638)
*Jun Takahashi,Yoshihiko Nishikawa*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究含Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya相互作用与Potts型各向异性的三维Heisenberg模型相图，发现绕转相与铁磁有序相之间存在一种"临界指数类一级、物理量却连续"的反常相变，并在微观模型中实现了此前仅见于平均场层面的图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: de Gennes的成核型相变理论与Dzyaloshinskii对手性磁体的计算均预示一种兼具一级与连续特征的反常临界行为，但其能否在超出平均场的微观模型中实现尚不清楚，本文即针对此问题展开。

Method: 采用三维Heisenberg模型（Heisenberg model），引入Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya相互作用（Dzyaloshinskii–Moriya interaction）与Potts型各向异性（Potts-like anisotropy），通过数值方法计算相图并考察临界点处的自由能（free energy）结构。

Result: 绕转相（winding phase）与铁磁有序相（ferromagnetically ordered phase）之间的相变为临界相变，其临界指数（critical exponents）与一级相变一致，但物理量保持连续；临界点处自由能局域极小值数目不断增长，支持"无穷多个不连续转变汇聚成临界性"的图像。

Conclusion: 数值结果将de Gennes的成核型相变（nucleation-type transition）理论与Dzyaloshinskii关于手性磁体（chiral magnets）的计算统一起来，表明此类反常临界性可在平均场之外的微观模型中实现。

Abstract: We study the phase diagram of a three-dimensional Heisenberg model with Dzyaloshinski--Moriya interaction under the presence of Potts-like anisotropy. The phase transition between the winding phase and the ferromagnetically ordered phase exhibits a critical phase transition with anomalous features such as critical exponents consistent with that of a first-order transition while maintaining continuity of physical quantities. Furthermore, at the critical point, we see a growing number of free energy local minima, supporting the picture of infinitely many discontinuous transitions forming criticality. Our numerical result unify de Gennes' theory of nucleation-type transitions and Dzyaloshinskii's calculation of chiral magnets by demonstrating that such anomalous criticality can be realized in microscopic models beyond mean-field.

</details>


### [155] [Phase-sensitive quantum-state texture during thermalization of a Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya Heisenberg dimer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26736)
*Onofre Rojas,Zhirayr Adamyan,Fadwa Benabdallah,Vadim Ohanyan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究在各向异性自旋-1/2海森堡二聚体（Heisenberg dimer）中，纵向磁场与Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya（DM）相互作用下热弛豫过程的量子态织构（quantum-state texture）；通过全局弱耦合Lindblad方程揭示DM调控的相干性与织构瞬态特征，提出一种相位敏感的弛豫诊断量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在刻画热弛豫中态织构（texture）的演化，并厘清DM相互作用、相干性（coherence）、自旋手性（spin chirality）与纯度等量在加热/冷却协议下的关系，弥补仅凭相干幅度难以捕捉的相位敏感信息。

Method: 从局域横向与纵向玻色浴（bosonic bath）耦合出发，构建全局弱耦合Lindblad方程，识别跃迁通道并讨论长期近似（secular approximation）的适用性与局限；在局域自旋基下显式求得Gibbs态，并比较rugosity、基于保真度的织构（fidelity-based texture）与迹距离织构（trace-distance texture）同相干性、矢量自旋手性与纯度的行为。

Result: Gibbs态显示DM耦合通过改变能级劈裂与旋转手性相位，同时控制单激发相干（single-excitation coherence）的模与相位。弛豫动力学将布居热化（population thermalization）与能基相干的衰减和进动分离；二者叠加产生依赖DM的织构过冲（overshoots），冷却时出现阻尼振荡与相干求积分量（coherence quadratures）的符号变化，且这些瞬态区别于相干幅度与纯度的演化。弱耗散计算表明长期近似模型内织构极大值仍被保留。

Conclusion: 结果表明织构可作为弛豫的相位敏感诊断量（phase-sensitive diagnostic），其行为依赖于所用量化方式（重叠型与迹距离型织构对瞬态的权重不同），并受DM耦合调控。

Abstract: We investigate quantum-state texture during the thermal relaxation of an anisotropic spin-$1/2$ Heisenberg dimer subject to a longitudinal magnetic field and a Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya (DM) interaction. Starting from local transverse and longitudinal couplings to a bosonic bath, we construct a global weak-coupling Lindblad equation, identify its transition channels, and discuss the applicability and limitations of the secular approximation. The Gibbs state is obtained explicitly in the local spin basis, revealing that the DM coupling controls both the modulus and phase of the single-excitation coherence by modifying the energy splitting and rotating the chiral phase. For heating and cooling protocols, we compare rugosity, fidelity-based texture, and trace-distance texture with coherence, vector spin chirality, and purity. The relaxation dynamics separates population thermalization from the decay and precession of deliberately prepared energy-basis coherence. Their combined contribution to local coherence produces DM-dependent texture overshoots and, during cooling, damped oscillations and sign changes of the coherence quadratures. These transients differ from the evolution of coherence magnitude and purity, while trace-distance texture weights them differently from the overlap-based quantifiers. Calculations at reduced dissipation preserve the texture maxima within the secular model. The results identify a phase-sensitive diagnostic of relaxation.

</details>


### [156] [A McLennan formula for equiaccessible steady states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26755)
*Jacob Calvert*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文通过围绕等可达稳态（equiaccessible steady states）展开，导出了可适用于任意远离平衡情形的 McLennan 公式类比形式：零阶占据由局部逃逸率决定，一阶修正刻画流量失衡对状态可达性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 McLennan 公式以瞬时不可逆熵流（transient irreversible entropy flux）修正近平衡的玻尔兹曼分布（Boltzmann distribution），但其有效性局限于近平衡区域，无法刻画远离平衡的稳态占据规律。

Method: 以等可达稳态（equiaccessible steady states）替代平衡态作为展开基准，对占据概率进行微扰展开，逐阶分析零阶与一阶项的物理来源。

Result: 零阶项表明稳态占据仅由局部逃逸率（local escape rates）决定，给出“低抖动”（low rattling）活性物质原理的强形式；一阶修正衡量流量失衡（traffic imbalances）对各状态可达性的影响。

Conclusion: 该展开揭示非局域的相对占据决定因素（nonlocal determinants of relative occupation）的出现，为远离平衡稳态下的占据分布提供了可推广的理论框架。

Abstract: Near equilibrium, the Boltzmann distribution is corrected by the transient irreversible entropy flux, a result known as the McLennan formula. By expanding around equiaccessible steady states instead, we derive an analogue that can hold arbitrarily far from equilibrium. At zeroth order, steady-state occupation is determined solely by local escape rates, yielding a strong form of the active-matter principle of "low rattling." The first-order correction measures the impact of traffic imbalances on the accessibility of each state, marking the onset of nonlocal determinants of relative occupation.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Where should we look for the deviation from quantum mechanics?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24857)
*Jerome Nicolas,Albert Schwarz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出以亚稳态氦（metastable helium）的寿命与衰变律测量作为检验量子力学（quantum mechanics）偏离的实验途径，并针对量子力学的四次变形（quartic deformation）分析其衰变行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找量子力学偏离的实验检验方案具有基础意义，而亚稳态氦的寿命与衰变律测量被论证为一种有前景的途径。

Method: 在量子力学的四次变形框架下分析亚稳态的衰变，并在维格纳—魏斯科普夫近似（Wigner–Weisskopf approximation）下考察修正理论中的衰变律。

Result: 即使修正很小，平均寿命（mean lifetime）的绝对变化也可能显著；在维格纳—魏斯科普夫近似下，修正理论中偏离指数律（exponential law）的时间尺度可与寿命相当。

Conclusion: 亚稳态氦的寿命与衰变律测量有望用于探测量子力学偏离，且非指数衰变特征可能出现在与寿命同量级的时间尺度上。

Abstract: We argue that the experimental measurement of the lifetime and decay law of metastable helium is a promising way to find a deviation from quantum mechanics. We analyze the decay of the metastable state for a quartic deformation of quantum mechanics. We find that the absolute change of the mean lifetime can be significant even if the modification is small. We show that, in the Wigner--Weisskopf approximation, the deviation from the exponential law in the modified theory can occur on time scales comparable with the lifetime.

</details>


### [158] [Translation-Invariant Pure States with Exotic Half-Chain Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24154)
*Ruizhi Liu,Jinmin Yi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文构造了一维格点上平移不变的纯准自由费米子态，其半链冯·诺依曼代数可为II_∞型或任意III_λ型因子，并通过Jordan-Wigner-Araki变换得到相应的纯自旋链态，从而证明半链因子不限于I型或III型，且纠缠熵增长速率不能决定因子类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有认识中，一维平移不变纯态的半链代数常被视为I型或III型；本文旨在探究能否实现其他类型的冯·诺依曼代数因子，并厘清纠缠熵标度与因子类型之间的关系。

Method: 构造平移不变的纯准自由费米子态（quasifree fermionic states），借助Jordan-Wigner-Araki变换映射为纯自旋链态，并构造以这些态为唯一基态、无能隙且具超多项式衰减相互作用的母哈密顿量（parent Hamiltonian）。此外，对有限味道数、指数衰减跃迁的规范不变二次哈密顿量进行严格分析。

Result: 半链代数可实现II_∞型及任意0<λ≤1的III_λ型因子；相同因子类型下区间纠缠熵可从任意缓慢发散至近幂律增长，表明熵标度不决定因子类型。相对地，有限味道数、指数衰减跃迁的规范不变二次哈密顿量满足二分法：基态或满足面积律且半链为I型因子，或呈对数熵增长且为III_1型因子。

Conclusion: 一维平移不变纯态的半链冯·诺依曼代数不限于I型或III型，纠缠熵行为与因子类型相互独立；而在受限的二次哈密顿量类中则出现I型/III_1型的严格二分。该反例在AI辅助下被发现。

Abstract: We construct translation-invariant pure quasifree fermionic states on the one-dimensional lattice whose half-chain von Neumann algebras are hyperfinite factors of type $\mathrm{II}_\infty$ or $\mathrm{III}_λ$ for any $0<λ\leq 1$. Via the Jordan-Wigner-Araki transformation, these yield translation-invariant pure spin-chain states with the same half-chain factors, demonstrating that such factors need not be of type I or III. Each state is the unique ground state of a gapless, translation-invariant parent Hamiltonian with superpolynomially decaying interactions. For any fixed factor type, the interval entanglement entropy can exhibit widely varying growth rates, from arbitrarily slow divergence to nearly power-law growth, showing that entropy scaling does not determine the factor type. In contrast, we prove that gauge-invariant quadratic Hamiltonians with finitely many flavours and exponentially decaying hopping exhibit a dichotomy: their ground states either satisfy an area law with a type I half-chain factor, or have logarithmic entropy growth with a type $\mathrm{III}_1$ factor. The counterexample was discovered with AI assistance.

</details>


### [159] [Exact probability laws of random inner products: Dirichlet splines, overlaps, uncertainty, and Bargmann invariants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25196)
*Lin Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文揭示随机矩阵与随机量子态的内积（orbital inner products、期望值、重叠、循环 Bargmann 不变量）由两个底层机制决定：诱导随机态对角元的 Dirichlet 分布，以及随机纯态在秩一或 2×2 算符上的期望值为椭圆盘上的数值影子（numerical shadow），并据此推导一系列精确分布律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在统一解释随机矩阵与随机量子态各类内积量的概率行为，将看似分散的分布结果（Dirichlet 分布、数值影子、样条、方差-gamma 律等）归结为少数机制，并刻画模与均值分离、大环境极限及循环不变量的普适性。

Method: 把内积视为诱导随机态的对角元与秩一/2×2 算符上的期望值；利用 Dirichlet 分布（Dirichlet distribution）与椭圆盘上的数值影子（numerical shadow）两个机制，结合 Curry–Schoenberg 样条、单纯形上的矩密度、完全椭圆积分（complete elliptic integral）、三参数马尔可夫链（Markov chain）及大数定律与中心极限定理（central limit theorem）等解析工具进行精确推导。

Result: 对维度 K 的环境诱导态，任意可观测量的期望值是每个特征值重复 K 次的 Curry–Schoenberg 样条，矩向量在单纯形上有显式密度，qutrit 方差律以有限形式给出；两随机 qubit 态重叠对所有旋转不变系综众数等于均值，而 Hilbert–Schmidt qutrit 在均值处密度导数严格为负，众数与均值分离，大环境下中心化重叠收敛到方差-gamma 律（variance-gamma law）而非高斯；两非对易 qubit 可观测量标准差的联合律对任意 K 给出，接近最优方差和的小距离概率呈幂律，Bloch 轴正交时指数不同；三 Haar 随机 qubit 态 Bargmann 不变量密度以完全椭圆积分表达，并给出局部行为、边缘分布、实部为负（基无关相干性）的概率及全部混合矩；任意长度循环由精确三参数马尔可夫链驱动，模的对数满足大数定律与中心极限定理。

Conclusion: 随机量子态内积的统计行为可由 Dirichlet 对角机制与数值影子几何机制统一刻画，所得结果给出众数—均值关系、非高斯极限、精确不变量密度与循环的马尔可夫链结构，为随机态与随机矩阵的定量分析提供精确工具。

Abstract: Inner products of random matrices and random quantum states---such as orbital inner products, expectation values, overlaps, and cyclic Bargmann invariants---are shown to follow from two underlying mechanisms: the diagonal of an induced random state is Dirichlet distributed, and the expectation value of a random pure state in a rank-one or two-by-two operator is a numerical shadow on an elliptical disk. For states induced by an environment of dimension $K$, the expectation value of any observable is a Curry--Schoenberg spline with each eigenvalue repeated $K$ times, and the vector of moments has an explicit density on a simplex; for a qutrit, the variance law is obtained in finite form. The overlap of two random qubit states attains its mode at its mean for every rotation-invariant ensemble, but for Hilbert--Schmidt qutrits the density at the mean has a strictly negative derivative, so mode and mean separate; for large environments, the centred overlap converges to a variance-gamma law rather than a Gaussian. For two noncommuting qubit observables, the joint law of the two standard deviations is derived for all $K$, and the probability of coming within a small distance of the optimal sum of variances scales as a power of that distance, with a different exponent when the Bloch axes are orthogonal. For three Haar-random qubit states, the exact density of the Bargmann invariant is given in terms of a complete elliptic integral, together with its local behaviour and elementary marginals. In every dimension, the probability that its real part is negative---certifying basis-independent coherence---is computed, along with all mixed moments. For cycles of arbitrary length, the invariant is shown to be driven by an exact three-parameter Markov chain, and the logarithm of its modulus obeys a law of large numbers and a central limit theorem.

</details>


### [160] [Spatial Discretization Error for Quantum Chemistry Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25260)
*Di Fang,Jiaqi Zhang*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立 Sobolev-Galerkin 框架，估计无界哈密顿量量子模拟中的空间离散化误差，并给出分子与电子动力学量子模拟的首个严格端到端复杂度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非相对论量子化学基于连续无界空间中的薛定谔方程，而经典与量子计算通常截断空间域并引入有限维基组（finite-dimensional basis），需回答该近似在多大程度上刻画原始连续问题。

Method: 发展通用的 Sobolev-Galerkin 框架，应用于多体库仑系统（many-body Coulomb systems），建立周期化（periodization）与 Galerkin 误差估计；针对动力学中的额外投影误差引入内积论证（inner-product argument）加以控制，并借助具有显式系统尺寸多项式依赖的多体 Sobolev 估计。

Result: 波函数动力学与基态能量的空间收敛速率在定量上不同：固定元胞尺寸下基态能量达到尖锐的 M^{-1} 速率（M 为单粒子基函数数目），而一般 H^2 初值下实时波函数误差无法获得一致的 M^{-1} 速率；结合先进量子算法得到首个空间误差被显式控制的严格端到端复杂度界。

Conclusion: 基函数数目可直接由连续问题确定而无需正则化（regularization），该框架为无界哈密顿量量子模拟提供了严格的空间误差与复杂度保证。

Abstract: Nonrelativistic quantum chemistry is based on the Schrödinger equation in continuous, unbounded space. Computational approaches on classical and quantum computers often truncate the spatial domain and introduce a finite-dimensional basis, reducing the continuum problem to a finite-dimensional one. There is therefore a fundamental question: to what extent does this approximation capture the original continuum problem? In this work, we develop a general Sobolev-Galerkin framework for estimating spatial discretization errors in quantum simulation with unbounded Hamiltonians. We apply this framework to many-body Coulomb systems and establish both periodization and Galerkin error estimates for wave-function dynamics and ground-state energies. For dynamics, the finite-basis projection error of the exact wave function does not capture the full basis-discretization error: an additional dynamical projection error enters our bound at a different rate, and we introduce an inner-product argument to control this contribution. Interestingly, the spatial convergence rates for wave-function dynamics and ground-state energies differ quantitatively. For ground-state energies, we establish a sharp $M^{-1}$ convergence rate at fixed cell size, where $M$ is the number of one-particle basis functions. We also show that a uniform $M^{-1}$ convergence rate is impossible for the corresponding real-time wave-function error with general $H^2$ initial data. The analysis relies on many-body Sobolev estimates with explicit polynomial dependence on system size. Combining these estimates with state-of-the-art quantum algorithms, we obtain, to our knowledge, the first rigorous end-to-end complexity bounds for quantum simulation of molecular and electronic dynamics with all spatial errors explicitly controlled. The number of basis functions is determined directly from the continuum problem, without regularization.

</details>


### [161] [\textit{In situ} certification of multiparticle indistinguishability in a generic interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25471)
*Valery Shchesnovich*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种可扩展的原位（in situ）方法，借助量子并行性（quantum parallelism）与玻色子的聚束（bunching）/费米子的反聚束（anti-bunching）特性，在任意线性干涉仪上评估全同粒子（identical bosons/fermions）的不可分辨性（indistinguishability），并给出多项式采样复杂度与抗损耗鲁棒性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模量子干涉实验（如玻色采样，Boson Sampling）中，粒子不可分辨性是决定干涉效果的核心资源，但现有评估手段难以在通用线性干涉仪上扩展，且实际实验存在损耗。

Method: 方法利用量子并行性及聚束/反聚束倾向，对占据Haar随机酉干涉仪（Haar-random unitary interferometer）不同输入端口的多粒子态进行原位认证；该认证流程适用于任意通用线性干涉仪，不依赖特定干涉仪结构。

Result: 理论分析表明：在Haar随机酉干涉仪上，达到给定统计精度（statistical precision）所需实验运行次数以高概率随粒子数、干涉仪规模及精度倒数的多项式增长；对均匀粒子损耗（uniform particle losses），当单粒子存活概率（single-particle survival probability）的倒数有多项式界时，多项式标度关系仍保持。

Conclusion: 该方法在大规模干涉实验中认证单光子不可分辨性方面尤具适用性，可用于量子优势（quantum advantage）区间的玻色采样实验，为不可分辨性提供具备可扩展性与抗损耗能力的认证工具。

Abstract: We propose a scalable and efficient \textit{in situ} method for assessing the multiparticle indistinguishability of identical bosons or fermions in quantum interference experiments on linear interferometers by harnessing quantum parallelism and the bunching/anti-bunching tendencies of these particles. The proposed certification method can be applied to any generic linear interferometer. For particles occupying distinct input ports of a Haar-random unitary interferometer, the number of experimental runs required to attain a prescribed statistical precision is polynomial in the number of particles, the interferometer size, and the inverse precision, with high probability. Our method is robust under losses: for uniform particle losses, the polynomial scaling is preserved when the inverse single-particle survival probability is polynomially bounded. Our scheme is especially suitable for certifying the indistinguishability of single photons in large-scale interference experiments, such as Boson Sampling in the regime of quantum advantage over classical computation.

</details>


### [162] [Topological Quantum Gates on FCIs with Bounded Quantum Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25472)
*Hisham Sati,Urs Schreiber*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了动量空间局域 Bloch 双荷子（Bloch Hopfions）所对应的双带二维分数量子陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulators, FCIs）的任意子序，指出沿一原胞布里渊区（Brillouin zone）回路的积分量子度规（quantum metric）上界可将拓扑序细化为 |C| 个不同编织可观测量，并给出可实现的量子门分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作表明双带二维 FCI 原则上可承载与动量空间 Bloch 双荷子相关的非常规任意子序；本文旨在进一步刻画该拓扑序的结构，并探索其在容错量子计算中的潜在价值。

Method: 对沿一个原胞布里渊区回路的积分量子度规施加约束（该约束等价于对杂化 Wannier 态（hybrid Wannier states）实空间展宽的上界），分析陈数（Chern number）|C| > 1 时编织可观测量的结构，并对该拓扑序上可实现的量子门进行分类。

Result: 当 |C| > 1 时，拓扑序被细化为 |C| 个不同的编织可观测量，并可由晶格平移循环置换；该循环结构包含弱非阿贝尔（mildly non-abelian）编织相位，并与杂化 Wannier 态的谱流（spectral flow）一致。量子门分类中含非 Clifford 的 T 门与非 Clifford 的纠缠 CCZ 门。

Conclusion: 在 FCI 已知轨道结构内存在通向容错量子过程的新途径，可为未来拓扑量子计算机硬件相关的实验探索提供参考。

Abstract: Two-band 2D fractional Chern insulators (FCIs) have recently been shown, in principle, to admit unusual anyonic order, associated with Bloch Hopfions localized in momentum space. Here we show that bounding the integrated quantum metric along one primitive Brillouin-zone cycle may refine this topological order to $|C|$ distinct braiding observables, cyclically permuted by lattice translations, for Chern numbers $|C| > 1$. The cyclic structure entails mildly non-abelian braiding phases and otherwise matches the spectral flow exhibited by the FCI's hybrid Wannier states, with the metric bound equivalently a bound on the states' real-space spread.
  We give a classification of quantum gates in principle implementable on this topological order; among them we find the non-Clifford T-gate and the non-Clifford entangling CCZ-gate. This points to novel pathways to fault-tolerant quantum processes within the known orbital structure of FCIs, potentially informing experimental searches towards hardware for future topological quantum computers.

</details>


### [163] [A Readout-Aware Theory of Approximate Heisenberg Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26222)
*Yubraj Bhandari,Jakob S. Kottmann,Adelina Bärligea*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出“广义泡利传播（generalized Pauli propagation）”这一统一代数框架，将泡利传播、李代数模拟与Krylov子空间方法纳入同一体系；借助加权对易图（weighted commutator graphs）推导精确恒等式，把近似误差与被舍弃算符分量的带符号贡献联系起来，并确立“延迟可观测性（delayed observability）”作为核心结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典方法计算量子期望值（quantum expectation values）时通常在海森堡绘景（Heisenberg picture）中演化可观测量，为保持可计算性需在演化中丢弃部分算符分量；然而这些分量的大小本身并不能决定期望值误差，误差还取决于其后续演化与输入态，因而需要更精确的误差刻画。

Method: 构建涵盖泡利传播（Pauli propagation）、李代数模拟（Lie-algebraic simulation）与Krylov子空间方法的通用代数框架，利用加权对易图（weighted commutator graphs）推导将近似误差与省略及丢弃算符分量的带符号贡献相联系的精确恒等式，并据此给出有限阶误差估计（finite-order error estimates）与依赖态的选择规则（state-dependent selection rules）。

Result: 核心结果确立延迟可观测性（delayed observability），即给出被丢弃分量贡献在传播时间低阶消失的条件；并证明在适当对称性条件下，保留弱微扰（weak perturbation）的对称保持部分可消除一阶期望值误差。

Conclusion: 该框架揭示了近似误差如何影响目标期望值，为更具信息量的误差分析以及读出感知（readout-aware）的模拟策略设计提供了基础。

Abstract: Classical methods for computing quantum expectation values often propagate observables in the Heisenberg picture. To remain tractable, these methods rely on approximations that discard operator components during evolution. Yet the magnitude of these components alone does not determine the resulting expectation-value error, which also depends on their subsequent evolution and the input state. We develop \emph{generalized Pauli propagation}, a common algebraic framework encompassing Pauli propagation, Lie-algebraic simulation, and Krylov subspace methods. Using weighted commutator graphs, we derive exact identities that relate approximation errors to the signed contributions of omitted and discarded operator components. Our central result establishes \emph{delayed observability}, identifying conditions under which these contributions vanish at low orders in propagation time. We use this structure to develop finite-order error estimates and state-dependent selection rules. We further prove that retaining the symmetry-preserving part of a weak perturbation eliminates first-order expectation-value errors under suitable symmetry conditions. By revealing how approximation errors affect the desired expectation value, our framework provides a basis for more informative error analysis and the development of readout-aware simulation strategies.

</details>


### [164] [Squeezing and Decoherence of Axion Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25466)
*Vasileios Fragkos,Igor Pikovski*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文分析轻暗物质（light dark matter）在退相干（decoherence）存在下的压缩（squeezing）行为，指出压缩持续产生且速度足以在耗散中存活，并可能在热涨落与退相位下仍保留。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）的玻色子（bosonic particles）如轴子（axions）是暗物质主要候选者；小质量情形通常用纯经典场描述，但新近研究表明该描述不完整——其内禀引力自相互作用（gravitational self-interactions）可挤压场，产生超出经典描述的量子特征。

Method: 在退相干存在的情况下，对轻暗物质的压缩进行解析/理论分析，考察压缩产生速率与耗散（dissipation）、热涨落（thermal fluctuations）及退相位（dephasing）的相对关系。

Result: 压缩被持续产生，其速率足够快以在耗散中存活；同时在热涨落与退相位条件下压缩仍可能保留。

Conclusion: 暗物质可能具有持久的量子特征（persistent quantum features），这为暗物质探测（dark matter detection）的相关含义提供了进一步研究动机。

Abstract: Bosonic particles beyond the Standard Model, such as axions, are among the leading candidates for dark matter. If such particles have a small mass, they are usually described as a purely classical field. However, it was recently shown that this description is incomplete: their inherent gravitational self-interactions can squeeze the field, producing a quantum feature beyond the classical description. Here we analyze squeezing of light dark matter in the presence of decoherence. We show that squeezing is continuously generated, and rapidly enough to survive dissipation, and may also remain present under thermal fluctuations and dephasing. Our results demonstrate that dark matter might possess persistent quantum features and motivate further investigation of their implications for dark matter detection.

</details>


### [165] [Majorization for superpositions of nonorthogonal states and the existence of golden states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26483)
*Gökhan Torun*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在相对于线性无关非正交参考态的叠加资源理论中，厘清了“黄金态（golden states）”存在性的决定因素及其Löwdin基结构。研究表明稳健性度量与几何度量由同时与所有参考态等重叠的态最大化，其最大值由对偶基重叠上经典XY模型的基态能量确定；并在Kraus型自由操作下给出Gram占优判据与三参考态黄金态存在的充要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作仅在两个参考态及特殊重叠矩阵情形下已知黄金态，且它们均在Löwdin基下最大相干，缺乏对黄金态存在性判定依据及其Löwdin结构的系统理解。

Method: 采用叠加资源理论框架，分析稳健性与几何叠加度量在等重叠态上的最大化性质，借助对偶基重叠上经典XY模型的基态能量刻画最大值；对Kraus型自由操作证明纯态转换的Gram占优（Gram majorization）充要判据，并推导三非正交参考态下黄金态存在条件。

Result: 最大化稳健性与几何叠加度量的态恰为与每个参考态等重叠的态，其最大值由XY模型基态能量决定；在最大无叠加自由操作下这些态（且仅这些）为黄金态，但未必在Löwdin基下最大相干。Kraus型操作的黄金态必须在Löwdin基下最大相干，但该条件不保证存在性；三非正交参考态存在黄金态当且仅当重叠具共同模s且Bargmann相位Φ满足s≥2cos[(π+|Φ|)/3]。等重叠态还给出Löwdin基下完整的叠加见证族。

Conclusion: 黄金态的存在性由参考态的重叠结构（共同模与Bargmann相位）决定，而非仅由Löwdin基下的最大相干性决定；除非参考态正交，参考态张成空间上的白噪声亦构成一种资源。

Abstract: In the resource theory of superposition relative to linearly independent, nonorthogonal reference states, golden states are known for two reference states and for special overlap matrices, and all of them are maximally coherent in the Löwdin basis. We clarify what decides their existence and their Löwdin structure. The robustness and the geometric measure of superposition are maximized by the same states, which overlap equally with every reference state; their maximal values are set by the ground-state energy of a classical $XY$ model on the overlaps of the dual basis. For every overlap matrix, these states, and only these, are golden under the maximal superposition-free operations, but they need not be maximally coherent in the Löwdin basis. For the Kraus-type free operations, we prove a necessary and sufficient criterion, Gram majorization, for converting pure states into targets with nonzero coefficients; it reduces to majorization for orthonormal reference states. It shows that golden states of these operations must be maximally coherent in the Löwdin basis, but that this does not ensure their existence: three reference states that are not orthonormal admit a golden state if and only if their overlaps have a common modulus $s$ and their Bargmann phase $Φ$ obeys $s\geq2\cos[(π+|Φ|)/3]$. Equal-overlap states also yield a complete family of superposition witnesses in the Löwdin basis, which show that white noise on the span of the reference states is a resource unless these states are orthonormal.

</details>


### [166] [Local Measurement Theory for Quantum Cellular Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26587)
*Tommaso Brambilla,Alessandro Tosini,Paolo Perinotti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文为量子元胞自动机（quantum cellular automata）建立了局域测量理论，用局域完全正映射（completely positive maps）组成的有限非自适应协议刻画测量过程，并证明非局域测量效应源自局域操作的因果传播与组合而非原初的非局域干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论因果性（relativistic causality）与可观测代数（observable algebras）的局域性同测量引起的动力学态变换之间存在张力，阻碍了兼容因果结构的量子测量表述。

Method: 以离散时间、因果可逆演化的有限维量子系统点阵为平台，将测量过程描述为局域完全正映射，组织成沿自动机局域动力学所生成因果锥（causal cone）分布的有限非自适应局域仪器（local instruments）序列，并推导相应的空间支撑界（spatial support bounds）与测量架构。

Result: 每个协议在初态时间切片上诱导有效测量，其支撑受因果结构约束；并构造一维最近邻量子比特自动机的两步协议，在适当参数下经局域结果粗粒化（coarse-graining）后于相邻两格点实现贝尔基投影测量（Bell-basis projective measurement），偏离该点时得到纠缠与稳定子 Rényi 非经典性（stabilizer Rényi non-classicality）可显式量化的形变贝尔测量连续族。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了由严格局域操作合成空间延展量子测量的因果一致算子代数（operator-algebraic）机制。

Abstract: The formulation of quantum measurements compatible with relativistic causality is obstructed by the tension between the locality of observable algebras and the dynamical state transformations induced by measurement processes. We develop a local measurement theory for quantum cellular automata, lattices of finite-dimensional quantum systems with a causal reversible dynamics evolving in discrete time steps. Measurement processes are described by localized completely positive maps and organized into operational measurement protocols, finite non-adaptive sequences of local instruments distributed across the causal cone induced by the automaton local dynamics. Each protocol induces an effective measurement on the initial time slice, whose support is constrained by the causal structure generated by the automaton. Non-local measurement effects therefore arise through causal propagation and composition of local operations, rather than from primitive non-local interventions or instantaneous collapse prescriptions. We establish the corresponding spatial support bounds and characterize the resulting measurement architecture. As an explicit example, we construct a two-step protocol for a one-dimensional nearest-neighbour qubit automaton which, for suitable automaton parameters, realizes a Bell-basis projective measurement on two neighbouring sites after coarse-graining of the local outcomes. Away from this point, the same protocol generates a continuous family of deformed Bell measurements whose entanglement and stabilizer Rényi non-classicality can be quantified explicitly. The framework thus provides a causally consistent operator-algebraic mechanism for synthesizing spatially extended quantum measurements from strictly local operations.

</details>


### [167] [Constrained dissipation and the lifetime of quantum many-body scars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.22223)
*Weyner Ccuiro,Rosario Fazio,Alessandro Silva*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究马尔可夫耗散下 PXP 模型中量子多体疤痕（quantum many-body scars）的命运，指出当跃迁算符（jump operators）保持哈密顿量对称性时，Liouvillian 谱呈现疤痕态塔；疤痕是否主导长时动力学由耗散率之比 γ₊/γ₋ 决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子多体疤痕在封闭系统中已有广泛研究，但其在开放、耗散环境下的稳定性与性质尚不清楚，本文旨在探讨耗散对 PXP 模型疤痕结构的影晌。

Method: 采用 Lindblad 主方程描述马尔可夫耗散（Markovian dissipation），分析 Liouvillian 谱结构；考察跃迁算符满足哈密顿量对称性时的谱特征，并比较对称与非对称耗散（γ₊ > γ₋）、交错跃迁算符以及用部分投影算符替换完全投影算符等情形。

Result: 当跃迁算符保持对称性时，Liouvillian 全谱出现与标准量子疤痕类似的态塔：与 Néel 态有较大重叠，虚部能隙等间距且由单一频率决定；非对称耗散下疤痕本征值实部最小，保真度振荡增强并可被交错跃迁算符进一步稳定，而部分投影算符破坏对称性并摧毁耗散疤痕（dissipative scars）。

Conclusion: 耗散疤痕的存在依赖于跃迁算符与哈密顿量的对称性一致性，其长时动力学的主导地位由本征值实部（即两个弛豫率之比）决定；对称性破缺会使疤痕结构完全消失。

Abstract: We investigate the fate of quantum many-body scars in the PXP model subject to Markovian dissipation. We show that when local jump operators respect the same symmetries of the Hamiltonian the full Liouvillian spectrum displays a tower of states with properties analogous to standard quantum scars: large overlaps with Néel states and equally spaced gaps in the imaginary part of the eigenvalues, set by a single frequency. Whether the long-time dynamics is dominated by scars is determined by the real parts of the eigenvalues, which are set by the ratio between the two relaxation rates $γ_{\pm}$ describing spin flips down-to-up or up-to-down respectively. For asymmetric dissipation ($γ_+ > γ_-$) the scarred eigenvalues have the smallest real parts: the resulting enhanced fidelity oscillations are further stabilized by staggered jump operators. In contrast, replacing the full projection operator in the dissipators with a partial one breaks the symmetries of the problem and is seen to destroy dissipative scars altogether.

</details>


### [168] [Certifying Nonlocal Spin Quantum Entanglement from Local Tunneling Spectra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24440)
*Payman Kazemikhah,Ruolun Zhang,Xiangyu Luo,Byunghoon Kim,Yu-Peng Zhu,Md. Shafayat Hossain,M. Zahid Hasan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一种方法，使逐点测量的局域隧穿谱（tunneling spectroscopy）即可认证自旋纠缠（spin entanglement），无需相干控制或绝对强度定标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠本质上是非局域的，而隧穿谱一次仅探测一个位点，二者存在表观矛盾；研究旨在弥合这一测量方式与纠缠非局域性之间的鸿沟。

Method: 利用精确的自旋求和规则（spin sum rule）将高偏压电导作为内禀归一化，使非弹性自旋翻转权重给出局域量子费舍尔信息（quantum Fisher information）的下界；再借总自旋守恒（total-spin conservation）确定不可直接测量的位点间涨落，将两个局域谱转化为集体纠缠见证（entanglement witness）的界限；并通过自旋泄漏（spin leakage）的谱学界限纳入衬底耦合，引入阈值层级区分自旋间纠缠与环境纠缠，且计入仪器展宽与噪声。

Result: 所得纠缠证书（certificate）可在给定置信水平下成立，并能区分自旋间纠缠与自旋—环境纠缠。

Conclusion: 研究确立了一条从平衡态局域谱学中提取非局域量子信息的途径。

Abstract: Quantum entanglement is intrinsically nonlocal, whereas tunneling spectroscopy measures one site at a time. We show that this apparent mismatch can be overcome: local tunneling spectra can certify spin entanglement without coherent control or absolute intensity calibration. An exact spin sum rule turns the high-bias conductance into an internal normalization, allowing the inelastic spin-flip weight to place a lower bound on the local quantum Fisher information. Total-spin conservation then fixes the otherwise inaccessible intersite fluctuation, converting two local spectra into a bound on a collective entanglement witness. We incorporate coupling to the substrate through a spectroscopic bound on spin leakage, while a hierarchy of thresholds distinguishes entanglement between the spins from entanglement with their environment. Including instrumental broadening and noise, the resulting certificate holds at a stated confidence level. These results establish a route for extracting nonlocal quantum information from equilibrium local spectroscopy.

</details>


### [169] [Concatenated continuous-drive protection of exchange-based spin-qubit gates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24582)
*Takuma Kuno,Takeru Utsugi,Andrew J. Ramsay,Hideo Arimoto,Tetsuo Kodera,Hiroyuki Mizuno*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将级联连续驱动（concatenated continuous driving, CCD）通过全局微波驱动的相位调制，推广至双缀饰自旋量子比特（double-dressed spin qubits）间的交换型（exchange-based）SWAP 与 √SWAP 门，用于抑制失谐噪声与控制误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半导体自旋量子比特中的交换型两比特门对低频失谐噪声（detuning noise）与控制误差敏感，需评估连续驱动保护机制在双比特门层面的有效性。

Method: 以全局微波驱动的相位调制实现 CCD，作用于双缀饰自旋量子比特间的交换型 SWAP 与 √SWAP 操作；并建立部分相关准静态失谐噪声（partially correlated quasistatic detuning noise）模型进行数值/理论分析。

Result: CCD 在保持对准静态共模失谐误差（common-mode detuning errors）强保护的同时，相较传统连续缀饰（continuous dressing）显著提升了对拉比频率误差（Rabi-frequency errors）及比特间拉比频率失配的鲁棒性；但门保真度（gate fidelity）受差分失谐误差（differential detuning errors）影响。噪声平均保真度强烈依赖比特频率涨落的空间相关性，正相关通过抑制差分噪声分量增强 CCD 保护效果。

Conclusion: 比特频率噪声的对称性与空间相关性是决定耦合自旋量子比特体系中连续驱动保护效果的关键因素。

Abstract: Exchange-based two-qubit gates in semiconductor spin qubits are sensitive to low-frequency detuning noise and control errors. Here, we extend concatenated continuous driving (CCD), implemented by phase modulation of a global microwave drive, to exchange-based SWAP and $\sqrt{\mathrm{SWAP}}$ operations between double-dressed spin qubits and investigate its effectiveness for protecting two-qubit gates. We find that CCD retains strong protection against quasistatic common-mode detuning errors while substantially improving robustness against Rabi-frequency errors and qubit-to-qubit Rabi-frequency mismatch compared with conventional continuous dressing. By contrast, the gate fidelity is impacted by differential detuning errors. By modeling partially correlated quasistatic detuning noise, we further show that the noise-averaged gate fidelity depends strongly on the spatial correlation of the qubit-frequency fluctuations, with positive correlations enhancing CCD protection by suppressing the differential-noise component. These results identify the symmetry and spatial correlation of qubit-frequency noise as key factors governing continuous-drive protection in coupled spin-qubit systems.

</details>


### [170] [Quantum control as renormalization group flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26154)
*Zuo Wang,Paul M. Schindler,Modesto Orozco-Ruiz,Nguyen H. Le,Florian Mintert,Marin Bukov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文将重整化群（renormalization group, RG）思想移植到量子控制领域，通过构造含时哈密顿量（time-dependent Hamiltonian）空间中的流（flow），在保持目标幺正操作（unitary）不变的前提下单调压低控制子空间之外的哈密顿量权重，从而把任意控制方案"重整化"为实验可及的形式，并借助流方程工程（flow-equation engineering）扩展到周期性与开区间平滑协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 哈密顿量RG揭示不同尺度有效描述之间的关系并诱导哈密顿量空间中的流，而量子控制同样需要将理想但难以实现的哈密顿量转化为实验可及的形式；现有高频展开等微扰方法适用范�围有限，缺乏统一视角。作者因此希望建立控制层面的RG框架，统一哈密顿量工程与幺正量子控制。

Method: 在含时哈密顿量空间中构造流，以仅含实验可及项的预设控制子空间（control subspace）作为不动点流形（fixed-point manifold）；该流保持目标幺正操作不变，并单调减少子空间外的哈密顿量权重（Hamiltonian weight）。进一步利用构造流的自由度提出流方程工程，以适配含规定端点的时周期协议与开区间平滑协议等多样控制问题。

Result: 给出两个应用展示框架效用：(i) 超越高频展开适用范围的强驱动Floquet工程（nonperturbative Floquet engineering），在Haldane模型上构造出可实现的时间周期协议；(ii) 从单位保真度绝热捷径（counterdiabatic）演化得到可及的快进驱动（fast-forward driving），在大横向场Ising链（transverse-field Ising chains）上远离绝热区，将长程多体绝热规范势（adiabatic gauge potential）压缩进原有的含时Ising控制调度中。

Conclusion: 该控制RG框架为哈密顿量工程与幺正量子控制提供了统一视角，并可作为一种组织原则（organizing principle）来理解表面上互不相同的非平衡量子动力学（nonequilibrium quantum dynamics）。

Abstract: The Renormalization Group (RG) establishes relations between effective descriptions of physical systems at different scales; in Hamiltonian RG, these relations induce a flow in the space of Hamiltonians. In direct correspondence, we design an RG framework for quantum control by constructing a flow in the space of time-dependent Hamiltonians. A prescribed control subspace, selected to contain only experimentally accessible terms, forms the fixed-point manifold, while the flow preserves the target unitary and monotonically reduces the Hamiltonian weight outside this subspace. Exploiting the freedom in constructing the flow, we introduce flow-equation engineering to enhance the versatility of the framework for diverse control problems, including time-periodic and open-interval smooth protocols with prescribed endpoints. We provide two disparate applications to exhibit the utility of our approach: (i) Nonperturbative Floquet engineering beyond the regime of validity of high-frequency expansions, which we demonstrate by constructing an implementable time-periodic protocol for the Haldane model; (ii) Accessible fast-forward driving from unit-fidelity counterdiabatic evolution, which we showcase for large transverse-field Ising chains away from the adiabatic regime by compressing the long-range multibody adiabatic gauge potential into the original time-dependent Ising control schedules. Our control RG framework reveals a unifying perspective on Hamiltonian engineering and unitary quantum control, and can serve as an organizing principle behind seemingly disparate nonequilibrium quantum dynamics.

</details>


### [171] [Sidewall Metrology Protocol for Superconducting Qubit Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26223)
*Jørgen Garnæs,Henrik S. Guttesen,Astrid T. Rømer,Oscar Danielsson,Emily P. Duggan,Zhenhai Sun,Asen Lyubenov Georgiev,Li Wei Chang,Morten Kjaergaard,Mark Bieler,Lukas Grünhaupt,Sabbir A. Khan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出基于扫描探针显微镜的侧壁定量表征框架，引入新的侧壁粗糙度与曲率等效半径描述符，并形成可推广的侧壁表征流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 侧壁形貌是影响超导量子比特（superconducting qubit）性能的潜在重要因素，但因缺乏标准化测量协议，其定量表征仍具挑战。

Method: 构建基于扫描探针显微镜（scanning probe microscopy）的测量框架，提出改进的侧壁均方根横向粗糙度描述符（root mean square lateral sidewall roughness descriptor）与量化垂直侧壁形貌的曲率等效半径（curvature-equivalent radius），并将其应用于量子比特器件的不同组件。

Result: 描述符与测量流程在器件不同组件上得到应用，据此提出一套侧壁表征协议（sidewall characterization protocol）。

Conclusion: 该工作为跨实验室比较制备工艺提供定量基础，并为后续研究侧壁几何与量子比特性能之间的关联创造条件。

Abstract: Sidewall morphology is a potentially important factor influencing superconducting qubit performance, yet its quantitative characterization remains challenging due to the absence of standardized measurement protocols. In this work, we develop a scanning probe microscopy-based measurement framework for the quantitative characterization of superconducting qubit sidewalls. The framework introduces a refined root mean square lateral sidewall roughness descriptor and a new curvature-equivalent radius for quantifying vertical sidewall morphology. The descriptors and measurement procedures are applied to different components of qubit devices, forming the basis of a proposed sidewall characterization protocol. This provides a foundation for quantitative comparisons of fabrication processes across laboratories, enabling future investigations of correlations between sidewall geometry and qubit performance.

</details>


### [172] [Universal Quantum Criticality from a Gaussian Variational Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26246)
*Reyhaneh Khasseh,Fabian Ballar Trigueros,Markus Heyl,M. A. Rajabpour*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一套通过费米子高斯态（fermionic Gaussian states）构造量子相变最佳无相互作用费米子描述的系统性方法，并在长程横场伊辛链（long-range transverse-field Ising chain）上验证：由此得到的自洽欧拉方程（Euler equation）给出与独立数值基准高度吻合的临界线（critical line），并可解析预测连续变化的临界性质（critical properties），尽管高斯近似在相变附近对精确基态的保真度最差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续相变（continuous phase transitions）的普适行为由维度与对称性控制的长波长不动点理论（fixed-point theories）刻画，但何种系统能被无相互作用理论捕获、从而获得解析且精确的预测，仍是一个核心问题。

Method: 以费米子高斯态为变分类，建立获取量子相变最佳无相互作用费米子描述的系统性流程；应用于相互作用的长程横场伊辛链，将问题化为一个自洽欧拉方程求解。

Result: 自洽欧拉方程给出的临界线与独立数值基准（numerical benchmarks）高度吻合；其奇异的动量结构（singular momentum structure）可对连续变化的临界性质给出解析预测；但精确对角化（exact diagonalization）显示，高斯态对精确基态的保真度在相变附近最低、非高斯性（non-Gaussianity）最强。

Conclusion: 即使高斯变分描述的微观波函数近似在相变附近最弱，其仍能给出临界性质的准确预测，说明临界性质的准确性与波函数近似的微观质量可以解耦。

Abstract: Universal behavior at continuous phase transitions is encoded in long-wavelength fixed-point theories controlled by dimensionality and symmetries. It is a central question for which systems this universal behavior and the underlying long-wavelength description can be captured by a noninteracting theory allowing for analytical and accurate predictions. In this work we provide a systematic procedure for obtaining the best noninteracting fermionic description of a quantum phase transition through fermionic Gaussian states. We demonstrate the performance of our procedure for the interacting long-range transverse-field Ising chain. This formulation reduces the problem to a self-consistent Euler equation that yields a critical line in close agreement with independent numerical benchmarks, while its singular momentum structure provides analytic predictions for continuously varying critical properties. Yet exact diagonalization shows that Gaussian-state fidelity to the exact ground state is poorest, and non-Gaussianity strongest, near the transition. Thus, accurate predictions for critical properties can emerge from the Gaussian variational description even where its microscopic wave-function approximation is weakest.

</details>


### [173] [End-to-End Optimization of Partial Fault Tolerance in Neutral-Atom Arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26471)
*Stefano Veroni,Aydin Deger,Andrew J. Daley*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作研究部分容错（partially fault-tolerant）"STAR"架构与中性原子（neutral-atom）平台的协同设计，从原子物理层面出发构建逻辑错误模型并评估应用级性能，证明在中等错误率（$p\sim 10^{-3}$）与数千量子比特规模下，部分容错方案在开销与错误率上均优于全容错方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算（fault-tolerant quantum computation）开销巨大，部分容错的STAR架构通过放宽非Clifford操作（non-Clifford operations）的容错要求来降低通用计算成本，但增加了对硬件噪声的暴露，因此需要与底层硬件进行细致的协同设计以最大化其性能与适用性。

Method: 在中性原子平台上开展STAR架构的协同设计：物理层面识别$^{171}\mathrm{Yb}$里德堡门（Rydberg gates）中的噪声偏置（noise bias）并借助误差选择性脉冲优化（error-selective pulse optimization）加以增强；逻辑层面识别内禀错误抑制机制；随后对表面码（surface code）实现进行电路级仿真，并针对周期边界费米-哈伯德模型（Fermi-Hubbard model）设计适配早期实现的模拟电路。

Result: 电路级仿真显示逻辑错误较此前估计降低一个数量级以上；逻辑相干噪声（logical coherent noise）在领头阶消失。部分容错的门合成（gate synthesis）相较全容错方案在态注入深度（state-injection depth）上约降低一个数量级，Clifford资源（Clifford resources）降幅更大。

Conclusion: 在中等错误率$p\sim 10^{-3}$与数千量子比特条件下，部分容错方法可同时实现更低开销与更低错误率，为早期量子优势（early quantum advantage）提供了具有竞争力的路径。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant (FT) quantum computation offers a pathway to quantum advantage, but its realization is hindered by its significant overhead. The partially FT "STAR" architecture reduces the cost of universal computation at the price of relaxing FT requirements for non-Clifford operations, increasing exposure to hardware noise. Therefore, maximizing its performance and applicability demands careful co-design with the underlying hardware. In this work we investigate the co-design of the STAR architecture with neutral-atom platforms, connecting the underlying atomic physics to logical error modeling and application-level performance. At the physical level, we identify a favorable noise bias in $^{171}\mathrm{Yb}$ Rydberg gates, and show how it can be enhanced through error-selective pulse optimization. At the logical level, we identify an intrinsic error suppression mechanism, yielding vanishing logical coherent noise in leading order. Detailed circuit-level simulations of surface code implementations show more than an order of magnitude reduction in logical error relative to previous estimates. As a concrete target application, we focus on the Fermi-Hubbard model with periodic boundaries, for which we design a simulation circuit particularly suited to early implementations. We quantify the resource tradeoff of partially and fully FT approaches to gate synthesis, with the former showing approximately an order of magnitude reduction in state-injection depth, and an even larger reduction in Clifford resources. Comparing with classical and fully FT approaches, we identify regimes where partial fault tolerance provides both lower overheads and lower error rates. In our estimates, this is accessible with moderate error rates $p\sim 10^{-3}$ and a few thousand qubits, underlining that for these types of problems, partial FT approaches provide a compelling pathway to early quantum advantage.

</details>


### [174] [Non-Markovian exceptional points and information backflow: a Heisenberg reaction-coordinate approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26620)
*Daniel Holst,Gianmichele Blasi,Shishir Khandelwal*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于反应坐标（reaction-coordinate）形式的海森堡方程方法，获得精确解析动力学，用于研究非马尔可夫（non-Markovian）开放量子系统中的高阶例外点（exceptional points, EPs），并澄清EP与动力学复活（revivals）之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究开放量子系统例外点主要依赖马尔可夫主方程（Markovian master equations），但近期发现EP也可出现在非马尔可夫情境中，通常借助赝模（pseudomodes）方法处理结构化环境；本文寻求更简洁且精确的分析途径。

Method: 采用基于反应坐标形式（reaction-coordinate formalism）的海森堡方程（Heisenberg-equation）方法，获得精确解析动力学，并与主方程方法进行对比分析。

Result: 该方法显著简化了分析；研究显示“EP标志复活起始”的普遍看法并非普遍成立；推导出高阶非马尔可夫EP下复活次数的界，并考察了高阶EP附近的增强渐近弛豫（asymptotic relaxation）现象。

Conclusion: 进一步研究了工程化库（engineered reservoirs）中的非马尔可夫EP，表明其可用于调控不可逆信息丢失（irreversible information loss）的速率。

Abstract: The primary pathway to the investigation of exceptional points (EPs) in open quantum systems has been the use of Markovian master equations. However, recently it has been realised that they can arise in settings that show non-Markovian behaviour. The main approach in this direction has been the use of pseudomodes, which allow the influence of a structured environment to be replaced by new damped auxiliary modes. In this work, we use a Heisenberg-equation approach which relies on the reaction-coordinate formalism to obtain exact analytical dynamics. First, we show how our method considerably simplifies the analysis compared to the use of master equations. Second, we utilise it to discuss non-Markovian revivals in systems with second and higher-order EPs, showing that the notion that EPs demarcate the onset of revivals is not generally true. We then utilise our results to discuss the dynamical effects of higher-order non-Markovian EPs, deriving bounds on the number of revivals and investigating enhanced asymptotic relaxation near higher-order EPs. Finally, we investigate non-Markovian EPs with engineered reservoirs and show how they can be utilised to control the rate of irreversible information loss.

</details>


<div id='cs.MS'></div>

# cs.MS [[Back]](#toc)

### [175] [QRecoupling.jl: numerical and exact computation of classical and quantum recoupling coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24074)
*Seth K. Asante*

Main category: cs.MS

TL;DR: QRecoupling.jl 是一个 Julia 软件包（package），可在统一接口下对经典与量子重耦合系数（recoupling coefficients）、量子阶乘的有限和进行数值、精确及符号计算，并在经典极限、主酉层级（principal unitary levels）及实/复形变下保持评测精度；在较小自旋下性能与 WIGXJPF 相当，较大自旋下快约 6–10 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该文关注经典与量子重耦合系数（如 3j、6j 符号）跨经典极限与形变区间的统一评测需求，现有工具多针对单一情形，缺乏兼顾精确判定、符号化与数值加速的一体化接口。

Method: 以紧凑的阶乘规则（factorial rule）及其分圆指数（cyclotomic exponents）为核心，揭示因子消去、多项式结构与层级支撑（level support）；数值上采用自适应数值求值（adaptive numerical evaluation），并与对“和为零”情形的精确判定相结合。

Result: 经典 3j、6j 符号在最小自旋下速度与基于素因子分解表的 WIGXJPF 相当，自旋截断为 500 时约快 6 倍和 10 倍；在实、单位根（root-of-unity）与复形变下，自旋至 400 时相对独立高精度参考的最大相对误差为 3.5×10⁻¹⁴；401 通道聚变矩阵（fusion matrices）计算比逐元素循环约快 100 倍。

Conclusion: 该包在精度与效率两方面均具竞争力，能够以单一接口覆盖经典极限、主酉层级与形变情形，并为用户自定义求和提供统一框架。

Abstract: QRecoupling.jl is a Julia package for numerical, exact and symbolic evaluation of classical and quantum recoupling coefficients and generic finite sums of quantum factorials, with one interface across the classical limit, principal unitary levels and real or complex deformation. A compact factorial rule and its cyclotomic exponents expose factor cancellation, polynomial structure and level support. The package covers quantum Clebsch-Gordan coefficients, 3j and 6j symbols and user-defined sums. Its adaptive numerical evaluation is combined with exact decisions for sums that cancel to zero. Classical 3j and 6j symbols are evaluated at a speed comparable to that of WIGXJPF, which is based on prime factorization tables, at the smallest spins, and about 6 and 10 times faster at spin cutoff 500. Under real, root-of-unity and complex deformation, the largest relative error measured against independent high-precision references is $3.5 \times 10^{-14}$ for spins up to 400. Fusion matrices with 401 channels are computed about 100 times faster than by loops over their entries.

</details>


<div id='hep-th'></div>

# hep-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Light EFT Strings and Asymptotic Accelerated Expansion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24062)
*Veronica Collazuol,Alessandra Grieco,Irene Valenzuela*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究四维𝒩=1理论微扰无穷远极限附近粒子塔（particle towers）、EFT弦（EFT strings）与EFT膜（EFT membranes）之间的相互作用，指出EFT膜的标度向量（scaling vectors）位于EFT弦所生成格中，并据此评估通量紧致化（flux compactifications）中渐近加速膨胀（asymptotic accelerated expansion）的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于厘清无穷远极限（infinite-distance limits）处各类延展对象（塔、弦、膜）标度关系，并检验渐近加速膨胀在四维有效场论（EFT）中是否可实现，特别是填补此前工作（arXiv:2209.11821）遗留的漏洞。

Method: 采用自下而上（bottom-up）的有效场论分析，结合F理论（F-theory）与Type IIA Calabi-Yau紧致化中的具体计算，比较EFT弦与EFT膜的相对标度，并考察量子阻碍（quantum obstructions）对Kähler势（Kähler potential）渐近形式的影响。

Result: EFT膜的标度向量落在EFT弦标度向量生成的格（lattice）中，与粒子塔的既有结论一致；自下而上分析未给出普遍阻碍，但F理论中两条候选加速轨迹因EFT弦变得比哈勃尺度（Hubble scale）更轻而被排除，从而使四维EFT失效。

Conclusion: 该结果封闭了arXiv:2209.11821中的漏洞，表明弦轻于哈勃尺度可排除特定加速解；近期关于𝒩=1无穷远极限量子阻碍的结果或可通过改变Kähler势渐近形式提供具体解决方案。

Abstract: We study the interplay among towers of particles, EFT strings, and EFT membranes near perturbative infinite-distance limits of four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ theories. We show that the scaling vectors of EFT membranes lie in the lattice generated by those of the EFT strings, as previously found for particle towers, and determine their specific relative scaling in F-theory and Type IIA Calabi-Yau compactifications. We then leverage these objects to assess the viability of asymptotic accelerated expansion in flux compactifications. While our bottom-up analysis does not yield a general obstruction, we rule out two candidate accelerating trajectories in F-theory because an EFT string becomes lighter than the Hubble scale, invalidating the four-dimensional EFT. This closes the loophole found in arXiv:2209.11821. We also study how recent results on quantum obstructions to $\mathcal{N}=1$ infinite-distance limits may provide a concrete resolution by changing the asymptotic form of the Kähler potential.

</details>


### [177] [Angular Momentum Loss at Fourth Post-Minkowskian Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24072)
*Alessandro Georgoudis,Carlo Heissenberg,Gustav Mogull,Rodolfo Russo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 $\mathcal{O}(G^4)$ 阶计算了两个非自旋点状物体的引力散射（gravitational scattering）中总角动量损失，结合振幅（amplitudes）与世界线（worldline）方法，并由此导出 $\mathcal{O}(G^5)$ 阶、首次自作用力（self-force）阶的守恒偏转角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在将引力散射中角动量损失的计算推进到更高后闵可夫斯基阶（$\mathcal{O}(G^4)$），弥补已有文献在该阶及质量偶极损失、偏转角等高阶量上的不足。

Method: 方法融合振幅方法（amplitudes）与世界线方法（worldline）：从两份引力波形（gravitational waveform）构造被积函数，其巨质量线割线（cuts on massive lines）自然来自世界线；再胶合两份拷贝，并用反向幺正性（reverse unitarity）按振幅思路计算相空间积分（phase-space integrals）。

Result: 分别得到辐射角动量（radiated angular momentum）与静态贡献；后者在该阶完全由初始剪切（initial shear）和线性记忆效应（linear memory）决定。合并两项后给出角动量损失的相对论表达式及质量偶极损失（mass dipole loss）红外有限部分（infrared-finite component）的表达式，低速极限与文献结果一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明，角动量损失可分解为辐射与静态两部分，且静态部分由剪切与线性记忆完全刻画；该结果进一步用于推导 $\mathcal{O}(G^5)$ 阶、首次自作用力阶的守恒偏转角（conservative deflection angle）。

Abstract: We calculate the total loss of angular momentum for a gravitational scattering of two non-spinning point-like objects at $\mathcal{O}(G^4)$. Our methodology combines use of both amplitudes and worldline-based methods. We derive the integrand from two copies of the gravitational waveform with cuts on massive lines arising naturally from the worldline. We then glue these two copies and evaluate the resulting phase-space integrals using reverse unitarity, in the amplitudes spirit. We obtain separately the radiated angular momentum and the static contribution that, at this order, is entirely determined by the initial shear and the linear memory. Combining these two terms, we derive relativistic expressions for the angular momentum loss and for the infrared-finite component of the mass dipole loss, which in the low-velocity limit match results available in the literature.
