We also use this result to derive the conservative deflection angle at $\mathcal{O}(G^5)$ and first self-force order.

</details>


### [178] [On the Positivity of de Sitter Group Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24073)
*Donald Marolf,Xuyang Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在全局 de Sitter（dS$_d$）背景下研究微扰量子引力（perturbative quantum gravity）的约束问题，证明在权重要求 $\mathrm{Re}\sum_{i=1}^N \Delta_i > d-1$ 下，群平均内积（group-averaging inner product）对含任意自旋的 $N$ 粒子态具有正定性，并指出更小权重态可能出现负范数及其对理论的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dS 等距变换（isometries）定义的约束要求线性化态中所有荷为零，而标准 Fock 空间中唯一可归一化解为真空；Higuchi 提出用群平均内积替代，但其正定性此前仍未解决，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在标准全局 dS$_d$ 模（global dS$_d$ modes）下，对粒子权重满足 $\mathrm{Re}\sum_{i=1}^N \Delta_i > d-1$（群平均绝对收敛判据）的 $N$ 粒子态（含任意自旋）证明群平均内积的正定性；并将 dS$_2$ 中玻色子与费米子（整数与半整数自旋）作为简化模型处理。

Result: 对 $d>2$ 及上述权重条件，所有此类 $N$ 粒子态的群平均内积均为正；但亦识别出在更小权重下群平均仍绝对收敛的态类，其中部分态具有负的群平均范数（negative group-averaging norms）。

Conclusion: 群平均内积的正定性在给定权重条件下成立，但小权重态负范数的存在表明其物理解释并非普遍适用，作者由此讨论了该现象对微扰 de Sitter 量子引力的含义。

Abstract: Perturbative quantum gravity about a global de Sitter (dS$_d$) background involves constraints that restrict the state of the linearized theory but which appear only at higher orders in perturbation theory. In particular, for familiar gravitational theories with $d>2$ these constraints require all charges defined by continuous de Sitter isometries to vanish in the linearized state. While standard Fock spaces have the property that the only normalizable state satisfying these constraints is the vacuum, Higuchi suggested that this lack of allowed states should be remedied by instead using the so-called group-averaging inner product. This inner product satisfies important uniqueness conditions, but its positivity has to date remained an open question. For $d > 2$, we establish this positivity here for all such states with $N$ particles (including particles of arbitrary spin) in standard global dS${}_d$ modes where the particle-weights $Δ_i$ satisfy the condition ${\rm Re} \, \sum_{i=1}^N Δ_i > d-1$, which is the criterion for group averaging to converge absolutely for arbitrary finite linear combinations of such states. We also treat the case of bosonic and fermionic (integer and half-integer spin) particles in dS$_2$ as a toy model for the more general case.
  However, we can identify certain classes of states for which group averaging continues to converge absolutely for smaller weights. Interestingly, in some cases these smaller-weight states turn out to have {\it negative} group-averaging norms. We thus also discuss the implications of this observation for perturbative de Sitter quantum gravity.

</details>


### [179] [Wrapping M5-branes on a non-spin manifold: Spin$^h$ structures and AdS$_7$ black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24084)
*Okan Günel,Cameron Gibson,C. N. Pope*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在七维极大规范超引力（maximal gauged supergravity）中构造了 AdS$_2\times M_5$ 解，其中内部空间 $M_5$ 为 Wu 流形 $SU(3)/SO(3)$，尽管该流形不具有自旋（spin）或 spin$^c$ 结构，却凭借 spin$^h$ 结构实现超对称，并进一步提升至 $D=11$ 超引力、扩展为黑洞并讨论全息与重整化群流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统超对称解通常要求内部流形具有自旋结构（spin structure），而 Wu 流形既无 spin 也无 spin$^c$ 结构，构成对既有构造范式的挑战，本文旨在说明此类流形仍可支撑超对称 AdS$_2$ 解。

Method: 在七维极大规范超引力（maximal gauged supergravity）中求解 AdS$_2\times M_5$ 形式的解，利用 spin$^h$ 结构：普通旋量因 holonomy 中的负号而定义不良，该负号由旋量携带 $SO(3)$ Yang-Mills 荷、处于 $SO(3)$ 的双覆盖（double cover）$SU(2)$ 的半整数同位旋（isospin）表示所产生的额外负号补偿；并将解提升（lift）至 $D=11$ 超引力，内部空间为 Wu 流形的单位切丛（unit tangent bundle）$M_9$。

Result: 得到超对称 AdS$_2\times M_5$ 解及其 $D=11$ 提升 AdS$_2\times M_9$；将七维解扩展为黑洞并讨论其全息（holographic）解释与 UV 到 IR 流；重新审视已知的 AdS$_2\times H^3\times H^2$ 解，指出其流方程（flow equations）存在精确解；详细比较 AdS$_2\times S^5$ 提升中内部空间（$S^5$ 的单位切丛）与 Wu 流形情形的异同；并构造对应非紧致对偶解，取 $M_5=SL(3,\mathbb R)/SO(3)$。

Conclusion: Wu 流形虽缺乏 spin 与 spin$^c$ 结构，仍可通过 spin$^h$ 结构支撑超对称 AdS$_2$ 解，并在 $D=11$ 层面获得一致的几何与拓扑描述；相应的黑洞扩展与 UV–IR 流分析、以及与 $S^5$ 情形的对比表明该类构造具有更普遍的结构性意义。

Abstract: We construct a solution of seven-dimensional maximal gauged supergravity of the form AdS$_2\times M_5$, where $M_5$ is the five-dimensional Wu manifold $SU(3)/SO(3)$. The solution is supersymmetric, despite the fact that the Wu manifold admits neither a spin structure nor a spin$^c$ structure. Rather, it admits a spin$^h$ structure, wherein the ill-definedness of ordinary spinors due to a minus sign in their holonomy is compensated by a further minus sign arising through the spinors carrying $SO(3)$ Yang-Mills charge, in a half-integer isospin representation of the $SU(2)$ double cover of the $SO(3)$. We show how the configuration can be lifted to a solution of $D=11$ supergravity of the form AdS$_2\times M_9$, where $M_9$ is the unit tangent bundle of the Wu manifold. We discuss the geometry and topology of $M_9$, and study the supersymmetry from the eleven-dimensional standpoint. We extend the seven-dimensional solution to a black hole, and discuss some aspects of its holographic interpretation and the UV to IR flow. We revisit a previously-known AdS$_2\times H^3\times H^2$ solution, and its UV to IR flow, showing that the flow equations in fact admit of an exact solution. Additionally, we give a detailed discussion of the lift of the AdS$_2\times S^5$ solution to $D=11$, exhibiting similarities and differences between the properties of its internal space $M_9$ (the unit tangent bundle of $S^5$) and the Wu manifold example of our main discussion. We also construct the non-compact dual of the Wu manifold solution, where $M_5$ is taken to be $SL(3,\mathbb R)/SO(3)$.

</details>


### [180] [Topological deformations of local gauge theories: a presymplectic BV-AKSZ approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24111)
*Ivan Dneprov,Alexey Donchenko,Maxim Grigoriev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出"拓扑形变（topological deformations）"机制：对系统某一对称性进行规范化（gauging）会使其耦合到BF型分区（BF-type sector），从而在不引入新局域自由度（local degrees of freedom）的前提下，把具有不同规范结构但局域自由度相同的系统联系起来，并统一解释了标量—2-形式对偶与单模引力等情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相同的局域自由度可由规范结构（gauge structure）与场内容不同的系统描述，如四维标量场与2-形式规范场的对偶、以及宇宙学常数成为整体自由度的单模引力（unimodular gravity）。既有理解缺乏统一说明这些系统间关系的构造性框架，本文旨在提供这一框架并厘清其在一致相互作用与时空整体结构敏感性上的差异。

Method: 在预辛BV-AKSZ方法（presymplectic BV-AKSZ approach）中系统化地实施构造：对一般阶数（generic degree）的对称性进行规范化，相当于修改底层的预辛规范PDE（presymplectic gauge PDE），使系统耦合到BF型分区而不增添局域自由度。

Result: 规范化0阶（degree-0）对称性可识别由整体对称性相联系解，复现标量—2-形式对偶（scalar–2-form duality）与主手征模型—Freedman–Townsend对偶；规范化1阶（degree-1）对称性（即形变）则产生新的整体自由度，如单模引力中的宇宙学常数。

Conclusion: 规范不等价形式解空间（spaces of gauge-inequivalent formal solutions）仅相差有限维分区（finite-dimensional sector）的系统，可由拓扑形变自然联系；因规范分区不同，其一致相互作用及对时空整体结构的敏感性可能显著不同。

Abstract: The same local degrees of freedom can be described by systems with different gauge structure and field content. Standard examples are the duality between the scalar field and the 2-form gauge field in four dimensions, and unimodular gravity, where the cosmological constant becomes a global degree of freedom. In a precise sense, the spaces of gauge-inequivalent formal solutions of such systems differ by a finite-dimensional sector. Moreover, because their gauge sectors are different, their consistent interactions and their sensitivity to the global structure of spacetime may differ substantially. We show that such systems are naturally related by what we call topological deformations: gauging a symmetry of generic degree couples the system to a BF-type sector without introducing new local degrees of freedom. The construction is carried out systematically within the presymplectic BV-AKSZ approach, where it amounts to a modification of the underlying presymplectic gauge PDE. Gauging degree-0 symmetries identifies solutions related by global symmetries, which reproduces the scalar--2-form and principal chiral model--Freedman--Townsend dualities. Gauging degree-1 symmetries, i.e. deformations, gives rise to new global degrees of freedom, such as the cosmological constant in unimodular gravity.

</details>


### [181] [Gravitational Cheshire effect from Yang-Mills instantons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24127)
*Borja Diez*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 四维Yang-Mills瞬子的维数约化可自然产生三维中具有异常引力屏蔽机制的物质构型，其完全性或部分性隐身取决于所选取的四维引力瞬子背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在建立四维（反）自对偶性（(anti-)self-duality）与三维BPS型关系之间的联系，并探讨在四维中引力不可见（gravitationally invisible）的瞬子扇区经维数约化后如何表现为隐身物质构型。

Method: 从欧氏Yang-Mills理论出发，沿瞬子背景的U(1)等距生成的紧致方向约化（anti-）自对偶规范构型；规范联络分解为三维Yang-Mills场与伴随希格斯场（adjoint Higgs field），度规则给出伸缩子（dilaton）与阿贝尔规范联络。分别在Taub-NUT与Eguchi-Hanson背景下分析约化场的引力反作用。

Result: Taub-NUT背景下，约化场组成两个相互屏蔽的配对，阿贝尔与非阿贝尔扇区的反作用各自内部抵消，整体物质内容在平坦三维空间上支持复合隐身（composite stealth）构型；Eguchi-Hanson背景下，非阿贝尔物质扇区反作用抵消，但阿贝尔扇区保持引力活跃并变形三维背景几何，形成部分隐身（partially stealth）物质构型。

Conclusion: 四维（反）自对偶性与三维BPS型关系存在直接联系；四维中引力不可见的瞬子扇区经维数约化后可降为完全复合隐身构型或部分隐身物质构型，取决于背景瞬子的具体几何。

Abstract: We show that the dimensional reduction of four-dimensional Yang-Mills instantons naturally gives rise to nontrivial matter configurations in three dimensions exhibiting an unusual mechanism of gravitational screening. Starting from Euclidean Yang-Mills theory, we reduce (anti-)self-dual gauge configurations along a compact direction generated by a $U(1)$ isometry of the instanton background. Under this reduction, the gauge connection decomposes into a three-dimensional Yang-Mills field and an adjoint Higgs field, while the metric gives rise, as usual, to a dilaton and an Abelian gauge connection. For instantons on the Taub-NUT background, the reduced fields organize themselves into two mutually screening pairs: the backreaction of the Abelian sector cancels internally, and the same occurs independently in the non-Abelian sector. As a result, the full matter content supports a composite stealth configuration on flat three-dimensional space. By contrast, the dimensional reduction of instantons on the Eguchi-Hanson background yields a non-Abelian matter sector whose gravitational backreaction cancels, while the Abelian sector remains gravitationally active and therefore deforms the three-dimensional background geometry. These examples reveal a direct connection between four-dimensional (anti-)self-duality and three-dimensional BPS-type relations, and illustrate how instantonic sectors that are gravitationally invisible in four dimensions can descend into either fully composite stealth configurations or partially stealth matter configurations after dimensional reduction.

</details>


### [182] [Landscape of Higgs vacua in 5d $\mathcal{N}=1$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24176)
*Amihay Hanany,Elias Van den Driessche*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究强耦合五维 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 规范理论的 Higgs 分支，借助 1/2-BPS 算符及其代数关系刻画真空模空间，并分析 Chern-Simons 能级对 chiral ring 关系的形变以及在 $SU(N_c)_k$ 与 $SO(N_c)$ 两类理论中的具体结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合五维 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 规范理论具有由 1/2-BPS 算符标识的 Higgs 分支模空间，但其在不同相之间的映射、Chern-Simons 能级对环关系的形变机制以及无穷耦合下的约束结构尚需系统刻画。

Method: 以 1/2-BPS 算符及其代数关系为工具，对 $SU(N_c)_k$ 理论映射不同相下的模空间，并考察 Chern-Simons 能级 $k$ 对介子（mesons）、重子（baryons）与瞬子（instantons）之间 chiral ring 关系的形变；对 $SO(N_c)$ 理论构造无穷耦合下的 chiral ring 约束；同时通过将 chiral ring 按作用量变量（action variables）展开，把 Higgs 分支识别为经典可积系统（classical integrable system）。

Result: 在 $SU(N_c)_k$ 理论中给出了跨相模空间的映射，确定了 $k$ 对介子、重子与瞬子间 chiral ring 关系的形变方式，并识别出特定的对称性增强（symmetry enhancement）模式；在 $SO(N_c)$ 理论中构造了无穷耦合下的 chiral ring 约束；并将 Higgs 分支表述为经典可积系统。

Conclusion: 五维 $\mathcal{N}=1$ 强耦合规范理论的 Higgs 分支可由 1/2-BPS 算符的 chiral ring 代数统一描述，Chern-Simons 能级与规范群选择决定其约束与对称性增强模式，且该分支与经典可积系统存在对应关系。

Abstract: Strongly coupled 5d $\mathcal{N}=1$ gauge theories have a Higgs branch of their moduli space of vacua, identified by 1/2-BPS operators and their algebraic relations. For $SU(N_c)_k$ theories, we map the moduli space across different phases and we establish how the Chern-Simons level $k$ deforms the chiral ring relations among mesons, baryons, and instantons, while determining specific patterns of symmetry enhancement. For $SO(N_c)$ theories, we construct the corresponding chiral ring constraints at infinite coupling. Finally, we identify the Higgs branch as a classical integrable system, through the expansion of the chiral ring in terms of action variables.

</details>


### [183] [Covariant One-Loop Corrections from a Non-Covariant Expansion: The Scalar Field Case](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24227)
*Lucas Ducastelo de C. C. Lima,Riccardo Fecchio*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在线性化引力（linearized gravity）的非协变框架下计算了四维标量场四次自相互作用对标量场与引力非最小耦合（non-minimal coupling）的单圈修正，涵盖发散与有限部分，并借此讨论耦合的运行及退耦定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标量场与引力的非最小耦合在弯曲时空量子场论中具有重要意义，但其由物质自相互作用诱导的单圈修正尚需在更简便的框架下系统计算与验证，尤其是有限部分的协变性恢复及与协变方法结果的一致性。

Method: 采用线性化引力（linearized gravity）的非协变框架，显式计算四次自相互作用诱导的非最小耦合单圈费曼图（Feynman diagram），包含发散部分与有限部分；通过协变性（covariance）要求构造单圈有效作用量（one-loop effective action），并与热核技术（heat kernel technique）的已知结果对比。

Result: 所得单圈有效作用量与热核方法的协变结果一致；由此研究了非最小耦合的重整化群运行（running）、其紫外（UV）与红外（IR）极限，并在动量减除（momentum subtraction）重整化方案下验证了退耦定理（decoupling theorem）的成立。

Conclusion: 线性化引力方法亦可用于推导标量场的有效势（effective potential）并给出其协变形式；与严格协变方法结果的一致性表明该方法具有较好的通用性与实用性。

Abstract: We compute the one-loop correction to the non-minimal coupling of a scalar field theory to gravity, induced by a quartic self-interaction. We adopt the non-covariant framework of linearized gravity and explicitly compute the corresponding Feynman diagram, including both the divergent and the finite parts. The corresponding contribution to the one-loop effective action is obtained by the requirement of covariance and compared with the known results obtained by the heat kernel technique. We study the running of the non-minimal coupling, its UV and IR limit and verify the validity of the decoupling theorem in the momentum subtraction scheme of renormalization. The linearized gravity approach is also used for deriving the effective potential for the scalar field and its covariant version is stated. The agreement with results derived using strictly covariant methods is verified and highlights the versatility of the linearized gravity approach.

</details>


### [184] [Bosonization for the Schwinger effect at small fermion mass: analytic formula for the 1+1D current in an arbitrary time-dependent, inhomogeneous electric field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24272)
*Samuel E. Gralla,Morifumi Mizuno*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文在1+1维中，对任意含时且空间不均匀的外电场下Schwinger对产生（Schwinger pair-production）的电流期望值，按费米子质量m作微扰展开给出解析公式，精确到m²阶，并借助"混合方法"（hybrid method）结合玻色化（bosonization）完成计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schwinger对产生在任意含时、空间不均匀电场中的电流期望值缺乏便于计算的解析表达式；直接计算面临真空定义与电流算符重整化（regularization）的困难，需要一种能同时处理这两点的框架。

Method: 采用新提出的"混合方法"（hybrid method）：部分计算在费米子一侧进行，部分在玻色一侧进行。费米子一侧利用无穷远过去的自由理论定义真空；玻色子一侧因电流算符对玻色场线性，可在无需重整化（regularization）的情况下计算电流。在空间均匀背景场的特例中，另用绝热重整化（adiabatic regularization）完全在费米子一侧独立计算。

Result: 得到贡献出现于m⁰、m² log m和m²阶，O(m)阶无贡献。空间均匀情形下两种方法的结果细节一致，构成对费米-玻色对偶（duality）的高度非平凡检验。

Conclusion: 混合方法可有效计算任意含时、空间不均匀电场下的Schwinger电流，并借助均匀场情形下绝热重整化结果的吻合，验证了所依赖的对偶关系。

Abstract: We derive an analytic formula for the expectation value of the current due to Schwinger pair-production in an arbitrary time-dependent, spatially inhomogeneous external electric field in 1+1 dimensions, as a perturbative expansion in the fermion mass $m$. We work to order $m^2$, finding contributions at order $m^0$, $m^2 \log m$ and $m^2$; there is no contribution at $O(m)$. The calculation is enabled by a new ``hybrid method'' using bosonization, where part of the calculation is performed on the fermion side and part is performed on the boson side. The fermion side allows us to define the vacuum using the free theory in the infinite past, while the boson side allows us to compute the current without regularization, since the current operator is linear in the boson field. In the special case of a spatially homogeneous background field, we also perform the calculation purely on the fermion side using adiabatic regularization. The detailed agreement of the results provides a highly non-trivial check of the duality.

</details>


### [185] [Gravity as Large Scale Structure: Families of CFTs and the Higher-Spin Gap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24496)
*Eric Perlmutter*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在大 $N$ 的 $d\geq 2$ 维共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）中存在一个看似普适的参数化间隙界 $\Delta_{\rm gap} \lesssim (\delta c)^{1/d}$，其中 $\delta c$ 为相邻理论间的中心荷（central charge）增量；该文以「CFT 族（families of CFTs）」概念为其提供更精确的框架，并讨论其全息（holographic）含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单迹高自旋间隙（single-trace higher-spin gap）随中心荷增量的这一参数化标度行为看似普适，但缺乏清晰的理论解释；作者希望为此类现象寻找更精确的统一框架，并厘清其在 AdS/CFT 对应中的意义与若干疑难。

Method: 先借助在理论空间中不同共形点之间插值的广义膜（generalized branes）概念对间隙界作启发式解释；继而借鉴数论中 $L$-函数族（families of $L$-functions）的思想，给出 CFT 族的可操作定义，并表述该间隙界的族层面（family-level）版本。

Result: 将族层面的间隙界在全息上解释为 AdS$_{d+1}$ 真空族（families of AdS$_{d+1}$ vacua）的约束：在适用情形下，该界排除普朗克尺度分离（Planckian scale separation），限制额外维度的尺寸与数目，并解释弦论/M 理论（string/M-theory）中简单的标度模式；同时说明所有已知大 $N$ 矢量模型（vector models）的间隙有界。

Conclusion: CFT 族为理解大 $N$ CFT 中高自旋间隙的参数化标度提供了统一框架，并可作为全息构造的判据，对尺度分离与额外维度给出非平凡限制，并解释若干已知模型与弦/M 理论的标度特征。

Abstract: We observe an apparently universal behavior in large $N$ conformal field theory (CFT) in $d\geq 2$ dimensions: the single-trace, higher-spin gap obeys the parametric bound $Δ_{\rm gap} \lesssim (δc)^{1/d}$, where $δc$ is the central charge increment between successive theories. The origin of this bound is explained heuristically, using a generalized notion of branes that interpolate between conformal points in theory space. Seeking a more precise framework for this picture leads to the central concept of families of CFTs, which tacitly underlies various features and puzzles of AdS/CFT. We develop a working definition of families, with inspiration from families of $L$-functions in number theory, and formulate a family-level version of the gap bound. Interpreted holographically for families of AdS$_{d+1}$ vacua, this bound, when applicable, rules out Planckian scale separation, constrains the size and number of extra dimensions, and explains simple scaling patterns in string/M-theory. It also accounts for why all known large $N$ vector models have a bounded gap.

</details>


### [186] [Strong generation of the chiral algebra of $\mathcal N=4$~$SU(N)$ super Yang--Mills theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24594)
*Yifan He*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文证明：由单个伴随标量（adjoint scalar）的不变量多项式构造的小型 $\mathcal N=4$ 短多重态（short multiplets）强生成（strongly generate）相应的手征代数（chiral algebra），从而在 $SU(N)$ 情形下确立 Beem 等人的强生成猜想（strong-generation conjecture）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四维 $\mathcal N=4$ 超杨–米尔斯理论（super Yang–Mills theory）的 Schur 扇区（Schur sector）包含无穷多受保护算符（protected operators），而单一伴随标量的不变多项式仅由有限个 Casimir 次数组织；二者之间的有限—无穷张力构成研究的出发点。

Method: 证明综合运用循环迹上同调（cyclic trace cohomology）、量子提升（quantum lifting）与任意次数的四次—奇传输恒等式（quartic–odd transport identity）；并以精确场关系（exact field relations）和有限秩牛顿障碍（finite-rank Newton obstruction）排除额外最低空间（lowest space），同时显式导出所需的正规排序修正（normal-ordering corrections）与 BRST 原语（BRST primitives）。

Result: 对任意 $N\ge2$，对应于次数 $2,\ldots,N$ 的全部场分量即已足够，覆盖标准相对量子 BRST 上同调（relative quantum BRST cohomology）中的每一个共形权重（conformal weight）与次数；包括非标量味表示（non-scalar flavor representations）在内的所有额外最低空间均被排除。

Conclusion: 所得完备性定理（completeness theorem）表明有限族超对称多重态即可组织无界量子谱：每个 Schur 类都是其分量与导数的正规多项式（normal polynomial），从而为亚纯算符乘积（meromorphic operator products）提供统一的局域输入，在指标（index）抵消前界定受保护态的重数，并给出受保护迹算符（protected trace operators）有限秩重组的代数解释。

Abstract: The Schur sector of four-dimensional $\mathcal N=4$ super Yang--Mills theory contains infinitely many protected operators, while the invariant polynomials of a single adjoint scalar are organized by finitely many Casimir degrees. I prove that the small $\mathcal N=4$ short multiplets built from these invariants strongly generate the associated chiral algebra, thereby establishing the $SU(N)$ case of the strong-generation conjecture of Beem et al. For every $N\ge2$, all field components associated with degrees $2,\ldots,N$ suffice, in every conformal weight and every degree of the standard relative quantum BRST cohomology. The proof brings together cyclic trace cohomology, quantum lifting, and an arbitrary-degree quartic--odd transport identity. Exact field relations and a finite-rank Newton obstruction exclude every additional lowest space, including non-scalar flavor representations; the required normal-ordering corrections and BRST primitives are derived explicitly. The resulting completeness theorem explains how a finite family of supersymmetric multiplets organizes an unbounded quantum spectrum. Every Schur class is a normal polynomial in their components and derivatives, even when its gauge-theory representative involves fermions and arbitrarily many derivatives. This supplies a uniform set of local inputs for meromorphic operator products, bounds protected state multiplicities before cancellations in an index, and gives an algebraic account of the finite-rank reorganization of protected trace operators.

</details>


### [187] [Holomorphic extremal surfaces for pseudoentropy in dS/CFT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24698)
*Giorgos Anastasiou,Ignacio J. Araya,Avijit Das,Javier Moreno*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在全纯坐标下构建 de Sitter 空间（de Sitter space）中余维二超曲面的极值化问题，计算全息赝熵（holographic pseudoentropy）对球形区域调和形变的普适展开，并分析复化极值面上同调类选择带来的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 de Sitter 背景下，赝熵的极值面构造通常依赖分段 Hartle–Hawking 方案，需明确复化极值面与边界锚定条件的一般表述，并检验赝熵的普适性是否在同调类选择下保持。

Method: 采用全纯坐标（holomorphic coordinates）表述余维二超曲面的极值化方程 \(K^I=0\)，在未来无穷远施加实锚定条件，得到复化体实现；对任意大小与维度的球形区域做调和形变（harmonic deformations），计算其全息赝熵；并针对 dS\(_3\) 中以 Schwarzschild–dS 质量参数化的锥形缺陷（conical defects）做检验。

Result: 赝熵的普适展开在形变参数二阶得到确认，且在每个同调类（homology class）内与积分围道（integration contour）选择无关；不同同调类由离散整体坐标等距对应的整数标记，在奇数边界维中仅相差普适项的符号，在偶数维中相差实有限平移。对 dS\(_3\) 锥形缺陷还验证了赝熵第一定律（first law of pseudoentropy）及每个同调类内围道简并的持续性。

Conclusion: 复化极值面框架将分段 Hartle–Hawking 构造自然推广为复化体实现，赝熵的普适行为在同调类内部稳健，而同调类间的差异由边界维数奇偶性决定其表现为符号翻转或实平移；赝熵第一定律在锥形缺陷情形下成立。

Abstract: We formulate the extremization problem for codimension-two hypersurfaces in de Sitter space in holomorphic coordinates, with real anchoring conditions at future infinity. The resulting equation ($K^I=0$) describes complex bulk realizations extending the usual piecewise Hartle--Hawking construction. We compute holographic pseudoentropy for harmonic deformations of spherical regions of arbitrary size and dimension, confirming its universal expansion through second order in the deformation parameter. The result is independent of the integration contour within each homology class on the complexified extremal surface. Inequivalent classes, labeled by an integer associated with a discrete global-coordinate isometry, differ by a sign of the universal term in odd boundary dimensions and by real finite shifts in even dimensions. For conical defects in dS$_3$ parametrized by a Schwarzschild-dS mass, we also verify the first law of pseudoentropy and the persistence of contour degeneracy within each homology class.

</details>


### [188] [Bootstrapping Fermionic Veneziano Amplitudes from Regge Zeros](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24754)
*Pongwit Srisangyingcharoen*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文将 Veneziano 振幅的 Regge-zero bootstrap 推广到含外部费米子的树级散射，通过因式分解出携带 little-group 权重的旋量结构并对约化振幅施加重子/玻色子通道的 Regge 零点约束，证明两条 Regge 轨迹均为线性、由对偶性将约化振幅固定为 Beta 函数，并在谱简并要求下得到与开超弦振幅一致的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 Regge-zero bootstrap 主要针对玻色振幅（Veneziano 振幅），尚缺乏对含外部费米子散射的对应处理；本文旨在检验该 bootstrap 框架能否推广至费米子参与的四点振幅。

Method: 考虑无质量标量（scalar）与无质量自旋-1/2 费米子（spin-1/2 fermion）的有色序四点振幅，s 通道出现费米子共振、t 通道出现玻色子共振；剥离携带 little-group 权重的旋量结构后，对约化振幅（reduced amplitude）施加各通道留数在由对侧轨迹控制的 Regge 零点（Regge zeros）处消失的约束，并假设超软 Regge 行为（ultrasoft Regge behaviour）及留数除 Regge 零点外无其他零点。

Result: 在该假设下，两条 Regge 轨迹（Regge trajectories）均为线性，最大自旋随能级增长为 J_F = n + 1/2 与 J_B = m + 1；费米子极点展开与玻色子极点展开之间的对偶性（duality）将约化振幅固定为 Beta 函数（Beta function），仅剩归一化与两条 Regge 斜率之比未定。

Conclusion: 要求费米子谱与玻色子谱简并（degenerate）即使两斜率相等，所得振幅与开超弦振幅（open superstring amplitude）完全一致，表明 Regge-zero bootstrap 可自然推广至含外部费米子的情形并重现超弦结果。

Abstract: We extend the Regge-zero bootstrap of the Veneziano amplitude to tree-level scattering with external fermions. We consider the colour-ordered four-point amplitude of a massless scalar and a massless spin-$\frac12$ fermion, in which fermionic resonances appear in the $s$-channel and bosonic resonances in the $t$-channel. Factoring out a spinor structure that carries the little-group weight of the amplitude, we bootstrap the resulting reduced amplitude, whose residues in each channel vanish at Regge zeros controlled by the trajectory of the opposite channel. Assuming ultrasoft Regge behaviour and that the residues have no zeros other than Regge zeros, we show that both Regge trajectories are linear and that the maximal spins grow with the level as $J_F=n+\frac12$ and $J_B=m+1$. Duality between the fermionic- and bosonic-pole expansions then fixes the reduced amplitude to a Beta function, up to its normalisation and the ratio of the two Regge slopes. Requiring the fermionic and bosonic spectra to be degenerate equates the slopes, and the resulting amplitude coincides with the open superstring amplitude.

</details>


### [189] [Complex spinodals and supercritical crossovers in Kerr-Newman-AdS$_4$ black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24978)
*Bum-Hoon Lee,Hocheol Lee,Somyadip Thakur*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文在固定角动量与电荷条件下，研究四维 Kerr-Newman-AdS 黑洞的跨临界（supercritical crossover）行为，证明物理临界点唯一、四次 Landau 系数恒正，并给出共存熵、潜热及跨临界面上 Widom 型线的精确标度规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超临界区域缺乏相变、但仍可能存在跨临界（crossover）结构，而黑洞热力学中该区域的组织方式尚不清楚，尤其是 Landau 理论与自旋odal（spinodal）在临界点以上的延续形式。

Method: 以精确焓（enthalpy）为基础，对电荷-旋转参数族证明临界点唯一性与四次 Landau 系数的正定性；采用精确四次约化（quartic reduction）求共存熵与潜热；将复共轭自旋odal对的实部温度投影定义为 Widom 型线，并随电荷-旋转比变化组装成跨临界曲面；结合端点精确解与数值延拓（numerical continuation）验证。

Result: Landau 尖点（cusp）确定虚熵与虚温度分别随偏离临界压力的平方根与 3/2 次幂标度；投影折线与局域热容脊（heat capacity ridge）共享临界切线，其温度间隔在临界点附近为二次型，且热容脊位于更高温度；尖点指数在该参数族中普适。

Conclusion: Kerr-Newman-AdS 黑洞的跨临界结构可由 Landau 理论统一刻画，尖点指数为族内普适，而跨临界温度依赖所采用的热力学判据。

Abstract: We study supercritical crossovers in four-dimensional Kerr-Newman-AdS black holes at fixed angular momentum and charge. From the exact enthalpy, we prove uniqueness of the physical critical point and positivity of the quartic Landau coefficient throughout the charge-rotation family, treating the uncharged Kerr-AdS endpoint in separate scaling variables. An exact quartic reduction determines the coexistence entropies and latent heat throughout the subcritical regime. Above criticality, the two physical spinodals continue as a complex conjugate pair. Their real temperature projection defines a Widom-type line, and varying the charge-rotation ratio assembles these lines into a crossover surface. The Landau cusp fixes the imaginary entropy and temperature to scale with the square root and three-halves powers of the pressure displacement from criticality. The projected fold and the local heat capacity ridge share a critical tangent. We derive their leading quadratic temperature separation and prove that the ridge lies at a higher temperature near criticality. Exact endpoint solutions and numerical continuation check these predictions. The cusp exponents are common to the family, while the crossover temperature depends on the thermodynamic criterion used to define it.

</details>


### [190] [The Anyon Atlas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25439)
*Jonah Berean-Dutcher,Clay Cordova,Julio Parra-Martinez,Richard Tuddenham*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作通过模张量范畴与超模张量范畴的模数据，对2+1维玻色情形（≤22个任意子）与费米情形（≤26个任意子）的可能拓扑量子场论进行分类，并发现无法由Chern-Simons理论经任意子凝聚或普通全局对称性规范化获得的“全新”拓扑量子场论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2+1维拓扑量子场论（topological quantum field theory）的分类是凝聚态与拓扑物态研究的核心问题，但现有理论多与Chern-Simons理论及其凝聚、规范化构造相关，是否存在超出此类框架的新理论缺乏系统检验。

Method: 利用模张量范畴（modular tensor category）与超模张量范畴（supermodular tensor category）的模数据（modular data），借助SL(2,Z)的同余表示（congruence representation）与可扩展至高秩的数值优化算法（numerical optimization algorithm）构造并枚举候选数据，同时梳理任意子凝聚（anyon condensation）与Chern-Simons实现之间的关系。

Result: 对11与12种任意子类型的样例显式求解五边形与六边形方程（pentagon and hexagon equations），证明相应模数据可由完整的酉拓扑量子场论（unitary topological quantum field theory）实现；并给出https://www.AnyonAtlas.org在线资源。

Conclusion: 发现若干模数据无法由任何Chern-Simons理论经任意子凝聚或普通全局对称性规范化（gauging of ordinary global symmetries）得到，代表了超出规范理论（gauge theory）范畴的定性新型拓扑量子场论。

Abstract: We classify possible topological quantum field theories in 2+1 dimensions with up to 22 anyons in the bosonic case and up to 26 in the fermionic case, through the modular data of modular and supermodular tensor categories. We construct such data via congruence representations of $\mathrm{SL}(2,\mathbb{Z})$ using a numerical optimization algorithm that scales to high rank. We chart the relations between the resulting theories, including anyon condensations and Chern-Simons realizations. We find examples of modular data that do not appear to arise from any Chern-Simons theory by any sequence of condensations of anyons or gaugings of ordinary global symmetries. These examples give qualitatively new topological quantum field theories, which go beyond any notion of gauge theory. For examples in this class with 11 and 12 anyon types, we solve the pentagon and hexagon equations explicitly, showing that these modular data are realized by full unitary topological quantum field theories. We also introduce https://www.AnyonAtlas.org, an online resource for exploring the data.

</details>


### [191] [Remarks on the Parameter Dependence of QCD$_{2A}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25567)
*Uwe Trittmann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文给出数值证据表明，二维伴随QCD谱的微弱 $N_c$ 依赖并非由少数算符主导，所有算符对哈密顿量的忠实表示均不可少；并完整列出单迹与多迹态之间的相互作用，讨论大组分质量极限下的eLCQ行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前研究显示仅有少数算符贡献二维伴随QCD（adjoint QCD）谱中极弱的 $N_c$ 依赖，本文检验这些算符中是否存在主导者，并厘清单迹（single-trace）与多迹（multi-trace）态间的完整相互作用结构。

Method: 采用数值方法检验各算符对谱的贡献；按迹数块（trace-number block）完整枚举单迹与多迹态之间的相互作用；并在eLCQ（exact light-cone quantization，或按原文eLCQ方法）框架下考察大 $N_c$ 时的大组分质量区制 $m^2/g_{\rm YM}^2 N_c \gg 1$。

Result: 数值证据显示无一算符占主导，即所有算符对哈密顿量的忠实表示（faithful representation）都是必要的，从而保证某些多粒子态质量的精确简并（exact degeneracies）；所给相互作用完整列表与Kutasov-Schwimmer普适性（universality）预期的迹数块结构一致，并可用于有针对性地形变理论以使部分质量成为 $N_c$ 无关。

Conclusion: 尽管可通过定向形变使某些质量变为 $N_c$ 不变，理论中少量的 $N_c$ 依赖仍不可避免；在大组分质量极限下，随组分质量增大，本征态在部分子数（parton number）上趋于更纯，符合预期。

Abstract: Recently it was shown that there are only a few operators that contribute to the (very weak) $N_c$ dependence of the spectrum of two-dimensional adjoint QCD. We provide numerical evidence that none of these operators is dominant. In other words, all operators are necessary to guarantee the faithful representation of the Hamiltonian, resulting in, for instance, exact degeneracies of certain multi-particle state masses.
  We provide a complete list of interactions between single- and multi-trace states, which differ by trace-number block, as expected from the Kutasov-Schwimmer universality. The completed list allows for a targeted deformation of the theory to render some masses $N_c$-invariant. Nonetheless, we argue that a small amount of $N_c$ dependence of the theory is unavoidable.
  We also investigate the large constituent mass regime $m^2/g^2_{\rm YM}N_c\gg 1$ of the theory at large $N_c$ in the eLCQ approach. We find, among other things, that as the constituent mass grows, eigenstates get purer in parton number, in line with expectations.

</details>


### [192] [Non-Riemannian Holography: Long Strings and Soft Hair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25669)
*Shaun D. Hampton,Hyun-Cheol Kim,Jae-Hyuk Oh,Jeong-Hyuck Park*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文为 Gomis–Ooguri 边界数据的两种体（bulk）完备化构造了 O(D,D)-协变的全息字典（holographic dictionary）：纯 NS–NS 通量支撑的 Riemannian AdS₃ 与处处非黎曼的弦牛顿–嘉当几何。两者共享对角广义度规单点函数，但仅非黎曼分支携带独立的可归一化软毛，且超对称非黎曼鞍点可实现"超超对称"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gomis–Ooguri 边界数据存在多种体完备化方案，需澄清其在 O(D,D)-协变框架下的全息描述及可观测量的异同。

Method: 构造 O(D,D)-协变的全息字典，并借助 O(D,D)-协变的 Codazzi 恒等式（Codazzi identity）导出边界守恒律（boundary conservation law）。

Result: 两分支具有相同的对角广义度规（generalized metric）单点函数；仅非黎曼分支存在独立可归一化的软毛（soft hair），其与长弦（long strings）耦合，但不贡献表面荷（surface charges）与重整化在壳作用量（renormalized on-shell action）。

Conclusion: 非超对称层面两体完备化在广义度规层面不可区分，但软毛自由度与超对称结构可将其区分；超对称非黎曼鞍点（saddles）允许任意手征 Killing 旋量剖面，对应"超超对称（supersupersymmetry）"。

Abstract: We propose an $\mathbf{O}(D,D)$-covariant holographic dictionary for two bulk completions of the Gomis--Ooguri boundary data: Riemannian $AdS_3$ supported by pure NS--NS flux and an everywhere non-Riemannian string Newton--Cartan geometry. An $\mathbf{O}(D,D)$-covariant Codazzi identity yields the boundary conservation law. Both branches share diagonal generalized-metric one-point functions, but only the non-Riemannian branch carries independent normalizable soft hair that couples to long strings without contributing to surface charges or the renormalized on-shell action. Supersymmetric non-Riemannian saddles admit arbitrary chiral Killing-spinor profiles, realizing "supersupersymmetry."

</details>


### [193] [Subsystem quantum Rényi-Bhattacharyya distance in 2D CFTs and its holographic applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25685)
*Yihao Wang,Jiaju Zhang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文提出利用扭曲算子（twist operators）算子乘积展开（operator product expansion, OPE）系统计算二维共形场论（CFT）中的子系统保真度与Rényi保真度，并用于子系统量子Bhattacharyya距离（BD）与Rényi-Bhattacharyya距离（RBD）的研究。结果表明，在全息CFT中两态的可区分性由经典引力（order c）或微扰量子引力（order 1/c）效应决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态区分度量（如Bhattacharyya距离）在共形场论与全息框架下缺乏系统、可推广的计算方法，尤其缺乏对子系统版本及与经典/量子引力对应关系的理解。研究旨在建立此类系统性计算工具，并厘清全息CFT中不同态之间可区分性的引力含义。

Method: 基于扭曲算子的算子乘积展开（OPE）发展子系统保真度与Rényi保真度的系统计算方法，并推广至BD与RBD；先在自由无质量费米子与玻色子理论中与精确结果对照验证，再应用于全息CFT，计算重初态之间、热态之间以及重初态与热态之间的BD与RBD。

Result: 当两态在经典（order-c）数据上不同时，距离为order c，态在经典引力层面即可区分；当主导数据相同时，各阶短区间展开中距离均为order 1/c，可区分性成为微扰量子引力效应。对重初态与其热态对应态，BD的主导贡献出现在order ℓ^8且为order c^0，而RBD一般为order c，与“二者共享order-c纠缠熵但order-c Rényi熵不同”的既有结论一致。

Conclusion: 扭曲算子OPE方法为二维CFT中子系统保真度类度量提供了系统计算框架，并在全息CFT中揭示态可区分性与经典/微扰量子引力效应的对应关系；重初态与热态在BD与RBD上的阶数差异进一步支持二者纠缠结构的精细差别。

Abstract: We develop a systematic method to compute the subsystem fidelity and Rényi fidelity in two-dimensional conformal field theories (CFTs) from the operator product expansion of twist operators, and apply it to the subsystem quantum Bhattacharyya distance (BD) and Rényi-Bhattacharyya distance (RBD). We validate the method against exact results in the free massless fermion and boson theories, and then use it to compute the BD and RBD in holographic CFTs between two heavy primary states, between two thermal states, and between a heavy primary state and a thermal state. When the two states differ in their classical, order-$c$ data, the distances are of order $c$, and the states are distinguishable already at the level of classical gravity; when the leading data coincide, the distances are of order $1/c$ at every order of the short interval expansion, so that the distinguishability becomes a perturbative quantum gravity effect. For a heavy primary state and its thermal counterpart, the leading contribution to the BD appears at order $\ell^8$ and is of order $c^0$, whereas the RBD is generically of order $c$, in line with the earlier finding that a heavy primary state and a thermal state share the same order-$c$ entanglement entropy but have different order-$c$ Rényi entropies in the short interval expansion.

</details>


### [194] [The long-range Ising CFT: perturbative and nonperturbative approaches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25940)
*Zechuan Zheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文从多条独立方向系统研究长程伊辛模型（long-range Ising model）在1、2、3维中由一参数族非局域共形场论（nonlocal conformal field theory）描述的临界点，并在此过程中拓展了微扰展开、修正了哈密顿量截断（Hamiltonian truncation）并交叉验证了结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长程伊辛模型是幂律相互作用（power-law interactions）临界性的原型，其各维度的临界点构成一族在两端弱耦合、中间不弱耦合的非局域共形场论，单靠一种方法难以可靠刻画整族理论。

Method: 研究采用多种互补手段：将围绕广义自由场（generalized free field）的展开从三圈推广至八圈，并从短程端展开；修正一处截断假象后证明哈密顿量截断适用于该非局域理论；同时与数值重整化群（numerical renormalization group）和蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）进行比较。

Result: 在可比较的范围内，重求和展开（resummed expansions）、哈密顿量截断、数值重整化群与蒙特卡洛模拟对领头Z2偶标量（leading Z2-even scalar）维度的结果在亚百分比（sub-percent）水平上一致，符合普适性（universality）的要求。

Conclusion: 多种独立方法对长程伊辛临界标量维度的亚百分比一致性表明，所发展的展开与哈密顿量截断技术能可靠地处理这类非局域共形场论，为整族理论提供了统一的定量刻画。

Abstract: The long-range Ising model is the prototype of criticality with power-law interactions. In each dimension its interacting critical points form a one-parameter family of nonlocal conformal field theories, weakly coupled at both ends but not in between. We study this family in one, two and three dimensions from several independent directions. We extend the expansion about the generalized free field from three to eight loops and expand about the short-range end. We show that Hamiltonian truncation applies to this nonlocal theory once a truncation artifact is corrected. Where they can be compared, the resummed expansions, Hamiltonian truncation, the numerical renormalization group and Monte Carlo simulations agree on the dimension of the leading $\mathbb{Z}_2$-even scalar at the sub-percent level, as universality requires.

</details>


### [195] [Higher-Dimensional Test of the Holographic Dual of Entanglement Cohomology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25969)
*R. ~C. ~Rashkov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作将纠缠上同调（entanglement cohomology）的全息检验从二维推广到高维共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT），在 d=3 与 d=4 情形下于微扰一阶验证了边界与体结果的一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有伴随论文仅在 d=2 情形下定量检验了纠缠上同调的全息对偶，本文旨在将这一验证推广至更高维 CFT，以检验其普适性。

Method: 对具有球形纠缠区域（spherical entangling region）的 d 维 CFT，利用已知的真空模哈密顿量（vacuum modular Hamiltonian）与共形 Ward 恒等式（conformal Ward identities）计算边界相对熵（relative entropy）；体侧将微扰对偶为 AdS$_{d+1}$ 中的标量场，并借助 JLMS 关系计算体正则能量（bulk canonical energy）。

Result: 在 d=3 和 d=4 情形下显式计算表明，边界与体结果在微扰一阶一致，系数同为 κ_d Δ²/C_T，其中 κ_3 = π²/12、κ_4 = 3π³/16。

Conclusion: 结果将纠缠上同调全息对偶的定量验证拓展到 d=2 之外，并确认模哈密顿量微扰（modular Hamiltonian perturbation）正是第一纠缠上同调类（first entanglement cohomology class）的正确全息像；文中还讨论了共形反常（conformal anomaly）、纠缠楔形状（entanglement wedge）及 TsT 形变背景的前景。

Abstract: We extend the holographic test of entanglement cohomology from $d=2$ to higher-dimensional conformal field theories. For a $d$-dimensional CFT with a spherical entangling region $A$, the vacuum modular Hamiltonian is known explicitly, and the boundary relative entropy of a primary-operator perturbation can be computed via conformal Ward identities. On the bulk side, the perturbation is dual to a scalar field in AdS$_{d+1}$, and the bulk canonical energy is computed using the JLMS relation. We carry out the computation explicitly for $d=3$ and $d=4$ and find that the boundary and bulk results agree at first order in the perturbation, with identical coefficients $κ_d Δ^2 / C_T$, where $κ_3 = π^2/12$ and $κ_4 = 3π^3/16$. The result extends the quantitative verification of the holographic dual of entanglement cohomology beyond the $d=2$ case treated in the companion paper and confirms that the modular Hamiltonian perturbation is the correct holographic image of the first entanglement cohomology class. We discuss the role of the conformal anomaly, the shape of the entanglement wedge, and an outlook on TsT-deformed backgrounds. \keywords{Entanglement entropy \and Cyclic cohomology \and von Neumann
  algebras \and Holography \and Modular Hamiltonian}

</details>


### [196] [One-loop unifying relations between Yang-Mills theory and the nonlinear sigma model from Berends-Giele currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25972)
*Zhenhua Guo,Xin Yan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究杨-米尔斯理论与非线性sigma模型之间的统一关系，将树级振幅关系推广至离壳多粒子流，并通过缝合两个离壳腿得到单圈被积函数关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作用微分算子表达杨-米尔斯理论与非线性sigma模型之间的树级振幅关系，但该关系局限于在壳振幅，难以直接推广到圈图层面，因此需要寻找可延伸至圈图的表述方式。

Method: 将基于微分算子的树级振幅关系（tree-level amplitude relations）推广到离壳多粒子流（off-shell multiparticle currents），再通过缝合两个离壳腿（sewing two off-shell legs）构造单圈被积函数关系。

Result: 成功获得杨-米尔斯理论与非线性sigma模型之间的单圈被积函数关系（one-loop integrand relations），其来源是对离壳流的缝合操作。

Conclusion: 离壳方法（off-shell approach）为振幅关系向圈图推广提供了可行路径，并可能进一步适用于更高圈被积函数（higher-loop integrands）。

Abstract: We study unifying relations between Yang-Mills theory and the nonlinear sigma model. We extend tree-level amplitude relations expressed through differential operators to off-shell multiparticle currents and obtain one-loop integrand relations by sewing two off-shell legs. This off-shell approach may also apply to higher-loop integrands.

</details>


### [197] [Hubble Susceptibility: Beyond Effective-Mass Spectroscopy in the Cosmological Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26014)
*Shuntaro Aoki,Masahide Yamaguchi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出宇宙对撞机质量（cosmological-collider masses）的哈勃磁化率（Hubble susceptibility）概念，利用压缩极限振荡（squeezed-limit oscillation）的尺度依赖性，区分暴胀期间哈勃诱导的质量修正（Hubble-induced mass corrections）与内禀质量贡献（intrinsic mass contribution）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重场（heavy fields）在暴胀（inflation）期间通常获得哈勃诱导的质量修正，这会对宇宙对撞机质量的测量产生污染，因此需要一种能将其与内禀质量贡献区分开来的可观测手段。

Method: 引入宇宙对撞机质量的哈勃磁化率，并将其编码于压缩极限振荡的尺度依赖性中；分析局部振荡频率（local oscillation frequency）及其漂移（drift）在领头慢滚（leading slow-roll）与绝热阶（adiabatic order）下的表现。

Result: 局部振荡频率及其漂移能够在领头慢滚与绝热阶下将哈勃诱导的质量修正与内禀质量贡献区分开来。

Conclusion: 哈勃磁化率及压缩极限振荡的尺度依赖构成一种可行探针，用于在宇宙对撞机质量中分离哈勃诱导修正与内禀质量贡献。

Abstract: In this note, we introduce the Hubble susceptibility of cosmological-collider masses, encoded in the scale dependence of the squeezed-limit oscillation. Heavy fields generically receive Hubble-induced mass corrections during inflation, and we show that the local oscillation frequency and its drift can disentangle such corrections from an intrinsic mass contribution at leading slow-roll and adiabatic order.

</details>


### [198] [Millicharged particles and the degree-3 Prouhet-Tarry-Escott problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26092)
*Ben Gripaios*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文用几何方法求解数论问题：对任意 m，给出方程 Σx_i^k = Σy_i^k（k∈{1,2,3}）的全部整数解，m≥5 时结果属新发现；物理上对应带微小电荷（millicharged）费米子的反常消除条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 所需解决的算术问题在 m≥5 时此前似无已知结果，而其整数解恰是含 m 个可自然变轻、携带小电荷费米子模型的规范反常消除（anomaly cancellation）条件，因此具有物理动机。

Method: 借助几何（geometry）方法处理该算术问题；物理图景上，小电荷来自标准模型 U(1) 规范对称性与未破缺的非手征（non-chiral，即矢量型 vector-like）U(1) 的动能混合（kinetic mixing），质量则由自发破缺的手征（chiral）U(1) 压低。

Result: 对任意 m 求得了 k∈{1,2,3} 时方程的全部整数解，其中 m≥5 的情形结果具有新颖性。

Conclusion: 几何方法可有效给出该族幂和等式的完整整数解，并将结果直接落实为毫电荷（millicharged）粒子模型的反常消除条件，连接数论与粒子物理模型构造。

Abstract: We use geometry to solve a problem in arithmetic relevant for physics. Namely, for any $m$ we find all integer solutions to the equations $\sum_{i=1}^m x_i^k = \sum_{i=1}^m y_i^k$, where $k \in \{1,2,3\}$; this seems to be new for $m \geq 5$.
  In physics, these are the anomaly cancellation conditions for models with $m$ fermions which can be naturally light and carry small electric charges (a.k.a. millicharged or minicharged particles). The small charges arise by kinetic mixing of the Standard Model $U(1)$ gauge symmetry with an unbroken non-chiral (a.k.a. vector-like) $U(1)$ and the masses are suppressed by a spontaneously-broken chiral $U(1)$.

</details>


### [199] [Quantum Noise from Massive Lorentz--Violating Gravitons: Interferometer Signatures in Very Special Linear Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26177)
*Jorge Alfaro,Alex Soto,Alessandro Santoni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在非常特殊线性引力（Very Special Linear Gravity）框架下分析经典引力探测器中引力子（graviton）的可能信号，采用 Vernon 方法得到耗散项及引力子质量平方阶的额外项，并发现压缩态（squeezed state）下出现可区分压缩相位符号的特征贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该框架引入了具有两种极化（two polarizations）的规范不变有质量引力子（gauge invariant massive graviton），为在经典引力探测器中寻找引力子信号提供了理论出发点。

Method: 采用 Vernon 方法（Vernon approach）在非常特殊线性引力框架下计算经典引力探测器的响应，并考虑压缩态情形。

Result: 所得耗散项（dissipative terms）与其他工作一致，并出现引力子质量平方阶（order of the graviton mass squared）的额外项；在压缩态下存在可区分压缩相位（squeezing phase）符号的贡献。

Conclusion: 非常特殊线性引力框架预言了探测器中可辨识的引力子特征信号，其中压缩态下相位符号的区分性贡献可作为潜在观测判据。

Abstract: In this work we analyse under the Very Special Linear Gravity framework, which introduces a gauge invariant massive graviton with two polarizations, possible signatures of gravitons in a classical gravitational detector. Following the Vernon approach we found dissipative terms consistent with other works and extra terms of order of the graviton mass squared. The model also presents a characteristic feature when a squeezed state is considered. In such case, a contribution that distinguishes the sign of the squeezing phase is present.

</details>


### [200] [Quantum Relaxation Dynamics of Vacuum Energy from Pre-Geometric Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26197)
*Andrea Addazi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在MacDowell–Mansouri前几何引力框架下，通过Higgs场驱动的自发对称破缺SO(1,4)→SO(1,3)，真空中能量密度被量子化为离散能级，并经由量子隧穿沿无限极小值景观单调衰减至渐近吸引子Λ∞=0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨真空能密度（vacuum energy density）与宇宙学常数（Cosmological Constant）为何取极小值这一长期问题，寻求一种由量子弛豫（quantum relaxation）而非精细调节来解释其起源的机制。

Method: 采用MacDowell–Mansouri前几何引力（pre-geometric gravity）框架，引入周期Higgs势（periodic Higgs potential）及其间势垒，使SSB后出现可数无限、对应不同拓扑扇区（topological sectors）的真空态；据此推导连接普朗克质量、宇宙学常数与Gauss–Bonnet耦合、θ项等拓扑参数的dual与see-saw关系，并计算穿越极小值景观的量子隧穿（quantum tunneling）跃迁概率。

Result: SSB后真空能呈离散量子化能级；从对应大真空能的⟨Φ⟩=v₁出发，系统在无限极小值景观中隧穿，且跃迁概率在有序序列Λ₁>⋯>Λ_k>⋯上被最大化，宇宙学常数沿该序列单调递减并趋于渐近吸引子Λ∞=0。

Conclusion: 该机制表明宇宙学常数的微小值可由量子隧穿驱动的有序弛豫自然获得，而非依赖初始条件微调；其可检验含义在于真空能量子化及基本尺度与拓扑参数间的dual/see-saw关系。

Abstract: We investigate a quantum relaxation mechanism for the vacuum energy density arising from the spontaneous symmetry breaking (SSB) $SO(1,4)\to SO(1,3)$, driven by a Higgs field $Φ$ in a MacDowell--Mansouri pre-geometric gravity framework. The theory predicts a countably infinite set of vacua, corresponding to distinct topological sectors, for a periodic Higgs potential with intervening barriers. This structure gives rise to a series of dualities and see-saw relations connecting fundamental scales---the Planck mass and the Cosmological Constant---to topological parameters such as the Gauss-Bonnet coupling and the $θ$-term, all of which emerge only after SSB. A striking consequence is the quantization of the vacuum energy into discrete levels. Starting from a vacuum expectation value $\langle Φ\rangle = v_1$ associated with a large vacuum energy, the system evolves via quantum tunneling through the infinite landscape of minima. Crucially, we demonstrate that the transition probability is maximized for the ordered sequence $ Λ_1 > \cdots > Λ_k > \cdots$, where the Cosmological Constant decreases monotonically toward the asymptotic attractor $Λ_\infty = 0$.

</details>


### [201] [Boundary preparation of strong Mpemba states in holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26218)
*Yu Shi,Xinwu Liu,Liping Li,Chikun Ding,Yuebing Zhou,Changqing Liu,Sheng Long*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在固定AdS黑洞膜上的探针Abelian-Higgs模型中，相同终态电荷密度（charge density）的有限边界驱动可通过电荷密度历史（density history）选择不同弛豫通道；纯粹由低阶方程得到的立方慢振幅（cubic slow amplitude）能预测补偿脉冲，使非线性演化中源振幅从O(ε³)降至O(ε⁵)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究以相同电荷密度为终点的有限边界驱动是否以及如何选择不同弛豫通道（relaxation channel），并探讨允许稳定正常端点的探针Abelian-Higgs（Abelian-Higgs）设定中密度历史与径向麦克斯韦反馈（radial Maxwell feedback）对慢模态（slow mode）的贡献。

Method: 在固定AdS黑洞膜（AdS black brane）上构建探针Abelian-Higgs模型，分析制备过程中的电荷交换（charge exchange）对标量传播与源耦合的修正；结合低阶方程计算立方慢振幅，并用全非线性演化（full nonlinear evolution）及单脉冲、双脉冲与三脉冲调谐协议进行验证。

Result: 在ρ_f=3的测试协议中，密度历史项与净径向反馈（net radial feedback）相长干涉，其模为总立方系数的85–89%；低阶方程预测的补偿脉冲（compensating pulse）经非线性演化确认，源振幅ε=0.05时抑制约600倍；完全调谐的双、三脉冲制备在数值分辨率内分别抑制一个和两个慢模态；电荷中性对照（charge-neutral control）在计入全部后续控制时长时仍保留总时间优势。

Conclusion: 即便线性慢模取消后，密度历史与径向麦克斯韦反馈仍留下立方慢振幅；补偿可延长快通道弛豫窗口而不改变最终准正规模谱（quasinormal-mode spectrum）；局部分析确立强Mpemba态（strong Mpemba state）的有限脉冲可达性，即所选渐近慢模系数可为零。

Abstract: Finite boundary drives ending at the same charge density can select different relaxation channels. We demonstrate this in a probe Abelian--Higgs model on a fixed AdS black brane with a stable normal endpoint. Charge exchange during preparation modifies scalar propagation and source coupling. Density history and radial Maxwell feedback during and after the drive leave a cubic slow amplitude even after linear slow-mode cancellation. For the tested protocols at $ρ_f=3$, the density-history term interferes constructively with the net radial feedback; its modulus is 85--89\% of that of the total cubic coefficient. Calculated from the low-order equations alone, this total coefficient predicts the compensating pulse. Full nonlinear evolution confirms the reduction from $O(ε^3)$ to $O(ε^5)$ in the source amplitude $ε$, with approximately 600-fold suppression for the representative protocol at $ε=0.05$. Compensation extends the window of fast-channel relaxation without changing the final quasinormal-mode spectrum; early distance crossing already arises from linear interference. Fully tuned two- and three-pulse preparations suppress one and two slow modes, respectively, within numerical resolution. Local analysis establishes finite-pulse reachability of strong Mpemba states with vanishing selected asymptotic slow-mode coefficients. Charge-neutral controls retain a total-time advantage from a common input when the full subsequent control duration is included.

</details>


### [202] [Dispersion of three-dimensional oscillons: a standing-wave perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26281)
*I. V. Barashenkov,R. De Sousa,N. V. Alexeeva,A. Dika,E. V. Zemlyanaya*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文以有限半径球内的球对称驻波（含弱辐射交换）作为φ⁴振荡子（oscillon）绝热演化中间阶段的模型，利用多尺度变分框架（multiscale variational framework）推导能量-频率关系，并与二维数值解对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: φ⁴振荡子（oscillon）的绝热演化存在中间阶段缺乏解析描述的问题，需要一个能刻画核心与边界弱辐射交换的简化模型来理解其驻波结构与能量-频率关系。

Method: 构建有限半径球内球对称驻波加弱辐射交换的模型，采用多尺度变分框架（multiscale variational framework）给出近似描述与色散曲线（dispersion curve）；同时在一维空间加时间的二维区域上数值求解φ⁴方程边值问题（boundary value problem）作为对照。

Result: 在连续谱端点（endpoint of the continuous spectrum）附近，变分解与数值解吻合极好；当频率ω降至0.85 ω₀以下时，吻合度变差。

Conclusion: 变分近似在连续谱端点附近有效，低频区偏差归因于变分拟设（ansatz）中忽略的辐射翼（radiation wing）的增长；该模型可作为振荡子绝热演化中间阶段的定性描述。

Abstract: A spherically symmetric standing wave confined within a finite-radius ball, with weak radiative exchange between its core and boundary, provides a model for an intermediate stage in the adiabatic evolution of the $φ^4$ oscillon. We employ a multiscale variational framework to derive an approximate description of these standing waves and their energy-frequency relation. The resulting dispersion curve is compared with that obtained from numerical solutions of the boundary value problem for the $φ^4$ equation on a two dimensional (time and radius) domain. The agreement between the numerical and variational results is excellent near the endpoint of the continuous spectrum but deteriorates as $ω$ is reduced below $0.85 ω_0$. We attribute this discrepancy to the growth of the radiation wing, which is neglected in the variational ansatz.

</details>


### [203] [U(1) Current Diffusion in Gauss-Bonnet Holography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26515)
*A. Naveena Kumara,Shreyas Punacha,Kartheek Hegde*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 在五维Gauss-Bonnet-AdS黑膜背景中，借助探针Maxwell场研究全息U(1)电荷输运，解析与数值给出了流体力学区及一般频率、动量下的扩散函数与电、磁导率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究高阶曲率修正（Gauss-Bonnet耦合）对全息U(1)电荷输运性质的系统影响，特别是扩散常数、频率依赖及磁导率等输运系数的修正。

Method: 采用五维Gauss-Bonnet-AdS黑膜背景中的探针Maxwell场，在全程线性化流体/引力（fluid/gravity）框架下导出离壳本构关系（off-shell constitutive relation），并在流体力学区解析求解、在一般频率与动量下数值求解扩散函数与电、磁导率。

Result: 在Gauss-Bonnet耦合的一阶近似下，所采用归一化下的领头扩散常数值不变，而其频率依赖与电导率获得非平庸修正；形变还产生非零的领头磁导率。超出流体力学区后，输运剖面被改变，但扩散函数的阻尼行为得以保留。

Conclusion: 对应的记忆核（memory kernel）在负时间处为零，支持推迟型本构响应（retarded constitutive response），表明该框架下输运描述具有因果性一致的结构。

Abstract: We study holographic $U(1)$ charge transport using a probe Maxwell field in a five-dimensional Gauss-Bonnet-AdS black-brane background. Within the all-order linearised fluid/gravity framework, we derive the off-shell constitutive relation and determine the diffusion function and electric and magnetic conductivities analytically in the hydrodynamic regime and numerically at generic frequency and momentum. To first order in the Gauss-Bonnet coupling, the leading diffusion constant remains unchanged in our normalization, while its frequency dependence and the electric conductivity receive nontrivial corrections. The deformation also generates a nonvanishing leading magnetic conductivity. Beyond the hydrodynamic regime, it modifies the transport profiles while preserving the damped behaviour of the diffusion function. The corresponding memory kernel vanishes at negative times, supporting a retarded constitutive response.

</details>


### [204] [The $A_3$ Narain family over $\mathbb{Z}_4$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26610)
*Rajae Sammani,Abdellah Sebbar*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文将分裂型 $A_3$ 根-权重粘合构造实现为普通构造A（Construction A），保持洛伦兹配对与正能量形式，并据此在中心荷六与三的情形下精确区分出 T-对偶类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在把分裂 $A_3$ 根-权重粘合（root-weight gluing）纳入普通构造A框架，以便在保持洛伦兹配对（Lorentzian pairing）与正能量形式的前提下研究自对偶码与相应背景的 T-对偶（T-duality）分类。

Method: 以码 $\{000,220,202,022\}\subset\mathbb{Z}_4^3$ 实现上述粘合；对两对双曲型 $A_3$ 块，将二十二个偶自对偶粘合码归为七个块单项类（block-monomial classes），并用显式度规与 $B$-场代表及 theta 级数（theta series）加以区分；同时以粘合码的交集刻画有限平移轨形（shift orbifold）。

Result: 在中心荷六时该家族恰有七个 T-对偶类，单对家族在中心荷三时有两个类；对任意 $d$，任一侧的手征根系统（chiral root system）为 $A_1^{3m}$，其中 $m$ 统计粘合码中 $(2e_i,2e_i)$ 词的数目；恰有一个双对类不存在零 $B$-场 T-对偶标架。

Conclusion: 结果表明分裂 $A_3$ 粘合码可完全置于构造A语言下，且块的单项类与 T-对偶类在给定中心荷下精确对应；粘合码的交集给出背景间有限平移轨形，其中目标码规定扭曲扇区投影（twisted-sector projections）。

Abstract: The code $\{000,220,202,022\}\subset\mathbb{Z}_4^3$ realizes the split $A_3$ root-weight gluing construction as ordinary Construction A, preserving the Lorentzian pairing and the positive energy form. For two hyperbolic pairs of $A_3$ blocks, the twenty-two even self-dual gluing codes form seven block-monomial classes. Explicit metric and $B$-field representatives and theta series distinguish exactly seven T-duality classes in this family at central charge six; the one-pair family has two classes at central charge three. For arbitrary $d$, the chiral root system on either side is $A_1^{3m}$, where $m$ counts the words $(2e_i,2e_i)$ in the gluing code. Exactly one two-pair class has no zero-$B$ T-duality frame. Intersections of gluing codes determine finite shift orbifolds between the backgrounds, with the target code specifying the twisted-sector projections.

</details>


### [205] [On Integrable Deformations of CFTs and Integrable Two-Dimensional Sigma Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26672)
*Mikhail Alfimov*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究对应 η-形变（η-deformed）O(N) 与 OSp(N|2m) sigma 模型的筛选荷（screening charges）系统，基于 O(N) 情形提出关于 OSp(N|2m) 对应共形场论筛选荷系统构造的假设，并在 OSp(5|2)、OSp(7|2)、OSp(7|4) 三个具体情形中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: η-形变 sigma 模型可视为某些共形场论（conformal field theory）的可积形变（integrable deformation），而刻画这些共形场论需要明确其筛选荷系统；O(N) 情形已有 A.V. Litvinov 等人的猜想，OSp(N|2m) 情形尚待补充。

Method: 借鉴 O(N) sigma 模型对应 W(D(n+1|n)) 与 W(D(n|n)) 代数的既有猜想，作者提出刻画 η-形变 OSp(N|2m) sigma 模型所对应共形场论筛选荷系统的假设，限定于 N=2n+1 奇数、渐近自由（asymptotically free）条件 N-2m-2>0 且 n≥2、m≥1 的情形。

Result: 该假设在 OSp(5|2)、OSp(7|2)、OSp(7|4) 三个具体案例中得到显式验证，这些案例此前由 M.N. Alfimov 等人独立研究过。

Conclusion: 所提出的筛选荷系统构造假设与已有具体计算结果一致，为 η-形变 OSp(N|2m) sigma 模型对应的共形场论描述提供了候选方案，但仍需更一般的验证。

Abstract: In this paper, we analyze the systems of screening charges corresponding to $η$-deformed $O(N)$ and $OSp(N|2m)$ sigma models. These sigma models can be represented as integrable deformations of certain conformal field theories. Based on the case of $O(N)$ sigma models, which were conjectured in previous works by A.V. Litvinov and co-authors to be an integrable deformation of the conformal field theory corresponding to the algebras $W(D(n+1|n))$ and $W(D(n|n))$ for the cases $N=2n+2$ and $N=2n+1$ at $n \geq 1$, respectively, we formulate a hypothesis on how to write down the system of screening charges for the conformal field theory whose integrable deformation defines the $η$-deformed $OSp(N|2m)$ sigma model for the case of odd $N=2n+1$, asymptotically free models with $N-2m-2>0$ and $n \geq 2$, $m \geq 1$. This hypothesis is explicitly verified for the cases of $OSp(5|2)$, $OSp(7|2)$, and $OSp(7|4)$, which were separately investigated in the work of M.N. Alfimov and co-authors.

</details>


### [206] [An explicit product for the on-shell higher-spin algebra in $AdS_d$-space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26704)
*M. A. Povarnin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 $d>3$ 时，直接在 AdS-无迹表示（AdS-traceless representatives）上构造了玻色型在壳高自旋代数（bosonic on-shell higher-spin algebra）的乘积，给出了封闭形式的无迹投影算子与全部线性关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高自旋代数（higher-spin algebra）的乘积通常在含迹的表示上定义，需要处理迹部分带来的冗余；在 AdS-无迹表示上直接构造乘积可简化结构并规避迹模（trace modes）的干扰。

Method: 构造封闭形式（closed form）的无迹投影算子（traceless projector），确定所有产生迹的算子（trace-producing operators）之间的线性关系，并证明每个模 $\mathfrak{sp}(2)$ 理想（ideal）的等价类存在唯一无迹代表。

Result: 所得乘积为显式的微分算子（differential operator），并在 $\mathfrak{so}(d-1,2)$ 生成元上进行了检验；玩具模型（toy model）表明该分解（factorisation）可从双线性乘积提升到最小 $A_\infty$-代数（minimal $A_\infty$-algebras）中的三线性乘积。

Conclusion: 结果给出了 AdS-无迹表示上高自旋代数乘积的显式表达，并借助 $\mathfrak{sp}(2)$ 理想的唯一代表性质与玩具模型，提示其可推广至 $A_\infty$ 结构的高阶乘积。

Abstract: For $d>3$ we construct the product of the bosonic on-shell higher-spin algebra directly on the AdS-traceless representatives. We find the traceless projector in closed form, determine all linear relations among the trace-producing operators, and prove that every class modulo the $\mathfrak{sp}(2)$ ideal has a unique traceless representative. The resulting product is an explicit differential operator, which we test on the $\mathfrak{so}(d-1,2)$ generators. A toy model shows how the factorisation lifts from bilinear products to trilinear ones in minimal $A_\infty$-algebras.

</details>


### [207] [Large-Level Limit of Doubled Chern-Simons Theory Part I: Regular Sector, Boundaries and Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26735)
*Shengyi Guan,Qiang Jia,Jiahua Tian,Yi-Nan Wang*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文证明：在固定差、随能级 \(k\) 线性增长的最高权（highest weights）条件下，双 Chern–Simons 理论（doubled Chern–Simons theory）\(G_k\times G_{-k}\) 的任意固定模变换（modular transformation）在强-* 拓扑下收敛到三维 \(BF\) 理论的对应结果，从而连接有限与连续对称性的对称拓扑场论（symmetry TFT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于建立有限对称性与连续对称性对应的对称拓扑场论之间的桥梁，即以大能级极限下的双 Chern–Simons 理论刻画 \(BF\) 理论，并厘清能级趋于无穷时环面态（torus states）与模变换的收敛行为。

Method: 将能级 \(k\) 的环面态表示为归一化的通量–电荷波包（flux–charge wave packets）；取两最高权之差固定、随 \(k\) 线性增长，使磁通量由其平均值确定、电荷等于其差值，从而得到 \(BF\) 线；再证明固定可归一化态在固定模变换下的强-* 收敛性，并分别分析 Dehn twist 与 \(S\) 变换。

Result: Dehn twist 在任意能级下对每条线给出精确的 Aharonov–Bohm 相位；\(S\) 变换收敛为交换通量与电荷的傅里叶变换（Fourier transform），且不逃逸至奇异完整和乐（singular holonomies）或大电荷区域；在大 \(k\) 下趋于奇异通量的态不在覆盖范围内。

Conclusion: 论文推测自然边界二维理论在阿贝尔情形 \(G=U(1)\) 下为退紧致化玻色子（decompactified boson），一般 \(G\) 情形为 \(\dim G\) 个非紧致自由标量；对 \(SU(2)\)，讨论了相邻能级间的有能隙界面（gapped interfaces），其须将部分通量或电荷平移有限量，并猜想 \(k\rightarrow\infty\) 极限下该界面为 \(c=1\) 的 Runkel–Watts 理论。

Abstract: We study three-dimensional $BF$ theory as the large-level limit of the doubled Chern--Simons theory $G_k\times G_{-k}$ for simply connected, compact, simple $G$, a limit that connects the symmetry topological field theories of finite and continuous symmetries. Lines whose two highest weights grow linearly with $k$ at fixed difference become $BF$ lines, with magnetic flux set by their average and electric charge equal to their difference. Representing level-$k$ torus states as normalized flux--charge wave packets, we prove that every fixed modular transformation of any fixed normalizable state with regular flux converges in the strong-$*$ topology to its $BF$ counterpart. The Dehn twist gives each line exactly its Aharonov--Bohm phase at every level, while $S$ converges to a Fourier transform exchanging flux and charge, without escaping to singular holonomies or large charges. States that approach singular fluxes at large $k$ are not covered. The natural boundary 2d theory is speculated to be a decompactified boson in the abelian case $G=U(1)$, and $\dim G$ non-compact free scalars for general $G$. For $SU(2)$, we discuss the gapped interfaces between adjacent levels, which must shift some flux or charge by a finite amount. In the $k\rightarrow\infty$ limit the interface is conjectured to be the $c=1$ Runkel--Watts theory.

</details>


### [208] [Rényi Multi-entropy for Stabilizer States: A Finite Field Gauss Sum Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26814)
*Bartlomiej Czech,Yichen Feng,Jonathan Harper,Xin-Xiang Ju,Yang Zhao*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文建立了有限域高斯和（Gauss sum）框架，用于计算稳定子态（stabilizer states）的 Rényi 多熵（Rényi multi-entropy），并给出树图态与大量环图态的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rényi 多熵可探测超越二分谱（bipartite spectra）的多分结构，但其在一般量子态中的行为及所刻画的纠缠结构仍缺乏理解。

Method: 引入有限域高斯和框架计算稳定子态中的 Rényi 多熵；发现在副本收缩（replica contraction）化为二次高斯和（quadratic Gauss sum）当且仅当该态局部酉等价于稳定子态时成立。

Result: 得到全部树图态（tree graph states）及大量环图态（loop graph states）的 Rényi 多熵解析表达式；稳定子态一般具有非平坦的 Rényi 多熵谱，与其平坦的二分 Rényi 谱形成对比。

Conclusion: Rényi 多熵能揭示稳定子态中二分谱无法显现的多分纠缠结构，高斯和框架为稳定子态中的此类计算提供了系统工具。

Abstract: Rényi multi-entropy probes multi-partite structure beyond bipartite spectra, but its behavior in general quantum states and the entanglement structure it captures remain poorly understood. We develop a finite field Gauss sum framework for calculating it in stabilizer states. Within this framework, the replica contraction reduces to a quadratic Gauss sum if and only if the state is locally unitarily equivalent to a stabilizer state. We obtain analytic expressions for the Rényi multi-entropy of all tree graph states and large families of loop graph states. In contrast to their flat bipartite Rényi spectra, stabilizer states generically have nonflat Rényi multi-entropy spectra.

</details>


### [209] [Mechanical angular momentum loss at $\mathcal{O}(G^4)$ from static and radiative gravitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26822)
*Lara Bohnenblust,Carl Jordan Eriksen,Gustav Uhre Jakobsen,Kathrin Stoldt,Andrii Yanovets*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文利用世界线量子场论（worldline quantum field theory, WQFT）计算了两个引力散射体在 \(\mathcal{O}(G^4)\) 阶的机械角动量损失 \(L^\mu_{\rm loss}\)，阐明了引力记忆效应（gravitational memory effect）的静态贡献来源，并给出与既有后牛顿（post-Newtonian）结果一致的三圈（three-loop）计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确刻画引力散射过程中的角动量损失（含引力记忆效应带来的静态贡献）是检验后牛顿展开与引力波物理的重要环节，而 \(\mathcal{O}(G^4)\) 阶的完整计算此前尚待完成。

Method: 采用 WQFT 框架，将计算分为静态与动力学两部分：线性记忆（linear memory）通过静态关联函数（static correlators）贡献，非线性记忆（nonlinear memory）因对称性在该阶消失；动力学部分的积分生成采用在每个顶点守恒四动量的 WQFT 费曼规则（Feynman rules）重表述，三圈积分约化为主积分（master integrals），并通过微分方程及后牛顿极限下的边界匹配求解。

Result: 与本阶保守效应不同，结果中仅出现多重对数（polylogarithms）；动力学部分的各单独贡献虽发散，但在 \(L^\mu_{\rm loss}\) 中相互抵消。结果的辐射二次项（quadratic-in-radiation part）重现了已有结果，完整结果与现有后牛顿结果一致。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了 \(\mathcal{O}(G^4)\) 阶角动量损失的 WQFT 计算，明确了引力记忆效应对静态贡献的作用机制，并验证了结果与后牛顿展开的一致性。

Abstract: We determine the loss of mechanical angular momentum $L^μ_{\rm loss}$ of two gravitationally scattering bodies at $\mathcal{O}(G^4)$ using worldline quantum field theory (WQFT). The gravitational memory effect, a phenomenon of the early- and late-time behaviour of the gravitational field, leads to static contributions to $L^μ_{\rm loss}$. We illuminate how these arise: the linear memory contributes through so-called static correlators, while the nonlinear memory vanishes at this order due to symmetry considerations. The computation of the remaining, dynamical part resembles that of the $\mathcal{O}(G^4)$ impulse. We generate its integrand using a reformulation of the WQFT Feynman rules that conserves four-momentum at every vertex and plainly exhibits the classical loops. The three-loop integrals are reduced to master integrals, whose differential equations and boundary matching in the post-Newtonian limit we describe in some detail. In contrast to conservative effects at this order, only polylogarithms appear. While individual contributions to the dynamical part are divergent, these cancel in $L^μ_{\rm loss}$. The quadratic-in-radiation part of our result reproduces earlier results, while the complete result agrees with existing post-Newtonian results.

</details>


### [210] [Field theory guides Monte Carlo in the one-dimensional long-range Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26838)
*António Antunes,José Matos*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文以长程伊辛模型（long-range Ising, LRI）为一维可数值研究的临界点范例，重新审视其格点模型的蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo, MC）方法，以较低计算成本获得主要与次主要算符的标度维数及多点关联函数，并论证其可作为检验强耦合共形场论非微扰方法的理想平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维 LRI 模型提供了一族最简单的非精确可解临界点，其对应长程共形场论（conformal field theory, CFT）已有微扰展开与部分精确结果，但缺乏高效的非微扰数值检验手段；本文因此重新考察其格点蒙特卡洛途径，以推动强耦合 CFT 非微扰方法的普适检验。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛方法研究一维长程伊辛格点模型，并指出该模型相较其他常用 MC 研究对象具有诸多有利特性，从而可在很小的计算成本下开展数值模拟。

Result: 以非常适度的计算代价，估计出主要（leading）与次主要（subleading）算符的标度维数（scaling dimensions）、相应的三点函数（three-point functions），甚至部分四点函数（four-point functions）。

Conclusion: 一维 LRI 共形场论是检验强耦合 CFT 非微扰方法与思想的极佳试验场（playground），其数值结果可为更一般的非微扰研究提供基准与参照。

Abstract: The statistical long-range Ising (LRI) model in one dimension provides a family of some of the simplest non-exactly solvable critical points. These critical points are described by long-range conformal field theories (CFTs) that are well understood analytically with perturbative expansions and some exact results. Guided by this, we revisit the Monte Carlo (MC) approach to the corresponding lattice model which has many favorable features compared to other systems studied with MC methods. In particular, with very modest computational cost, we are able to estimate scaling dimensions of leading and subleading operators as well as the associated three-point functions, and even certain four-point functions. These results help highlight the fact that 1d LRI CFTs are a phenomenal playground to more generally test non-perturbative methods and ideas for strongly coupled CFTs.

</details>


### [211] [Complete evaporation of black holes and Page curves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2202.00548)
*Irina Aref'eva,Igor Volovich*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究黑洞完全蒸发（complete evaporation）时的温度发散问题，指出对满足 Q>M-C M^3（小质量 M、C>0）的 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞，可实现温度不发散的完全蒸发，辐射熵遵循 Page 曲线（Page curve）并在蒸发终点趋于零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hawking 提出的问题是：Schwarzschild 黑洞完全蒸发时质量 M→0 会导致温度 T=1/8πM 爆发（blow-up）。本文旨在考察能否在带电黑洞情形下避免该温度奇异性，从而实现无温度发散的完全蒸发。

Method: 以 Reissner-Nordström 黑洞为对象，在态方程（surface of state equations）曲面上刻画使蒸发过程可完整进行的轨迹，并分析小质量区间的质量-电荷约束条件 Q>M-C M^3（C>0）。

Result: 当 Q>M-C M^3 成立时，黑洞可完全蒸发且温度不爆发；沿所描述的态方程曲线运动可实现完全蒸发，辐射熵（radiation entropy）遵循 Page 曲线并在蒸发结束时为零。类似结果亦在 Kerr、Schwarzschild-de-Sitter 及 Reissner-Nordström-(Anti)-de-Sitter 黑洞中被讨论。

Conclusion: 完全蒸发过程中温度发散并非不可避免，引入电荷（及相应约束）可提供无温度爆发的蒸发路径；辐射熵行为与 Page 曲线一致，且该结论可推广至旋转与带宇宙学常数的黑洞背景。

Abstract: The problem of complete evaporation of Schwarzschild black holes raised by Hawking is that one has an explosion of the temperature $T=1/8πM$ for vanishing black hole mass M. We consider the Reissner-Nordstrom black hole and note that if mass $M$ and charge Q<M satisfy the bound Q>M-C M^3, C>0 for small M then the complete evaporation of black holes without blow-up of temperature is possible. We describe curves on the surface of state equations such that the motion along them provides complete evaporation. The radiation entropy follows the Page curve and vanishes at the end of evaporation.
  Similar results for rotating Kerr, Schwarzschild-de-Sitter and Reissner-Nordstrom-(Anti)-de-Sitter black holes are discussed.

</details>


### [212] [Bose Gas Modeling of the Schwarzschild Black Hole Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.19827)
*I. Ya. Aref'eva,I. V. Volovich*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文指出D=5与D=6施瓦西黑洞热力学可由d维玻色气体建模，其单粒子能量为ε(k)=k^α并分别满足α=-d/3与α=-d/4；通过引入截断做最小重整化，并用解析正则化（黎曼zeta函数）验证两种方法结果一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞违反热力学第三定律（third law of thermodynamics），导致黑洞熵（entropy）的微观描述存在困难；此前仅对D=4施瓦西黑洞给出玻色气体解析延拓的描述，本文拟将其推广到更高维情形。

Method: 将D=5、D=6施瓦西黑洞热力学映射为d维（d=1,2,3...）非相对论型玻色气体（Bose gas），单粒子能量取ε(k)=k^α，分别对应α=-d/3与α=-d/4；因玻色气体自由能（free energy）出现发散，引入截断（cut-off）进行最小重整化（minimal renormalization），并采用解析正则化（analytical regularization）交叉验证。

Result: 在所述条件下玻色气体自由能存在发散；最小截断重整化与基于黎曼zeta函数（Riemann zeta-function）的解析正则化给出相同结果，从而为D=5、D=6施瓦西黑洞热力学提供一致的玻色气体建模。

Conclusion: D=5与D=6施瓦西黑洞热力学可通过特定单粒子能量指数的d维玻色气体描述；最小截断重整化与解析正则化等价，支持该微观描述方案的有效性。

Abstract: Black holes violate the third law of thermodynamics, and this gives rise to difficulties with the microscopic description of the entropy of black holes. Recently, it has been shown that the microscopic description of the Schwarzschild black hole thermodynamics in $D = 4$ spacetime dimensions is provided by the analytical continuation of the entropy of Bose gas with non-relativistic one particle energy to d =-4 negative spatial dimension. In this paper, we show that the D=5 and D=6 Schwarzschild black holes thermodynamics can be modeled by the d-dimensional Bose gas, d=1,2,3..., with the one particle energy $\varepsilon(k)=k^α$ under conditions $α=-d/3$ and $α=-d/4$, respectively. In these cases the free energy of the Bose gas has divergences and we introduce a cut-off and perform the minimal renormalizations. We also perform renormalizations using analytical regularization and prove that the minimal cut-off renormalization gives the same answer as the analytical regularization by the Riemann zeta-function.

</details>


### [213] [Black Brane/Bose Gase Duality and Third Law of Thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2411.01778)
*Irina Aref'eva,Daniil Stepanenko,Igor Volovich*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文将黑洞与玻色气体的热力学对偶（duality）推广至黑膜（black brane），建立 Poincare 黑膜与高维非相对论玻色气体、Lifshitz 膜与准粒子玻色气体之间的对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近平直空间黑洞违反热力学第三定律（third law of thermodynamics），其熵无法用常规统计力学（statistical mechanics）一致描述；而部分黑膜解保持第三定律。作者据此希望将既有的黑洞—玻色气体对偶扩展到黑膜体系。

Method: 通过把先前建立的黑洞与玻色气体热力学对偶推广到黑膜，具体给出维度对应：$D$ 维时空的 Poincare 黑膜对应 $2(D-2)$ 维空间中的非相对论玻色气体；含指数 $\alpha$ 的 Lifshitz 膜对应 $D-2$ 维空间中能量为 $k^\alpha$ 的准粒子玻色气体。

Result: 确立了黑膜与玻色气体的对偶映射：Poincare 黑膜映射到 $2(D-2)$ 维非相对论玻色气体；Lifshitz 膜（指数 $\alpha$）映射到 $D-2$ 维、能量色散 $k^\alpha$ 的准粒子玻色气体。

Conclusion: 黑洞—玻色气体热力学对偶可自然推广至黑膜，且与黑膜满足第三定律、熵在零温趋于零的性质相一致，为黑膜熵的统计力学描述提供了新框架。

Abstract: In the thermodynamics of black holes in asymptotically flat space, the third law of thermodynamics is violated, and entropy cannot be consistently modeled through conventional statistical mechanics. Notably, the third law of thermodynamics is violated for the Schwarzschild black hole, and its entropy can only be described using an unconventional model, such as a Bose gas in negative dimensions.
  In contrast, for certain black brane solutions such as Poincare AdS black branes, Lifshitz black branes, and anisotropic Lifshitz-type black branes, the third law is preserved, with entropy vanishing as temperature approaches zero. In this paper, we extend the previously established duality between black hole and Bose gas thermodynamics to black branes. Specifically, the Poincare black brane in $D$ spacetime dimensions corresponds to a non-relativistic Bose gas in $2(D-2)$ spatial dimensions. Furthermore, the duality between Lifshitz branes and Bose gases relates a Lifshitz brane with exponent $α$ in $D$-dimensional spacetime to a Bose gas of quasi-particles with energy $k^α$ in $D-2$ spatial dimensions.

</details>
