### [214] [CME-SpectrumBench: Can LLMs Analyze Condensed Matter Spectral Data?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24304)
*Jin Gene Wong,Anjney Midha,Joseph Mathew Tennyson,Wei-Lin Chiang,Ion Stoica,Zhi-Xun Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 CME-SpectrumBench，一个由专家设计的 190 题基准，用于评估前沿大语言模型（large language models, LLMs）对凝聚态实验（condensed matter experiments, CME）研究级谱学数据的分析能力，发现模型直接查询时表现逊于手写代码，但借助工具调用可接近手写代码水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准多聚焦理论化、符号化的专家级数学与物理推理，缺乏评估前沿研究中数据分析能力的实验性基准，而这是未来领域专用基础模型（domain-specific foundation models）必备的能力。

Method: 构建含 190 道专家设计问题的基准，使用模拟 CME 谱学数据，要求模型在噪声与仪器分辨率受限条件下从二维强度阵列（2D intensity arrays）中识别结构，以数值答案逼近真值（ground truth）的程度评分，并对比直接查询、手写代码、工具调用（Python 解释器）与 ReAct 提示等设置。

Result: 直接查询下模型得分普遍不及手写代码：能发现简单特征但难以追踪模式或计算派生量，且性能随数据分辨率及噪声/卷积水平上升而下降；允许工具调用后，模型表现可与手写代码相当。

Conclusion: 前沿模型可通过代码与数据交互充当研究助手，但难以直接从大规模数据集中处理信息，这是未来基础模型必须克服的关键障碍；代码与数据已公开。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are presently well assessed by expert-level mathematics and physics benchmarks which focus on theoretical, symbolic reasoning. However, we lack experimental benchmarks that evaluate data analysis in frontier research problems, a capability that future domain-specific foundation models must have. To this end, we present CME-SpectrumBench, an expert-designed benchmark consisting of 190 questions that measures how reliably frontier LLMs analyze research-level spectral scientific data in condensed matter experiments (CME). Using simulated CME spectral data, we prompt models to identify structures from 2D intensity arrays in the face of noise and limited instrumental resolution, a test of dense context reasoning. Models are scored by how accurately their numerical answers approach the ground truth. Under direct query, scores generally pale in comparison to handwritten code due to clear failure modes: models can find simple features but struggle to trace patterns or compute derived quantities, and performance typically degrades with increased data resolution and noise/convolution levels. LLM performance becomes comparable to handwritten code by allowing tool use (access to a Python interpreter) with/without ReAct prompting, in effect mapping questions onto agentic code generation problems. Our findings show that frontier models can serve as research assistants by interfacing with data through code, but still face difficulties processing information directly from large datasets - a key barrier future foundation models must overcome. The code and data are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/jingene/CMESpectrumBench.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [215] [Surface corrections and vortex energetics in the Thomas--Fermi limit of a self-bound unitary Fermi superfluid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24565)
*Bao-Duy Le*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文在扩展 Thomas–Fermi（Thomas–Fermi）密度泛函框架下研究 $1/r$ 吸引相互作用的自旋平衡幺正费米超流体（unitary Fermi superfluid），其大粒子数极限为指数 $3/2$ 的 Lane–Emden 多重多方球（polytrope）。作者证明基态能量的首阶梯度修正与密度振幅的强收敛，并通过表面问题与涡旋匹配给出中心涡旋与表面集体激发的能量阈值及数值验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清 Thomas–Fermi 体相描述与梯度物理之间的精度分工，并定量刻画表面与涡旋核心（vortex core）对能量的贡献，从而将涡旋能量学与尚未解决的非线性涡旋进入（nonlinear vortex entry）问题区分开来。

Method: 采用扩展 Thomas–Fermi 密度泛函，将大 $N$ 极限归结为指数 $3/2$ 的 Lane–Emden 多重多方球；证明首阶梯度修正定理，建立重整化后的表面问题（surface problem），并将单量子化涡旋核心与多方球体相匹配。辅以径向定态、球对称 Bogoliubov–Poisson 与轴对称实时（real-time）数值计算。

Result: 获得体相能量首阶梯度修正与密度振幅强收敛的严格结果；由表面问题预测次阶能量修正与积分密度误差。参考梯度系数下涡旋常数为 $c_1=-0.535$，表面 Landau 标度为 $1.417\,\omega_K(\hbar_{\rm eff}^2/2R_{\rm TF})^{1/6}\propto N^{-1/9}$，其随 $N$ 的下降慢于中心涡旋阈值。$N\simeq4.6\times10^5$ 的扇区能量与推断涡旋阈值吻合至 $10^{-4}$，表面模极小值追踪至 $N\simeq4\times10^6$。

Conclusion: 结果区分了 Thomas–Fermi 体相的高精度与主导表面及涡旋核心的梯度物理，并明确将涡旋能量学与非线性涡旋进入这一开放问题分开；表面集体激发在自然频率单位下随 $N$ 更缓慢下降，暗示有限尺寸下表面模可先于中心涡旋成为能量上可达的激发通道。

Abstract: We study a spin-balanced unitary Fermi superfluid bound by an attractive $1/r$ interaction within an extended Thomas--Fermi density functional. Its large-particle-number limit is the Lane--Emden polytrope of index $3/2$. We prove the leading gradient correction to the ground-state energy and strong convergence of the density amplitude. A rescaled surface problem predicts the next energy correction and the leading integrated density error. Matching a vortex core to the polytropic bulk gives the energetic threshold of a singly quantized central vortex, including the constant beyond the leading logarithm. Linearizing the same surface problem predicts a local energetic threshold for collective surface excitations that, in the natural TF frequency units, decreases more slowly with particle number than the central-vortex threshold. Radial stationary, spherical Bogoliubov--Poisson, and axisymmetric real-time calculations test the predicted energy corrections and vortex matching, and resolve finite-size surface-mode minima whose approach to the continuum surface coefficient is followed to $N\simeq4\times10^6$. For the reference gradient coefficient the vortex constant is $c_1=-0.535$, and the surface Landau scale is $1.417\,ω_K(\hbar_{\rm eff}^2/2R_{\rm TF})^{1/6}\propto N^{-1/9}$; sector energies at $N\simeq4.6\times10^5$, computed after the fit, agree with the extrapolated vortex threshold to $10^{-4}$. These results distinguish the accuracy of the Thomas--Fermi bulk from the gradient physics that controls the surface and vortex core, and separate vortex energetics from the open problem of nonlinear vortex entry.

</details>


### [216] [Self-Trapping and Liouvillian Spectral Transitions in a Dissipative Bose-Hubbard Dimer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25222)
*Subhendu Saha,Raka Dasgupta*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文用平均场理论（mean-field theory）与精确对角化（exact diagonalization）研究耗散双势阱（dissipative double wells）中的自囚禁转变（self-trapping transition），发现耗散本身即可诱导类似自囚禁的局域化，且相互作用与耗散驱动的转变分别呈不连续与连续特征，二者共存时产生三种动力学区，并与刘维尔谱（Liouvillian spectrum）的例外点及谱隙峰值相关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自囚禁转变由相互作用驱动（从振荡动力学转向自囚禁），本文旨在探究纯耗散是否能单独诱导类似局域化，以及相互作用与耗散共存时二者如何相互影响。

Method: 采用平均场理论与精确对角化方法，分析开放双势阱中相互作用与耗散共同作用下的动力学，并将转变与刘维尔谱（例外点、刘维尔谱隙峰值）联系起来。

Result: 发现纯耗散可诱导瞬态但持续较长时间的自囚禁式局域化；平均场层面相互作用驱动的转变是不连续的、耗散驱动的是连续的。二者共存时出现三种区：弱相互作用下耗散增强驱动振荡到自囚禁；中等相互作用下呈现“弱耗散自囚禁—中等耗散振荡—强耗散再入自囚禁”；强相互作用下自囚禁持续但被耗散削弱。纯耗散驱动的转变与谱中例外点（exceptional point）及刘维尔谱隙（Liouvillian gap）峰值重合，标志量子芝诺区（quantum-Zeno regime）的开始；有相互作用时，芝诺区起始被推移至比自囚禁转变更大的耗散强度。

Conclusion: 耗散本身可作为一种独立机制诱导自囚禁式局域化，并与相互作用产生非单调的复杂动力学相图；刘维尔谱的例外点与谱隙特征可刻画这些转变，但在相互作用存在时芝诺区起始与自囚禁转变不再重合。

Abstract: We study the self-trapping transition in dissipative double wells using mean-field theory and exact diagonalization. We find that, similar to the conventional interaction-driven transition from oscillatory dynamics to self-trapping, dissipation alone can also induce self-trapping-like localization in one well. The resulting state, although transient and decaying, persists for a substantial time. At the mean-field level, the interaction- and dissipation-driven transitions are discontinuous and continuous, respectively. When interaction and dissipation both are present, their interplay produces three dynamical regimes: (i) at weak interaction, increasing dissipation drives a transition from oscillations to self-trapping; (ii) at moderate interaction, self-trapping at weak dissipation, oscillatory dynamics at intermediate dissipation, and re-entrant self-trapping at strong dissipation; and (iii) at strong interaction, self-trapping persists but is weakened by dissipation. We connect the transition with the Liouvillian spectrum. It is observed that purely dissipation-driven transition coincides with an exceptional point in the spectra, and a peak in the Liouvillian gap, marking the onset of the quantum-Zeno regime. In the presence of interactions, however, the onset of the Zeno regime is shifted to dissipation strengths larger than that required for the self-trapping transition.

</details>


### [217] [Near self-duality and spatial reorganization of anyon vortex lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25412)
*Bao-Duy Le*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 在几乎玻色型 Chern–Simons–Schrödinger 平均场模型中，采用规范不变的正定局域缺陷量化超出磁束缚的协变动能，发现剩余偏离自对偶的贡献集中于携带未屏蔽统计通量的无涡旋外壳，使磁饱和与密度均匀性发生分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触吸引使束缚任意子气体（anyon gas）的自生涡旋晶格趋向自对偶（self-duality），但积分能量无法揭示剩余偏离自对偶的具体空间位置，本文旨在定位该残余。

Method: 在几乎玻色（almost-bosonic）Chern–Simons–Schrödinger 平均场模型中构造正定、规范不变的局域缺陷，度量超出磁束缚（magnetic bound）的协变动能；在三个统计通量（statistical flux）下数值求解低能态，并使用精确伸缩与接触响应恒等式作一致性检验。

Result: 逼近稳定线时积分缺陷下降约两个数量级而密度调制增强，磁饱和与密度均匀性分离；残余缺陷集中于无涡旋外壳，约化为统计流与密度衰减间的平滑径向失配。该效应对网格与盒子细化稳健，幅度依赖通量与低能分支；排斥作用呈相反趋势。

Conclusion: 剩余偏离自对偶并非分布于体内晶格，而是定域于无涡旋外壳中未屏蔽统计通量引起的径向失配；该局域缺陷判据比积分能量更能揭示自对偶趋近的结构细节。

Abstract: Contact attraction drives the self-generated vortex lattice of a trapped anyon gas towards self-duality, but integrated energies do not reveal where the remaining departure from it is located. We address this question in the almost-bosonic Chern--Simons--Schrödinger average-field model using a positive, gauge-invariant local defect that measures the covariant kinetic energy in excess of the magnetic bound. Numerically computed low-energy states at three statistical fluxes show that, as the stability line is approached, the integrated defect falls by about two orders of magnitude while the density modulation grows, so that magnetic saturation and density uniformity separate. The residual defect concentrates in a vortex-free outer shell that carries unscreened statistical flux. There it reduces to a smooth radial mismatch between the statistical flow and the density decay, which the interior lattice cannot cancel, whereas the covariant kinetic and amplitude-gradient energies show no comparable enrichment. The effect is robust to grid and box refinement, while its magnitude depends on flux and on the low-energy branch. Exact dilation and contact-response identities relate the squared energy to the density shape and provide consistency checks. Repulsion produces the opposite trend: a smoother density with a larger defect.

</details>


### [218] [Two-Dimensional Quantum Droplets in a Mixture of Bose-Einstein Condensates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26449)
*Yue Zhang,Liangchao Chen,Wei Han,Linshan Gong,Yazhou Wang,Zekui Wang,Xinhao Liang,Wenjie Sui,Pengjun Wang,Lianghui Huang,Zengming Meng,Zhuxiong Ye,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作在半二维几何中，通过将单组分玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensates, BECs）转移为处于Lee-Huang-Yang（LHY）区的自旋混合态，首次观测到二维量子液滴（quantum droplet）的形成，并进一步生成二维Townes孤子，表明二维量子液滴比Townes孤子具有更宽的稳定参数范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子液滴是超越平均场（beyond-mean-field）物理的重要体现，其性质对维度高度敏感；量子液滴已在一维和三维实验实现，但二维实现长期缺失，而二维中LHY效应明显偏离三维形式，因此二维量子液滴的观测成为待解决的关键问题。

Method: 采用准二维几何（quasi-2D geometry），将单组分BECs转移为LHY区内的自旋混合态以形成二维量子液滴；作为对照，将初始BECs转移至具有负散射长度（scattering length）的另一单自旋态以生成二维Townes孤子，并对两类自束缚态（self-bound states）进行比较。

Result: 成功观测到二维量子液滴的形成，且该方案中的二维量子液滴比此前报道的量子液滴更稳定、寿命更长；同时实现了二维Townes孤子。对比显示，二维Townes孤子仅在由散射长度决定的临界原子数附近稳定，而二维量子液滴在更宽参数范围内保持良好定义的自束缚特性。

Conclusion: 二维量子液滴与二维Townes孤子的实现为自束缚态提供了新的认识，并为探索量子液体（quantum liquids）中的非线性动力学（nonlinear dynamics）开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Quantum droplets represent a striking manifestation of beyond-mean-field physics, with properties that depend sensitively on dimensionality. Although quantum droplets have been experimentally realized in one and three dimensions, their realization in two dimensions has remained elusive. Here, we observe the formation of a two-dimensional (2D) quantum droplet in a quasi-2D geometry by transferring the single-component Bose-Einstein condensates (BECs) into a spin mixture in the Lee-Huang-Yang (LHY) regime, where the LHY effect evidently deviates from its three-dimensional form. We find that, in our scheme, 2D quantum droplets are more stable and exhibit longer lifetimes than previously reported quantum droplets. By instead transferring the initial BECs into another single-spin state with a negative scattering length, we generate 2D Townes solitons. A comparison between the two types of self-bound states shows that, whereas 2D Townes solitons are stable only near a critical atom number determined by the scattering length, 2D quantum droplets remain well-defined and self-bound over a much broader parameter range. These realizations of quantum droplets and Townes solitons in two dimensions provide new insights into self-bound states and open new avenues for exploring nonlinear dynamics in quantum liquids.

</details>


### [219] [Non-local Stabilization and Synchronization of Larmor Precession in Trapped Polariton Condensate Dimer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26499)
*Stepan Baryshev,Alexey Yulin,Ivan Gnusov,Sergey Alyatkin,Pavlos G. Lagoudakis*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文在实验上演示了空间分离的光学捕获极化激元凝聚体（polariton condensates）之间拉莫尔进动（Larmor precession）的相互同步，并证明交叉关联函数可用于表征凝聚体耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机是探索空间分离凝聚体之间的同步机制，并建立可用于非平衡时周期驱动系统与高速自旋光电子网络（spinoptronic networks）的可扩展平台。

Method: 采用光学捕获的极化激元凝聚体二聚体（dimer），以跨凝聚体关联函数 g(2)1,2(τ) 作为耦合的敏感度量，分析由极化激元外流（polariton outflow）介导的进动同步及其对光谱重叠（spectral overlap）与进动绕向（precession winding）的共振依赖。

Result: 凝聚体二聚体中的进动同步由极化激元外流介导并共振依赖于光谱重叠与进动绕向；对其中一个格点进行全光搅拌（all-optical stirring）可非局域地延长其静态邻点的赝自旋退相干时间（pseudo-spin dephasing time），使二聚体中的锁相（phase-locking）延长一个数量级。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了研究非平衡时周期驱动系统的可扩展平台，并为高速自旋光电子网络提供了实现途径。

Abstract: We demonstrate mutual synchronization of Larmor precessions between spatially separated, optically trapped polariton condensates. We identify the cross-condensate correlation g(2)1,2(τ) as a sensitive metric for condensate coupling. Synchronization of precessions in a condensate dimer is mediated by polariton outflow and resonantly depends on spectral overlap and precession winding. All-optical stirring of one site non-locally increases the pseudo-spin dephasing time of its static neighbor, prolonging phase-locking in a dimer by an order of magnitude. Our work establishes a scalable platform for studying non-equilibrium time-periodically driven systems and for high-speed spinoptronic networks.

</details>


### [220] [Exploring ground state phase diagrams of Rydberg atom arrays with finite projected entangled-pair states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26520)
*Konrad Schröder,Erik Lennart Weerda,Matteo Rizzi*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 采用基于采样的投影纠缠对态（projected entangled-pair states, PEPS）方法，研究里德堡原子阵列（Rydberg atom arrays, RAA）中受阻挫几何结构下的基态性质，涵盖Kagomé晶格上的自旋玻璃（spin-glass）物理与Ruby晶格上超越PXP模型的拓扑序（topological order）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 里德堡原子阵列被视为量子模拟奇异量子态（exotic quantum states）的有前景平台，但二维情形下基态性质的数值预测困难；长程相互作用（long-range interactions）的忠实处理对判别竞争相（competing phases）的归宿至关重要，却通常代价高昂。已有工作表明，基于采样的PEPS可像处理短程项一样处理此类长程项，本文据此切入受争议的物理问题。

Method: 采用基于采样优化（sampling-based approach）的投影纠缠对态（PEPS），在不同受阻挫（frustrated）的RAA几何构型中计算基态性质；该工具将长程相互作用项与短程项置于同等地位处理。

Result: 在Kagomé晶格上考察自旋玻璃（spin-glass）物理的出现，在Ruby晶格上考察超越PXP模型的拓扑序（topological order）；结果为这些此前存在争议的物理问题提供了数值证据。

Conclusion: 本文有助于确立PEPS作为构建RAA实验“数字孪生（digital twins）”的合适工具。

Abstract: Rydberg atom arrays (RAA) have been advocated as a promising avenue for the quantum simulation of different exotic quantum states. Numerical predictions for the ground state properties of this experimental platform can, however, be challenging in two-dimensional setups. In particular, faithfully treating long-range interactions is often crucial for distinguishing the fate of competing phases, but it usually comes at considerable cost. Projected entangled-pair states (PEPS), optimized via a sampling-based approach, have been shown to deal with such terms on the same ground as shorter-range ones. In this work, we employ these tools to investigate debated physical questions in different frustrated geometries of RAA, from the emergence of spin-glass physics on the Kagomé lattice to topological order beyond the PXP model on the Ruby lattice. By that, we contribute to establishing PEPS as well-suited tools for creating digital twins of RAA experiments.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [221] [Shifted quantum affine symmetric pairs: finite presentations and graded coideal structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25597)
*Jian-Rong Li,Tomasz Przezdziecki*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究单连通分裂型（simply-laced split type）的反优势移位量子仿射对称对代数（anti-dominantly shifted quantum affine symmetric pair algebras），构造其Levendorskii型有限表示，并在A型中给出到移位量子仿射代数的嵌入，得到分次余理想结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移位量子仿射对称对代数（shifted quantum affine symmetric pair algebras）作为移位扭曲杨戈代数（shifted twisted Yangians）的三角类比被引入，其截断预期量子化广义仿射Grassmannian切片中的不动点轨迹；但其原始定义为含无穷多生成元与关系的环路表示（loop presentation），缺乏便于操作与应用的有限描述。

Method: 针对单连通分裂型的反优势移位量子仿射对称对代数，采用Levendorskii式方法构造有限表示（finite presentation），替代原有的无穷环路表示；随后在A型（type A）情形下利用该有限表示构造嵌入映射。

Result: 得到反优势情形下该代数的Levendorskii型有限表示；在A型中建立了移位量子对称对代数（shifted quantum symmetric pair algebra）到相应移位量子仿射代数（shifted quantum affine algebra）的嵌入，并证明其具有分次余理想（graded coideal）结构。

Conclusion: 有限表示的建立为移位量子仿射对称对代数提供了可计算的代数描述；A型嵌入与分次余理想结构揭示了对称对代数与仿射代数之间的层级关系，为后续量子化广义仿射Grassmannian切片不动点轨迹的研究提供了代数工具。

Abstract: Shifted quantum affine symmetric pair algebras were recently introduced as a trigonometric analogue of shifted twisted Yangians, whose truncations are expected to quantize fixed point loci in generalized affine Grassmannian slices. They are defined via a loop presentation with infinitely many generators and relations. In this paper, we consider anti-dominantly shifted quantum affine symmetric pair algebras of simply-laced split type. Our main result is a construction of a finite presentation à la Levendorskii. We apply it in type $\mathsf{A}$ to construct an embedding of a shifted quantum symmetric pair algebra into the corresponding shifted quantum affine algebra, yielding a graded coideal structure.

</details>


### [222] [Decomposing the Action of Macdonald Operators for Classical Types](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26389)
*Hieu Trung Vu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文研究 B_N、C_N、D_N 型经典根系对应的 Macdonald 算子（Macdonald operators），给出其累积作用（cumulative action）分解为低秩算子（lower-rank operators）与辅助算子（auxiliary operators）之和的显式公式，并在椭圆情形（elliptic case）即 Ruijsenaars 算子中讨论同一思路，指出三角极限（trigonometric limit）下该恒等式退化为 A_N 型分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅表明研究目标是对 B、C、D 型经典根系的 Macdonald 算子及椭圆型 Ruijsenaars 算子的累积作用进行结构化分解。

Method: 采用算子分解的方法：将经典根系（classical root systems）B_N、C_N、D_N 型 Macdonald 算子的累积作用显式表示为低秩算子与辅助算子作用之和，并将该分解思想推广至椭圆 Ruijsenaars 算子情形。

Result: 得到 B_N、C_N、D_N 型 Macdonald 算子累积作用的显式分解公式；在椭圆情形下给出相应讨论，并证明在三角极限下恒等式退化为 A_N 型分解。

Conclusion: 非 A 型经典根系及椭圆 Ruijsenaars 算子的累积作用可分解为低秩与辅助算子之和，且该分解在三角极限下与已知的 A_N 型情形相容，构成统一框架。

Abstract: We study Macdonald operators associated to classical root systems of types $B_N$, $C_N$, and $D_N$. We provide explicit formulas for decomposing the cumulative action of these operators as the sum of the actions of lower-rank operators and auxiliary operators. We also discuss the same idea in the elliptic case, also known as Ruijsenaars operators, and show that under the trigonometric limit, the identity reduces to type-$A_N$ decomposition.

</details>

### [223] [Colour-Field Based Particle Categorization for Residual Stress Detection and Reduction in Solid SPH Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24210)
*Gizem Kayar*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 该文基于光滑粒子流体动力学（Smoothed Particle Hydrodynamics, SPH）中的颜色场（colour-field）方法，提出一种追踪并消除固体中残余应力（residual stress）的方案，以避免材料的过早失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 残余应力（residual stress）在无外力和热压时仍存在于物体中，可能引发显著塑性变形；当其对材料产生不利影响时，需要有效手段追踪并消除该应力。

Method: 采用光滑粒子流体动力学（SPH）模拟固体力学，利用其固有的颜色场生成方法（colour-field generation approach）作为残余应力的追踪手段，并提出基于颜色场消除残余应力的实现方式。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果；仅指出颜色场方法是有前景的残余应力追踪工具，且所提方案可用于消除残余应力并防止固体对象的过早失效。

Conclusion: 颜色场方法可同时用于追踪与消除残余应力，从而避免固体材料因残余应力导致的非期望过早破坏（premature failure）。

Abstract: Residual stress remains in an object even in the absence of external forces or thermal pressure, which, in turn, may cause significant plastic deformations. In case the residual stress creates unwanted effects on the material and so is undesirable, an efficient solution is necessary to track and eliminate this stress. Smoothed Particle Hydrodynamics has been extensively used in solid mechanics simulations and the inherent colour-field generation approach is a promising tracker for the residual stress. In this paper, we propose a way to use the colour-field approach for eliminating the residual stress and prevent the undesirable premature failure of solid objects.

</details>

# cond-mat.other [[Back]](#toc)

### [224] [A project of the thermal triple axis spectrometer at the PIK reactor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26076)
*Maria Iuzviuk,Nikita Kovalenko,Vladislav Tolichev,Aleksandr Ovsianikov,Oleg Usmanov,Anna Matveeva,Igor Zobkalo,Vladislav Tarnavich*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该文介绍 PIK 反应堆上新型热中子三轴谱仪（thermal triple axis spectrometer）IN1 的设计项目，借助蒙特卡罗模拟（Monte Carlo simulation）优化主要参数，估计在 100 MW 功率、约 36 meV 中子能量下样品处中子通量可达 10⁹ n/cm²/s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文动机是提出 PIK 反应堆上新建的 IN1 热中子三轴谱仪项目，并通过参数优化获得高性能谱仪。

Method: 采用蒙特卡罗模拟优化谱仪主要参数，并对单色器（monochromator）、分析器（analyzer）、虚拟源（virtual source）及仪器屏蔽（shielding）等中子环节进行建模。

Result: 模拟优化后估计，在 100 MW 设计功率、中子能量约 36 meV 条件下，样品位置中子通量可达约 10⁹ n/cm²/s。

Conclusion: 蒙特卡罗建模可有效优化 IN1 谱仪各中子环节参数，从而获得最佳谱仪性能。

Abstract: A project of the novel thermal triple axis spectrometer IN1 at the PIK reactor is presented. Monte Carlo simulations were used for optimization its main parameters. The optimization of the instrument gives an estimated neutron flux as high as 109 n/cm2/s at the sample position at neutron energy about 36 meV at the designed 100 MW power. The modelling of neutron stages like monochromators, analyzers, virtual source, as well as instrument shielding was fulfilled to obtain the best performance of the spectrometer.

</details>


### [225] [Finite-size scaling of spin-wave scattering and enhancement in magnonic Fabry-Pérot nanoresonators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26277)
*Anton Lutsenko,Sebastiaan van Dijken*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 通过微磁学模拟（micromagnetic simulations）研究200 nm宽CoFeB条带与55 nm厚YIG薄膜构成的混合磁振子Fabry-Pérot谐振腔（hybrid magnonic Fabry-Pérot resonator）在条带长度从无限长缩短至640 nm时的响应演化，揭示亚微米尺度下由焦散束（caustic beams）主导的可重构非线性节点特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁振子神经网络（magnonic neural networks）需要紧凑、能高效散射自旋波（spin wave）、集中自旋波能量且可重构的共振节点；已有混合磁振子Fabry-Pérot谐振器研究仅局限于条带长度远大于自旋波波长的情形，亚微米短条带的响应尚不清楚。

Method: 采用微磁学模拟，考察200 nm宽CoFeB条带（置于55 nm厚YIG薄膜上）在长度由无限长缩减至640 nm过程中的自旋波传输响应，并对比平行（parallel）与反平行（antiparallel）磁化构型。

Result: 无限长条带作为一维腔（one-dimensional cavity），在一阶共振（平行1.70 GHz、反平行1.47 GHz）处打开传输禁带，并使YIG膜面外进动幅度分别增强约5倍和12倍且不畸变波前；几微米长度时条带端部发射的后向体自旋波（backward-volume spin waves）调制进动并碎裂波前；低于约1.2 μm时谐振器在整个等频轮廓（isofrequency contour）上散射入射自旋波，焦散束在反平行构型中最显著；该区间平行构型仅支持弱增强的量子化模式，反平行构型支持单一模式，面外增强约15倍；条带宽度、厚度与形状可在50–100 MHz范围调谐该模式，强增强将非线性频移（nonlinear frequency shift）与折叠（fold-over）的起始阈值降至饱和磁化强度的百分之几。

Conclusion: 亚微米磁振子谐振器（sub-micrometer magnonic resonators）有望作为磁振子网络中的可重构非线性节点（reconfigurable nonlinear nodes）。

Abstract: Magnonic neural networks require compact resonant nodes that scatter spin waves efficiently, concentrate spin-wave energy and can be reconfigured. Hybrid magnonic Fabry-Pérot resonators, formed by a ferromagnetic-metal stripe dipolar-coupled to a yttrium iron garnet (YIG) film, provide low-loss and programmable control of spin-wave transmission, but have so far been studied only for stripes that are long compared with the spin-wave wavelength. Here we use micromagnetic simulations to determine how the response of a 200-nm-wide CoFeB stripe on a 55-nm-thick YIG film evolves as the stripe length is reduced from infinity to 640 nm. An infinitely long stripe acts as a one-dimensional cavity that opens a transmission gap at the first-order resonance (1.70 GHz and 1.47 GHz for the parallel and antiparallel magnetic configuration) and enhances the out-of-plane precession amplitude in the YIG film by factors of about 5 and 12, without distorting the transmitted wavefront. For lengths of a few micrometers, backward-volume spin waves emitted from the stripe ends modulate the precession along the stripe and fragment the reflected and transmitted wavefronts. Below about 1.2 micrometer, the resonator scatters incident spin waves over the entire isofrequency contour of the film, with caustic beams that are most pronounced in the antiparallel configuration. In this regime the parallel configuration supports weakly enhanced quantized modes, whereas the antiparallel configuration supports a single mode with an out-of-plane enhancement of about 15. The width, thickness and shape of the stripe tune this mode by 50-100 MHz, and its strong enhancement lowers the onset of nonlinear frequency shift and fold-over to incident amplitudes of a few percent of the saturation magnetization. Sub-micrometer magnonic resonators are therefore promising reconfigurable nonlinear nodes for magnonic networks.

</details>


<div id='nucl-th'></div>

# nucl-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [226] [$Δ(1232)$ isobars and weak-interaction bulk viscosity in neutron-star merger matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25224)
*Bharat Kumar*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 该工作将体黏滞（bulk viscosity）计算扩展到含Δ(1232)等压体的中微子透明（neutrino-transparent）并合物质，发现Δ^-接管轻子负电荷从而开启核子直接Urca过程，使共振温度下移、kHz密度振荡阻尼加快数倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双中子星并合（binary neutron star merger）可探测数倍核饱和密度、数十MeV温度下的核物质，此时Δ(1232)等压体可能出现；而以往并合物质的体黏滞计算仅考虑核子物质（含/不含μ子）与超子物质，未纳入Δ等压体，形成研究空白。

Method: 推导带电流Δ→N与Δ→Δ'跃迁的相对论矩阵元（relativistic matrix elements），在核子宽度近似（nucleon width approximation）下对全部电子与μ子Urca过程做全相空间积分，并计入带Pauli阻塞的介质内Δ→Nπ宽度；体黏滞由电子与μ子分数在有限温度味平衡（flavor equilibrium）附近的耦合线性响应（linear response）求得。

Result: Δ^-接管轻子所携带的负电荷，抬高质子分数并开启核子直接Urca过程；在约3 MeV以下，控制味平衡的是这一成分变化而非Δ反应本身。因此对所有考察的振荡频率，体黏滞的共振峰（resonant maximum）移至更低温度，共振附近的kHz密度振荡阻尼比核子物质快数倍；此外对核子物质，线性化所依据的平衡态选择在高密度下可使共振温度移动达数MeV。

Conclusion: Δ等压体通过改变物质组分（而非其自身弱反应）主导低温区味平衡，显著增强并合物质中kHz振荡的体黏滞阻尼；平衡态选取的不确定性亦对共振温度有可观影响，需在后续计算中加以约束。

Abstract: Binary neutron star mergers probe nuclear matter at densities of a few times nuclear saturation density and temperatures of tens of MeV, conditions under which heavier baryons such as the $Δ(1232)$ isobars may appear. Previous calculations of the bulk viscosity of merger matter have considered nucleonic matter, with and without muons, and hyperonic matter, but not $Δ$ isobars. In this work, we extend these calculations to neutrino-transparent matter in which $Δ$ isobars are present in strong equilibrium. We derive the relativistic matrix elements for the charged-current $Δ\to N$ and $Δ\toΔ'$ transitions, evaluate all electronic and muonic Urca processes over the full phase space using the nucleon width approximation, and include the in-medium $Δ\to Nπ$ width with Pauli blocking. The bulk viscosity is obtained from the coupled linear response of the electron and muon fractions about the finite-temperature flavor equilibrium. We find that the $Δ^-$ takes over the negative charge from the leptons, which raises the proton fraction and opens the nucleonic direct Urca process. Up to about 3~MeV this change in composition, rather than the $Δ$ reactions themselves, controls flavor equilibration. As a consequence, the resonant maximum of the bulk viscosity moves to lower temperatures for all oscillation frequencies we consider, and kHz density oscillations near resonance are damped several times faster than in nucleonic matter. We also show that, for nucleonic matter, the choice of the equilibrium state about which the rates are linearized shifts the resonance temperature by up to a few MeV at high density.

</details>

### [227] [Fate of Arnold tongues and canards in Mobius systems with noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26354)
*Artem Alexandrov*

Main category: nlin.CD

TL;DR: 该文研究二维环面上由莫比乌斯变换（Mobius transformations）支配的噪声动力系统，结合WKB方法与大偏差理论，刻画有限时间旋转数的涨落与稀有相位滑移，并给出亚稳锁相、Arnold舌头（Arnold tongues）熔化及canard轨道持续性的定量判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是在有噪声且观测时间有限的现实条件下，定量判断锁相（phase-locking）何时稳定、何时失效，弥补确定性带隙图像与随机动力学之间的缺口。

Method: 采用Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin（WKB）方法在慢-快极限下刻画周期势薛定谔方程的带隙结构；用大偏差理论（large-deviation theory）描述有限时间旋转数的涨落，用弱噪声逃逸作用量（escape actions）确定稀有相位滑移的指数尺度；并对canard段给出充分条件分析。

Result: 得到亚稳锁相与Arnold舌头熔化的定量判据；在给定进入误差控制下，沿排斥分支的涨落指数放大导致canard持续所需噪声尺度在慢强迫极限下指数小。

Conclusion: 噪声系统中锁相与canard现象可由指数尺度作用量定量刻画，为有限观测时间下的亚稳性判别提供可操作准则。

Abstract: We consider a class of noisy dynamical systems on the two-dimensional torus whose phase evolution is governed by Mobius transformations. In the deterministic limit, these systems favor phase-locking that is described by the band-gap structure of an associated Schrodinger equation with a periodic potential. We use the Wentzel-Kramers-Brillouin method to characterize this structure in the slow-fast limit. Then we show that large-deviation theory describes fluctuations of the finite-time rotation number, whereas weak-noise escape actions determine the exponential scale of rare phase slips. These estimates provide quantitative criteria for metastable phase-locking and melting of Arnold tongues over a prescribed observation time. We derive sufficient conditions for stochastic trajectories to follow prescribed canard segments. With controlled entrance errors, exponential amplification of fluctuations along repelling branches leads to an exponentially small sufficient noise scale for canard persistence in the slow-forcing limit.

</details>

### [228] [Undulatory Swimming in Sand: Subsurface Locomotion of the Sandfish Lizard](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24934)
*Ryan D. Maladen,Yang Ding,Chen Li,Daniel I. Goldman*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该研究结合高速X射线成像（high-speed x-ray imaging）与颗粒阻力（granular drag）测量，建立了经验模型来预测沙鱼蜥（sandfish, Scincus scincus）在干沙中的游动速度，结果表明其非惯性游动（noninertial swimming）发生在摩擦流体（frictional fluid）之中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沙鱼蜥在兼具固体与流体行为的干沙中运动，而流体中黏性流体动力学（viscous hydrodynamics）可预测游动速度，颗粒介质中却缺乏等效理论，因此需要建立可预测沙中游动速度的模型。

Method: 利用高速X射线成像观察沙面以下的身体运动，测量小圆柱体在不同取向角下所受的颗粒阻力，并将这些力沿动物运动轮廓（movement profile）求和，构建经验模型。

Result: 成像显示沙面以下蜥蜴不再用四肢推进，而是沿身体传播波动行波（undulatory traveling wave）产生推力以克服阻力；经验模型预测的游动速度与实验观测结果一致。

Conclusion: 模型与实验的吻合表明，沙鱼蜥的非惯性游动发生于摩擦流体之中，为颗粒介质中的阻力与推进提供了经验性定量框架。

Abstract: The desert-dwelling sandfish (Scincus scincus) moves within dry sand, a material that displays solid and fluidlike behavior. High-speed x-ray imaging shows that below the surface, the lizard no longer uses limbs for propulsion but generates thrust to overcome drag by propagating an undulatory traveling wave down the body. Although viscous hydrodynamics can predict swimming speed in fluids such as water, an equivalent theory for granular drag is not available. To predict sandfish swimming speed, we developed an empirical model by measuring granular drag force on a small cylinder oriented at different angles relative to the displacement direction and summing these forces over the animal movement profile. The agreement between model and experiment implies that the noninertial swimming occurs in a frictional fluid.

</details>

### [229] [Cohomological Milnor Fibrations of Landau--Ginzburg Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25825)
*Huijun Fan,Jieran Wang*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画完备 Kähler 流形（complete Kähler manifold）上本质驯顺 Landau--Ginzburg 模型（fundamentally tame LG model）的全局拓扑：证明超势（superpotential）满射、正则值上局部平凡纤维化，并给出相对同调集中于中间次数且秩等于全局 Milnor 数（global Milnor number）的结果，还构造了正、负无穷处的相对上同调丛及其平坦 Gauss--Manin 联络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 Milnor 纤维化（Milnor fibration）与 Ehresmann 型纤维化（Ehresmann-type fibration）依赖紧性/固有性（properness）假设，在完备非紧 Kähler 流形上不再直接适用；因此需要建立非固有（non-proper）情形下的全局纤维化与同调结构理论。

Method: 在完备 Kähler 流形上先证超势满射；证明其在正则值集合上限制为局部平凡纤维化；在临界点有限假设下采用 Morse 理论（Morse-theoretic）论证相对同调与相对同伦型；并对 Stein 流形（Stein manifold）上满足一致驯顺条件的形变构造正、负无穷处的相对上同调丛。

Result: 超势 f 满射到复数域；f 在正则值上为局部平凡纤维化，从而给出经典 Milnor 纤维化的全局推广与非固有情形下的 Ehresmann 型纤维化。临界点有限时，相对同调 H_*(M, f^{-1}(c))（c 为正则值）集中于中间次数，秩等于全局 Milnor 数 μ_f；纤维的相对同伦型可等同于 f 在无穷远处次水平集（sublevel set）的相对同伦型。形变情形下，正、负无穷处的相对上同调丛均带自然平坦 Gauss--Manin 联络。

Conclusion: 非固有设定下，本质驯顺 LG 模型的全局拓扑具有刚性结构：中间次数的相对同调由单一不变量 μ_f 控制，纤维同伦型由无穷远处次水平集刻划，且形变理论给出带平坦 Gauss--Manin 联络的相对上同调丛。这些结论将 Milnor 纤维化与 Ehresmann 纤维化框架推广至完备 Kähler 流形。

Abstract: We study the global topology of \emph{fundamentally tame} Landau--Ginzburg (LG) models on complete Kähler manifolds. We first show that the superpotential $f$ is surjective onto $\mathbb{C}$, then prove that $f$ restricts to a locally trivial fibration over the set of its regular values, which yields a global generalization of the classical Milnor fibration and provides an Ehresmann-type fibration in the non-proper setting. Assuming that $f$ has finitely many critical points, we use a Morse-theoretic argument to show that the relative homology of $\bigl(M,f^{-1}(c)\bigr)$, for a regular value $c$, is concentrated in the middle degree and has rank equal to the global Milnor number $μ_f$. We further identify the relative homotopy type of the fibers with that of the sublevel sets of $f$ at infinity.Finally, for deformations satisfying a uniform tameness condition on a Stein manifold, we construct the relative cohomology bundles at positive and negative infinity, each equipped with a natural flat Gauss--Manin connection.

</details>

### [230] [Algebraic decomposition and topological fibrations of the $U(4) \to SU(2)$ quotient space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23859)
*Dimitris Mastoridis,Konstantinos Kalogirou*

Main category: physics.gen-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究幺正群（unitary group）$U(4)$ 经非标准连续对称破缺（continuous symmetry breaking）至 $SU(2)_H$ 子群后的代数与拓扑性质，证明商空间 $SU(4)/SU(2)_H$ 可同时容纳闭合的 $\mathfrak{su}(3)$ 李代数（Lie algebra）与拓扑稳定的非线性余集（coset）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察非单纯（non-simple）幺正群能否自发地提供一种数学严格的几何框架（geometric template），以在同一结构内同时容纳连续规范对称性（continuous gauge symmetry）与非微扰的稳定拓扑扇区（topological sector）。

Method: 通过将未破缺的 $SU(2)_H$ 显式嵌入基本矩阵表示（fundamental representation）的右下象限，分析 $SU(4)/SU(2)_H$ 的代数闭合性，并借助流形的整体球面纤维化（spherical fibration）研究其拓扑结构，用第三同伦群（third homotopy group）对三维边界映射进行分类。

Result: 12 维商空间发生严格非对称几何划分：8 维扇区保持闭合对易关系（commutation relations）并构成 $\mathfrak{su}(3)$ 子代数；4 维非对角扇区代数上不闭合，形成纯非线性拓扑余集，其约化为 $\mathbb{C}P^1 \cong S^2$ 底空间，从而 $\pi_3(S^2) \cong \mathbb{Z}$ 给出整数量子化的 Hopf 不变量（Hopf invariant）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，非单纯幺正群可自然产生一个数学严格的几何模板，同时承载连续规范对称性与稳定的非微扰拓扑扇区，并由 Hopf 不变量的整数量子化保证真空（vacuum）的绝对稳定性。

Abstract: We investigate the algebraic and topological properties of the unitary group $U(4)$ subjected to a non-standard continuous symmetry breaking cascade down to an $SU(2)_H$ subgroup. By explicitly embedding the unbroken $SU(2)_H$ symmetry into the lower-right quadrant of the fundamental matrix representation, we demonstrate that the resulting 12-dimensional quotient space, $SU(4)/SU(2)_H$, undergoes a strictly asymmetric geometric partition. This specific breaking pattern uniquely isolates an 8-dimensional sector that retains closed commutation relations, forming a rigorous $\mathfrak{su}(3)$ Lie subalgebra. Concurrently, it isolates a 4-dimensional off-diagonal sector that fails to close algebraically, generating a purely non-linear topological coset. By analyzing the global spherical fibrations of the manifold, we show that this non-linear territory reduces to a $\mathbb{C}P^1 \cong S^2$ base space. Consequently, continuous mappings from a 3-dimensional spatial boundary are classified by the third homotopy group, $π_3(S^2) \cong \mathbb{Z}$, guaranteeing absolute vacuum stability via the integer-quantized Hopf invariant. Ultimately, this framework demonstrates that a non-simple unitary group can organically provide a mathematically rigorous geometric template capable of simultaneously accommodating both continuous gauge symmetries and stable, non-perturbative topological sectors.

</details>

### [231] [Physics-Constrained Modeling and Inference of the Equation of State for Dissociative Materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26791)
*Jolypich Pek,Ben Seiyon Lee,Jason Bernstein,Philip Myint*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分区统计框架（partitioned statistical framework），从状态方程（EOS）数据中直接推断解离性材料的摩尔质量面（molar-mass surface）与德拜温度曲线（Debye-temperature curve），在构造上保证热力学一致性（thermodynamic consistency），并在二氧化碳案例中验证了物理量复原与预测精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有EOS表依赖针对特定材料的手工调参（hand-tuning），过程繁琐、难以跨材料与跨条件扩展，且无法保证热力学一致性。

Method: 将离子—热自由能项建模为由摩尔质量面与德拜温度曲线支配的两个潜在过程，二者不直接观测，仅由自由能的导数（内能与压强）在热力学约束下反演；采用多项式基函数（polynomial basis functions）在分区上刻画局部摩尔质量行为，德拜温度用I样条基（I-spline basis）建模。

Result: 在模拟实验与二氧化碳案例研究中，该方法准确复原了潜在物理量，提升了对留出能量与压强数据（held-out data）的预测能力，并提供不确定性量化（uncertainty quantification），且可随EOS表规模扩展。

Conclusion: 该分区统计框架为解离性材料EOS建模提供了可自动化、可扩展且热力学自洽的替代方案，优于依赖手工调参的传统建表流程。

Abstract: Equations of state (EOSs) describe how a material's internal energy and pressure change with temperature and density. They are inputs to hydrodynamic simulations in materials science and planetary science. We model the ion-thermal free-energy term for dissociative materials, where molecular bonds break apart to form new species under extreme conditions. This term is governed by two latent physical processes, a molar-mass surface and a Debye-temperature curve, neither of which is observed directly. Instead, both must be recovered from internal energy and pressure, which are derivatives of the free energy, subject to thermodynamic constraints. Existing EOS tables are typically built through material-specific hand-tuning, a process that is tedious, does not scale across materials or conditions, and offers no guarantee of thermodynamic consistency. We propose a partitioned statistical framework that infers molar mass and Debye temperature directly from EOS data, guaranteeing thermodynamic consistency by construction. Local molar-mass behavior is captured with polynomial basis functions across domain partitions, while the Debye temperature is modeled with an I-spline basis. In simulations and a carbon dioxide case study, the method accurately recovers the underlying physical quantities, improves prediction of held-out energy and pressure data, and provides uncertainty quantification while scaling with EOS table size.

</details>


<div id='physics.hist-ph'></div>

### [232] [The Geometry of the "Gauge Argument"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26049)
*James Owen Weatherall,Benjamin H. Feintzeig*

Main category: physics.hist-ph

TL;DR: 本文将常被批评为特设（ad hoc）的“规范论证（gauge argument）”重构为关于向量丛（vector bundle）几何的严格论证，并主张其具有说服力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 规范论证在哲学文献中广受批评，被认为缺乏根据、不具说服力；本文旨在回应这一批评，为其寻找更严格的表述形式。

Method: 以向量丛几何为框架重构规范论证，将其分为两部分：第一部分与把伽利略时空（Galilean spacetime）作为牛顿引力（Newtonian gravitation）适当背景的 Weyl–Stein 论证高度类比；第二部分援引 Yang–Mills 理论（Yang–Mills theory）中由 Utiyama（1956）给出的经典结果。

Result: 重构后的规范论证由两个可分离的环节构成，其一借助时空几何的类比获得支持，其二依托 Utiyama 关于相互作用不变理论解释的既有定理。

Conclusion: 规范论证并非本质上特设，经向量丛几何重构后可成为严格且（作者认为）令人信服的论证。

Abstract: We show how the "gauge argument", widely criticized by philosophers as ad hoc and unconvincing, may be reconstructed rigorously, and, we believe, convincingly, as an argument concerning the geometry of vector bundles. In this form, the gauge argument has two parts, the first of which is strongly analogous to the Weyl-Stein argument for Galilean spacetime as the appropriate setting for Newtonian gravitation; and the second of which invokes a classic result in the Yang-Mills theory literature, due to Utiyama [1956, "Invariant Theoretical Interpretation of Interaction", Phys. Rev. 101, 1597].

</details>


<div id='physics.ins-det'></div>

# physics.ins-det [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Development of microwave multiplexed readout for athermal phonon TES-based detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24300)
*Tran Tsan,Douglas A. Bennett,Daniel T. Becker,Rebecca Carney,Johnathon D. Gard,John A. B. Mates,Joel N. Ullom,Michael R. Williams,John C. Groh*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本工作报道了面向无热声子（athermal phonon）TES 探测器的高复用微波读出（microwave readout）研发进展，验证了读出功能与噪声水平，并给出对寄生背景负载的约束，为亚GeV暗物质搜索等稀有事例实验的大规模扩展提供可行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚eV量热能测量依赖无热声子探测通道，可服务低质量暗物质搜索、相干弹性中微子-核散射（CEνNS）与量子信息科学；但过渡边缘传感器（TES）虽阈值最低，却因传统SQUID读出架构的固有限制而面临严重的可扩展性瓶颈。

Method: 借鉴高规模化的毫米波、X射线与伽马射线TES系统的进展，将其适配到低阈值稀有事例测量的需求，在稀释制冷机（dilution refrigerator）测试平台上搭建高复用微波读出系统并量化其基本读出功能与噪声，测试多种低饱和功率TES器件，并讨论低温在线射频滤波（cryogenic inline RF filtering）方案。

Result: 实现了低于毫米波和X射线器件的低饱和功率TES器件的运行，完成背景事例脉冲的概念验证复用读出，并将读出对寄生TES背景负载的贡献约束在<1 fW。

Conclusion: 结果针对下一代规模化无热声子探测器实现前必须解决的多项关键挑战给出了实用解法，显示出微波复用读出在传感器数量与靶质量大幅提升方面的应用潜力。

Abstract: Continued advancements in sub-eV calorimetric measurements are being enabled through the athermal phonon detection channel, with applications spanning a diverse array of fields, including low-mass dark matter searches, coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering, and quantum information science. While Transition Edge Sensors (TESs) have achieved the lowest thresholds to date, they face significant scalability bottlenecks due to the inherent constraints of conventional SQUID readout architectures. To address this limitation, we present an update on R&D to develop practical, highly multiplexed, microwave readout for athermal phonon TESs, which can enable a dramatic increase in both sensor count and target mass, with a focus on sub-GeV dark matter searches as a target application. This work leverages advances from highly scaled mm-wave, x-ray, and gamma-ray TES systems, adapting them to the unique needs of low-threshold rare event measurements. We quantify the basic readout functionality and noise level in our dilution refrigerator testbed and demonstrate operation of a variety of TES devices with saturation powers significantly lower than mm-wave and x-ray devices. We demonstrate proof-of-concept multiplexed readout of background event pulses, bound the readout's contribution to the parasitic TES background load to <1 fW, and discuss a cryogenic inline RF filtering scheme. These results demonstrate practical solutions to several of the key challenges that must be solved before a next-generation athermal phonon detector at scale is realized.

</details>


### [234] [Characterization of the R14374-10 photomultiplier tube for the Pacific Ocean Neutrino Experiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26299)
*O. Aghaei,M. Agostini,A. Alexander Wight,K. Baş,N. Baily,P. S. Barbeau,A. J. Baron,S. Bash,C. Bellenghi,M. Boehmer,M. Brandenburg,Z. Brisson-Tsavoussis,D. Brussow,P. Bunton,N. Cedarblade-Jones,K. Clark,B. Crudele,M. Danninger,T. DeYoung,M. Dinkel,N. Exconde,A. Gärtner,J. Garriz,D. Ghuman,L. Ginzkey,V. Gousy-Leblanc,D. Grant,A. Grimes,C. Haack,R. Halliday,M. Heesemann,D. Hembroff,F. Henningsen,M. Herle,O. Janik,V. Jourdenais,W. Kang,S. Karanth,T. Kerscher,S. Kerschtien,S. Koch,K. Kopański,C. Kopper,P. Krause,C. B. Krauss,N. Kurahashi,C. Lagunas Gualda,A. Lam,T. Lavallee,K. Leismüller,R. Li,S. Loipolder,C. Magee,S. Magel,P. Malecki,T. Martin,A. Maunder,C. Miller,N. Molberg,R. Moore,B. Nührenbörger,B. Nichol,W. Noga,B. Owens,R. Ørsøe,L. Papp,N. Park,V. Parrish,P. Pfahler,J. Pflanz,B. Pirenne,E. Price,A. Rahlin,M. Rangen,E. Resconi,N. Retza,S. Robertson,M. F. Rodriguez-Pilco,A. Šaina,D. Salazar-Gallegos,A. Scholz,L. Schumacher,P. Senyk,S. Sharma,S. Shively,C. Spannfellner,J. Stacho,I. Taboada,K. Tchiorniy,J. Thwaites,J. P. Twagirayezu,M. Un Nisa,B. Veenstra,M. Velazquez,L. von der Werth,C. Weaver,N. Whitehorn,B. Winnicky-Lewis,L. Winter,R. Wroński,J. P. Yañez,S. Yun-Cárcamo,A. Zaalishvili*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文报道太平洋中微子实验（P-ONE）首条探测器线所用320支3英寸光电倍增管（PMT）的系统表征：全部PMT满足验收标准，平均渡越时间弥散（TTS）为1.73±0.15 ns，增益5×10⁶下电荷分辨率为0.43±0.03 pC；并对6支PMT子集进行了面向探测器模拟的深入表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: P-ONE是计划部署于太平洋的立方千米级中微子望远镜（neutrino telescope），依托加拿大海洋网络（ONC）的深海基础设施；在部署前需确认首条探测器线所集成的PMT满足全部探测器验收标准（acceptance criteria），并为探测器模拟提供关键参数。

Method: 对拟装入首条探测器线的全部PMT进行系统表征，并与实验室数字化系统交叉验证集成的数据采集（DAQ）系统；随后对6支PMT子集深入测量线性度、后脉冲率、量子效率（quantum efficiency）与相对收集效率（collection efficiency）等模拟所需性质。

Result: 所有PMT均达标：平均TTS为1.73±0.15 ns，增益5×10⁶下平均电荷分辨率为0.43±0.03 pC，平均后脉冲率约6%；在完整P-ONE读出电子学下，增益5×10⁶时响应在150个光电子（photoelectron）以内保持线性；子集中识别出Cs⁺、He⁺等离子的后脉冲贡献；395 nm处峰值量子效率为27.0±0.7%；相对探测效率测量显示收集效率在测量波长范围内存在与波长无关的10–15%偏移。

Conclusion: 首条探测器线所用PMT全部满足指定性能标准，其系统表征与子集深入测量为P-ONE探测器的部署与模拟提供了经过验证的参数基础。

Abstract: The Pacific Ocean Neutrino Experiment (P-ONE) is a planned cubic-kilometer-scale neutrino telescope that will be deployed in the Pacific Ocean, leveraging deep-sea infrastructure operated by Ocean Networks Canada (ONC). The first detector line will deploy 320 3-inch Hamamatsu R14374-10 photomultiplier tubes (PMTs) to detect Cherenkov light produced by charged particles propagating through water, as well as light from calibration sources and optical backgrounds. This article presents the systematic characterization of all PMTs to be integrated into the first detector line prior to deployment, ensuring compliance with all detector acceptance criteria. All PMTs satisfy the specified performance criteria: the average transit-time spread (TTS) was measured to be $1.73\pm 0.15\,$ns, and the average charge resolution at a gain of \num{5e6} was $0.43\pm 0.03\,$pC; the average after-pulse rate was measured to be around 6$\,\%$. The integrated P-ONE data acquisition (DAQ) system was also tested and cross-validated against a laboratory digitization system. The second part of the paper presents an in-depth characterization of a subset of six PMTs, focusing on the key properties required for detector simulations, including linearity, after-pulse rate, quantum efficiency, and relative collection efficiency. PMT linearity was measured at a gain of \num{5e6} using the complete P-ONE readout electronics, and the response was found to remain linear for signals up to 150 photoelectrons. After-pulse contributions from $\text{Cs}^{+}$, $\text{He}^{+}$, and other ions were identified in the subset. The peak quantum efficiency was measured to be $27.0\pm0.7\,\%$ at 395\,nm. Using measurements of the relative detection efficiency, the collection efficiency was found to differ by a wavelength-independent offset of 10--15$\%$ across the measured wavelength range.

</details>


### [235] [Optical imaging in dual-phase xenon time projection chambers for neutrinoless double beta decay searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26739)
*Adam D. Tidball,Jingke Xu,Brian G. Lenardo,Nicholas Antipa*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该文提出一种可集成于大型双相氙时间投影室（dual-phase xenon TPC）的广角光学成像系统，通过成像气体电致发光（gas electroluminescence）为MeV量级信号提供毫米级横向位置分辨，从而在同一装置中兼顾WIMP暗物质搜寻与氙-136无中微子双贝塔衰变（0νββ）搜寻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WIMP暗物质信号与0νββ信号能量差异悬殊，需不同的探测器优化；液氙0νββ搜寻需高效排除0νββ能量附近由背景伽马射线产生的近距离多重散射（multiscatter），而现有双相氙TPC仅在纵向维度具备此能力。

Method: 设计高度定制化的广角光学系统，可便捷集成于大型氙TPC，通过对气体电致发光光进行成像来实现MeV信号的毫米级横向位置分辨；文中给出该系统设计、模拟性能评估及其对未来液氙0νββ搜寻意义的讨论。

Result: 模拟结果表明该成像系统可提供毫米级横向位置分辨，从二维上补足多重散射甄别能力；同时保持TPC的低能阈灵敏度，使单一实验可同时优化暗物质与0νββ两类物理目标。

Conclusion: 该广角成像方案为未来液氙0νββ搜寻提供了一种在不牺牲低能性能前提下增强背景甄别的可行途径，并支持单台探测器兼顾暗物质与0νββ物理项目。

Abstract: Dual-phase xenon time projection chambers (TPCs) are the leading technology for WIMP dark matter searches and have also demonstrated competitive sensitivities to neutrinoless double beta decays (0$νββ$) of \ce{^{136}Xe}. However, the large energy disparity between these two signals requires different detector optimizations. In particular, 0$νββ$\ searches in liquid xenon desire efficient rejection of closely located multiscatters of background gamma rays near the 0$νββ$\ energy, a capability achieved only in the vertical dimension in existing dual-phase xenon TPCs. We propose a highly customized, wide-angle optical system that can be conveniently integrated into large xenon TPCs and provide mm-scale transverse position resolution for MeV signals by imaging gas electroluminescence light. This approach preserves a TPC's low-energy sensitivity and optimizes a single experiment for both dark matter and 0$νββ$\ searches. This paper discusses the design, simulated performance, and implications of the imaging system for future liquid xenon-based 0$νββ$\ searches.

</details>


### [236] [Phonon-dominated thermal conductivity of a superconducting amorphous MoSi thin film](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26136)
*Jacopo Piantanida Chiesa,Tom Q. Post,Martin P. van Exter,Michiel J. A. de Dood*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 采用差分 3ω 法（differential 3ω method）在 50–300 K 温区测量约 90 nm 厚 Mo0.78Si0.22 薄膜的热导率，发现其不满足维德曼–弗兰兹定律（Wiedemann-Franz law），表明热输运由类声子激发（phonon-like excitations）而非电子主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多超导器件电热模型（electro-thermal models）默认热输运由电子主导，本文旨在直接测量 MoSi 薄膜的热导率以检验这一假设。

Method: 使用差分 3ω 法（differential 3ω method），在闭循环低温恒温器（closed-cycle cryostat）中覆盖 50–300 K 温区，对以 Al2O3 覆盖、Pt 线探测的约 90 nm Mo0.78Si0.22 薄膜进行温度依赖热导率测量。

Result: 测得热导率不满足维德曼–弗兰兹定律（Wiedemann-Franz law），说明热输运不由电子主导，而由类声子激发（phonon-like excitations）主导；作者认为该性质可能与电阻率–温度曲线中可见的弱局域化（weak localization）有关。

Conclusion: MoSi 的热输运机制与超导器件电热模型中常见的电子主导假设相悖，弱局域化可能是其成因之一。

Abstract: We use the differential 3ω method to directly measure the temperature dependent thermal conductivity for about 90 nm Mo0.78Si0.22 thin films. The measurements are performed in a closed-cycle cryostat to access the temperature range between 50 K and 300 K. The MoSi thin films are capped with Al2O3 and probed with a Pt wire. The results show that the thermal conductivity does not satisfy the Wiedemann-Franz law. This implies that thermal transport in MoSi is not dominated by electrons but by phonon-like excitations, contrary to what is assumed in many electro-thermal models of superconducting devices. We argue that this property might be related to the weak localization visible in the resistivity as a function of temperature curves of the superconductor.

</details>
