### [237] [Reduction of structure groups for noncommutative principal bundles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26590)
*Stefan Wagner*

Main category: math.OA

TL;DR: 该文研究非交换主丛的结构群约化，借助Ellwood意义下的自由C*-动力系统建模，给出约化的Tannakian刻画及正规子群情形下的障碍理论，并构造了一个无法约化到SO(n)的非交换O(n)实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典主丛的结构群约化有成熟理论，而非交换主丛（noncommutative principal bundles）的约化尚缺乏系统刻画，尤其缺少与Tannakian重构、K-理论障碍相联系的统一框架。

Method: 以C*-代数自由动力系统建模非交换主丛；先描述从H到G的诱导（induction）在相伴对应双模及其张量结构上的行为，再通过重构（reconstruction）用相伴张量函子给出约化的Tannakian刻画；对正规子群情形用中心等变同态（central equivariant homomorphisms）刻画约化。

Result: 得到约化的Tannakian等价刻画；对正规子群给出基于中心等变同态的约化判据，并导出模论与K-理论（K-theoretic）两类障碍；构造非交换O(n)例子，证明其不存在到SO(n)的约化。

Conclusion: 非交换结构群约化可由张量范畴与同调/模论不变量系统刻画，且经典情形下的直觉（如O(n)总可约化到SO(n)）在非交换设定中不再成立。

Abstract: Let $G$ be a compact group and let $H \subseteq G$ be a closed subgroup. We study reduction of the structure group for noncommutative principal bundles, modelled by free C${}^*$-dynamical systems in the sense of Ellwood. We first describe how induction from $H$ to $G$ behaves on associated correspondences and their tensor structure. Using reconstruction, this leads to a Tannakian characterization of reduction of the structure group in terms of the associated tensor functor. For normal subgroups, we characterize reduction in terms of central equivariant homomorphisms and derive module- and $K$-theoretic obstructions. We conclude with a noncommutative $O(n)$-example which admits no reduction to $SO(n)$.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.PE'></div>

# q-bio.PE [[Back]](#toc)

### [238] [The resource cost of cooperative invasion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26235)
*Abhishek Chowdhury*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 在固定种群规模下，量化接触网络（contact network）使合作者（cooperator）获得优势所需的接触强度对比、入侵时间与测量成本，并发现“最省接触”“最快”“最易测量”的网络设计彼此不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触网络可通过保护一个谱系（lineage）直至其后代以群体形式与背叛者（defector）竞争，从而促进合作；但实现该优势所需的接触强度、入侵时间与测量投入尚需定量确定。

Method: 个体按平均合作收益（averaged cooperation payoffs）繁殖，后代通过双向等权接触替换邻居；以“接触对比度（contact contrast，最强与最弱接触之比）”为度量，在六位点路径（six-site path）上做弱选择（weak selection）分析，收益成本比 b/c=1+ε 趋于 1，并分别在均匀放置突变体与新生后代突变两种情形下求解最小对比度，兼顾线性与指数收益加权下的优化。

Result: 均匀放置突变体时最小对比度按 656ε^{-2} 增长，新生后代突变时为 4050ε^{-3}；稀有替换既保护位点又抑制该处突变到达，故后者需更强接触。全局界（global bounds）迫使两个弱耦合群体与最小对比度接触的精确镜像对称（mirror symmetry）。均匀引入下，额外对比度将无选择时灭绝或固定的主导平均时间由 12/ε 缩短至 6/ε，并最终破坏最快设计的镜像对称。

Conclusion: 在固定相对精度下、对比度高于最小值固定倍数时，主导累计试验时长按 ε^{-3} 增长；高效测量以较小优势换取更短试验。故最省、最快与最易测量的网络并不相同，网络设计需在优势强度与实验成本间权衡。

Abstract: A contact network can favor cooperation by sheltering a lineage until its descendants compete with defectors as a group. We determine the contact strengths, invasion times and measurement effort needed to realize this advantage at fixed population size. Individuals reproduce according to averaged cooperation payoffs, and offspring replace neighbors through contacts of equal weight in both directions. Contact contrast is the ratio of strongest to weakest contact. Under weak selection on a six-site path, the least contrast needed to favor a single cooperator over the reverse invasion by a defector grows as $656ε^{-2}$ for uniformly placed mutants and $4050ε^{-3}$ for mutations in newborn offspring, as the benefit-cost ratio $b/c=1+ε$ approaches one. The extra power arises because rare replacement protects a site while suppressing mutation arrival there. Global bounds force two weakly coupled groups and exact mirror symmetry of the least-contrast contacts for sufficiently small $ε$. Under uniform introduction, additional contrast shortens the leading mean time to extinction or fixation in the absence of selection, from $12/ε$ toward $6/ε$ reproduction attempts, and eventually breaks the mirror symmetry of the fastest design. Estimating the uniform-introduction advantage from fixation outcomes of independent completed invasions defines a third optimum. Within the six-site family with mirror-symmetric, strongly separated contacts, we optimize contacts and selection strength with reproduction weighted linearly or exponentially by payoff. At fixed relative precision, with contrast a fixed factor above its minimum, the leading summed trial duration grows as $ε^{-3}$. Efficient measurement accepts a smaller advantage in exchange for shorter trials. The cheapest, fastest and most efficiently measured networks therefore differ.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [239] [Universal electrical resistivity in iron-based high-temperature superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23865)
*E. F. Talantsev*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究提出最近邻并联电阻率模型可统一描述多种铁基超导体（iron-based superconductors, IBS）的面内电阻率 ρ_ab(T)，并将其形态差异归因于德拜温度与跳跃激活能的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物、铁基与镍基超导体中温度相关的电阻率 ρ(T) 仍是凝聚态物理未解问题，其绝对数值常超出 Ioffe-Rigel 判据，且不同掺杂样品的 ρ(T) 形状差异显著。

Method: 采用最近邻并联电阻率模型（nearest neighbors parallel resistivity model），假设 ρ_ab(T) 由两个并联导电通道相加构成：一个由 Bloch-Grüneisen 方程描述，另一个由 Arrhenius 跳跃方程（Arrhenius hopping equation）描述；由此拟合 11、111、122、1111、1144 和 42214 型铁基超导体中自旋密度波相（spin density wave）或向列序（nematic order）被掺杂抑制的样品。

Result: 该模型能够良好描述所研究铁基超导体的面内电阻率 ρ_ab(T)；不同样品 ρ_ab(T) 的多样性可解释为德拜温度（Debye temperature）Θ_D 与跳跃激活能 E_a 的变化，且导出的德拜温度被用于估算电子-声子耦合常数（electron-phonon coupling constant）λ_e-ph。

Conclusion: 并联双通道图像为铁基超导体面内电阻率的统一描述提供了可行框架，并可由拟合参数进一步提取电子-声子耦合强度。

Abstract: Temperature-dependent electrical resistivity $ρ(T)$ in cuprate, iron-based, and nickelate superconductors represents one of unresolved problems in modern solid-state physics, because of the high absolute value of $ρ(T)$, which, in many cases, exceeds the Ioffe-Rigel criterion, and a large difference in the $ρ(T)$ shape for samples with different doping. Here I show that the nearest neighbors parallel resistivity model (arXiv: 2607.23484) can well describe the in-plane resistivity $ρ_{ab}(T)$ in iron-based superconductors (IBS) of type 11, 111, 122, 1111, 1144 and 42214 in which the spin density wave phase or nematic order is suppressed by doping. The model is based on the postulate that $ρ_{ab}(T)$ is a sum of two parallel conduction channels, where one channel is described by the Bloch-Grüneisen equation and the other by the Arrhenius hopping equation. Consequently, the variety of $ρ_{ab}(T)$ in the IBS is explained as a variation of the Debye temperature $Θ_D$ and the hopping activation energy $E_a$. The derived Debye temperatures were used to estimate the electron-phonon coupling constant $λ_{e-ph}$in the studied IBS.

</details>


### [240] [Resonant suppression of the $π$-type Josephson component in a Kitaev-ladder junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23875)
*Cheng-Rong Xie,Hiroki Tsuchiura,Yukio Tanaka*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作研究连接两个 Kitaev 梯超导体的有限正常段中的约瑟夫森输运（Josephson transport），指出离散能级失谐（discrete-level detuning）可通过分支选择机制（branch-selection mechanism）控制平衡约瑟夫森耦合的符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有认识通常将正常段中的能级共振（resonance）视为增强约瑟夫森透射的因素，本文旨在揭示共振与失谐改写的并非透射强度，而是哪些低能支参与约瑟夫森耦合，从而影响耦合符号与谐波结构。

Method: 采用有限正常段连接两个 Kitaev 梯超导体的模型；通过分支选择、螺旋度分解（helicity decomposition）以及受控的甜点（sweet-point）有限段理论进行分析，并结合数值计算验证共振诱导的抑制在可解相位选择之外仍然存在。

Result: 在非共振情形下，失谐分离两个竞争低能支，使腿间杂化（inter-leg hybridization）夹持趋向 0 相的分支而保留 π 相分支的色散，负一阶谐波由弱耦合的分支切换响应过渡为平滑的 π 结（π-junction）区；共振时两个夹持阈值重合，仅单一分支色散区间消失，腿间耦合超过共振夹持阈值后两支均与相位无关，剩余有限能态产生 0 型耦合。在 φ=π 时得到任意有限段长度下精确夹持恒等式；非共振四阶谐波为负，孤立共振在共振夹持区内留下正残余谐波。

Conclusion: 正常段的能级共振不仅增强约瑟夫森透射，更关键的是改变了参与约瑟夫森耦合的低能支构成，由此实现对耦合符号及谐波符号的调控，说明分支选择是理解该类杂化结构中 0–π 转变（0–π transition）的核心机制。

Abstract: We investigate Josephson transport through a finite normal segment connecting two Kitaev-ladder superconductors and show that discrete-level detuning controls the sign of the equilibrium Josephson coupling through a branch-selection mechanism. Away from resonance, detuning separates two competing low-energy branches, allowing inter-leg hybridization to clamp the $0$-phase-favoring branch while leaving the $π$-favoring branch dispersive. The resulting negative first harmonic evolves from a branch-switching response at weak coupling into a smooth $π$-junction regime. At resonance the two clamping thresholds coincide, so the finite interval in which only one branch remains dispersive disappears. Once the inter-leg coupling exceeds the resonant clamping threshold, both low-energy branches become independent of the Josephson phase. The remaining finite-energy states then generate a $0$-type Josephson coupling. At $φ=π$, the helicity decomposition yields an exact clamping identity for arbitrary finite normal-segment length. Within the controlled sweet-point finite-segment theory, serial propagation fixes the off-resonant fourth-order harmonic to be negative, whereas an isolated resonance leaves a positive residual harmonic within the resonant clamping regime. Numerical calculations demonstrate that the resonance-induced suppression persists away from the solvable phase choice. Thus normal-level resonance does not merely enhance Josephson transmission; it changes which low-energy branches contribute to the Josephson coupling.

</details>


### [241] [Quantum state tomography through multi-frequency Josephson effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24391)
*Xian-Peng Zhang,Ting-Rui Hu,Zhen-Biao Yang,Rubén Seoane Souto,Yugui Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文通过分析有限动量超导体与量子点约瑟夫森结等超导纳米器件的时间演化，解析推导出源于多体本征态间量子干涉的宏观超电流（dynamic interference supercurrent），并指出其由φ空间Berry联络支配，可用于Andreev自旋量子比特的量子纠缠测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宏观量子效应（macroscopic quantum effects）如何在可见尺度上由无数微观量子态的相干行为集体涌现，其微观波函数层面的起源尚待阐明；特别是有限动量库珀对（finite-momentum Cooper pairs）所载超电流的机制需要严格分析。

Method: 严格分析超导纳米器件（包括有限动量超导体与量子点约瑟夫森结（quantum-dot Josephson junctions））的时间演化，解析推导宏观超电流，并将其归因于不同多体本征态之间的量子干涉；进一步以双Andreev自旋量子比特（Andreev spin qubits）系统为对象，用φ空间Berry联络（Berry connection）刻画本征函数对超导相位φ变化的响应。

Result: 推导出普遍存在的动态干涉超电流（dynamic interference supercurrent），其在无外加电压下于双Andreev自旋量子比特系统中表现出多频约瑟夫森效应（multi-frequency Josephson effect），并由C_{rr'}=⟨r|∂_φ|r'⟩所描述的Berry联络决定；该超电流是叠加态本身的内禀属性，可实现对该量子比特的先进量子纠缠测量（quantum entanglement measurement）。

Conclusion: 研究结果提供了从波函数层面研究超导纳米器件性质的新途径，对固态超导器件中量子计算（quantum computer）、通信、存储与传感等量子科学与技术的发展具有重要启示。

Abstract: Macroscopic quantum effects emerge when the coherent behavior of countless microscopic quantum states manifests collectively at a visible scale, as exemplified by the supercurrent carried by innumerable finite-momentum Cooper pairs. By rigorously analyzing the time evolution of superconducting nanodevices including finite-momentum superconductors and quantum-dot Josephson junctions, we analytically derive the ubiquitous macroscopic supercurrent that originates from quantum interference between distinct many-body eigenstates, which we term as the dynamic interference supercurrent. The dynamic interference supercurrent in a system of two Andreev spin qubits exhibits multi-frequency Josephson effect in the absence of external voltage and is governed by the response of the many-body eigenfunctions ($\vert r\rangle$) to variations in the superconducting phase $φ$, determined by the $φ$-space Berry connection $C_{rr'}=\langle r|\partial_φ|r'\rangle$. The macroscopic dynamic interference supercurrent, an inherent property of the superposition state itself, enables an advanced quantum entanglement measurement of the Andreev spin qubits. These findings open new avenues for investigating superconducting nanodevice properties from a wave-function level, with substantial implications for advancing quantum science and technology in solid-state superconducting devices, such as quantum computer, communication, memory, and sensor.

</details>


### [242] [Second Chern Number Protected Gapless Vortex Modes in Superconducting Weyl Semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24401)
*Zhicheng Zhang,Jiangping Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究时间反演对称的超导线性与二次Weyl半金属中的涡旋束缚态，发现每个Weyl点周围存在$\vert n\chi\vert$个无能隙涡旋模，并给出其拓扑刻画与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解超导Weyl半金属中涡旋（vortex）束缚态的形成机制及其拓扑性质，特别是涡旋涡量（vorticity）与Weyl点手性（chirality）如何共同决定无能隙模的数目。

Method: 对具有时间反演对称性（time reversal symmetry）的超导线性与二次Weyl半金属进行解析研究，并通过格点模型（lattice model）的数值计算进行验证；同时基于有效BdG哈密顿量（BdG Hamiltonian）在合成四维空间中定义第二陈数（second Chern number）来刻画无能隙模。

Result: 对于涡量$n$、Weyl点手性$\chi$的情形，每个Weyl点位置附近存在$\vert n\chi\vert$个无能隙涡旋模；该现象源于$k_z$与$-k_z$处所获Chern绝缘体（Chern insulator）之间通量束缚态（flux bound states）的不平衡泵浦（imbalanced pumping）；数值计算证实了该物理图像，且这些无能隙模对破坏旋转对称性（rotational symmetry）的项具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 超导Weyl半金属中Weyl点附近的无能隙涡旋模由涡量与手性的乘积决定，可由合成四维空间的第二陈数刻画，并受拓扑保护而对抗旋转对称性破缺扰动。

Abstract: We have studied the vortex bound states of superconducting linear and quadratic Weyl semimetals with time reversal symmetry. For vortex with vorticity $n$ (vortex line along $z$ direction) and Weyl semimetals with Weyl point chirality $χ$, there are $\vert nχ\vert$ gapless vortex modes around the location of each Weyl point in superconducting Weyl semimetals. We show that the appearance of $\vert nχ\vert$ gapless vortex modes originates from the imbalanced pumping of flux bound states between Chern insulators obtained at $k_{z}$ and $-k_{z}$ in Weyl semimetals. Such a physical picture is verified by numerical calculations on lattice models. Moreover, the gapless modes around each Weyl point can be characterized by the second Chern number defined in a synthetic four-dimensional space based on the effective BdG Hamiltonian. Finally, these gapless vortex modes are found to be robust against rotational symmetry-breaking terms.

</details>


### [243] [Strain-Tunable High-$T_c$ Superconductivity in Layered R3m B$_2$CN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24799)
*Durjay Dhali,Arafat Rahman,Tareq Mahmud,Alamgir Kabir*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 利用密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT）与Wannier插值的EPW计算表明，0–6%各向同性拉伸应变（isotropic tensile strain）可同时窄化费米面处B:2p/C:2p能带并软化强耦合声子支，使电声耦合常数λ从0.967升至2.620，超导转变温度Tc由53.5 K单调升至84.5 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究应变调控对电声耦合（electron–phonon coupling）强度及超导转变温度（Tc）的影响，验证拉伸应变作为提升Tc手段的可行性。

Method: 采用密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT）与Wannier插值矩阵元，在EPW框架下计算；检验Born–Huang力学与动力学稳定性判据；求解完全各向异性Migdal–Eliashberg方程得到Tc，并与EPW中基于SISSO的机器学习模型结果对比。

Result: λ由0.967增至2.620；四种应变构型均满足Born–Huang判据、无虚频声子模；各向异性Migdal–Eliashberg方程给出的Tc从53.5 K单调升至84.5 K，在6%应变处越过液氮沸点；SISSO机器学习模型与Migdal–Eliashberg的Tc在整条应变序列上偏差在10 K以内。

Conclusion: 各向同性拉伸应变通过能带窄化与声子软化协同增强电声耦合，可显著提升Tc；SISSO机器学习模型可作为各向异性Migdal–Eliashberg计算的高效近似替代。

Abstract: Using DFPT and Wannier-interpolated matrix elements in EPW, we find that isotropic tensile strain of 0--6\% narrows the B:2$p$/C:2$p$ bands at the Fermi level while softening the phonon branches most strongly coupled to them, driving the coupling constant $λ$ from 0.967 to 2.620. All four strained configurations remain mechanically and dynamically stable, satisfying the Born--Huang criteria with no imaginary phonon modes at any strain level. The fully anisotropic Migdal--Eliashberg equations give a $T_c$ that rises monotonically from 53.5~K to 84.5~K across the strain series, crossing the liquid-nitrogen boiling point at 6\% strain. The SISSO-based machine-learning model in EPW agrees with the anisotropic Migdal--Eliashberg $T_c$ values to within 10~K across the strain series.

</details>


### [244] [Vortexable Chern Bands Sit Exactly on the Topological Floor of Flat-Band Superfluid Stiffness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24877)
*Zhi-Wei Wang,Samuel L. Braunstein*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 涡旋性陈能带（vortexable Chern bands）通过轨道对角吸引与时间反演配对后，在固定陈数、填充与配对幅度下构成刚度最低的平带超导体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨涡旋性陈能带用于实现理想分数量子陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulators），并研究其时间反演配对后超导刚度的下界问题。

Method: 对均匀配对与非简并配对态，推导零温（T=0）刚度行列式的精确表达式，将其表示为拓扑下界乘以修正因子，修正因子由偏离涡旋性的量（Torb、TLVP）度量。

Result: 刚度行列式等于拓扑下界乘以 1+(Torb/2π|C|)^2，且该值不超过 1+(TLVP/2π|C|)^2；即涡旋性陈能带在相同条件下具有最小刚度。

Conclusion: 刚度增强须以牺牲涡旋性（vortexability）为代价；不等式取严格不等号仅当内部各分量共享同一格点，例如异质应变双层（heterostrained bilayers）体系。

Abstract: Vortexable Chern bands host ideal fractional Chern insulators. Paired with time-reversed partners by orbital-diagonal on-site attraction, they are the least stiff flat-band superconductors at fixed Chern number $C$, filling, and pairing amplitude. For uniform pairing and a nondegenerate paired state, the exact $T=0$ stiffness determinant is the topological floor times $1+(\Torb/2π|C|)^2\le1+(\TLVP/2π|C|)^2$, where $\Torb$ and $\TLVP$ measure departures from vortexability. Enhancement costs vortexability, and the inequality is strict only if internal components share a site, as in heterostrained bilayers.

</details>


### [245] [Thermal Responses of Spin Density and Spin Current Induced by Bogoliubov Fermi Surfaces in Proximity Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25478)
*Yingwen Zhang,Cong Xiao,Xiao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作研究Fe(Te,Se)类s波近邻超导系统中Bogoliubov费米面（Bogoliubov Fermi surfaces）上的温度梯度驱动自旋极化与自旋流响应，发现其由贝里曲率（Berry curvature）及自旋相关混合贝里曲率导致，并在低温下随温度线性增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: s波近邻超导系统中的Bogoliubov费米面为在自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）下产生非平凡自旋响应提供了独特平台，但其热驱动自旋响应的机制尚待阐明。

Method: 针对与Fe(Te,Se)相关的近邻超导系统，分析面内磁化（in-plane magnetization）、超导与向列性（nematicity）的相互作用所诱导的贝里曲率与自旋相关混合贝里曲率，并据此计算温度梯度驱动的非平衡自旋极化与自旋能斯特效应（spin Nernst effect）。

Result: Bogoliubov费米面上出现的贝里曲率与自旋相关混合贝里曲率可导致有限的热驱动非平衡自旋极化与自旋能斯特效应；这些响应因Bogoliubov费米面的出现而显著增强，并在低温下与温度成线性关系。

Conclusion: Bogoliubov费米面赋予该近邻超导系统独特的热自旋电子学（spintronic）特性，其温度梯度驱动响应可作为该类系统自旋输运的判别性特征。

Abstract: The emergence of Bogoliubov Fermi surfaces in s-wave proximity superconducting systems provides a unique platform for generating nontrivial spin responses in the presence of spin-orbit coupling. In this work, we investigate temperature-gradient driven responses of spin polarization and spin current in such a proximity system related to Fe(Te,Se). The interplay of in-plane magnetization, superconductivity, and nematicity gives rise to nontrivial Berry curvature and spin-related hybrid Berry curvature on the Bogoliubov Fermi surfaces, which are shown to lead to finite thermally driven nonequilibrium spin polarization and spin Nernst effect. We find that the responses are strongly enhanced by the emergence of Bogoliubov Fermi surfaces, and are linear in temperature at low temperatures, rendering a unique spintronic character of Bogoliubov Fermi surfaces.

</details>


### [246] [Effects of Nonmagnetic Impurities on Nonequilibrium Phase Transitions in Microwave-Driven Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25859)
*Taira Kawamura*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 基于Floquet-Nambu-Keldysh格林函数与自洽Born近似，研究了含非磁性杂质散射与能量弛豫的微波驱动BCS超导体的非平衡稳态，发现非平衡相图强烈依赖杂质散射强度，并出现两类双稳态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡态s波超导对非磁性杂质散射具有鲁棒性（Anderson定理式行为），而微波驱动下的非平衡超导相变是否同样不受非磁性杂质影响尚不明确，本文旨在厘清杂质散射在非平衡稳态相图中的角色。

Method: 采用Floquet-Nambu-Keldysh格林函数方法（Floquet-Nambu-Keldysh Green's function method）结合自洽Born近似（self-consistent Born approximation），在含非磁性杂质散射与能量弛豫的条件下自洽求解微波驱动稳态的超导序参量。

Result: 非平衡相图强烈依赖杂质散射强度：无杂质或弱散射时，每个温度与驱动频率下序参量为单值；中等散射强度下出现微波增强态与微波抑制态之间的双稳态（bistability）；散射进一步增强时，双稳态转变为超导态与正常态之间的共存。

Conclusion: 上述演化源于微波驱动两种效应的竞争：经由热激发准粒子再分布实现的Eliashberg型增强（Eliashberg-type enhancement），与对破配对诱导准粒子产生所致的抑制。与平衡态s波超导对非磁性杂质的鲁棒性相反，非磁性杂质散射在决定微波驱动下非平衡超导相变性质中起核心作用。

Abstract: We investigate the nonequilibrium steady states of a microwave-driven BCS superconductor in the presence of nonmagnetic impurity scattering and energy relaxation. Using the Floquet-Nambu-Keldysh Green's function method together with the self-consistent Born approximation, we self-consistently determine the superconducting order parameter in the microwave-driven steady state. We find that the nonequilibrium phase diagram depends strongly on the impurity-scattering strength. When impurity scattering is absent or weak, the steady-state order parameter is single-valued at each temperature and drive frequency. At intermediate scattering strengths, we find bistability between microwave-enhanced and microwave-suppressed superconducting states. As the impurity-scattering strength increases further, we instead find bistability between the superconducting and normal states. This evolution reflects competition between two effects of microwave driving: Eliashberg-type enhancement through the redistribution of thermally excited quasiparticles and suppression through pair-breaking-induced quasiparticle generation. In contrast to the robustness of equilibrium $s$-wave superconductivity against nonmagnetic impurity scattering, our results demonstrate that nonmagnetic impurity scattering plays a central role in determining the character of nonequilibrium superconducting phase transitions under microwave driving.

</details>


### [247] [Time-Reversal Symmetry Breaking in the Centrosymmetric Nonsymmorphic Superconductor RuB2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26264)
*Ritu Gupta,Reena,Dibyendu Samanta,Jaskaran Singh,Thomas J. Hicken,Hubertus Luetkens,Jonas Krieger,Sudeep Kumar Ghosh,Yogesh Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究结合零场与横场μ子自旋弛豫（muon spin relaxation, muSR）、第一性原理计算与Ginzburg-Landau对称性分析，指出RuB2在Tc=1.6 K以下存在时间反演对称性破缺（time-reversal symmetry breaking, TRSB）与全能隙超导态，并提出内部反对称的非酉自旋三重态（nonunitary spin-triplet state）为候选序参量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中心对称非简单空间群（nonsymmorphic）AB2（A = Ru, Os）硼化物表现出反常超导性质，但其超导基态的微观本质尚未解决，故以RuB2为对象加以探究。

Method: 采用零场与横场muSR实验、第一性原理电子结构计算以及Ginzburg-Landau对称性分析三类手段相互印证。

Result: 零场muSR在Tc=1.6 K以下观测到自发内磁场，为TRSB提供直接证据；横场测量表明超导态为全能隙，能隙大小接近BCS弱耦合极限；计算显示RuB2是多带Z2拓扑金属（topological metal），并具有显著自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）。

Conclusion: Ginzburg-Landau分析表明内部反对称的非酉自旋三重态是候选序参量，与TRSB和全能隙观测均相容；结果虽不能唯一确定序参量，但缩小了候选范围，并提示非简单空间群对称性可能塑造RuB2的超导性。

Abstract: Centrosymmetric nonsymmorphic AB2 (A = Ru, Os) borides exhibit anomalous superconducting properties, but the microscopic nature of their superconducting ground state remains unresolved. Here, we investigate RuB2 using zero- and transverse-field muon spin relaxation (muSR), first-principles electronic-structure calculations, and Ginzburg-Landau symmetry analysis. Zero-field muSR reveals spontaneous internal magnetic fields below Tc = 1.6 K, providing direct evidence for time-reversal symmetry breaking (TRSB). Transverse-field measurements establish a fully gapped superconducting state, with a gap magnitude close to the BCS weak-coupling limit. Density-functional calculations show that RuB2 is a multiband Z2 topological metal with appreciable spin-orbit coupling. Guided by the experimental observations and the symmetry of the orthorhombic crystal structure, our Ginzburg-Landau analysis suggests that an internally antisymmetric, nonunitary spin-triplet state is a promising candidate for the superconducting order parameter. This candidate is consistent with both the observed TRSB and the fully gapped superconducting state. These results establish RuB2 as a promising platform for investigating unconventional superconductivity arising from the interplay of crystal symmetry, multiband electronic structure, and spin-orbit coupling, and motivate further studies of related nonsymmorphic borides. The two muSR measurements provide complementary constraints: zero-field data probe spontaneous magnetism associated with TRSB, while transverse-field data characterize superconducting field distribution and support a full gap. The topological electronic structure and spin-orbit coupling provide context for unconventional pairing. Although these results do not uniquely determine the order parameter, they narrow candidates and highlight that nonsymmorphic symmetry may shape superconductivity in RuB2.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [248] [BeAGLE++: A modern modular framework for eA physics at the EIC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24095)
*Arjun Kumar,Abhay Deshpande,Zhoudunming Tu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: BeAGLE++ 是面向高能轻子-原子核碰撞的下一代通用蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）事件发生器，作为 BeAGLE 的后继版本，引入成区（formation-zone）核内级联与核退激处理，并与 E665 数据及旧版计算结果进行了对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能轻子-核碰撞中末态相互作用（final-state interactions, FSIs）与核破碎的描述存在模型依赖性，需要更精细且模块化的模拟框架，以服务电子-离子对撞机（Electron-Ion Collider, EIC）相关物理研究。

Method: 采用成区核内级联（formation-zone intranuclear cascade）描述强子末态相互作用，随后对残余核进行统计退激（statistical de-excitation）；相较旧版实现，级联中引入离散时间有序（discrete time-ordered）的强子相互作用处理及核内输运中的轻团簇产生（light-cluster production）。

Result: 与 E665 测量及旧版 BeAGLE 计算的对比显示，所选观测量总体吻合，但在对 FSIs 敏感的末态上存在差异；这些差异可用于评估核破碎预言的模型依赖性并约束级联动力学。

Conclusion: BeAGLE++ 以模块化架构（modular architecture）为 EIC 提供灵活的模拟框架，并可作为在现有数据与未来 EIC 测量下开发与检验新物理模型的平台。

Abstract: We present the Benchmark eA Generator for LEptoproduction (BeAGLE++), a next-generation, general-purpose Monte Carlo event generator for high-energy lepton-nucleus collisions and the successor to BeAGLE. The framework incorporates a formation-zone intranuclear cascade to describe hadronic final-state interactions (FSIs), followed by statistical de-excitation of the residual nucleus. Compared with the legacy implementation, the BeAGLE++ cascade introduces a discrete time-ordered treatment of hadronic interactions and light-cluster production during intranuclear transport. Comparisons with E665 measurements and legacy BeAGLE calculations show broad agreement for selected observables, while revealing differences in final states sensitive to FSIs. These differences provide opportunities to assess the model dependence of nuclear-breakup predictions and constrain the underlying cascade dynamics. With its modular architecture, BeAGLE++ provides a flexible simulation framework for the Electron-Ion Collider (EIC) and a platform for developing and testing new physics models against existing data and future EIC measurements.

</details>


### [249] [Collider event distances from the phase space sphere](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24319)
*Nathaniel Craig,Brian Henning,Andrew J. Larkoski,Iñigo Valenzuela Lombera*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于距离的分类方法，利用洛伦兹不变相空间（Lorentz-invariant phase space）的全局拓扑结构并保留重建质量信息，用于区分产生对撞事件的物理过程；并给出多项式时间（polynomial-time）近似算法以处理高事件分辨率的困难任务，在多个模拟基准上取得良好判别效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：相空间中对撞机事件（collider events）的分布能否用于区分产生这些事件的物理过程；对于分类难度高、需要更高事件分辨率（event resolution）的任务，精确距离计算代价过大。

Method: 提出距离驱动的分类方法，基于洛伦兹不变相空间的全局拓扑结构构建事件间距离并保留重建质量（reconstructed masses）；进一步发展多项式时间近似来近似计算该距离，以应对高分辨率分类任务。

Result: 所得分类器（classifiers）在多个模拟基准（simulated benchmarks）上实现较强判别能力，包括全强子（fully hadronic）四顶夸克产生（four-top production）与 t tbar + jets 的区分。

Conclusion: 结果表明相空间事件的整体拓扑与质量信息可用于有效的过程判别，且所提出的多项式时间近似使该类方法在高分辨率任务上具备可行性。

Abstract: Can the distribution of collider events in phase space be used to distinguish the processes that produced them? We present a distance-based classification method that uses the global topology of Lorentz-invariant phase space and retains reconstructed masses. To tackle particularly challenging classification tasks that may benefit from higher event resolution, we develop a polynomial-time approximation to compute the distances. The resulting classifiers achieve strong discrimination across several simulated benchmarks, including fully hadronic four-top production against $t\bar t+\text{jets}$.

</details>


### [250] [Common origin of the Belle-BESIII line-shape difference of $Λ_c^+\barΛ_c^-$, $D_s D_{s1}$ and $D_s D_{s2}^*$ from $e^+ e^-$ annihilation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24634)
*Cheng-Xi Liu,Tian-Le Gao,Cheng-Ping Shen,Xiang Liu*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出基于高激发粲偶素（higher charmonia）辐射产生的机制，用以统一解释Belle与BESIII在三个遍举过程线形上的长期分歧，并顺带给出在Belle II寻找缺失粲偶素态的途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Belle与BESIII在e^+e^-→Λ_c^+ Λ̄_c^-、D_sD_{s1}(2536)与D_sD_{s2}^*(2573)线形上存在长期未解的分歧（discrepancy）：Belle观测到增强结构，而BESIII中缺失或显著偏弱，缺乏统一解释。

Method: 提出高激发粲偶素（higher charmonia）辐射产生机制，认为此类过程可混入Belle的初始态辐射（initial-state radiation, ISR）样本，并经光度归一化（luminosity normalization）放大其在截面提取中的贡献；以BESIII数据标定矢量（vector）贡献后，引入赝标量（pseudoscalar）η_c(6S)与χ_c2(5P)组分拟合增强结构。

Result: 在矢量贡献按BESIII数据缩放的前提下，η_c(6S)贡献可描述重子道的增强，而引入的χ_c2(5P)贡献可同时描述两个介子道的增强。

Conclusion: 该机制为上述分歧提供了可能的共同解释，并提示可在Belle II搜寻尚未发现的高激发粲偶素态。

Abstract: The longstanding Belle--BESIII discrepancies in the $e^+e^-\toΛ_c^+\barΛ_c^-$, $D_sD_{s1}(2536)$, and $D_sD_{s2}^{*}(2573)$ line shapes remain an unresolved puzzle, with enhancements observed by Belle that are absent or much weaker in the BESIII measurements. In this work, we propose a new mechanism based on the radiative production of higher charmonia to explain these differences. Such processes may contribute to Belle's initial-state radiation samples, and the luminosity normalization increases their contribution to the extracted cross sections. With the vector contributions scaled by BESIII data, a pseudoscalar charmonium $η_c(6S)$ contribution can describe the baryonic enhancement, while an introduced $χ_{c2}(5P)$ contribution can describe the enhancements in both meson channels. This mechanism offers a possible common explanation for these differences and, as a bonus, suggests a way to search for missing higher charmonia at Belle II.

</details>


### [251] [Global analyses of PDFs in the precision era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24691)
*Juan M. Cruz-Martinez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该摘要指出，部分子分布函数（parton distribution functions, PDFs）通过不断扩充的数据集进行全局拟合（global fits）确定，理论精度已从约20年前的覆盖不完整的次次次领头阶（NNLO）提升至当今的近似 N³LO 全局拟合，并强调拟合协作组需在复杂计算与数据分析之间保持一致性，以确保所报告的 PDF 不确定度带（uncertainty bands）依然可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着固定靶实验与 HERA 的深度非弹性散射（deep-inelastic scattering）、以及 Tevatron 与 LHC 的强子对撞（hadron collisions）数据不断增加，PDF 全局拟合所用数据集持续扩张，因而需要在更高理论精度下重新审视拟合与不确定度评估的可靠性。

Method: 采用对日益增多的实验数据进行全局拟合（global fits）的方式确定 PDFs，并在微扰精度上从约20年前部分覆盖的 NNLO 提升到当前的近似 N³LO 计算。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值结果，仅说明理论精度由部分 NNLO 覆盖提升至近似 N³LO 全局拟合，且数据集范围已扩展至固定靶深度非弹性散射、HERA、Tevatron 与 LHC 强子对撞数据。

Conclusion: 拟合协作组（fitting collaborations）必须协调复杂的理论计算与数据分析，同时确保所给出的 PDF 不确定度带仍然可靠。

Abstract: Parton distribution functions or PDFs are determined through global fits to an ever-increasing array of data, including deep-inelastic scattering at fixed-target experiments and HERA, and hadron collisions at the Tevatron and LHC. As the dataset has expanded, theoretical accuracy has improved from partial next-to-next-to-leading order (NNLO) coverage about 20 years ago to approximate N$^3$LO global fits today. The fitting collaborations must reconcile these complex calculations and data analyses while ensuring that quoted PDF uncertainty bands remain reliable.

</details>


### [252] [Where are the higher excited $B$ and $B_s$ mesons with coupled-channel effects?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25125)
*Ru-Hui Ni,Jia-Jun Wu,Xian-Hui Zhong*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于新观测的 $B_{s0}^*(5700)^0$ 与既往无淬火预测的吻合，本文在同一无淬火夸克模型（unquenched quark model）中系统计算了更高激发态（$3S$、$2P$、$2D$、$1F$ 波）$B$ 与 $B_s$ 介子的质量谱及 OZI 允许的两体强衰变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新观测的 $B_{s0}^*(5700)^0$ 质量与作者此前对 $B_s(1^3P_0)$ 的无淬火预测高度一致，为检验耦合道效应（coupled-channel effects）在重轻介子谱中的作用提供了契机，并促使对更高激发态进行统一研究。

Method: 采用与先前预测相同的无淬火夸克模型，计算 $3S$、$2P$、$2D$、$1F$ 波 $B$ 与 $B_s$ 介子的质量谱，并计算其 OZI 允许的两体强衰变（OZI-allowed two-body strong decays）。

Result: 耦合道效应显著降低质量并改变能级间距：标量态 $B(2^3P_0)$ 质量被大幅下移、主要衰变为 $B\pi$；矢量态 $B(2^3D_1)$ 与 $B(3^3S_1)$ 的质量次序发生反转，较轻的 $B(2^3D_1)$ 主要衰变为 $B_1(5721)\pi$；部分更高激发态被预言为较窄，可在 $B^{(*)}\pi$、$B^{(*)}K$、$B_1(5721)\pi/K$、$B_2^*(5747)\pi/K$ 道中寻找。

Conclusion: 结果为未来实验搜寻提供参考，并有助于厘清耦合道效应在重轻介子谱中的作用；建议优先在 $B\pi$ 与 $B_1(5721)\pi$ 道中分别寻找 $B(2^3P_0)$ 与 $B(2^3D_1)$。

Abstract: Inspired by the newly observed $B_{s0}^*(5700)^0$, whose mass agrees well with our previous unquenched prediction for $B_s(1^3P_0)$ [Phys.~Rev.~D \textbf{109}, 116006 (2024)], we study the mass spectra and OZI-allowed two-body strong decays of the higher $3S$-, $2P$-, $2D$-, and $1F$-wave $B$ and $B_s$ mesons in the same unquenched quark model. Our results show that coupled-channel effects significantly lower the masses and modify the relative level spacings. The scalar state $B(2^3P_0)$ is strongly affected: its mass is lowered well below the quenched quark model predictions, and it mainly decays into $Bπ$. Furthermore, the mass ordering of the vector states $B(2^3D_1)$ and $B(3^3S_1)$ is reversed, and the lighter $B(2^3D_1)$ state mainly decays into $B_1(5721)π$. To test these results, the $B(2^3P_0)$ and $B(2^3D_1)$ states are worth searching for in the $Bπ$ and $B_1(5721)π$ channels, respectively. In addition, some higher excitations are predicted to be relatively narrow and can be searched for in the $B^{(*)}π$, $B^{(*)}K$, $B_1(5721)π/K$, and $B_2^*(5747)π/K$ channels. These predictions provide useful references for future experimental searches and help clarify the role of coupled-channel effects in the heavy-light meson spectrum.

</details>


### [253] [Is there gluonium in the $η^\prime$? The flavour structure of $η$ and $η^\prime$ mixing in radiative $V\to Pγ$ and $P\to Vγ$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25216)
*Rafel Escribano,Emilio Royo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文检验辐射衰变 $V\to P\gamma$、$P\to V\gamma$ 中的重叠因子（overlap factors）是否可改由 η、η′ 与一个额外赝标量态混合来解释，采用四基态间的 $SO(4)$ 转动做全局拟合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统描述以组分夸克质量差（constituent-quark mass differences）与唯象波函数重叠因子作为味对称性破缺（flavour-symmetry breaking）来源，其物理起源不明，故探讨重叠因子能否被理解为态混合效应。

Method: 将 η、η′ 与一个额外赝标量态（additional pseudoscalar state）在四个基态间以 $SO(4)$ 转动（$SO(4)$ rotation）描述混合，对当前数据进行全局拟合（global fit），并与不含重叠因子的三态极限（three-state limit）比较。

Result: 拟合显著优于无重叠因子的三态极限，同时保持既有的 η-η′ 与矢量介子混合角（vector-meson mixing angles）；结果显示额外态主要与非奇异组分（non-strange component）混合，且传统重叠因子描述恰为该混合纯非奇异时的特例。

Conclusion: 非奇异混合模式既不利于味单态（flavour-singlet）掺入（如赝标量胶球（pseudoscalar glueball）混合所预期），也不支持以往分析的胶子球（gluonium）情形；辐射衰变未提供 η′ 中含胶子球的有力证据，重叠因子更适合解释为径向激发的非奇异态（radially excited non-strange state）。

Abstract: Radiative $V\to Pγ$ and $P\to Vγ$ decays are conventionally described using constituent-quark mass differences and phenomenological wavefunction-overlap factors as sources of flavour-symmetry breaking. We investigate whether the overlap factors can instead be understood as the effect of mixing of the $η$ and $η^\prime$ with an additional pseudoscalar state, described by an $SO(4)$ rotation among four basis states. A global fit to current data yields a significantly better description than the corresponding three-state limit without overlap factors, while preserving the established $η$-$η^\prime$ and vector-meson mixing angles, and shows that the additional state mixes predominantly with the non-strange component. The conventional overlap-factor description is recovered exactly as the special case in which this mixing is purely non-strange. The non-strange pattern disfavours both a flavour-singlet admixture, as expected from mixing with a pseudoscalar glueball, and the gluonium scenario considered in previous analyses. Radiative decays therefore provide no compelling evidence for gluonium in the $η^\prime$, and the overlap factors are better interpreted in terms of a radially excited non-strange state.

</details>


### [254] [Probing the two-pole structure of the $D_0^*(2300)$ with an $SU(3)$ flavor filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26175)
*Ying-Bo He,Liu-Lin Wang,Xiao-Hai Liu,Qiang Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出利用 \(SU(3)\) 味道过滤（flavor-filtering）策略，通过不同味道表示选择性的产生源（强衰变与弱衰变过程）区分 \(D_0^*(2300)\) 的双极点（two-pole）结构，并以不变质量谱的互补线型特征检验其味道六重态（sextet）成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: \(D_0^*(2300)\) 存在与双极点结构相关的疑难，且其味道六重态成分难以直接探测；需要一种能在实验不变质量分布中区分两极点并甄别味道表示的策略。

Method: 在单位化手征动力学（unitarized chiral dynamics）框架下，指出双极点主要源于耦合 \(\mathcal{D}\Phi\) 系统的不同不可约表示；并考察强衰变 \(\Upsilon\to\bar\Xi_{c}^{(\prime)}\Lambda(1520)\mathcal{D}\Phi\) 与弱衰变 \(\Xi_{b}^{(\prime)}\to\pi^-\Lambda(1520)\mathcal{D}\Phi\) 作为近似反三重态（antitriplet）与六重态选择性的产生源。

Result: 预测的 \(D\pi\)、\(D\eta\)、\(D_s\bar K\) 谱呈现互补特征：约 \(2.45\ \mathrm{GeV}\) 的高极点产生 \(D\eta\) 与 \(D_s\bar K\) 阈值间的特征增强及六重态偏好道中的近阈结构，而约 \(2.1\ \mathrm{GeV}\) 的低极点主导反三重态偏好道；线型差异直接反映相应级联重子标记所选择的味表示。

Conclusion: \(\Upsilon\) 模式提供可实验检验的观测量，用于确立 \(D_0^*(2300)\) 的双极点性质并识别以六重态成分为主的粲介子共振；弱衰变分析则用于检验线型模式对产生机制变化的稳健性。

Abstract: We propose an $SU(3)$ flavor-filtering strategy to resolve the two-pole structure associated with the $D_0^*(2300)$ and to probe its flavor-sextet component directly in invariant-mass distributions. In unitarized chiral dynamics, the two poles originate predominantly from different irreducible representations of the coupled $\mathcal{D}Φ$ system. We show that the strong decays $Υ\to\barΞ_{c}^{(\prime)}Λ(1520)\mathcal{D}Φ$ and the weak decays $Ξ_{b}^{(\prime)}\toπ^-Λ(1520)\mathcal{D}Φ$ provide approximately antitriplet- and sextet-selective production sources. The predicted $Dπ$, $Dη$, and $D_s\bar K$ spectra exhibit complementary signatures: the higher pole near $2.45~\mathrm{GeV}$ generates a characteristic enhancement between the $Dη$ and $D_s\bar K$ thresholds and a pronounced near-threshold structure in the sextet-favored channels, whereas the lower pole near $2.1~\mathrm{GeV}$ dominates the antitriplet-favored channels. The contrast between these line shapes directly reflects the flavor representation selected by the corresponding cascade-baryon tag. The $Υ$ modes therefore provide experimentally testable observables for establishing the two-pole nature of the $D_0^*(2300)$ and for identifying a charmed-meson resonance with a dominant flavor-sextet component, while the weak-decay analysis tests the robustness of the line-shape pattern against changes in the production mechanism.

</details>


### [255] [The NLL/NLO$^+$ QCD spectrum from Higgs-plus-charm correlations at the LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26373)
*Francesco Giovanni Celiberto,Francesca Lonigro*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在 √s=14 TeV 的 LHC 上，采用 NLL/NLO⁺ 混合因子化（hybrid factorization）研究伴随单粲强子产生的希格斯玻色子（Higgs boson）相关观测量，并检验高能分布对能标变化的稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对希格斯玻色子与单粲强子（singly charmed hadron）联合产生敏感的高能行为，并提供次领头修正的典型唯象应用，以评估高能 QCD 下希格斯产生的精确研究路径。

Method: 以 NLL/NLO⁺ 混合因子化（hybrid factorization）为参考框架，在标准共线图像（collinear picture）基础上加入次领头级及更高阶的能量对数（energy logarithms）重求和；采用多模块 JETHAD 接口实现的单离壳希格斯发射函数（singly off-shell Higgs emission function）次领头修正。

Result: 所得到的高能分布对能标变化（energy-scale variations）呈现自然稳定性，并据此评估缺失高阶不确定性（missing higher-order uncertainties）的权重；同时以轻味强子（light-flavored hadrons）作为对照。

Conclusion: 结果的自然稳定性为高能次领头阶 QCD（next-to-leading-order QCD）框架下希格斯产生的精确研究奠定基础。

Abstract: We investigate the high-energy behavior of observables sensitive to the production of a Higgs boson in association with a singly charmed hadron at the $\sqrt{s}=14$ TeV LHC, with light-flavored hadrons considered for comparison. Our reference formalism is the hybrid factorization at NLL/NLO$^+$, where the standard collinear picture is enhanced by the resummation of energy logarithms at the next-to-leading level and beyond. We present a prime phenomenological application of the next-to-leading correction to the singly off-shell Higgs emission function, as implemented in the multimodular JETHAD interface, a core element to test the stability of our high-energy distributions under energy-scale variations and to assess the weight of missing higher-order uncertainties. The resulting natural stability traces our path towards precision studies of Higgs production within high-energy next-to-leading-order QCD.

</details>


### [256] [HERA at low-x](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26393)
*A M Cooper-Sarkar,P R Newman*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文综述HERA深度非弹性散射（deep inelastic scattering, DIS）数据，重点讨论低x与低Q²区强子结构的QCD（量子色动力学, Quantum Chromodynamics）描述，并探讨胶子饱和（gluon saturation）的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HERA高能环加速器提供了低x、低Q²区深度非弹性散射的丰富实验数据，需要系统梳理这些数据对部分子分布函数（Parton Distribution Function）及QCD演化图像（包括DGLAP与BFKL）的检验意义。

Method: 介绍夸克部分子模型（Quark Parton Model）及其在微扰QCD中的扩展，定义部分子分布函数；综述HERA的包容（inclusive）与半包容（semi-inclusive）过程数据，并以传统DGLAP演化及低x区BFKL演化进行理论解读，进而讨论可能导致胶子饱和的新非线性区域。

Result: 低x、低Q²区数据在DGLAP与BFKL框架下得到解读，并提示可能出现新的非线性演化区域；在半准弹性（quasi-elastic）与衍射解离（diffractive dissociation）等衍射过程中，发现了更为明确的饱和迹象。

Conclusion: HERA数据整体上可由传统微扰QCD演化描述，但低x区的衍射过程为胶子饱和这一非线性新区域提供了更强提示，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: The field of Deep Inelastic Scattering is introduced with particular reference to the High Energy Ring Accelerator HERA. The formalism of the Quark Parton Model and its extension in perturbative Quantum Chromodynamics is outlined, defining the concept of a Parton Distribution Function. The HERA data on inclusive and semi-inclusive processes are reviewed with particular emphasis on results at low x and low Q 2 . Their theoretical imterpretation in terms of conventional QCD DGLAP evolution and its extension to BFKL evolution in the low-x regime is explored together with the possibility of the onset of a new non-linear regime which may lead to gluon saturation. The focus then moves to diffractive processes both quasi-elastic and diffractive dissociation, where more detailed hints of saturation are found.

</details>


### [257] [First observation of weak magnetism in neutron decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26792)
*Miriam Camarasa-Domene,Martín González-Alonso,Martín Novoa-Brunet*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作通过对β不对称度（β asymmetry）与中子寿命、超允许跃迁ℱt值联合分析，打破弱磁形状因子g_M与轴矢-矢量比λ的简并，首次在自由中子衰变中观测到弱磁（weak magnetism）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱磁效应（weak magnetism）由Gell-Mann提出，是守恒矢量流（conserved vector current, CVC）假设的标志性结果，但在最简单的β衰变系统即自由中子衰变中一直未被观测到。原因在于可观测的β不对称度（β asymmetry）无法将弱磁形状因子g_M与轴矢-矢量比（axial-to-vector ratio）λ区分开，存在简并。

Method: 将β不对称度测量与中子寿命（neutron lifetime）以及超允许0⁺→0⁺跃迁的ℱt值（superallowed 0⁺→0⁺ decays）联合分析，以打破g_M与λ之间的简并；并进一步评估在现有实验条件下利用不对称度能量依赖性（energy dependence of the asymmetry）对g_M提取的改进潜力。

Result: 现有数据给出g_M = 4.47(87)，与CVC预言的4.71一致，并在5.1σ水平上排除弱磁缺失假设；在所考虑的中子数据集合各种变动下（尽管数据内部存在已知张力），显著性仍保持在5.0σ至6.1σ之间。此外，对不对称度能量依赖性的完整分析在现有实验条件下只能将g_M提取精度改善几个百分点。

Conclusion: 该结果构成自由中子衰变中弱磁效应的首次观测，距Gell-Mann的预言已近七十年，为CVC假设提供了直接的实验验证。

Abstract: Weak magnetism, introduced by Gell-Mann as a hallmark consequence of the conserved vector current (CVC) hypothesis, has remained unobserved in the decay of the free neutron, the simplest $β$-decay system. The reason is that the relevant observable, the $β$ asymmetry, cannot separate the weak-magnetism form factor $g_M$ from the axial-to-vector ratio $λ$. We show that this degeneracy is lifted by combining the $β$ asymmetry measurements with the neutron lifetime and the $\mathcal{F}t$ value of superallowed $0^+\to0^+$ decays. Current data yield $g_M = 4.47(87)$, in agreement with the CVC prediction of $4.71$ and excluding the absence of weak magnetism at the $5.1σ$ level. Importantly, the significance remains between $5.0σ$ and $6.1σ$ for all considered variations of the neutron dataset, despite its well-known internal tensions. We further show that, under present experimental conditions, a full analysis of the energy dependence of the asymmetry would improve the $g_M$ extraction by only a few percent. These results constitute the first observation of weak magnetism in the decay of the free neutron nearly seven decades after Gell-Mann's prediction.

</details>


### [258] [Electric Flux as a Criterion for String Breaking and Schwinger Pair Production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24175)
*Sebastian Grieninger*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作以“中点电场降至半个通量单位”定义弦断裂（string breaking），在无质量Schwinger模型中用真空可观测量与介子质量做能量平衡预测断裂位置，并用纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）的二分不确定性解释断裂点与真空跃迁处的纠缠峰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弦断裂的判据与描述多依赖模型细节，缺乏仅用真空可观测量与介子质量即可确定的定量判据；同时弦断裂与θ-真空跃迁的纠缠结构是否存在统一解释尚不明确。

Method: 以中点电场降至半个通量单位定义弦断裂；在无质量Schwinger模型中用完整弦与屏蔽构型的能量平衡预测该分离点；结合数值计算检验半通量点与两构型等权重的对应关系；用纠缠不对称性（entanglement asymmetry）、Magic鲁棒性（Robustness of Magic）及受限玻尔兹曼机（restricted Boltzmann machine）拟态构造波函数进行分析。

Result: 数值上发现半通量点对应两构型等权重，从而解释纠缠熵峰为二值通量的最大不确定性；同一二值不确定性亦解释真空跃迁处的纠缠峰。断裂附近屏蔽将弦内外部分空间纠缠组织为近最大纠缠比特，近似编码的Bell对记录通量是否被屏蔽；纠缠容量（capacity of entanglement）在两类跃迁点均出现极小值的匹配结构。

Conclusion: 弦断裂与θ-真空跃迁共享以二值通量不确定性为核心的纠缠结构，跃迁以相干方式重排自由度；受限玻尔兹曼机拟态可高保真构造θ-真空与弦波函数。

Abstract: We define string breaking by the midpoint electric field falling to half a flux unit. In the massive Schwinger model, an energy balance between intact string and screened configuration predicts this separation using only vacuum observables and meson masses as input. We observe numerically that this half flux point corresponds to equal weights of the two configurations thus explaining the observed peak of the entanglement entropy as maximal uncertainty of the binary flux. The same binary uncertainty explains the entanglement peak at the vacuum transition. Near breaking, screening organizes part of the spatial entanglement between the string interior and exterior into a nearly maximally entangled bit. Within the selected flux sectors, this approximate encoded Bell pair records whether the flux is screened and nearly separates from the remaining correlations. The vacuum transition exhibits the same structure. The string and the $θ$-vacuum reveal a matching structure of the capacity of entanglement minimum at their transition point, linking their entanglement spectra. The transition rearranges degrees of freedom coherently as revealed by the entanglement asymmetry and Robustness of Magic of disjoint subregions. Finally, we construct the $θ$-vacuum and string wavefunctions with high fidelity using a restricted Boltzmann machine ansatz.

</details>


### [259] [Breaking the axion abundance-isocurvature relation: Scale invariance and high-scale inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24070)
*Mariaveronica De Angelis,Javier Rubio*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在保持经典作用量无量纲（标度不变）的前提下，通过动力场比值设定轴子动能归一化，可打破衰变常数与遗迹丰度/等曲率扰动之间的绑定，从而在高能标暴胀下重新开放经典轴子窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前暴胀错位机制（pre-inflationary misalignment）解释暗物质的QCD轴子受CMB等曲率（isocurvature）限制强约束：同一衰变常数同时控制遗迹丰度与原始轴子涨落，使经典轴子窗口与高能标暴胀相互矛盾。

Method: 在一大类标度不变（scale-invariant）模型中不引入任何有量纲参数，以动力场比值而非固定Peccei-Quinn能标确定轴子动能归一化；场空间度规（field-space metric）的今日值控制丰度、视界穿越时的值控制原始涨落。并在Higgs-Dilaton模型中通过变形Peccei-Quinn动能项实现该机制。

Result: 暴胀期足够大的度规可压低等曲率，使经典轴子窗口在高能标暴胀下成立，且错位角无需调节、衰变常数保持亚普朗克量级。精确标度对称性与Peccei-Quinn对称性还强制非转向（non-turning）暴胀吸引子，守恒超视界曲率扰动并保留单场绝热谱。该显式实现的中子今日衰变常数为超普朗克量级，落入人类学窗口（anthropic window），对应受黑洞超辐射（superradiance）约束的超轻QCD轴子。

Conclusion: 轴子动能归一化的动力学化可在标度不变框架内解耦丰度与等曲率约束，为高能标暴胀与经典轴子暗物质共存提供可行路径，并在Higgs-Dilaton模型中给出与α-attractor及Starobinsky点相容的具体实现。

Abstract: A QCD axion accounting for dark matter through pre-inflationary misalignment is strongly constrained by CMB isocurvature bounds, which place the classic axion window in tension with high-scale inflation. The tension arises because the same decay constant controls both the relic abundance and the primordial axion fluctuations. We show how this link can be broken in a broad class of scale-invariant models, without introducing any dimensionful parameter into the classical action. Rather than imposing a fixed Peccei-Quinn scale, the axion's kinetic normalisation is set by a dynamical field ratio. The resulting field-space metric controls the abundance through its value today and the primordial fluctuations through its value at horizon crossing. A sufficiently large metric during inflation suppresses isocurvature, allowing high-scale inflation in the classic axion window with an untuned misalignment angle and sub-Planckian decay constants. The exact scale and Peccei-Quinn symmetries also enforce a non-turning inflationary attractor, conserving the super-Hubble curvature perturbation and preserving the single-field adiabatic spectrum. We realise the mechanism in the Higgs-Dilaton model by deforming the Peccei-Quinn kinetic term. The same deformation turns the inflaton sector into an $α$-attractor pinned near the Starobinsky point. This explicit realisation has a super-Planckian present decay constant, which places it in the anthropic window, now reopened at high inflationary scales, with an ultralight QCD axion constrained by black-hole superradiance.

</details>


### [260] [Lorentz-Invariance Violation with neutrinos from gamma-ray bursts using IceCube High-Energy Starting Events 12-year dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25214)
*Mathieu Lamoureux,José Manuel Carmona,José Luis Cortés,Ardit Gkioni,Antoine Kouchner,Maykoll A. Reyes*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 利用IceCube 12年高能起始事件数据与746个已知红移伽马射线暴，通过似然分析搜寻洛伦兹不变性破缺（Lorentz-invariance violation, LIV）效应。线性LIV下数据相对纯背景假设有约1.9σ的微弱偏好，最佳拟合LIV能标为5.8×10¹⁷ GeV；未发现二次LIV证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能天体物理中微子（high-energy astrophysical neutrinos）为检验LIV提供独特探针：微小且依赖能量的光速偏差可在宇宙学距离上产生可测时延。研究旨在检验中微子与伽马射线暴（gamma-ray bursts）之间由LIV引起的相关性。

Method: 采用公开的12年IceCube高能起始事件（High-Energy Starting Event）数据集与746个具有红移测量的暴目录，构建似然分析（likelihood analysis），计入中微子方向与能量不确定度、中微子与伽马射线发射间可能的本征时间偏移，以及有效场论（effective field theory）预期的超光速中微子衰变。

Result: 线性LIV情形下数据对纯背景假设呈轻微偏好，p值为3.1%，对应单侧高斯显著性约1.9σ；最佳拟合LIV能标Λ_LIV=5.8×10¹⁷ GeV，且结果实际由亚光速（subluminal）成分主导，因超光速中微子在所分析能量下受LIV诱导衰变强烈抑制。二次LIV无证据。

Conclusion: 所偏好的LIV能标与最显著的中微子—伽马射线暴关联与基于不同统计方法的先前研究一致。结果支持利用互补中微子数据集、尤其是更低能量区间开展进一步搜寻，该区间超光速成分或可观测。

Abstract: High-energy astrophysical neutrinos provide a unique probe of Lorentz-invariance violation (LIV), as small energy-dependent deviations from the speed of light can produce measurable time delays over cosmological distances. We search for LIV-induced correlations between neutrinos and gamma-ray bursts using the public 12-year IceCube High-Energy Starting Event dataset and a catalog of 746 bursts with measured redshifts. We develop a likelihood analysis accounting for neutrino directional and energy uncertainties, a possible intrinsic time offset between neutrino and gamma-ray emission, and the decay of superluminal neutrinos expected within effective field theory. For linear LIV, the data show a mild preference over the background-only hypothesis, with a $p$-value of $3.1\%$, corresponding to a one-sided Gaussian significance of approximately $1.9σ$. The best fit corresponds to a LIV scale $Λ_{\rm LIV}=5.8\times10^{17}\,\mathrm{GeV}$ and is effectively driven by the subluminal component, as superluminal neutrinos are strongly suppressed by LIV-induced decays at the analyzed energies. The preferred LIV scale and the most significant neutrino--gamma-ray burst associations are consistent with previous studies based on different statistical approaches. No evidence for quadratic LIV is found. These results motivate further searches with complementary neutrino datasets, particularly at lower energies, where the superluminal component may become observable.

</details>


### [261] [Analytic results for the two-loop renormalization constants of Feynman gauge QED](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26750)
*Gregory S. Adkins,Elias Mitchell,A. Volkan Turan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文在费曼规范（Feynman gauge）下、采用量纲正则化（dimensional regularization）与在壳方案（on-shell scheme），解析计算了量子电动力学（quantum electrodynamics, QED）的两圈重整化常数（renormalization constants）Z_m 与 Z_1=Z_2，并给出其在量纲正则化参数 ε 中从 1/ε^2 到 ε^5 的展开系数的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QED 与量子色动力学（quantum chromodynamics, QCD）的多圈计算（multiloop calculations）需要高精度的重整化常数及其 ε 展开系数作为输入与校验基准，而现有结果对高阶展开项覆盖不足，故有必要补齐。

Method: 在费曼规范下，使用量纲正则化处理紫外与红外发散，并在在壳重整化方案中计算两圈自能与顶点贡献，从而得到重整化常数 Z_m 与 Z_1=Z_2 的解析结果及其 ε 展开系数。

Result: 获得了 Z_m 与 Z_1=Z_2 的解析表达式，并给出这些常数在 ε 中自 1/ε^2 至 ε^5 各阶展开系数的解析形式。

Conclusion: 所得高阶展开系数可服务于未来 QED 的多圈计算，并为 QCD 多圈计算提供有用的检验（check）依据。

Abstract: The two-loop renormalization constants $Z_m$ and $Z_1\!=\!Z_2$ of Feynman gauge quantum electrodynamics (QED) in the on-shell scheme are computed using dimensional regularization. Analytic results are obtained for these constants. In addition, analytic expressions are obtained for their expansion coefficients in the dimensional regularization parameter $ε$ from order $1/ε^2$ through order $ε^5$. The high-order expansion coefficients will be needed for future multiloop calculations in QED and will provide a useful check for multiloop calculations in QCD.

</details>


### [262] [Gravitational Dark Matter Production in Einstein-Cartan Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26237)
*Kamil Mudrunka,Kazunori Nakayama*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 将挠率（torsion）引入广义相对论（General Relativity）会产生对费米子（fermions）的普适四点相互作用，从而不可避免地影响费米子暗物质（fermion dark matter）。本文在爱因斯坦-嘉当理论（Einstein-Cartan theory）四费米子相互作用基础上，进一步计入s道引力子交换（s-channel graviton exchange）过程，并引入霍尔斯特项（Holst term），计算其对总截面与暗物质丰度（dark matter abundance）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有暗物质产生的计算仅考虑爱因斯坦-嘉当理论的四费米子相互作用，未计入s道引力子交换这一额外贡献过程；此外霍尔斯特项对耦合常数与相互作用结构的修正此前未被系统讨论。因此需要更完整地评估挠率相关相互作用对费米子暗物质产生的影响。

Method: 在爱因斯坦-嘉当理论框架下，将s道引力子交换作为额外的暗物质产生过程纳入计算，得到对总截面（total cross section）以及动量依赖的玻尔兹曼方程碰撞项（Boltzmann equation collision term）的修正；同时引入霍尔斯特项，分析其对四费米子耦合常数的修改及新引入的两个四费米子相互作用。

Result: 计算并讨论了s道引力子交换对总截面和动量依赖碰撞项的影响，同时给出霍尔斯特项对耦合常数与新四点相互作用的修正，并计算其对最终产生的暗物质丰度的影响。

Conclusion: 挠率诱导的普适四点费米子相互作用及其附加过程（s道引力子交换、霍尔斯特项效应）应被纳入费米子暗物质产生的完整计算，摘要表明这些修正会影响总截面、碰撞项与暗物质丰度，具体数值结论需参见正文。

Abstract: Introducing torsion into General Relativity leads to new four-point interactions for fermions. These are universal and must also affect any fermion dark matter. We improve existing calculations of dark matter production through the Einstein-Cartan theory four-fermion interaction by also considering the s-channel graviton exchange as another contributing process. The effect on both the total cross section as well as the momentum-dependent Boltzmann equation collision term is calculated and discussed. We also introduce the Holst term, which generally modifies the four-fermion interaction coupling constant as well as introduces two new four-fermion interactions, and discuss and compute its effect on the total produced dark matter abundance.

</details>


<div id='math.AP'></div>

# math.AP [[Back]](#toc)

### [263] [On Convergence to Equilibrium for the Boltzmann Hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24089)
*Joseph K. Miller*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究硬截断玻尔兹曼方程层级（Boltzmann hierarchy）的相关解向可因子化麦克斯韦平衡态（Maxwellian equilibrium）的松弛过程，通过适配的二阶 Sobolev 张量范数（Sobolev tensor norms）与几何活度权重（geometric activity weights）获得与粒子数无关的一致耗散不等式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在逐粒子坐标的相对意义下指定守恒量（质量、动量、能量）后，中心化可消除守恒模式，但碰撞仍将每一中心化层级与其上一层耦合；需要一种在粒子数上一致的控制手段来分析松弛与唯一性。

Method: 将适配的二阶 Sobolev 张量范数与几何活度权重相结合，对重标度后的中心化层级 H 建立能量-耗散不等式，其中矫顽性常数与碰撞常数（coercivity and collision constants）λ、C_Γ 均与粒子数无关；并引入时间依赖权重以获得与层级成正比的指数衰减。

Result: 对小活度 z，耗散项吸收耦合项，得到层级能量意义下的压缩性（contraction）与唯一性；时间依赖权重给出层级成比例的指数衰减。对整体解，密度与可观测量生成函数（observable generating functions）收敛至平衡态，连通对关联（connected pair correlations）以单粒子速率的两倍衰减。

Conclusion: 所得估计适用于已有的能量解（energy solutions），但一般存在性仍作为假设；充分接近参考麦克斯韦态、具有共同守恒矩的足够正则单粒子解的统计混合（statistical mixtures，含连续律）可作为示例。

Abstract: We study relaxation of correlated solutions of the hard-cutoff Boltzmann hierarchy toward factorized Maxwellian equilibrium. We prescribe the Maxwellian's mass, momentum, and energy in each full particle coordinate, conditional on the others. Centering removes these conserved modes, but collisions still couple each centered level to the next. To control this forcing uniformly in particle number, we combine adapted second-order Sobolev tensor norms with geometric activity weights and prove $\frac12\frac{d}{dt}E_z(H(t))^2 +(λ-\sqrt{2}C_Γz)D_z(H(t))^2\le0,$ where $H$ is the rescaled centered hierarchy, $E_z,D_z$ measure its energy and dissipation, and the coercivity and collision constants $λ,C_Γ$ are independent of particle number. For small activity $z$, dissipation absorbs the coupling and gives contraction and uniqueness in the hierarchy energy. Time-dependent weights yield level-proportional exponential decay. For global solutions, densities and observable generating functions converge to equilibrium, and connected pair correlations decay at twice the one-particle rate. The estimates apply to existing energy solutions. General existence remains an assumption, while statistical mixtures, including continuous laws, of sufficiently regular one-particle solutions uniformly close to the reference Maxwellian, with common conserved moments, provide examples.

</details>


### [264] [Condensation and Non-condensation in the Quasilinear 4-wave Kinetic Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25207)
*Siwei Luo,Jian-Guo Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究三维各向同性拟线性四波动力学方程（4-wave kinetic equation）在零频率处的有限时间凝聚（finite-time condensation），并证明相互作用系数指数 β 的尖锐阈值 β=1/4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对四波动力学方程在零频率（zero frequency）处是否发生有限时间凝聚这一问题，需要确定相互作用强度如何影响凝聚行为，并给出相应的阈值刻画。

Method: 在 Schrödinger 色散（Schrödinger dispersion）与指数 β∈(0,3/4] 的乘积型相互作用系数下，对任意具有有限波作用量（wave action）与能量的非负初始测度构造全局测度解（global measure solutions）。

Result: 证明尖锐阈值 β=1/4：当 β∈[1/4,3/4] 时构造解满足 g_t({0})=g_0({0})；当 β∈(0,1/4) 时，对 (g_0)_ac≠0 的解给出向 𝒩_0δ_0 收敛的全变差（TV）距离定量估计，并推出对任意 ζ∈(0,1) 存在有限时刻 T_ζ 使 g_t({0})≥(1-ζ)𝒩_0。

Conclusion: 结果表明零频率凝聚的发生与否由 β 与 1/4 的比较决定，β=1/4 为有限时间凝聚的临界指数。

Abstract: We study finite-time condensation at zero frequency for the isotropic quasilinear 4-wave kinetic equation in three dimensions. With Schrodinger dispersion and a product interaction coefficient with exponent $β\in(0,3/4]$, we construct global measure solutions $(g_t)_{t\geq0}$ for every nonnegative initial measure $g_0$ with finite wave action and energy. We then establish the sharp threshold $β=1/4$ for finite-time condensation of the constructed solutions. For $β\in[1/4,3/4]$, the constructed solutions satisfy $g_t(\{0\})=g_0(\{0\})$ for all $t\geq0$. For $β\in(0,1/4)$, every constructed solution with $(g_0)_{\mathrm{ac}}\neq0$ satisfies $\|g_t-\mathcal{N}_0δ_0\|_{\mathrm{TV}}\leq C\mathcal{N}_0(1+\mathcal{N}_0^2t)^{-(1-4β)/40}$ for all $t\geq0$, where $\mathcal{N}_0=g_0([0,\infty))$ and $C=C(β,g_0/\mathcal{N}_0)>0$. In particular, for every $ζ\in(0,1)$, there exists $T_ζ<\infty$ such that $g_t(\{0\})\geq(1-ζ)\mathcal{N}_0$ for all $t\geq T_ζ$.

</details>


### [265] [The inviscid limit of 2D Navier-Stokes near a self-similar vortex collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25367)
*Trinh T. Nguyen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究二维不可压缩 Navier–Stokes 方程在三个点涡（point vortices）自相似坍缩情形下的无粘极限（inviscid limit），证明粘性解可由 Lamb–Oseen 涡叠加逼近，并给出显式的 L^1 误差阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 点涡的无粘动力学在有限时间发生自相似坍缩，此时无粘解出现奇异性；要理解粘性（viscosity）如何在该奇点附近正则化无粘解，需要刻画坍缩时刻附近的逼近误差率。

Method: 采用适应自相似坍缩的坐标变换，将问题化为区间 0<s≤γlog(1/ν) 上的长时间无粘极限问题，其中含一个随时间变化并随 s→∞ 趋于零的重标度扩散系数（rescaled diffusion coefficient）η(s)。

Result: 对每个固定 0<γ<1，证明解可由位于点涡位置的 Lamb–Oseen 涡叠加逼近，在 0<t≤T_ν=1-ν^γ 上一致地得到 L^1 误差为 O(ν^{1-γ})。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在三涡自相似坍缩前的任意固定幂次时间窗内，粘性解与 Lamb–Oseen 涡叠加之间的偏差以接近 O(ν) 的速率受控，且误差估计直到 t=1-ν^γ 均一致成立。

Abstract: We study the inviscid limit of the two-dimensional Navier--Stokes equations with initial vorticity given by three point vortices whose inviscid trajectories collapse self-similarly at $t=1$. For every fixed $0<γ<1$, we prove that the solution is approximated by a superposition of Lamb--Oseen vortices centered at the point-vortex positions, with an $L^1$ error of order $O(ν^{1-γ})$, uniformly for $0<t\le T_ν=1-ν^γ$. We use coordinates adapted to the self-similar collapse and study a long-time inviscid limit problem on the interval $0<s\leγ\log(1/ν)$, with a time-dependent rescaled diffusion coefficient $η(s)$ that tends to zero as $s\to\infty$.

</details>


### [266] [Semiclassical measures for quantized elliptic and parabolic symplectic linear maps of the multidimensional torus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26382)
*Vincent Ferrari-Dominguez*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文研究环面（torus）上由整系数辛线性映射（integer symplectic linear maps）量子化所得量子映射的半经典测度（semiclassical measures），重点处理周期矩阵与抛物矩阵两类情形，并给出了半经典测度的完整刻画及部分量子遍历性（quantum ergodicity）结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在厘清可积量子映射（具体为 Hamilton 量 H(x,ξ)=Q(ξ)/2 生成流的时刻一映射）所对应的半经典测度结构，并考察周期矩阵与抛物矩阵情形下测度的分布规律，以推进对量子遍历性问题的理解。

Method: 采用半经典分析（semiclassical analysis）框架，研究整系数辛线性映射的量子化；对周期矩阵证明其半经典测度集与 A 的不变概率测度集一致；对 T² 上的抛物矩阵通过分析位置方向 Fourier 系数（Fourier coefficients）给出测度的显式刻画，并借助量子方差（quantum variance）的锐界估计。

Result: 周期矩阵 A 的半经典测度集恰好等于 A 的不变概率测度（invariant probability measures）集；T² 上抛物矩阵的半经典测度在位置方向上至多含三个非零 Fourier 系数；由此得到抛物矩阵的部分量子遍历性陈述及量子方差的锐界，并在高维构造了相应例子。

Conclusion: 在高维情形下存在位置投影仅含非零 Fourier 系数的半经典测度；若二次型 Q 在有理数上迷向（isotropic over the rationals），则存在位置投影集中于某些子环面（subtori）的半经典测度；论文还将其与环面上 Laplacian 的半经典测度建立了联系。

Abstract: We study the semiclassical measures associated to the quantum map on the torus obtained by quantizing integer symplectic linear maps, focusing on periodic matrices and certain parabolic matrices. More precisely, we consider the time-one map of the completely integrable flows generated by Hamiltonians of the form H(x, $ξ$) = Q($ξ$)/2 where Q is an integral quadratic form. We prove that the set of semiclassical measures associated to a periodic matrix A coincides with the set of invariant probability measures of A. The semiclassical measures of parabolic matrices on T${}^2$ are explicitly characterized by proving that they have at most three nonzero Fourier coefficients in position. A partial quantum ergodicity statement for parabolic matrices is obtained by proving a sharp bound on the quantum variance and some examples are built in higher dimensions. In particular, starting from T___ there always exist semiclassical measures whose projection on the position has only nonzero Fourier coefficients. Moreover, if Q is isotropic over the rationals, then there exist semiclassical measures whose projection on the position concentrate on some subtori. A link is made with the semiclassical measures of the Laplacian on the torus.

</details>

### [267] [Ultrafast Proton-Induced Collective Dynamics Regulated by Water-Mediated Hydrogen-Bond Networks in Liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26387)
*Xiaokun Liu,Bozhu Chen,Yizhu Zhang,Jiyu Xu,Meng Li,Yanjun Gao,Ji Qi,Yuqian Yang,Wennan Wang,Sheng Meng,Yuhai Jiang*

Main category: physics.atm-clus

TL;DR: 该研究结合光学泵浦-太赫兹探测光谱（optical-pump terahertz-probe spectroscopy）与实时含时密度泛函理论模拟（real-time time-dependent density-functional theory），揭示液体中烷基C-H位点可作为光激活质子源，并通过氢键网络调控后续质子与电子动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光诱导质子动力学将分子反应与非平衡电荷输运耦合，但烷基C-H位点作为质子源（proton source）的作用此前认识不足。

Method: 对液态乙醇（ethanol）与无羟基的乙酸乙酯（ethyl acetate）进行强场电离，并用同位素取代（isotope substitution）与对照实验辅助识别质子来源。

Result: 释放的质子在数百飞秒尺度上诱导准自由电子（quasi-free-electron）局域化，表现为太赫兹吸收中的特征V形瞬态；乙醇-水混合物中痕量水增强氢键网络连通性，促进质子向乙醇O-H位点转移并抑制该瞬态吸收凹陷。

Conclusion: 光激活的C-H位点是液体中的质子源，氢键网络调控随后的质子与电子动力学，从而将局域键断裂与周围液体的超快电荷响应联系起来。

Abstract: Photoinduced proton dynamics in liquids couple molecular reactions to nonequilibrium charge transport, yet the role of alkyl C-H sites as proton sources remains poorly understood. Combining optical-pump terahertz-probe spectroscopy with real-time time-dependent density-functional-theory simulations, we investigate ultrafast proton dynamics following strong-field ionization of liquid ethanol and hydroxyl-free ethyl acetate. Isotope-substitution and control experiments support the identification of alkyl C-H groups as the proton source. The released protons induce quasi-free-electron localization on a timescale of hundreds of femtoseconds, manifested as a characteristic V-shaped transient in terahertz absorption. In ethanol-water mixtures, trace water enhances hydrogen-bond-network connectivity and facilitates proton transfer to ethanol O-H sites, suppressing the transient absorption dip. These findings identify photoactivated C-H sites as proton sources in liquids and reveal how hydrogen-bond networks regulate the ensuing proton and electron dynamics, linking local bond cleavage to the ultrafast charge response of the surrounding liquid.

</details>

### [268] [Convergence of alternating score-system iterations for regularized Landau particle dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25780)
*Yeoneung Kim,Donghyun Lee,Sung-Jun Son*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文分析基于得分（score）的粒子近似方法中用于齐次朗道方程（homogeneous Landau equation）的交替迭代格式，在固定正则化下证明Tikhonov得分极小元的存在唯一性与$W_1$-Lipschitz稳定性，并给出关于粒子数一致的阶乘收敛（factorial convergence）、守恒性质及正则化移除障碍的刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为基于得分的粒子近似求解齐次朗道方程建立严格数学基础，厘清交替迭代、平均场极限（mean-field limit）与正则化参数三者之间的关系，并识别去除正则化时本质性的理论障碍。

Method: 采用交替迭代：每一步沿上一轨迹计算正则化得分，再以冻结的得分重算轨迹；在嵌入$C_b^2$的向量值再生核希尔伯特空间（reproducing kernel Hilbert space）中研究Tikhonov得分极小元，并用得分差抵消（score-difference cancellation）技巧推广至截断及未截断（$\gamma=-2$、三维库仑核）朗道核；正则化移除部分借助相对熵（relative entropy）建立条件性密度级收敛判据。

Result: 固定正正则化下证明Tikhonov得分极小元存在唯一且具$W_1$-Lipschitz稳定性；对有界Lipschitz碰撞矩阵（collision matrices）得到有限区间上到耦合正则化动力学的阶乘收敛，常数与粒子数无关，且全局目标间隙与系统残差可控非精确求解，结构性核假设保证动量与能量守恒，平均场极限与交替迭代极限可交换。对$\gamma\geq -2$的远场截断核及未截断$\gamma=-2$情形，借助得分差抵消推广理论；三维未截断库仑核在局部有界紧支撑密度下由独立连续构造得到局部阶乘收敛。另以热方程反例表明未正则化密度级Picard迭代可能发散。

Conclusion: 文章刻画了在固定粒子数下去除正则化的障碍，并在一致正则性与近似假设下用相对熵给出条件性密度级收敛准则；原子库仑（atomic Coulomb）情形及粒子数与正则化同时趋于零的联合极限仍为公开问题。

Abstract: We analyze an alternating iteration for score-based particle approximations of the homogeneous Landau equation. Each step computes a regularized score along the previous trajectory and recomputes the trajectory with that score frozen. First, at fixed positive regularization, we prove existence, uniqueness, and $W_1$-Lipschitz stability of a Tikhonov score minimizer in a vector-valued reproducing kernel Hilbert space embedded in $C_b^2$. For bounded Lipschitz collision matrices, this yields factorial convergence to the coupled regularized dynamics on every finite interval, with constants independent of particle number. Global objective gaps and system residuals control inexact solves, and structural kernel assumptions ensure momentum and energy conservation. The mean-field and alternating-iteration limits commute at fixed regularization in this regime. Second, score-difference cancellation extends the theory to far-field-truncated Landau kernels with $γ\geq -2$ and the uncut case $γ=-2$. For the uncut three-dimensional Coulomb kernel, a separate continuum construction gives local factorial convergence from bounded, compactly supported densities. Third, we characterize the obstruction to removing regularization at fixed particle number and establish a separate conditional density-level convergence criterion under uniform regularity and approximation assumptions, using relative entropy. A heat-equation counterexample shows that an unregularized density-level Picard iteration can diverge. Atomic Coulomb and simultaneous particle-zero-regularization limits remain open.

</details>


### [269] [A boundary integral method for computing electron repulsion integrals in heterogeneous dielectric environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26745)
*Xuanzhao Gao,Malte Rösner,Jason Kaye,Alex Barnett*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种边界积分方法（boundary integral method），用于计算分片常数介电常数环境中的电子排斥积分（electron repulsion integrals, ERIs），并将其应用于石墨烯/Si-SiO2 异质结器件几何下的 ERI 张量求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统裸库仑（bare-Coulomb）ERIs 忽略了现代二维材料器件中基底与封装层等结构介电环境带来的屏蔽效应，因此需要能纳入结构化介电屏蔽的 ERI 计算方法。

Method: 将问题表述为介电界面上感应表面电荷（induced surface charge）的第二类边界积分方程（second-kind boundary integral equation），用自适应加密面板上的 Nyström 方法离散；近场用傅里叶截断核方法（Fourier truncated-kernel method），远场用快速多极子方法（fast multipole method, FMM）加速求积，线性系统由 FMM 加速的 Krylov 迭代求解；针对长方体几何通过近接触面板求积（close-to-touching panel quadratures）保持高长宽比下的效率，并开发分布式流水线计算完整 ERI 张量。

Result: 在三层石墨烯板置于 Si/SiO2 异质结上的模型中，以中心石墨烯层 198 个类 p_z 轨道为例，保留 198^4 大小 ERI 张量中 2613^2 个不可忽略元素；模型在 130 万表面未知量上求解，相对误差 0.004%，代价为 22 节点小时（node-hours）。

Conclusion: 该方法可高效、高精度地计算结构化介电环境中的 ERI 张量，适用于具有高长宽比的立方体器件几何,为二维材料器件的电子结构计算提供了可行途径。

Abstract: We present a boundary integral method for evaluating electron repulsion integrals (ERIs) in dielectric environments of piecewise constant permittivity. This generalizes conventional bare-Coulomb ERIs by incorporating screening from structured dielectric surroundings, such as the substrates and/or encapsulating layers present in modern two-dimensional-material devices. We formulate the problem as a second-kind boundary integral equation for the induced surface charge on the dielectric interfaces, and discretize it with a Nyström method on adaptively refined panels. The incident potential generated by each smooth orbital source density is evaluated by a Fourier truncated-kernel method in or near to its source box, and by fast multipole method (FMM) accelerated quadrature farther away. The linear system is solved by a Krylov iteration also accelerated by the FMM. We focus on cuboidal device geometries, and retain efficiency at high aspect ratios via close-to-touching panel quadratures. We further develop a distributed pipeline that computes the full ERI tensor, comprising thousands of orbital pairs, embedded in a single dielectric geometry. We demonstrate the method on an effective three-layer graphene slab over a Si/SiO$_2$ heterojunction, focusing on the central graphene layer represented by $198$ $p_z$-like orbitals, retaining the $2613^2$ non-negligible entries of the $198^4$-sized ERI tensor. The model is resolved on $1.3$ million surface unknowns, achieving a relative error of $0.004\%$, at a cost of $22$ node-hours.

</details>

### [270] [Microphase Separation and Ionic Aggregation in Oppositely Charged End-Functionalized Polymer Blends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24361)
*Chao Duan,Zhen-Gang Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文采用离子关联增强的自洽场理论（ion-correlation-augmented self-consistent field theory, IC-SCFT）研究带相反电荷端基的二元聚合物共混物的相行为，指出强离子关联导致多链端基缔合（ion clustering），产生超出有效双嵌段图像的自组装结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前实验常以简单离子配对的二嵌段共聚物（diblock copolymer）图像解释端基带电聚合物共混物的行为；但在有机聚合物熔体的低介电（low dielectric）条件下，强离子关联可能使该简化图像失效，需更准确的理论刻画。

Method: 采用离子关联增强的自洽场理论（IC-SCFT），在低介电条件下考察带相反电荷端基聚合物共混物的相行为，并与有效二嵌段预测进行对比。

Result: 在中至强偏析（segregation）区，离子团簇使层状（lamellar）与柱状（cylindrical）相窗口变宽、抑制网络相（network phases），与端基带电二嵌段共聚物趋势相反；在弱偏析区，两种聚合物保持混溶（miscible）而离子团簇仍存在，且随链长缩短，团簇经历球—柱—层状（sphere-to-cylinder-to-lamella）形状转变。

Conclusion: 离子团簇而非简单成对键合（pairwise bonding）是决定端基带电聚合物共混物自组装（self-assembly）的关键因素；当一种聚合物缩至仅剩带电端基时，结果连续衔接至经典端基带电离聚物（ionomer）的聚集行为。

Abstract: We study the phase behavior of binary blends of polymers with oppositely charged termini using an ion-correlation-augmented self-consistent field theory (IC-SCFT). Under the low dielectric conditions typical for organic polymer melts, strong ion correlations drive association of multiple chain ends, leading to emergent multichain architectures that go beyond a simple ion-paired diblock copolymer picture previously used to interpret recent experiments. At moderate to strong polymer segregation, the resulting phase diagram differs considerably from this effective-diblock prediction: ion clustering broadens the lamellar and cylindrical windows while suppressing the network phases, the reverse of the trend for end-charged diblock copolymers. At weak segregation, the two polymers remain miscible even as ion clustering persists, and decreasing chain length drives the clusters themselves through a sphere-to-cylinder-to-lamella shape transition, connecting continuously to the aggregation behavior of classical terminally charged ionomers in the limit when one of the polymer species shrinks to just the charged end. These results reveal that ion clustering -- beyond simple pairwise bonding -- shapes the self-assembly of end-charged polymer blends.

</details>


### [271] [Calibrated shape-only recovery of membrane viscosities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24410)
*Julian Olszewski,Zijian Liu,Jie Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究指出，当两种膜黏度对应的松弛速率相近时，形状瞬态会导致二者难以区分；通过标定式制备（已知符号振幅比、标定相对增益与计时）两次独立释放，可在 Kelvin-Voigt 球模型中将剪切与膨胀速率分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 邻近的松弛速率（relaxation rates）使两种膜黏度（membrane viscosities）难以从形状瞬态中区分，存在局部灵敏度损失，需要一种消除该损失的实验制备方案。

Method: 在线性 Kelvin-Voigt 球（linear Kelvin-Voigt sphere）模型中考虑有限面积弹性（finite area elasticity）与本征膜耗散（intrinsic membrane dissipation），采用两次独立释放并固定线性组合，分离剪切与膨胀速率；并分析振幅投影灵敏度下界、单个切向释放记录，以及无界球 Stokes 扩展（unbounded spherical Stokes extension）。

Result: 对固定采样、正定权重、有界正速率与非零激励，即使速率重合，振幅投影灵敏度仍有正下界；未做相对标定时，成对灵敏度随速率差线性下降，单一切向释放记录则二次下降。标定下界与固定有限初始能量相容，且在高斯误差模型下固定正精度的比值标定仍保持正局部下界。

Conclusion: 标定式制备可恢复局部材料灵敏度，并借助已知弹性系数将恢复的速率换算为两种表面黏度；有界体耗散会改变模态图样，所给出的满雅可比判据（full-Jacobian criterion）为保持局部材料灵敏度提供充分条件。

Abstract: Nearby relaxation rates make two membrane viscosities difficult to distinguish from a shape transient. We show how calibrated preparation removes this loss of local sensitivity in a linear Kelvin-Voigt sphere with finite area elasticity and intrinsic membrane dissipation. Two independent releases, with a known signed amplitude ratio and calibrated relative gain and timing, yield normal-displacement records whose fixed linear combination separates the shear and dilatational rates. Their common amplitude remains unknown. For fixed sampling, positive-definite weights, bounded positive rates and nonzero excitation, the amplitude-projected sensitivity has a positive lower bound even at rate coincidence. Without relative calibration, the paired sensitivity decreases linearly with the rate gap; the single tangential-release record decreases quadratically. The calibrated bound is compatible with fixed finite initial energy. A positive local bound also persists with independent ratio calibration of fixed positive precision under a Gaussian error model. Supplied elastic coefficients convert the recovered rates into the two surface viscosities. An unbounded spherical Stokes extension quantifies how bulk dissipation changes the modal patterns and provides a sufficient full-Jacobian criterion for retaining local material sensitivity.

</details>


### [272] [Three-body corrections to Brownian hard-sphere viscosity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24948)
*Julian Olszewski,Zijian Liu,Jie Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文针对无流体动力学相互作用（hydrodynamic interactions）的布朗硬球（Brownian hard spheres）体系，给出三阶零剪切布朗黏度系数（third-order zero-shear Brownian viscosity coefficient）的条件性区间 1.8266948–1.8267157，并据此排除所比较的各闭合近似系数，为稀薄三体布朗应力（three-body Brownian stress）提供定量基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀薄三体布朗应力缺乏可靠的定量基准，而常用闭合近似（closure）如 Kirkwood 叠加（Kirkwood superposition）给出的预测需被检验。研究旨在以严格区间方法判断这些闭合系数是否可信。

Method: 在物理三体修正与有限能量弱解（finite-energy weak solution）一致、且对偶伴随恒等式（pair adjoint identity）中远端余项消失的条件下，先以初等径向恒等式（radial identity）揭示叠加预测为零源于三个非零对贡献的精确抵消；再通过修正共享接触通量误差（shared-contact flux error）定义唯一且具正 Dirichlet 能量的弱响应，结合剪切旋转结构与三球几何构造显式对称试探函数，得到变分下界与残差上界，并用精确积分与向外区间求值（outward interval evaluation）包围固定试探泛函。

Result: 得到三阶零剪切布朗黏度系数的条件性包围区间 [1.8266948, 1.8267157]。Kirkwood 叠加系数为零；在该区间及统一的接触应力约定（contact-stress convention）下，所比较的所有闭合系数均被排除。

Conclusion: 尽管叠加近似满足体方程（bulk equation），却遗留共享接触通量误差；对其修正可得唯一弱响应。所得严格区间排除了所考察的各类闭合预测，可作为稀薄三体布朗应力的定量基准，但结论依赖于摘要所述物理条件是否成立。

Abstract: For Brownian hard spheres without hydrodynamic interactions, we obtain a conditional enclosure between 1.8266948 and 1.8267157 for the third-order zero-shear Brownian viscosity coefficient. It applies when the physical three-body correction coincides with the finite-energy weak solution and the remote remainder in the pair adjoint identity vanishes. Kirkwood superposition gives zero because three nonzero pair contributions cancel exactly. An elementary radial identity exposes this cancellation, and a common contact-stress convention allows direct comparison of closure predictions. Although superposition solves the bulk equation, it leaves a shared-contact flux error. Correcting this error defines a unique weak response with positive Dirichlet energy. Explicit symmetric trials yield a variational lower bound and a residual upper bound using the rotational structure of shear and three-sphere geometry. Exact integration and outward interval evaluation enclose the fixed-trial functionals. Under the stated physical conditions, the resulting interval excludes all closure coefficients compared here and supplies a quantitative benchmark for dilute three-body Brownian stress.

</details>


### [273] [Active rheology shapes collective flows in uniaxially compressed 3D spheroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24959)
*Liyang Wang,Shabeeb Ameen,J. M. Schwarz,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文利用三维活性顶点模型（three-dimensional active vertex model）研究受限球状体（spheroid）在单轴压缩（uniaxial compression）下的运动响应，识别出活性依赖的运动性反转与压缩诱导的相干转动（coherent rotation）两种不同结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压缩如何改变细胞运动的幅度与组织方式尚不清楚，需明确组织状态与活性动力学如何共同塑造受限球状体对压缩的响应机制。

Method: 采用三维活性顶点模型，在极性对齐（polarity alignment）关闭与高对齐两种情形下施加单轴压缩，并辅以被动五细胞（five-cell）计算与受控扰动分析。

Result: 关闭极性对齐时，固态样（solid-like）球状体横向运动普遍增强，而流体样（fluid-like）响应随活性由加速转为减速，并由接触持续性（contact persistence）变化统一为共同经验趋势；高对齐情形下强压缩使固态样球状体产生相干转动，流体样则无，旋转固态体接触持续性指数更高且外周细胞呈切向组织。

Conclusion: 压缩诱导的活性依赖运动性反转与相干转动是加载的两种不同结果，可归因于接触持续性、局部重排代价与活性协调三者的耦合。

Abstract: How compression changes both the magnitude and organization of cell motion remains unclear. Using a three-dimensional active vertex model, we examine how tissue state and active dynamics shape the response of confined spheroids to uniaxial compression. With polarity alignment switched off, compression enhances transverse motion in solid-like spheroids throughout the tested activity range, whereas the fluid-like response reverses from acceleration at low activity to slowing at high activity. Changes in contact persistence organize these responses along a common empirical trend. Passive five-cell calculations reveal how compression reshapes local rearrangement barriers: the response depends on geometry and loading direction, and a high-strain increase in the fluid-like mean coexists with many low-barrier paths. In a separate high-alignment regime, strong compression induces coherent rotation in solid-like spheroids, while fluid-like spheroids show no comparable collective rotation. The rotating solids have a higher contact-persistence index than the fluids and exhibit tangential peripheral cell organization. Controlled perturbations reveal compression-induced softening of a differential-twist mode and slower relaxation with activity. Together, these results identify an activity-dependent reversal of the motility response and compression-induced coherent rotation as two distinct outcomes of loading, linking them to contact persistence, local rearrangement costs, and active coordination.

</details>


### [274] [Enhancement of liquid-liquid phase transition under shear flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25243)
*Kyohei Takae*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文利用两序参量（two-order-parameter）流体动力学模型研究剪切流（shear flow）下的液-液转变（LLT），揭示黏度与结构序参量耦合产生的正反馈可在临界剪切率以上使亚稳液体失稳并发生转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液-液转变中单一组分液体转变为密度与黏度不同的另一液体，流动与转变相互耦合，但剪切流对LLT的影响尚不清楚，本文旨在阐明其机制。

Method: 采用黏度依赖于非守恒结构序参量的两序参量流体动力学模型（hydrodynamic model），结合线性稳定性分析与流体动力学模拟（hydrodynamic simulations）进行研究。

Result: 线性分析表明，序参量涨落经黏度驱动压缩流动、进而改变密度并增强涨落，形成反馈，在临界剪切率（critical shear rate）以上对特定方向波矢使亚稳液体失稳；因流动使波矢旋转，涨落仅在有限时间内增长，其放大由无量纲剪切率与该速率和序参量热力学弛豫率之比Λ共同决定。模拟显示剪切率超过约临界值时，在Λ≳1条件下亚稳区发生转变，转变以畴（domain）形核方式进行且形貌呈各向异性，孵育时间（incubation time）随剪切率与Λ增大而缩短、黏度上升提前。

Conclusion: 剪切率可作为除温度与压力之外控制LLT的变量，决定亚稳液体是否转变以及如何转变。

Abstract: In a liquid--liquid transition (LLT), a single-component liquid transforms into another liquid with different density and viscosity. Flow therefore couples to the transition, but the effect of shear flow is not understood. Here we investigate the LLT under shear with a two-order-parameter hydrodynamic model in which the viscosity depends on the non-conserved structural order parameter. Under shear, a fluctuation of the order parameter drives a compressional flow through the viscosity, and the resulting density change enhances the order parameter fluctuation. In a linear analysis, this feedback destabilizes the metastable liquid above a critical shear rate $\dotγ_{\rm c}$ for wavevectors in particular directions. Because the flow rotates the wavevector, a fluctuation grows only for a finite time duration, and its amplification is determined by the dimensionless shear rate $\dotγ/\dotγ_{\rm c}$ and the second dimensionless number $Λ$, the ratio of the thermodynamic relaxation rate of the order parameter to $\dotγ_{\rm c}$. In hydrodynamic simulations, shear above about $\dotγ_{\rm c}$ induces the transition in the metastable region when $Λ\gtrsim1$.
