The transition proceeds by nucleation of domains whose morphology becomes anisotropic. The incubation time decreases and the onset of the viscosity increase is accelerated as $\dotγ$ and $Λ$ increase. These results imply that the shear rate is a control variable of LLT in addition to temperature and pressure, selecting whether and how a metastable liquid transforms.

</details>


### [275] [Mode coupling theory analysis of water dynamics detected by dielectric spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25328)
*Peter Lunkenheimer,Alois Loidl*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作以宽带介电谱（broadband dielectric spectroscopy）检验模式耦合理论（mode-coupling theory, MCT）对水及LiCl水溶液分子动力学的预言，发现数据与MCT一致，并给出纯水的临界温度Tc=222 K与系统参数lambda=0.55。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCT对液体分子动力学给出多项具体预言，但水的玻璃转变（glass transition）存在激烈争议；同时MCT预言的快速beta过程（fast beta process）在纯水中因不可避免的结晶而无法在低温下观测，因此需要借助可深度过冷的体系加以检验。

Method: 评述最新水与LiCl水溶液的宽带介电谱数据，考察其与MCT预言的兼容性，包括alpha弛豫（alpha relaxation）临界温度依赖性与非德拜谱形（non-Debye spectral shape）之间的关联；将LiCl溶液结果与纯水数据对比，并将介电弛豫时间与其他实验方法结果进行比较。

Result: 所有可得的宽带介电数据与MCT一致；在14.8 mol% LiCl溶液中检测到的快速beta过程亦符合理论；导出的MCT参数随盐浓度平滑演变，支持纯水的Tc=222 K与lambda=0.55。

Conclusion: 介电谱数据支持MCT用于描述水及水溶液的动力学；文中还讨论了这些数据能否提供关于水脆性-强性转变（fragile-to-strong transition）的信息，以及水的主导介电弛豫过程与alpha弛豫关联性的争议。

Abstract: Mode-coupling theory (MCT), one of the most prominent models of the glass transition, also makes various specific predictions for the molecular dynamics in the liquid state of matter. They can be best experimentally tested by evaluating susceptibility spectra covering a broad frequency range. In the present work, we clarify whether the most recent broadband dielectric-spectroscopy results on water, whose glass transition is highly disputed, are compatible with MCT. The so-called fast beta process, expected within this theory, should be detectable at low temperatures only, inaccessible in water due to its inevitable crystallization upon cooling. Therefore, we also investigate data on aqueous LiCl solutions, which can be easily supercooled. We find that all the available broadband dielectric data on these solutions and pure water are well consistent with MCT. Notably, this includes the expected interrelation of the critical temperature dependence of the alpha relaxation to its often-neglected non-Debye spectral shape. Moreover, the available data on the fast beta process, detected in a 14.8 mol% LiCl solution, are also well consistent with the theory. We find a smooth development of the derived MCT parameters with salt concentration which supports the validity of the critical temperature Tc = 222 K and system parameter lambda = 0.55 deduced here for pure water. We also compare the current dielectric relaxation-time results to those determined by other experimental methods and address the question whether information on the controversial fragile-to-strong transition of water can be obtained from such data. Finally, we discuss the significance of the dominant dielectric relaxation process of water for the glass transition as described by MCT, especially considering scenarios questioning its association with the alpha relaxation.

</details>


### [276] [Dependence of the acoustic propulsion of nano- and microcones on their orientation and aspect ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25383)
*Johannes Voß,Raphael Wittkowski*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究在平面行波超声场中考察不同长径比（aspect ratio）的纳米与微米锥形胶体粒子（cone-shaped colloidal particle）的推进特性，发现取向平均推进在长径比为 1/2 时最强，并给出便于复用的简单解析表达。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究揭示了平面行波超声波（planar traveling ultrasound wave）作用下锥形胶体粒子的取向依赖推进（orientation-dependent propulsion），但尚未系统考察长径比的影响，也缺少对各向同性超声场（isotropic ultrasound field）下取向平均推进的刻画。

Method: 采用数值模拟（simulation）方法，对具有不同长径比的纳米锥与微米锥进行建模，分析推进随粒子取向与长径比的变化，并对取向平均推进做参数扫描以确定最优长径比。

Result: 推进依赖于粒子取向与长径比；取向平均推进在长径比约为 1/2 时表现尤为强烈，对应各向同性超声场情形。作者进一步提供了与该结果对应的简单解析表示（analytic representation），以方便后续研究复用。

Conclusion: 长径比是调控锥形粒子超声推进的关键几何参数，长径比 1/2 可作为设计中增强取向平均推进的参考值；所提供的解析表达提升了模拟结果的可复用性。

Abstract: Recent research revealed the orientation-dependent propulsion of a cone-shaped colloidal particle that is exposed to a planar traveling ultrasound wave. Here, we extend the previous research by considering nano- and microcones with different aspect ratios and studying how the propulsion of a particle depends on its orientation and aspect ratio. We also study how the orientation-averaged propulsion of a cone-shaped particle, which corresponds to an isotropic ultrasound field, depends on its aspect ratio and identify an aspect ratio of 1/2 where the orientation-averaged propulsion is particularly strong. To make our simulation results easier reusable for follow-up research, we provide a corresponding simple analytic representation.

</details>


### [277] [Emergent Chirality Reversal in Magnetically Driven Rotor Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25537)
*Xinzhi Li,Ke Zhong,Mohammad F. Islam,Shiladitya Banerjee*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 磁性驱动的转子集合并不必然沿与其个体成员相同的方向旋转；因旋转—平移耦合（rotation-translation coupling）与偶极相互作用（dipolar interactions）的竞争，转子会自组装成团簇，其集体旋转方向可与每个成员的自转方向相反，且该手性（handedness）状态可通过系统尺寸与驱动频率调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文针对一个直观疑问：由旋转磁场驱动的转子集合是否必然与单个转子同向旋转。其动机在于探究集体旋转方向能否在外部场方向不变的前提下发生反转，即集体手性是否可独立于单转子旋转方向而出现。

Method: 研究基于旋转磁场驱动的转子体系，分析旋转—平移耦合与偶极相互作用这两种倾向相反集体旋转方向的机制之间的竞争，并考察系统尺寸、驱动频率以及流体动力学相互作用（hydrodynamic interactions）对平衡关系与转变边界的影响。

Result: 两种机制的平衡随系统尺寸与驱动频率变化，导致两种手性状态之间发生转变（transition）；流体动力学相互作用会使该转变边界发生移动，即对转变条件产生修正。

Conclusion: 集体手性可以出现在与场强加的单转子旋转相反的方向上，而无须反转外场，并可通过系统尺寸与驱动频率加以调控。

Abstract: Does a collection of magnetically driven rotors necessarily rotate in the same direction as its individual members? We show that rotors driven by a rotating magnetic field self-assemble into clusters whose collective rotation can oppose the self-rotation of every rotor within them. The reversal arises from competition between rotation-translation coupling and dipolar interactions, which favor opposite directions of collective rotation. Their balance varies with system size and driving frequency, producing a transition between the two handed states, while hydrodynamic interactions shift the transition boundary. Collective handedness can thus emerge opposite to the field-imposed single-rotor rotation without reversing the external field, and can be tuned through system size and driving frequency.

</details>


### [278] [Flaw-tolerant recycling of waste tires into high-performance rubber](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25572)
*Chenghai Li,Yu Zhou,Zheqi Chen,Jun Ho Min,Matthew Wei Ming Tan,Masako Yoshihara Yang,Yakov Kutsovsky,Zhigang Suo*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 论文提出“缺陷容忍型回收（flaw-tolerant recycling）”原理：不要求每个废胶颗粒本身可靠且细小，而是设计基体使类缺陷颗粒不引发宏观断裂。采用致密缠结、稀疏交联的长天然橡胶链网络与渗流（percolated）炭黑聚集体网络互穿构成的基体，掺入高达80 wt%商用废胶颗粒后复合材料仍保持强度，为废轮胎回收制新胎提供可能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 每年约十亿条轮胎报废，废胶粉复合物通常被降级回收（downcycled）为运动场地、室内地坪、橡胶沥青等低值产品，难以再制新胎。其瓶颈在于颗粒含量高或粒径大时复合材料强度低：单个废胶颗粒可作为缺陷（flaw）引发宏观断裂，削弱整体强度。

Method: 提出缺陷容忍型回收原理，使基体设计为可抑制类缺陷颗粒引发宏观断裂，而非要求颗粒本身细小可靠。具体构建由两种互穿网络（intertwined networks）组成的缺陷容忍基体：致密缠结、稀疏交联的长天然橡胶链网络，以及渗流（percolated）的炭黑聚集体网络；并向其中掺入最高80 wt%的商用废胶颗粒。

Result: 当缺陷容忍基体掺入高达80 wt%的商用废胶颗粒时，所得复合材料仍能保持强度，表明高填充量下强度损失问题得到缓解。

Conclusion: 缺陷容忍型回收原理有望推动废轮胎回收用于制造新轮胎，突破当前以降级回收为主的局限。

Abstract: Annually about one billion tires reach the end of life. Rubber from waste tires can be ground into particles and incorporated into a matrix. In practice, the resulting composites are commonly not recycled into new tires, but downcycled into low-value products such as sport surfaces, indoor flooring, and rubberized asphalt. Recycling waste tires into new tires remains a challenge. In particular, such composites typically have low strength, especially when the particle content is high or the particles are large. A single waste tire particle may act as a flaw to initiate macroscopic fracture and knock down the strength of the entire composite. Here we propose the principle of flaw-tolerant recycling. Rather than requiring every particle to be reliable and small, the matrix is designed so that flaw-like particles do not initiate macroscopic fracture. We demonstrate a flaw-tolerant matrix consisting of two intertwined networks: a network of densely entangled and sparsely crosslinked long natural rubber chains, and a percolated network of carbon aggregates. When the flaw-tolerant matrix is incorporated with up to 80 wt% of commercially available waste particles, the resulting composites still maintain strength. The principle of flaw-tolerant recycling may aid the development of recycling waste tires into new tires.

</details>


### [279] [Effect of chirality on active elastomers and solids in three dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25945)
*S. J. Kole,Gareth P. Alexander,Ananyo Maitra,Sriram Ramaswamy*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文构建了向列相与极性单轴弹性体及固体的通用流体力学方程，分析手性活性（chiral activity）的影响；结果表明弹性体的高阶对称性强烈约束手性活性效应，而破坏该对称性的单轴固体在固定背景介质中通常被手性活性失稳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清手性活性（chiral activity）在向列相与极性单轴弹性体（elastomer）和固体（solid）中的具体作用机制，特别是对称性对活性效应的限制与失稳条件。

Method: 构造这些相态的通用流体力学方程（hydrodynamic equations）；从具有刚性旋转不变性及单轴方向独立旋转不变性的弹性体出发，再通过自由能中加入对称性破缺项得到单轴固体，并分析固定背景介质与惯性区（inertial regime）中的力密度响应。

Result: 弹性体的高阶对称性强烈约束手性活性的效应；单轴固体在固定背景介质中手性活性会普遍导致失稳，对应的力密度随破缺场趋于零而消失；破缺场的存在还允许额外的奇弹性力密度（odd elastic force density），在惯性区普遍失稳动量守恒的手性与单轴固体。

Conclusion: 手性活性的效应高度依赖体系对称性：弹性体因高阶对称性而受强约束，而一旦通过破缺场降至单轴固体对称性，手性活性与奇弹性效应即可引发失稳。

Abstract: We examine the effect of chiral activity on nematic and polar uniaxial elastomers and solids by constructing the general hydrodynamic equations for these phases. We start with the elastomer, which is not only invariant under rigid rotations but also under an independent rotation of the uniaxial direction, and show that this higher symmetry strongly constrains the effects of chiral activity. We then break the elastomeric symmetry by adding a symmetry-breaking term to the free energy but still retain invariance under rigid rotations. The resulting state has the symmetry of a uniaxial solid, and we show that in a fixed background medium chiral activity generically destabilises it. The force density responsible for this {instability} vanishes as the field that breaks the elastomeric symmetry goes to zero. Further, the presence of the symmetry-breaking field also allows for an additional odd elastic force density that generically destabilises momentum-conserved chiral and uniaxial solids in the inertial regime.

</details>


### [280] [Chirality destroys oriented active solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25953)
*S. J. Kole,Gareth P. Alexander,Ananyo Maitra,Sriram Ramaswamy*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文构建了三维单轴固体中手性与主动力密度的流体力学理论，指出主动手性力密度通常会使固体失稳，但高对称性的极性弹性体（polar elastomer）可消除该不稳定性且其主导阶力学不受手性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究（如二维奇弹性 odd elasticity）已关注固体中的手性与主动效应，但三维单轴固体中主动手性力密度的作用及稳定性尚不清楚，且与生物体系（如细胞团在细胞外基质中迁移）相关。

Method: 采用流体力学理论（hydrodynamic theory）方法，在固定背景介质中分析主动手性力密度与主动力密度对三维单轴固体及手性各向同性固体的力学响应与稳定性影响，并考察极性弹性体等高对称材料。

Result: 主动手性力密度在梯度阶次上低于奇弹性，且通常使固体失稳；该不稳定性也影响各向异性介质中的各向同性手性固体；在极性弹性体中不稳定性被消除。此外，守恒动量的极性主动固体存在两个手性奇弹性系数（对比二维情形的一个），但极性弹性体的高对称性消除其中一个，剩余项等价于二维奇弹性力密度。

Conclusion: 手性与主动力密度对三维固体的稳定性有显著影响，材料对称性（如极性弹性体）可消除相关不稳定性；结论适用于多种生物与凝聚态体系。

Abstract: We construct a hydrodynamic theory of the effect of chiral and active force densities on three-dimensional uniaxial solids. We show that in a fixed background medium active chiral force densities---that arise at lower order in gradients than odd elasticity---generically destabilise the solid state. We further show this active instability also affects isotropic chiral solids in an anisotropic medium, a situation that is relevant for cell clusters moving through the extracellular matrix. Interestingly, the instability is eliminated in a higher-symmetry solid, known as the polar elastomer; indeed, we show that chirality does not affect the leading-order mechanics of such materials. We further demonstrate that momentum-conserving polar active solids have two chiral odd elasticities in contrast to the one discussed for two-dimensional solids. However, we again find that the higher symmetry of polar elastomers eliminates one of these with the remaining one being equivalent to the two-dimensional odd elastic force density. Our results should apply to numerous biological and condensed matter systems.

</details>


### [281] [evaluating local viscoelastic properties of artery-mimicking phantoms using high-resolution flexural wavenumber analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26075)
*Max Gattin,Julien de Rosny*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段高分辨率方法，从类动脉软管中的导波（guided waves）反演局部粘弹性（viscoelastic）材料参数：先用高分辨率复波数辨识（complex wavenumber identification）估计含损耗的局部频散关系（dispersion relation），再基于分数阶Kelvin-Voigt流变模型（fractional Kelvin-Voigt rheological model）与谱元法（spectral element method）拟合估计材料参数，并在充水软管实验中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 导波传播测量是评价动脉壁硬度（arterial wall stiffness）的有效手段，但更全面的粘弹性表征需要额外估计波衰减（attenuation）；同时，针对动脉粥样硬化斑块（atherosclerotic plaques）等局部病变，还需要空间局部化（spatially local）的估计。

Method: 方法分两阶段：第一阶段采用高分辨率复波数辨识方法估计含损耗的局部频率-波数频散关系；第二阶段假设管壁服从分数阶Kelvin-Voigt流变模型，将谱元法计算的频散关系与该估计拟合，从而反演粘弹性参数。实验上以高速相机结合数字图像相关（digital image correlation）测量充水软管中的弯曲波传播进行验证。

Result: 实验验证了方法的有效性；得益于方法的高分辨率，成功恢复了梯度模量（graded）体模的轴向模量变化。Cramér-Rao分析表明复波数估计器接近其理论极限，并揭示了局部衰减（local attenuation）本质上难以恢复的原因。

Conclusion: 该两阶段高分辨率框架可实现类动脉软管局部粘弹性参数的估计，支持局部化病理的粘弹性表征；同时分析指出局部衰减估计存在内在困难，为后续方法设计提供了理论依据。

Abstract: The measurement of guided wave propagation is an effective approach for evaluating arterial wall stiffness. A more comprehensive characterization of viscoelastic material properties could yield more informative biomarkers; however, this additionally requires estimation of wave attenuation. Furthermore, spatially local estimation is necessary for localized pathologies, such as atherosclerotic plaques. To this end, we propose an original two-stage high-resolution method to recover local viscoelastic properties from guided waves in artery-like soft tubes. First, the local frequency-wavenumber dispersion relation including losses is estimated using a high-resolution complex wavenumber identification method. Second, assuming a fractional Kelvin-Voigt rheological model for the tube wall, we estimate the viscoelastic material parameters by fitting the dispersion relation computed with a spectral element method to this estimate. We experimentally validate the proposed approach on flexural wave propagation along water-filled soft tubes, measured with a high-speed camera coupled with digital image correlation. Owing to the high resolution of the method, the axial modulus variation of a graded phantom is recovered. Finally, a Cramér-Rao analysis indicates that the complex wavenumber estimator operates near its theoretical limit, and reveals why local attenuation is intrinsically difficult to recover.

</details>


### [282] [Twitching fiberboids in the collective : A table-top, one-dimensional, active particle gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26087)
*Alex Arnhold,Falko Ziebert,Igor M. Kulić*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文报道了自滚动纤维（fiberboids）除持续滚动外的第二种运动状态——"抽动（twitching）"，并利用其在圆形受限区域中的集体行为构建了桌面级一维非平衡活性气体（one-dimensional non-equilibrium active gas）实验平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有自驱动纤维研究多集中于可持久滚动的纤维（由垂直于表面的通量驱动），其运动状态与集体行为尚未充分探索；本文旨在揭示纤维boid的另一种动力学状态并研究其集体效应。

Method: 采用热驱动的尼龙纤维在微弯曲玻璃表面上运动，实验观测并量化"twitching"状态；随后将纤维限制在圆形区域（circular arena）内使其仅沿单一轴滚动，构建一维活性气体，并建立考虑团簇内速度方向Viscek型对齐（Viscek-like alignment）的简单晶格模型（lattice model）进行解释。

Result: 发现纤维会短暂滚动后换向，在大尺度上实现类扩散运动（diffusion-like motion）；实验观测到活性团簇（active cluster）形成及其非平衡动力学，晶格模型与实验定性吻合良好。

Conclusion: 自滚动纤维集体可作为活性物质（active matter）的新类别，本文为研究其集体行为开辟了路径，并提供了简便的桌面级一维非平衡活性气体实现方案。

Abstract: Fiberboids are filaments that self-propel by rolling along a surface due to a flux normal to that surface. In the last few years, fibers using several materials and driving mechanisms have been developed that are able to roll persistently. Here we show using thermally driven nylon fibers on a slightly curved glass surface, that fiberboids also come in another state, that we call ``twitching'': the fiber rolls for a short time and then switches direction, implementing on large scales a diffusion-like motion. We rationalize the occurrence of this effect and experimentally quantify this new dynamic state. We then analyze ensembles of twitching fiberboids and by confining the fibers inside a circular arena to allow them to roll on a single axis only, we implement a simple, table-top realization of a one-dimensional non-equilibrium active gas. We study experimentally active cluster formation, their non-equilibrium dynamics and we rationalize the observations by a simple lattice model. This model takes into account a Viscek-like alignment of the velocity directions of the individual particles within clusters to yield good qualitative agreement with the experiments. Overall our work opens the path to study collectives of self-rolling fibers as an interesting new class of active matter.

</details>


### [283] [Superstability of micrometer viscous jets surrounded by weakly-elastic viscous shells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26150)
*A. Rubio-González,D. Fernández-Martínez,M. Vakili,J. M. Montanero*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 实验表明，黏弹性壳层（viscoelastic shell）对流动聚焦黏性射流（flow-focused viscous jets）具有显著稳定作用；当聚合物黏度超过临界阈值且伸展松弛时间足够大以发生线圈-伸展转变（coil-stretch transition）时，射流可被稳定，且所需聚合物浓度随壳层黏度升高而降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在阐明黏弹性壳层稳定黏性射流的物理条件，即聚合物黏度与伸展松弛时间需满足何种阈值关系，并考察壳层黏度对所需聚合物浓度的影响。

Method: 采用流动聚焦（flow-focusing）实验，在黏性射流外包裹黏弹性壳层，考察聚合物黏度（$η_p$）与伸展松弛时间（$λ$）对射流稳定性的作用，并分析液体加速过程中伸展黏弹性应力（extensional viscoelastic stresses）的发展。

Result: 当$η_p$超过临界阈值且$λ$足够大使聚合物分子完成线圈-伸展转变时，液体加速过程中产生显著的伸展黏弹性应力，射流趋于稳定；由于$η_p$与$λ$均随壳层黏度增大，稳定射流所需的聚合物浓度相应降低，中至高黏度射流可在极低聚合物浓度下稳定，且未观察到串珠状（beads-on-a-string, BOAS）不稳定性。

Conclusion: 黏弹性壳层的稳定效应由聚合物黏度阈值与伸展松弛时间共同控制，提高壳层黏度可显著降低所需聚合物浓度，从而实现极低浓度下对中高黏度射流的稳定，并抑制BOAS不稳定性。

Abstract: Our experiments demonstrate the strong stabilizing effect of a viscoelastic shell on flow-focused viscous jets. The results suggest that stabilization occurs when the polymer viscosity, $η_p$, exceeds a critical threshold, provided the extensional relaxation time, $λ$, is large enough to allow the polymer molecules to undergo the coil-stretch transition. Under these conditions, substantial extensional viscoelastic stresses develop as the liquid accelerates. Since both $η_p$ and $λ$ increase with shell viscosity, the polymer concentration required to stabilize the viscous jet decreases accordingly. Remarkably, moderately to highly viscous jets can be stabilized with very small polymer concentrations, for which no beads-on-a-string (BOAS) instability is observed.

</details>


### [284] [Tunable annealing of defects in self-aligning active crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26459)
*Kanka Ghosh,Andreas M. Menzel*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究证明，围绕平均极化态增强时空取向涨落（spatiotemporal orientational fluctuations）可提升活性晶体（active crystals）的晶序，实现从富含缺陷的多晶到无缺陷单晶的转变，并提出一条非平衡（nonequilibrium）的缺陷退火（defect annealing）路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 制备无缺陷单晶（defect-free single-crystals）在软物质（soft matter）与材料科学中兼具基础与应用价值；活性晶体（active crystals）涵盖从生命体系到人工超材料（metamaterials）的致密系统，具备自主结构重组（autonomous structural reorganization）的潜力，因此值得探究其晶序调控机制。

Method: 研究缺陷丰富、自取向的活性布朗晶体（active Brownian crystals），其平动—转动动力学相互耦合；通过将活性强度（activity magnitude）与其关联时间尺度解耦，考察持续长度（persistence length）与取向（alignment）时间尺度对缺陷退火能力的影响。

Result: 长寿命的持续性（persistence）配合缓慢取向可控制缺陷退火能力；调节持续长度能够克服热能势垒（thermal energy barriers）；同时调节持续性与取向时间尺度可使弛豫（relaxation）由"快"变"慢"，分别得到富含缺陷与无缺陷晶体。此外，点粒子活性晶体在通过调节速度弛豫时间尺度延迟群集（flocking）时也表现出类似的缺陷退火现象。

Conclusion: 该机制可能推广至更广泛的活性晶体类别；研究将生命软物质的统计物理（statistical physics）与缺陷及微结构力学相交叉，为可重构材料（reconfigurable materials）提出一条非平衡途径。

Abstract: Obtaining defect-free single-crystals is of crucial importance in soft matter and materials science, spanning both fundamental interests and practical applications. Active crystals, encompassing diverse dense systems from living organisms to artificial metamaterials, can offer a promising platform for autonomous structural reorganization. We demonstrate that increasing spatiotemporal orientational fluctuations around the mean polarized state can enhance crystallographic order in active crystals. Poly-to-single-crystal transformations in defect-rich, self-aligned active Brownian crystals with coupled translational-rotational dynamics are investigated. We show that long-lived persistence with slow alignment controls the defect-annealing capability. By disentangling activity magnitude from associated timescales, we reveal activity-assisted annealing. Tuning the persistence length enables overcoming thermal energy barriers. Simultaneously adjusting persistence and alignment timescales can tune relaxation from `fast' to `slow', yielding defect-rich and defect-free crystals, respectively. Furthermore, active crystals of point particles are found to exhibit similar defect annealing when flocking is delayed by tuning the timescale of the velocity relaxation towards the polarized state. These findings suggest that the mechanism may extend to a broader class of active crystals. Our results intersect statistical physics of living soft matter with the mechanics of defects and microstructures, proposing a nonequilibrium route towards reconfigurable materials.

</details>


### [285] [Modular Landau Analysis of Coupled Phase Transitions: Application to Ferroelectric Liquid Crystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26505)
*Sisi Guan,Yucen Han,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文提出模块化朗道分析框架，通过局域弛豫非临界母相变量构建有效自由能，并将其应用于铁电液晶多序参量耦合体系的相序预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合序参量系统中已有有序相的弛豫会影响后续相变，且新生模式间的协同与竞争可产生复杂相序列，既有理论分析难以刻画此类弛豫诱导效应与多模式耦合。

Method: 发展模块化朗道分析（modular Landau analysis），局域弛豫非临界母相（noncritical parent-phase）变量以得到有效单模式与双模式自由能，其系数刻画弛豫引起的相变特征变化与模式耦合；模块沿母相分支依次组装，通过比较各容许分支完全弛豫自由能选取平衡相序。随后用于铁电液晶（ferroelectric liquid crystals）单向模型并拟合实验数据。

Result: 框架应用于取向、极性、近晶与倾斜序相互作用的铁电液晶体系，可描述多竞争路径；单一热力学参数集可复现Strachan等的实验测得的相变温度、相变类型与相序，并捕捉主要双折射（birefringence）趋势；近晶层间距经拟合观测映射基于计算平衡倾角单独重构。

Conclusion: 模块化朗道分析为耦合序参量系统中的弛豫诱导相变与多模式耦合提供了系统化处理方式，并在铁电液晶中验证了其以单一参数集统一描述相序与部分观测量（双折射、层间距）的能力。

Abstract: In systems with coupled order parameters, the relaxation of existing order can modify subsequent transitions, while cooperation and competition between emerging modes can produce complex phase sequences. We develop a modular Landau analysis that locally relaxes noncritical parent-phase variables to obtain effective one- and two-mode free energies. The resulting coefficients quantify relaxation-induced changes in transition characteristics and mode coupling, including changes in transition order for even-symmetry modes. The resulting modules are assembled successively along parent branches, and the equilibrium phase sequence is selected by comparing the fully relaxed free energies of the admissible branches. We apply the framework to ferroelectric liquid crystals, where orientational, polar, smectic, and tilt orders interact and give rise to multiple competing pathways, using a uniaxial model with longitudinal polarization. We fit the model to the measurements reported by Strachan et al. [Adv. Sci. 12(3), 2409754 (2025)]. A single thermodynamic parameter set reproduces the observed transition temperatures, transition types, and phase sequence, while capturing the main birefringence trend. Smectic layer spacing is reconstructed separately through a fitted observation map based on the calculated equilibrium tilt.

</details>


### [286] [Dynamic clustering of active chemotactic Janus particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26657)
*Joël Mabillard,Frédéric van Wijland,Jean-Baptiste Fournier*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该工作将自驱动建模为涌现的扩散泳效应，在原始三维几何下以化学溶质浓度为控制参数，重访活性Janus胶体的动态团簇实验，发现动态团簇区占据相图中稳健的一部分，并借助线性稳定性分析解释气相失稳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解活性Janus胶体（active Janus colloids）动态团簇（dynamic clustering）实验，作者主张自驱动（self-propulsion）并非外加属性而是涌现的扩散泳（diffusiophoresis）效应，并希望回到实验原本的三维几何与化学浓度控制条件下给出解释。

Method: 将自驱动建模为涌现的扩散泳效应；在原始三维（three-dimensional）几何中进行研究，以化学溶质浓度（chemical solute concentration）作为控制参数绘制相图；并以线性稳定性分析（linear stability analysis）刻画气相向动态团簇的不稳定性。

Result: 动态团簇区占据相图中稳健的一定比例；线性稳定性分析能有效捕捉气相到动态团簇的不稳定性。动态团簇源于Keller-Segel塌缩（Keller-Segel collapse）与运动诱导相分离（motility-induced phase separation）的联合作用，两者被与化学场的取向耦合（orientational coupling）所抑制，与此前工作一致。

Conclusion: 在三维几何与化学浓度控制下，动态团簇是相图中的稳健特征；其机制可归为Keller-Segel塌缩与运动诱导相分离的叠加，并由取向耦合与化学场的相互作用所终止，印证了先前研究结论。

Abstract: Modeling self-propulsion as an emergent diffusiophoretic effect, we come back to the dynamic clustering experiment of active Janus colloids. Working in the original three-dimensional geometry, and using the chemical solute concentration as a control parameter, we find that the dynamic clustering region occupies a robust fraction of the phase diagram. This behavior is rationalized using a linear stability analysis that efficiently captures the instability of the gas phase to dynamic clusters. We also find that dynamic clustering results from the combined effects of a Keller-Segel collapse and of a motility-induced phase separation, either being arrested by the orientational coupling to the chemical field in agreement with previous work.

</details>


### [287] [Elastic theory of ideal liquid crystal elastomers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26688)
*Zumrat Usmanova,Ruobing Bai*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该综述系统梳理理想液晶弹性体（liquid crystal elastomer, LCE）的弹性理论，将单链统计与连续介质本构模型相衔接，阐明软弹性（soft elasticity）与自发变形的微观起源，并讨论向光热耦合、Frank弹性、非理想网络及黏弹性等情形的扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LCE中液晶基元有序度（mesogen order）与网络弹性之间存在双向耦合，产生大幅可逆刺激响应驱动与近乎零应力下的有限变形（软弹性）。为统一理解这些现象，需要一个贯通单链统计与连续介质描述的弹性理论框架。

Method: 基于各向异性高斯链（anisotropic Gaussian chains）无交联状态记忆、转动指向矢（director）不消耗自由能的"理想"假设，构造单畴LCE的新古典自由能（neo-classical free energy）；通过分析其非拟凸性（non-quasi-convexity）与拟凸松弛（quasi-convex relaxation），对多畴（polydomain）体系的宏观力学响应进行分类。

Result: 单畴新古典自由能非拟凸，存在连续的无应力基态族，从而解释热致自发变形与由共存条纹畴（stripe domains）承载的软弹性；其拟凸松弛将多畴LCE行为分为三个相，各相宏观力学响应随拉伸状态而不同。

Conclusion: 理想弹性理论可作为统一基础，进一步扩展以纳入热-力与光-力耦合、指向矢畸变引起的Frank弹性、网络非理想性导致的半软或非软行为，以及温度与速率依赖的黏弹性（viscoelasticity）。

Abstract: A liquid crystal elastomer (LCE) exhibits a two-way coupling between its mesogen order and network elasticity: the mesogen order sets the undeformed network conformation, and the network deformation reorients the mesogens. This coupling gives rise to large, reversible stimuli-responsive actuation, as well as to soft elasticity, a finite deformation at nearly zero stress. This article reviews the elastic theory of ideal LCEs, bridging single-chain statistics and continuum constitutive models. The "ideal" elastic behavior rests on the assumption of anisotropic Gaussian chains that retain no memory of the crosslinking state, such that rotating the director costs no free energy. The corresponding neo-classical free energy of a monodomain LCE is non-quasi-convex, with a continuous family of stress-free ground states, leading to thermally induced spontaneous deformation and soft elasticity accommodated by coexisting stripe domains. The quasi-convex relaxation of the free energy classifies the behavior of polydomain LCEs into three phases, each with a distinct macroscopic mechanical response depending on the stretch state. The ideal elastic theory can be further extended to incorporate additional behaviors and physical processes, including thermo- and photomechanical coupling, Frank elasticity from director distortion, semi-soft or non-soft behavior arising from network nonideality, and temperature- and rate-dependent viscoelasticity.

</details>


### [288] [Collective diffusion of anisotropic hard-particle mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26721)
*Anouar El Moumane,G. H. Philipp Nguyen,Klaus Mecke,Hartmut Löwen,René Wittmann*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文表明动力学密度泛函理论（Dynamical Density Functional Theory, DDFT）可简化为扩散方程（diffusion equation），并基于粒子间直接关联推导出对长时集体扩散系数的修正，解析求解了多种硬粒子体系的标量有效扩散系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测不同组分各向异性混合物（anisotropic mixtures）的动力学是理论上的难题；作者希望借助自由能泛函（free energy functional）驱动的单粒子密度（one-particle density）演化理论，找到可解析处理的简化途径。

Method: 将DDFT简化为扩散方程；由相互作用粒子间的直接关联（direct correlations）导出对常规长时集体扩散系数（collective diffusion coefficients）的修正，并对多个硬粒子（hard-particle）体系解析确定有效扩散系数（effective diffusion coefficients），包括硬球混合物、单轴纺锤柱（uniaxial spherocylinders）的各向同性相与向列相（nematic phase），以及双轴球三角（biaxial spherotriangles）。

Result: 获得了上述体系的解析有效扩散系数，并给出从直接关联修正长时集体扩散系数的量化框架，用以刻画动力学弛豫行为（dynamical relaxation behavior）。

Conclusion: 作者讨论了将该方法推广至具有位置序（positional order）体系的途径，并提出其可能应用于预测取向序参量（orientational order parameters）的弛豫时间。

Abstract: Predicting the dynamics of anisotropic mixtures of different species is a challenging theoretical task. We show that Dynamical Density Functional Theory (DDFT), a theory predicting time evolution of the one-particle-density through a free energy functional, can be simplified to a diffusion equation. To quantify the dynamical relaxation behavior, we deduce corrections to the regular long-time collective diffusion coefficients from the direct correlations between the interacting particles and determine these effective diffusion coefficients analytically for a number of hard-particle systems, including mixtures of hard spheres, as well as isotropic and nematic phases of uniaxial spherocylinders and biaxial spherotriangles. Finally, we discuss how to generalize our approach to systems with positional order and suggest a possible application to predicting relaxation times of orientational order parameters.

</details>


### [289] [Capillary forces between two identical spherical particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26806)
*Stefano Onofri,Thejas Hulikal Chakrapani,Stefan Luding,Elke Scholten,Ahmed Jarray,Wouter K. den Otter*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文系统比较了文献中常用的毛细桥力（capillary force）近似解析式与Young-Laplace方程数值解，指出其在接触角约40°以上、桥体积大于固体颗粒体积约2.5%时开始偏离，并提出覆盖接触角5°–80°、桥体积10⁻⁶–0.15的高精度高效表达式；同时用多体耗散粒子动力学（MDPD）验证了介观"流体尺度"模拟可再现连续介质毛细力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶体悬浮液（colloidal suspensions）与颗粒物质（granular matter）在加入少量可形成毛细桥（capillary bridges）的液体后，宏观力学与流变行为剧烈变化，因此需要可靠的桥力模型用于颗粒尺度模拟。Young-Laplace方程虽已知但缺乏便捷解析解，现有模拟多依赖适用范围有限（接触角≲40°、桥体积≲2.5%）的近似表达式。

Method: 将文献中若干常用近似表达式与理论毛细力的数值解进行对比，评估其偏差范围；在此基础上拟合/构造新的高精度且计算高效的毛细力表达式；并采用多体耗散粒子动力学（Multi-body Dissipative Particle Dynamics, MDPD）开展介观流体尺度模拟，检验粗粒化流体模型能否再现连续介质毛细力。

Result: 在上述原有参数范围内，近似表达式与数值解总体吻合良好，但超出其预设适用范围后出现明显偏差；作者提出的新表达式覆盖接触角5°–80°与桥体积10⁻⁶–0.15。MDPD模拟结果与连续介质毛细力吻合良好，即使液桥仅由数十个流体粒子描述亦然。

Conclusion: 该研究为毛细桥模拟提供了两种不同分辨率尺度的途径：一是高精度、计算高效的解析表达式，二是基于MDPD的介观模拟，二者均可在大范围桥体积与接触角下可靠描述毛细力。

Abstract: The properties of colloidal suspensions and granular matter change drastically upon adding a small amount of liquid capable of forming capillary bridges between the solid particles. The attractive forces exerted by these liquid bridges induce the formation of particle aggregates and networks, which radically changes the macroscopic mechanical and rheological behaviour. Predicting these forces across bridge sizes, particle separations and wettability is therefore central to particle-scale modelling of capillary suspensions and wet granular matter. While the Young-Laplace equation governing the properties of the liquid bridges is well known, it lacks a convenient analytical solution. Current simulations therefore employ approximate analytic expressions, typically developed for contact angles below ~40° and bridge volumes below ~2.5% of the solid particle's volume. We compare several frequently used expressions from the literature with numerical solutions of the theoretical capillary forces. An overall good agreement is observed in the aforementioned parameter ranges, while the approximate expressions start to deviate outside their intended domains. We propose accurate and computationally efficient expressions for the capillary force that model this behaviour, for contact angles from 5° to 80° and bridge volumes from $10^{-6}$ to 0.15. We also explore mesoscopic 'fluid-scale' simulations of bridges by Multi-body Dissipative Particle Dynamics (MDPD), to ascertain whether this coarse-grained fluid model faithfully captures the continuum capillary forces. Remarkably, a good agreement is observed even for liquid bridges described by a mere few dozen fluid particles. Together, these results offer two approaches, at different resolution scales, for simulating bridges over a wide range of bridge volumes and contact angles.

</details>


<div id='math.CO'></div>

# math.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [290] [Planar Disk Filters and Real Chromatic Roots at Beraha Numbers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23989)
*Reza Mactabi*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文构造了一类简单的平面三角剖分（plane triangulations），使其色多项式（chromatic polynomial）的实根在每个非整数 Beraha 数 \(B_\ell=4\cos^2(\pi/\ell)\) 处累积，从而把 Beraha 数作为实根极限点予以实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Beraha 数长期被视为色多项式零点可能的累积点，但此前缺乏在普通简单无权重平面三角剖分中实现任意非整数 Beraha 数的统一构造。本文旨在给出此类显式构造，并规避对单位根处半单性（semisimplicity）的依赖。

Method: 核心观察是：含四个边界顶点的三角化圆盘（triangulated disk）在大三角剖分中充当转移算子（transfer operator），属于一个交换的三参数代数。将此类圆盘旋转四分之一圈并串联复合，可可控地移动其特征值三元组，从而构造“物理滤波器”——第三个特征值严格支配两个竞争局部特征值。用两极夹住圆盘网格得到球面三角剖分，并用融合 Temperley–Lieb 迹恒等式（fused Temperley–Lieb trace identity）分扇区完全刻画其色多项式；单帽扇区用 Krein 型论证（Krein-type argument）证明每次扫描字恰有一个扩张特征值，其余扇区由 Gram 型惯性（Gram-form inertia）一致控制。奇数 Beraha 指标下扩张特征值的迹系数消失，偶数指标下两份该特征值在相邻扇区相消，再借实数变量引理转化为收敛到目标的实根。指标小于十一的情形用初等族、精确外舍入整数证书（outward-rounded integer certificates）以及 \(B_{10}\) 处一个显式圆盘结合 Tutte 黄金恒等式（Tutte's golden identity）处理。

Result: 所构造的普通、简单、无权重平面三角剖分的色多项式具有实根，且这些实根在每个非整数 Beraha 数处累积；整数 0,1,2,3 作为 \(K_4\) 的根单独出现。构造对所有非整数 Beraha 指标均成立，包括指标小于十一的补充处理。

Conclusion: Beraha 数可作为实色根的累积点，在简单平面三角剖分这一受限图类中通过转移算子代数与融合 Temperley–Lieb 迹恒等式实现；该论证不依赖单位根处的半单性，也不涉及奇异的更高阶 Jones–Wenzl 投影子。

Abstract: We construct simple plane triangulations whose chromatic polynomials have real roots accumulating at every noninteger Beraha number $B_\ell=4\cos^2(π/\ell)$; the integer values $0,1,2,3$ are roots of $K_4$. The construction rests on the observation that a triangulated disk with four boundary vertices acts, inside a larger triangulation, as a transfer operator lying in a commutative three-parameter algebra. Rotating such disks by a quarter turn and composing them in series moves their eigenvalue triples around this algebra in a controllable way, and for every fixed $q\in(B_{10},4)$ one can manufacture a finite disk whose two competing local eigenvalues are strictly dominated by the third -- a physical filter. Closing a grid of such disks between two poles produces a sphere triangulation, and a fused Temperley-Lieb trace identity accounts for its chromatic polynomial completely, sector by sector, with no appeal to semisimplicity at the root of unity itself -- generic semisimplicity enters only through polynomial identities continued from $δ>2$ -- and no singular higher Jones-Wenzl projectors. In the one-cap sector a Krein-type argument shows that every sweep word has exactly one expanding eigenvalue; Gram-form inertia controls every other sector uniformly in the width. At odd Beraha indices the trace coefficient of the expanding eigenvalue vanishes; at even indices two copies of that eigenvalue cancel between adjacent sectors, and a short real-variable lemma converts either situation into genuine real roots converging to the target. Indices below eleven are treated by elementary families, exact outward-rounded integer certificates, and one explicit disk at $B_{10}$ combined with Tutte's golden identity. All graphs are ordinary, simple, unweighted plane triangulations.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [291] [Multiple propagating modes in random polycrystals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24047)
*Ming Huang,Stanislav I. Rokhlin,Michael J. S. Lowe*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用三维多晶体的晶粒尺度有限元模拟，在频率—波数平面上直接观测到多重散射理论预言的相干弹性波多根现象；实际可传播模态数由衰减层级而非根的数目决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重散射理论（multiple-scattering theory）早已预言统计各向同性多晶体中的相干弹性波并非单一波，其色散方程对给定偏振存在多个根，但这些额外模态此前从未被观测到，亟需直接验证。

Method: 采用三维多晶体（three-dimensional polycrystals）的晶粒尺度有限元模拟（grain-scale finite-element simulations），在频率—波数（frequency–wavenumber）平面上解析相干场（coherent field），针对横波与纵波激发分别进行测量。

Result: 对横波和纵波激发均观测到预言的多重性：低频单模态在波长接近晶粒相关长度（grain correlation length）的几何区（geometric regime）分裂为两个模态；实测模态与色散方程的根一致，且该方程总含第三个根，其衰减（attenuation）超过其余根。

Conclusion: 决定可传播与可观测模态数量的是衰减层级（attenuation hierarchy）而非根的数目；随机介质中的相干波传播在几何区本质上是多模态的，而该工作首次在多晶体中达到这一区域。

Abstract: Multiple-scattering theory predicts that the coherent elastic wave in a statistically isotropic polycrystal is not a single wave. Its dispersion equation admits multiple roots of a given polarization, but these extra modes have never been observed. Here, using grain-scale finite-element simulations of three-dimensional polycrystals, we resolve the coherent field in the frequency--wavenumber plane and directly observe the predicted multiplicity for both transverse and longitudinal excitations: a single mode at low frequencies gives way to two in the geometric regime as the wavelength approaches the grain correlation length. The measured modes follow the roots of the dispersion equation, which always carries a third root whose attenuation exceeds that of the others. It is this attenuation hierarchy, not the root count, that dictates how many modes propagate and can be observed. Coherent wave propagation in random media is thus inherently multimodal in the geometric regime, which we reach here for the first time in polycrystals.

</details>


### [292] [Role of chirality in the polarizer-filter dichotomy in nonmagnetic junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24226)
*Sergei Urazhdin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 手性诱导自旋选择性（chirality-induced spin selectivity, CISS）中“极化器”与“滤波器”的功能分工由结（junction）的整体对称性决定，而非仅取决于分子手性；并据此提出可区分二者的非局域测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非磁性结可借助CISS实现自旋生成，但其对称性判据（symmetry requirements）长期存在争议，且线性两端磁阻（magnetoresistance）因互易性（reciprocity）无法探测极化，亟需澄清对称性条件并给出可行探测手段。

Method: 从结的对称性分析出发区分自旋极化器（spin polarizer）与自旋滤波器（spin filter），论证对称性作用于整个结而非仅分子；并以最小紧束缚模型（minimal tight-binding model）验证非手性分子在不同结几何下的两类行为，同时设计非局域测量（nonlocal measurement）方案。

Result: 自旋极化器要求手性结（chiral junction），而非手性结（achiral junction）可作为自旋滤波器；非手性分子依结几何可表现为极化器或滤波器，紧束缚模型结果与对称性分析一致；互易性禁止线性两端磁阻实现极化探测，非局域测量则可区分极化器与滤波器并判定结是否破坏时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）。

Conclusion: CISS的对称性判据应以结整体为单位，分子手性并非必要条件；实验上宜采用非局域测量替代两端磁阻来甄别极化器/滤波器并检验时间反演对称性破缺。

Abstract: Chirality-induced spin selectivity can enable spin generation by nonmagnetic junctions, but its symmetry requirements remain debated. We show that the symmetry of the junction constrains its polarizing behavior: a spin polarizer requires a chiral junction, whereas an achiral junction can act as a spin filter. This symmetry involves the junction as a whole, not only the molecule. Achiral molecules can act as polarizers or filters depending on junction geometry, as confirmed with a minimal tight-binding model. Reciprocity forbids polarization detection by linear two-terminal magnetoresistance; we propose a nonlocal measurement that distinguishes polarizers from filters and establishes whether the junction breaks time-reversal symmetry.

</details>


### [293] [Charge disproportionation and spin ordering in LiV$_2$O$_5$ and NaV$_2$O$_5$: a distributed moment point of view](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24263)
*Claudio Garcia,Ilteris K. Turan,Jerome Jackson,Swagata Acharya,Mark van Schilfgaarde,Walter R. L. Lambrecht*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作基于QSGW能带与线性响应方法，研究LiV2O5与NaV2O5中V4+/V5+电荷歧化（charge disproportionation）对交换相互作用及磁、电子、光学性质的影响，并在P2₁mn与Pmmn两种空间群下比较奈尔温度、介电函数与自旋波色散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的在于厘清钒氧化物中V4+与V5+之间电荷歧化如何影响局域磁矩间的交换相互作用，以及由此决定的磁有序温度与相关电子、光学响应，并检验不同晶体结构与近似方法的适用性。

Method: 采用基于QSGW能带结构的线性响应方法计算原子位点间局域磁矩的交换相互作用；在平均场近似（mean-field approximation, MFA）与随机相近似（random phase approximation, RPA）下计算奈尔温度（Néel temperature），并以(S+1)/S量子统计因子修正；用Bethe–Salpeter方程（Bethe Salpeter Equation, BSE）方法计算光学介电函数，并由含V与O磁矩的经典海森堡哈密顿量（Heisenberg Hamiltonian）求解自旋波。

Result: 钒氧基O_v与桥氧O_b上的诱导磁矩虽小，但其与V的交换相互作用大于V原子之间的相互作用；计算所得临界温度经量子统计因子修正后与实验较为吻合。除原胞中两条自旋链的低能弱相互作用自旋波外，模型还给出约1 eV、色散很弱的高能光学自旋波，对应V、O自旋不再保持基态共线锁定而各自进动的激发。

Conclusion: 作者认为这些高能光学自旋波是海森堡模型超出其适用范围的假象（artefact），在允许横向自旋与纵向自旋及电荷激发耦合的更完整理论中应被抑制；该结论界定了海森堡描述在电荷歧化钒氧化物中的有效边界。

Abstract: The effects of charge disproportionation between V$^{4+}$ and V$^{5+}$ on the exchange interactions and related magnetic, electronic and optical properties are studied in LiV$_2$O$_5$ and NaV$_2$O$_5$. The magnetic exchange interactions between local moments on atomic sites are calculated using a linear response approach based on the QS$GW$ band structure. While the induced magnetic moments are small on the vanadyl O$_v$ and bridge O$_b$, their exchange interactions with the V are found to be larger than between V atoms. The Néel temperature $T_N$ are calculated within the mean-field (MFA) and random phase (RPA) approximations for two different crystal structures with $P2_1mn$ and $Pmmn$ spacegroups. The critical temperatures are are corrected by a quantum statistical factor of (S+1)/S to obtain reasonable agreement with experiment.
  Band structures, optical dielectric functions calculated using the Bethe Salpeter Equation method and spin waves are also calculated and presented for both crystal structures and both compounds. The spin wave dispersions are calculated from the classical Heisenberg Hamiltonian including magnetic moments on both V and O atoms. Besides the lowest energy weakly interacting spin waves of the two chains of spins in the unit cell, the model predicts additional high energy optical spin waves, which correspond to more complex spin excitations in which the spins on the two V across a bridge, vanadyl O and bridge O precess independently from each other instead of staying locked together according to their collinear alignment in the ground state. These show only weak dispersion and extend up to about 1 eV but we argue that these are an artefact of using the Heisenberg model beyond its validity range and would be suppressed in a more complete theory that allows for coupling of the transverse spin with longitudinal spin and charge excitations.

</details>


### [294] [Tunable anisotropy of high-harmonic generation in ferroelectric NbOI2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24302)
*Inho Lee,Christopher Lane,Daniel A. Rehn,Jian-Xin Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究在铁电材料NbOI2中观测到二次与四次谐波在近红外波段具有不同的波长依赖性与各向异性，并揭示其源于费米能级附近窄带间跃迁偶极矩阵元与带间激发的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反演对称性破缺（inversion symmetry breaking）产生的偶数阶谐波谱已被广泛认识，但各阶谐波强度与角向各向异性（angular anisotropy）的可调性仍不清晰，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在铁电（ferroelectric）NbOI2中进行近红外波段的二次与四次谐波测量，将入射光波长从2150 nm调至2450 nm，考察各阶谐波偏振偏好轴的演化，并结合跃迁偶极矩阵元（transition dipole matrix elements）与带间激发（interband excitations）进行分析。

Result: 随波长从2150 nm调至2450 nm，二次谐波始终在沿铁电极轴（ferroelectric polar axis）激发时最强，而四次谐波的偏振偏好轴发生改变；该效应由费米能级附近极窄能带间的跃迁偶极矩阵元与带间激发的共同作用驱动。

Conclusion: 高阶谐波的可调各向异性（tunable anisotropy）有望用于新型光开关器件（optical switching devices）。

Abstract: he generation of even-order harmonic spectra from inversion symmetry breaking is well known, however, the tunability of the intensity and angular anisotropy on various harmonics orders remains unclear. Here, we demonstrate the second and fourth-harmonics in the ferroelectric NbOI2 exhibit distinct wavelength-dependence and anisotropies in the near-infrared regime. In particular, as the wavelength of incident light is tuned from 2150 nm to 2450 nm, the second-harmonic remains strongest for excitation along the ferroelectric polar axis, whereas the polarization of the fourth- harmonic changes preferred axis. This effect is found to be driven by the interplay of transition dipole matrix elements and interband excitations between very narrow bands near the Fermi level. We expect the tunable anisotropy of high-harmonics to enable novel optical switching devices.

</details>


### [295] [Atomistic prediction of magnetic behaviour across chemistry, structure and temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24407)
*Ruosong Shi,Chengbing Chen,Bicheng Liu,Daye Zheng,Duo Zhang,Zhengtao Huang,Teng Yang,Zefeng Cai,Xingliang Peng,Xinzijian Liu,Shenshen Yan,Xueqing Zhao,Jianbo Wang,Bin Liu,Han Wang,Ke Wang,Liuliu Han,Ben Xu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出原子尺度基础模型 MagPT，在 48 种元素、912,492 个自旋-晶格构型上预训练，覆盖 0~1,000 K 与 0~10 GPa 范围内的结构、取向与纵向磁涨落，实现对磁相与磁性质的通用预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过成分与外部条件调控磁相与磁性质是磁性材料开发的核心，但磁材料中原子结构与局域磁矩强耦合，温度与无序又驱动磁矩进入取向和大小不断变化的非共线态；现有广泛预训练的原子模型仅覆盖有限磁性多样性，而有限温度下晶格-磁涨落的耦合描述多局限于特定材料。

Method: 构建 MagPT 模型，将元素种类（species）、原子位置与矢量磁矩（vector moments）联合表征，并在跨 48 种元素、912,492 个自旋-晶格构型（spin-lattice configurations）的数据集上预训练，数据涵盖 0~1,000 K 与 0~10 GPa 条件下的结构、取向与纵向磁涨落。

Result: MagPT 可区分原子结构相同但自旋构型与能量不同的磁态；并在多种体系与尺度上再现磁性行为，包括过渡金属合金中的 Slater-Pauling 型成分-磁性关系、元素铁磁体与磁性形状记忆 Heusler 合金的有限温度相演化、手性磁体（chiral magnets）中涌现的长波长非共线自旋织构，以及高熵合金（high-entropy alloys）中局域化学-磁性耦合。

Conclusion: MagPT 使磁性材料的原子尺度研究超越材料特定（material-specific）方法，为跨多种化学组成与条件探索和设计磁性物质建立了通用框架。

Abstract: Controlling magnetic phases and properties through composition and external conditions is central to the development of magnetic materials. Atomistic foundation models offer a route to predicting such behaviour across diverse materials, but magnetic materials remain challenging because their atomic structure and local moments are strongly coupled, while temperature and disorder drive magnetic moments into non-collinear states with evolving orientations and magnitudes. Broadly pretrained atomistic models still sample only limited magnetic diversity, while finite-temperature descriptions of coupled lattice and magnetic fluctuations remain largely material-specific. Here we introduce MagPT, pretrained across 48 elements on 912,492 spin-lattice configurations spanning structural, orientational and longitudinal magnetic fluctuations over 0 $\sim$ 1,000 K and 0 $\sim$ 10 GPa. By treating species, atomic positions and vector moments together, MagPT distinguishes magnetic states that share the same atomic structure but differ in spin configuration and energy. It captures magnetic behaviour across composition, temperature and length scales, from Slater-Pauling-type composition-magnetism relations in transition-metal alloys and finite-temperature phase evolution in elemental ferromagnets and magnetic shape-memory Heusler alloys, to emergent long-wavelength non-collinear spin textures in chiral magnets and local chemistry-magnetism coupling in high-entropy alloys. MagPT moves atomistic studies of magnetic materials beyond material-specific approaches, establishing a general framework for exploring and designing magnetic matter across diverse chemistries and conditions.

</details>


### [296] [Reticulating Local Dynamics in Three-Dimensional Covalent Organic Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24418)
*Francesco Tavani,Saber Mirzaei,M. Saeed Mirzaei,Jian Yin,Yen-hsu Lin,Caden Myers,Cheng-Hung Lin,AM Milinda Abeykoon,Simon J. L. Billinge,Omar M. Yaghi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合同步辐射X射线对分布函数（pair distribution function, PDF）分析与机器学习势（machine learning interatomic potentials）加速的分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟，解析并比较了两种三维亚胺连接共价有机框架（covalent organic frameworks, COFs）的局部动力学，揭示构筑单元几何构型可调控骨架的局部运动模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在COFs中，构筑单元的几何构型预先决定了框架结构，但将COF连接单元（linker）与支配客体吸附、扩散及力学响应的局部运动相关联的可比设计原则仍未能建立。

Method: 采用同步辐射X射线PDF分析与机器学习势加速的MD模拟相结合的方法，比较两种以相同四连接方形平面连接单元PyTTA为基础、分别与12连接纳米石墨烯和6连接三蝶烯（triptycene）连接单元网络化形成的三维亚胺COFs（COF-412与COF-564）的局部动力学。

Result: PDF-MD方法解析了所有COF连接单元运动的幅度、时间尺度与空间范围，区分了稠环芳核的有限天平动（libration）、其所带侧挂芳环的较大重取向倾斜以及整个连接单元在网络中的平移；模拟表明以三蝶烯替换纳米石墨烯连接单元可调节PyTTA的平衡倾斜角与位置概率密度，提高其连接伙伴的倾斜幅度，并显著增强相邻连接单元平移之间的耦合。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解COFs的局部动力学性质提供了范式，表明这些性质可通过构筑单元的选择加以选取并具备可设计性。

Abstract: In covalent organic frameworks (COFs), the geometry of the building units predetermines the structure of the framework, yet comparable design principles relating the COF linkers to the local motions that govern guest adsorption and diffusion as well as mechanical response have remained beyond reach. Here, we combine synchrotron X-ray pair distribution function (PDF) analysis with molecular dynamics (MD) simulations accelerated by machine learning interatomic potentials to resolve and compare the local dynamics of two three-dimensional imine-linked COFs, chosen to differ in the geometry and rigidity of one of their two building units. Both are constructed from the same 4-connected square planar linker, $4,4',4'',4'''$-(pyrene-1,3,6,8-tetrayl)tetraaniline (PyTTA), reticulated with the 12-connected nanographene hexakis{[3,5-bis($p$-formylphenyl)-4,6-dimethoxyphenyl]}hexabenzocoronene to give COF-412, {[(HBC-LA$_{12}$)(PyTTA)$_3$]}$_{\mathit{imine}}$, and with the 6-connected triptycene 2,3,6,7,14,15-hexakis(4-formylphenyl)triptycene to give COF-564, {[(Trip-CHO)$_2$(PyTTA)$_3$]}$_{\mathit{imine}}$. The PDF-MD approach resolves the amplitude, the time scale, and the spatial extent of the motion of all COF linkers, separating the limited libration of the fused aromatic cores from the larger reorientational tilts of the pendant aryl rings they carry and from the translation of whole linkers within the net. The simulations further reveal that exchanging the nanographene- for the triptycene-based linker tunes the equilibrium tilt and the positional probability density of PyTTA, raising the tilt amplitude of its linker partner, and, notably, strengthens the coupling between the translations of neighbouring linkers. This approach provides a blueprint for understanding local dynamical properties in COFs, and shows them to be selectable through the choice of building unit and amenable to design.

</details>


### [297] [A GRACE Interatomic Potential for W--Ta Systems: Application to Ta Segregation and Pinning of Shear-Coupled Grain-Boundary Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24432)
*Omar Hussein,Laurent Capolungo,Danny Perez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文在GRACE框架下构建了可迁移的W-Ta原子间势（interatomic potential），训练数据通过熵最大化自动生成，并在多类物性与晶界偏析/迁移机制上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钨钽合金（tungsten-tantalum alloys）是有前景的聚变堆面向等离子体材料（plasma-facing materials），但其缺陷、力学与微结构耦合演化模拟需要在大范围原子环境下均保持精确的原子间势。

Method: 在GRaph Atomic Cluster Expansion（GRACE）框架内构建可迁移W-Ta势，主要训练集通过在原子相关特征空间中最大化信息熵（information entropy）自动生成，避免对特定下游应用所需结构与过程的人工枚举。

Result: 广泛验证显示其在单质与二元相稳定性（phase stability）、弹性（elasticity）、声子（phonons）、高压响应、表面、点缺陷（point defects）、位错能量学（dislocation energetics）、相变路径、合金热力学（alloy thermodynamics）及熔化行为上描述一致；应用于Σ5(120) W晶界（GB）时，Ta呈现强位点选择性偏析（site-selective segregation），偏析Ta阻碍由位错连接（disconnection）介导的晶界迁移，去钉扎应力（de-pinning stress）随Ta浓度先增后趋平台。

Conclusion: 研究建立了经验证范围较广的W-Ta势，并表明熵最大化自动数据生成可产生可迁移的原子尺度模型，能够解析训练中未显式针对的机制。

Abstract: Tungsten-tantalum alloys are promising plasma-facing materials for fusion reactors, but simulations of their coupled defect, mechanical, and microstructural evolution require an interatomic potential that remains accurate across diverse atomic environments. We develop a transferable W-Ta potential within the GRaph Atomic Cluster Expansion (GRACE) framework. The primary training dataset was generated automatically by maximizing information entropy in an atomically relevant feature space, avoiding manual enumeration of the structures and processes required for specific downstream applications. Extensive validation demonstrates a consistent description of unary and binary phase stability, elasticity, phonons, high-pressure response, surfaces, point defects, dislocation energetics, transformation pathways, alloy thermodynamics, and melting behavior.
  As a demonstration, the potential is applied to Ta segregation and shear-coupled migration of a $\Sigma5(120)$ W GB. Simulations reveal strongly site-selective segregation: Ta preferentially occupies a small subset of crystallographically distinct atomic rows. Under shear, segregated Ta impedes disconnection-mediated grain boundary migration. The de-pinning stress initially increases with Ta concentration but plateaus as the dominant pinning environment becomes saturated, while increasing temperature weakens both segregation and pinning. These results establish a broadly validated W-Ta potential and demonstrate how automated entropy-maximized data generation can produce transferable atomistic models capable of resolving mechanisms not explicitly targeted during training.

</details>


### [298] [Accurate Atomic Correlation Energies and Virial Trends for Correlation-Consistent Effective Core Potentials III: Quantitative Separation of Fixed-Node Biases and Localization Errors in Cs-Rn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24450)
*Omar Madany,Lubos Mitas*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作以耦合簇（coupled-cluster）至 CCSDT(Q) 与全组态相互作用（full configuration interaction）方法，在相关一致有效核势（ccECP）下建立第六周期元素（Cs–Rn）亚毫哈特里精度的原子基准，并系统刻画固定节点扩散蒙特卡洛（FNDMC）中的能量分配与偏差行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第六周期元素缺乏高精度原子基准，且赝势（pseudopotential）选择如何影响能量分配、节点拓扑（nodal topology）以及固定节点误差与定域误差的相对大小尚不明确，需要系统性参考数据加以澄清。

Method: 在 ccECP 框架下采用耦合簇方法（最高至 CCSDT(Q)）与全组态相互作用建立参考值，并据此分析电子关联、势能与动能分配比例（ξ）、固定节点与定域偏差（η），结合三维节点截面考察试探波函数（trial wavefunction）的节点结构。

Result: 价层动能占总能比 ξ 偏离非相对论全电子库仑维里极限（ξ=100%），介于 25.8%–37.6%；半芯壳层的去赝势化降低 ξ，ξ 呈 5d 平台（约 34%）、Hf 处镧系收缩驱动的极大值及 6p 区快速下降；η 在第 1/2 族起点与 6p 闭壳极限附近迅速升高，Rn 中达 5.42%，Bi 出现定域误差反转，Pb 误差量级相当，Tl 中三维节点截面显示束缚行走者的孤立环面区域。

Conclusion: 该研究完成了从 H 到 Rn 的全周期表 ccECP 数据库，揭示赝势选择对能量分配、节点拓扑及固定节点误差与定域误差平衡的支配作用。

Abstract: Atomic benchmarks for sixth-period elements (Cs-Rn) are established to sub-millihartree precision under correlation-consistent effective core potentials (ccECPs) using coupled-cluster [up to CCSDT(Q)] and full configuration interaction methods. These references systematically characterize electron correlation, potential-to-kinetic energy partitioning, and combined fixed-node and locality biases ($η$) in fixed-node diffusion Monte Carlo (FNDMC). The valence kinetic-to-total energy ratio ($ξ$) departs from the non-relativistic all-electron Coulombic virial limit ($ξ=100\%$), remaining confined between 25.8$\%$ and 37.6$\%$. The Un-pseudizing of semi-core shells decreases $ξ$ as the attractive potential energy deepens faster than the accompanying kinetic energy escalates. Periodic trends in $ξ$ reveal a $5d$ plateau near 34$\%$, with a lanthanide-contraction-driven maximum in Hf, followed by a sharp decline across the $6p$ block. In FNDMC, $η$ escalates rapidly at the onset of group-1/2 and toward the $6p$ closed-shell limit, peaking at 5.42$\%$ in Rn. Although fixed-node errors dominate $η$ in most sixth-period elements, a locality error inversion occurs in Bi, with Pb exhibiting comparable error magnitudes. Furthermore, core-valence partitioning alters trial wavefunction nodal topology: in Tl, three-dimensional nodal cross-sections reveal isolated toroidal domains that confine walkers. This work completes the periodic-table-wide ccECP database from H to Rn, revealing how pseudopotential choices govern energy partitioning, nodal topology, and the balance between fixed-node and localization errors.

</details>


### [299] [Understanding compression-extension error asymmetry in universal machine-learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24514)
*Hongwei Du,Dingyang Lv,Boyan Zhang,Yu Ren,Feng Yu,Xin He,Bonan Zhu,Yongda Huang,Jiahui Liu,Yongheng Li,Siqi Shi,Jianjun Liu,Ziheng Lu,Hong Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究以11种预训练通用机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potentials）为对象，系统比较同一母结构沿配对压缩与拉伸路径的可靠性，发现±10%应变下所有模型在压缩方向的中位力误差更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器学习原子间势已广泛用于材料研究，但其在方向性变形（directional deformation）下的可靠性尚不明确，且以往评估较少区分压缩与拉伸路径的对称性，缺乏系统性基准。

Method: 构建包含1,963个Materials Project轨迹（MPtrj）母结构与1,286个实验来源MinX-Eq母结构的基准，覆盖1%–10%六个应变幅度；以密度泛函理论（density-functional theory）提供参考，MPtrj含各向同性、单轴与双轴加载，MinX-Eq为各向同性加载；并将误差增长分解为DFT力尺度增长与相对误差演化，辅以径向与SOAP分析。

Result: 在±10%应变下，每种模型的压缩中位力误差均大于拉伸；各向同性压缩/拉伸误差比合并值为MPtrj 2.12、MinX-Eq 3.30。相对误差在两条分支上均下降，MPtrj中压缩侧下降更快，MinX-Eq中压缩侧反而更慢；力尺度匹配后误差比趋于1，但MinX-Eq仍保留压缩侧超额误差。压缩结构间距更短、偏离公共参考池更远，读出相关模型特征在压缩侧位移更大。

Conclusion: 结果将更强的物理响应与相对预测精度的变化区分开来，表明压缩-拉伸不对称性同时源于力尺度差异与相对误差演化，并为在变形模拟中评估通用势提供了基准方法。

Abstract: Universal machine-learning interatomic potentials are increasingly used across diverse materials, yet their reliability under directional deformation remains unclear. We compare compression and extension along paired deformation paths from the same parent structures for eleven pretrained potentials. The benchmark comprises 1,963 Materials Project trajectory (MPtrj) parents and 1,286 experimental-source MinX-Eq parents at six strain magnitudes from 1% to 10%. Density-functional theory provides references for isotropic, uniaxial and biaxial loading in MPtrj and isotropic loading in MinX-Eq. At $\pm 10\%$, every model has a larger median force error under compression, with pooled isotropic compression-to-extension ratios of 2.12 and 3.30 for MPtrj and MinX-Eq. Decomposing error growth into DFT force-scale growth and relative-error evolution shows that relative errors decrease on both branches, faster under compression in MPtrj but slower under compression in MinX-Eq. Force-scale matching moves the error ratios toward unity, although MinX-Eq retains a compression-side excess across models. Radial and SOAP analyses place compressed structures at shorter separations and farther from public reference pools, while readout-associated model features show larger compression-side displacements. These results distinguish stronger physical responses from changes in relative predictive accuracy and provide a basis for assessing universal potentials in deformation simulations.

</details>


### [300] [Structural signatures of a topological transition in a disordered tetrahedral network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24574)
*Florence Ellary,Thomas C. Nicholas,Dwaipayan Charkabarti,Andrew L. Goodwin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究表明，无需依赖实验上难以获取的纠缠描述符，仅凭基于环统计的中程拓扑描述符（CARVS）以及径向分布函数、结构因子等实验可及结构量，即可识别无序四面体网络材料中由压力驱动的拓扑转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑转变通常采用环连接（ring linking）、缠绕数（writhe）等纠缠（entanglement）描述符表征，但这些量实验上难以获取，因此需要寻找更易与实验对接的结构描述符来识别拓扑转变。

Method: 重新分析一个已知存在压力驱动非纠缠—纠缠态转变的胶体四面体网络模型构型，采用累积全环顶点符号（cumulative all-rings vertex symbol, CARVS）这一基于环统计的中程网络拓扑描述符进行表征，并与正则连续随机网络（continuous random networks）进行比较。

Result: CARVS 虽不含环连接的显式信息，却能清晰给出该拓扑转变的特征信号；网络纠缠还引起径向分布函数（radial distribution function）与结构因子（structure factor）的特征变化，包括非键合节点间短程关联的出现以及低角散射前峰（pre-peak）的消失。

Conclusion: 研究确立了无序四面体网络中拓扑转变的实验可及结构特征；与正则连续随机网络的对比提示，具有延伸连接链（extended linkers）的骨架材料可能是实验上寻找纠缠驱动拓扑转变的有前景候选体系。

Abstract: Topological transitions in disordered network materials are often characterised using descriptors of entanglement, such as ring linking and writhe, which can be difficult to access experimentally. Here we investigate whether a topological transition can instead be identified using structural descriptors more readily connected to experiment. We reanalyse configurations of a colloidal tetrahedral network model known to exhibit a pressure-driven transition between unentangled and and entangled states. We show that the cumulative all-rings vertex symbol (CARVS), a descriptor of medium-range network topology based on ring statistics, provides a clear signature of this transition despite containing no explicit information about ring linking. We further show that network entanglement produces characteristic changes in the radial distribution function and structure factor, including the emergence of short-range correlations between nonbonded nodes and the disappearance of a low-angle scattering pre-peak. These results establish experimentally accessible structural signatures of topological transitions in disordered tetrahedral networks. Comparison with canonical continuous random networks further suggests that framework materials with extended linkers may provide promising candidate systems in which to search experimentally for entanglement-driven topological transitions.

</details>


### [301] [Shear SAW piezoelectricity of single-crystalline $\mathrm{LiNbO_3}$ films grown on $42^{\circ}$ YX-$\mathrm{LiTaO_3}$ substrates by DLI-CVD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24639)
*Mikhail Mirushchenko,Roland Salut,Ludovic Gauthier-Manuel,Vincent Astié,Samuel Margueron,Jean-Manuel Decams,Yuancheng Ji,Farshad F. Bolamiri,Ventsislav Yantchev,Ausrine Bartasyte*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过直接液相注入化学气相沉积（direct liquid injection chemical vapor deposition）在同成分42° YX切LiTaO₃衬底上外延生长出320 nm厚、单畴、42° YX取向的LiNbO₃薄膜，并据此制备漏表面声波（leaky surface acoustic wave, LSAW）谐振器，机电耦合系数（electromechanical coupling coefficient）由8.5%提升至约13%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接生长具有技术相关非极性取向（nonpolar orientation）且呈单一铁电畴态（ferroelectric domain state）的单晶LiNbO₃薄膜仍具挑战性，而高质量、厚度可控的压电薄膜是高性能声学器件（acoustic devices）的关键需求。

Method: 采用直接液相注入化学气相沉积（direct liquid injection chemical vapor deposition）在同成分42° YX切LiTaO₃衬底上外延生长320 nm厚LiNbO₃薄膜，沉积温度限制在600 °C以保持衬底铁电畴态；用X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction）表征取向、畴态与结晶质量，并在LiNbO₃/LiTaO₃异质结构与裸LiTaO₃参考衬底上制备波长4 μm的单端口LSAW谐振器。

Result: XRD确认薄膜继承衬底取向与畴态且无可检测杂相，LiNbO₃摇摆曲线（rocking curve）半高全宽（full width at half maximum）为0.011°，接近仪器分辨率；约1 GHz下LiNbO₃/LiTaO₃谐振器机电耦合系数约13%，参考LiTaO₃器件为8.5%。

Conclusion: 结果表明直接外延生长可获得高质量、强压电性（strongly piezoelectric）的LiNbO₃薄膜，为面向高性能声学器件的厚度可控层状衬底（layered substrates）提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Direct growth of single-crystalline $\mathrm{LiNbO_3}$ films with technologically relevant nonpolar orientations and a single ferroelectric domain state remains challenging. Here, we demonstrate the epitaxial growth of a 320-nm-thick, single-domain $42°$ YX-oriented $\mathrm{LiNbO_3}$ film on a congruent $42°$ YX-cut $\mathrm{LiTaO_3}$ substrate by direct liquid injection chemical vapor deposition. The deposition temperature was limited to $600°\mathrm{C}$ to preserve the ferroelectric domain state of the $\mathrm{LiTaO_3}$ substrate. X-ray diffraction confirmed that the $\mathrm{LiNbO_3}$ film inherited the substrate orientation and domain state and exhibited no detectable secondary phases. The LN rocking-curve full width at half maximum was $0.011°$, approaching the instrumental resolution and demonstrating its high crystalline quality. Single-port leaky surface acoustic wave (LSAW) resonators with a wavelength of $4\,μ\mathrm{m}$ were fabricated on the $\mathrm{LiNbO_3/LiTaO_3}$ heterostructure and on a bare $\mathrm{LiTaO_3}$ reference substrate. At approximately 1 GHz, the $\mathrm{LiNbO_3/LiTaO_3}$-based resonators exhibited an electromechanical coupling coefficient of about 13%, compared to 8.5% for the reference $\mathrm{LiTaO_3}$-based resonators. These results demonstrate that direct epitaxial growth can produce high-quality, strongly piezoelectric $\mathrm{LiNbO_3}$ films and provide a route toward thickness-controlled layered substrates for high-performance acoustic devices.

</details>


### [302] [Epitaxial Displacement-etched GaAsSb Quantum Dots Emitting at Telecom Wavelengths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24768)
*Sadhvikas Addamane,Sanghyeok Park,Quinn T. Campbell,Evan M. Anderson,Thomas Rotter,Darryl Shima,Julia Deitz,Paul Kotula,Ganesh Balakrishnan,Oleg Mitrofanov,Igal Brener,Prasad P. Iyer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道一种位移刻蚀（displacement etch）技术，利用砷分子束（As₂）与锑化物表面发生热力学有利的置换反应，在无需应变驱动生长或液滴工艺的条件下可控形成发射于通信波段的GaAsSb量子点（quantum dots, QDs），其面密度可由As₂曝光量单一参数在10²–10⁵ μm⁻²三个数量级内调节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有外延生长（epitaxial growth）技术在生长复杂度、材料选择与密度可调性方面存在显著局限，亟需一种更简便、可调控的量子点制备途径以服务集成光子学与量子信息系统。

Method: 采用位移刻蚀技术，以As₂分子束与锑化物表面发生As替Sb的热力学有利置换反应形成三维量子限域结构；结合密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）分析两条竞争反应路径，并通过结构分析与变温光致发光（photoluminescence）表征量子点。

Result: DFT表明两条As-for-Sb交换路径在反应能与活化势垒上近乎等价，形貌控制取决于后续吸附原子动力学（adatom kinetics）而非表面反应本身；As₂曝光量这一单一参数可将量子点面密度从10²调至10⁵ μm⁻²。结构分析显示量子点为成分混合的GaAs/GaSb、无扩展缺陷；变温光致发光确认1550 nm（0.8 eV）C波段发射并呈典型量子点温度依赖特性。

Conclusion: 由于量子点在晶格匹配的GaSb表面单步生长而成，该技术为将C波段发射体集成进已用于中红外光源与探测器的GaSb/AlGaSb器件家族提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Semiconductor quantum dots (QDs) are essential components for integrated photonic and quantum information systems, yet current epitaxial growth techniques face significant limitations in complexity of growth, material selection, and density tunability. Here we report a displacement etch technique that enables controlled formation of GaAsSb QDs with emission at telecommunications wavelengths. Our approach uses arsenic molecular flux (As$_2$) to react with antimonide surfaces through thermodynamically favorable displacement reactions, creating three-dimensional quantum confined nanostructures without requiring strain-driven growth or complex droplet-based processes. Density functional theory shows the two competing As-for-Sb exchange pathways to be nearly equivalent in both reaction energy and activation barrier, which places morphological control in the subsequent adatom kinetics rather than in the surface reaction itself. A single process parameter, the As$_2$ exposure, sets the QD areal density across three orders of magnitude, from 10$^2$ to 10$^5$ $μm^{-2}$, covering both isolated emitters and dense gain media. Structural analysis reveals well-defined GaAsSb QDs with a mixed GaAs/GaSb composition and no extended defects. Temperature-dependent photoluminescence measurements confirm emission at 1550 nm (0.8 eV) within the telecommunications C-band, with characteristic QD-like temperature dependence. Because the QDs form on a lattice-matched GaSb surface in a single growth step, the technique offers a practical route to C-band emitters built into the GaSb/AlGaSb device family already used for mid-infrared sources and detectors.

</details>


### [303] [Molecular Doping of MoS$_2$ with N-Heterocyclic Carbenes: A Raman Study of Interfacial Charge Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24769)
*Ana Senkić,Arne Nalop,Xenia Sieverding,Ariane Ufer,Sebastian Roling,Frank Glorius,Ursula Wurstbauer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过在剥离的 MoS₂ 表面沉积氮杂环卡宾（N-heterocyclic carbene, NHC）分子，实现了高效电子掺杂；拉曼光谱显示 A₁g 声子软化，单层与双层中二维电子密度提升约两个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种化学方式调控半导体过渡金属硫族化合物（transition-metal dichalcogenides, TMDs）载流子密度的途径，类似于调制掺杂（modulation doping），使施主位于半导体晶格之外。

Method: 将 NHC 分子沉积到剥离的 MoS₂ 表面，利用拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）观测光学 A₁g 声子模式变化，并结合定量分析与文献值比较估计电子密度。

Result: NHC 沉积后 A₁g 声子模式显著软化，表明电子由 NHC 转移至 MoS₂；单层与双层位移最大，体相近乎可忽略，单双层二维电子密度增加约两个数量级。

Conclusion: 掺杂效率依赖层数，凸显 NHC/MoS₂ 界面电荷转移（interfacial charge transfer）的主导作用；NHC 表面沉积是化学调控 TMDs 载流子密度的可行方法，并为分子可调二维电子器件提供潜在路径。

Abstract: We demonstrate efficient electron doping of exfoliated MoS$_2$ through the deposition of N-heterocyclic carbene (NHC) molecules onto its surface. Raman spectroscopy reveals a pronounced softening of the optical A$_{1g}$ phonon mode after NHC deposition, consistent with electron transfer from the NHC molecules to the MoS$_2$ layers. The largest phonon-energy shifts are observed in monolayer and bilayer flakes whereas changes in bulk-like MoS$_2$ are negligible, indicating that the doping efficiency depends on the number of layers. This thickness dependence highlights the dominant role of interfacial charge transfer at the NHC/MoS$_2$ interface. Quantitative analysis of the Raman modes of as-exfoliated and NHC-covered MoS$_2$, together with comparison with literature values, allows us to estimate an increase in the two-dimensional electron density of about two-orders of magnitude in MoS$_2$ mono- and bilayers. These results demonstrate that NHC-based surface deposition is a promising approach for chemically tuning the carrier density of semiconducting transition-metal dichalcogenides and provide a potential route toward molecularly tunable two-dimensional electronic devices, in which, analogous to modulation doping, the donors reside outside the semiconductor lattice.

</details>


### [304] [Abaqus implementation of the phase-field approach to fracture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24820)
*Jiangwei Zhou,Victor Lefevre,Oscar Lopez-Pamies*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文给出Kumar–Francfort–Lopez-Pamies相场断裂模型的Abaqus用户子程序实现，通过将相场演化方程类比热传导方程（以UMAT模拟热源）并结合多个子程序实现交错求解与不可逆约束，用硅酸盐玻璃上的"九圆"算例验证了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相场断裂（phase-field fracture）模型虽在理论上成功，但其实现需要在通用有限元软件中可靠地求解位移场与相场的耦合方程；单体（monolithic）求解策略往往难以稳定收敛到正确物理解，故需要一个可在Abaqus中稳健部署的数值实现。

Method: 针对各向同性线弹性脆性材料（含Drucker-Prager强度面），利用热传导方程与相场演化方程的数学对应关系，通过UMAT子程序把含强度面的驱动力当作热源；采用UEXTERNALDB、UAMP、MPC、URDFIL子程序组合实现交错（staggered）求解，并用primal-dual active set方法施加相场的上下界与不可逆（irreversibility）不等式约束。

Result: 在硅酸盐玻璃（silicate glass）上模拟"弹性脆性断裂九圆（Nine Circles of Elastic Brittle Fracture）"标准算例，覆盖断裂形核与扩展的既有实验认知范围；并展示代码可使用CPS3、CAX3、C3D4等原生Abaqus单元运行。

Conclusion: 所提出的Abaqus实现能可靠、稳健地求解含强度面的相场断裂问题，交错策略结合主动集约束保证了物理正确性，且对多种单元类型通用。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce an Abaqus user-subroutine implementation of the phase-field model of fracture initiated by Kumar, Francfort, and Lopez-Pamies (\emph{J. Mech. Phys. Solids} 112, 523--551, 2018). The implementation is presented for the basic case of isotropic linear elastic brittle materials with a Drucker-Prager strength surface. The material inputs are therefore the Young's modulus $E$, the Poisson's ratio $ν$, the uniaxial tensile strength $\sts$, the uniaxial compressive strength $\scs$, and the fracture toughness $G_c$. The mathematical correspondence between the heat equation and the evolution equation for the phase field $v(\bfX,t)$ is exploited through a UMAT subroutine, wherein the driving force $c_{\texttt{e}}(\bfX,t)$ --- accounting for the material strength surface --- in the phase-field model plays the role of a heat source. To ensure that the equations for the displacement field $\bfu(\bfX,t)$ and the phase field $v(\bfX,t)$ are solved in a staggered manner --- a solution strategy that, as opposed to monolithic approaches, reliably yields the correct physical solution --- we make use of a combination of UEXTERNALDB, UAMP, MPC, and URDFIL subroutines. Within this approach, the MPC and URDFIL subroutines are additionally utilized to enforce the required bounding and irreversibility inequalities on $v(\bfX,t)$ via a primal-dual active set approach. The capabilities and robustness of the proposed implementation are demonstrated by simulating the ``Nine Circles of Elastic Brittle Fracture'' on a silicate glass --- a set of tests that span the entire range of well-settled experimental knowledge on fracture nucleation and propagation. These simulations also serve to illustrate the deployment of the code using several native Abaqus finite elements, including the simplicial elements CPS3, CAX3, and C3D4.

</details>


### [305] [Multi-property Feynman-Kac Steering of Crystal Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24828)
*Oded Mousai,Sapir Esther Yiflach,Barak Itzchak Davidovitch,Yuval Mualem,Sarit Kraus,Hannah-Noa Barad,Karsten Kreis,Gal Chechik*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: FK-ReMask 是一种无需训练、无需梯度的推理时（inference-time）引导方法，可同时调控预训练晶体扩散模型（crystal diffusion model）的连续通道与离散通道，用于按目标性质生成新材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成有用材料需满足特定化学与物理性质（如力学刚度、带隙）；现有方法或需针对新目标性质重新训练模型，或仅引导原子坐标与晶格而保持成分不变，忽视了成分对物理性质的决定性作用。

Method: 在生成过程中维护多条去噪轨迹（denoising trajectories），并借助 Feynman-Kac 粒子选择（particle selection）复制有前景的候选；针对原子类型通道中已确定元素无法修改的问题，引入原子重掩码机制（atom re-masking），在生成中重新开放已确定位点以探索新成分。

Result: 在四类电子、光伏与力学目标上，该方法提升了 SUN（stable, unique, novel）材料及性质达标材料的产出率；并进一步实现三种光伏性质的联合引导。

Conclusion: 多个物理目标可完全在推理阶段指定，无需重新训练，表明该方法为预训练晶体扩散模型提供了灵活的性质导向生成途径。

Abstract: Pretrained diffusion models provide powerful priors for generating new materials, including inorganic crystals, but generating useful materials requires satisfying specific chemical and physical properties, from mechanical stiffness to bandgaps. Existing approaches either retrain models for new target properties or steer only the atom coordinates and lattice during generation, leaving composition unchanged despite its fundamental role in determining physical properties. We present FK-ReMask, an inference-time, gradient-free method that steers both the continuous and discrete channels of a pretrained crystal diffusion model. During generation, FK-ReMask maintains multiple denoising trajectories and replicates promising candidates using Feynman-Kac particle selection. However, selection alone is insufficient: in the atom-type channel, committed elements cannot be revised, so cloning propagates existing compositions and limits the population's ability to explore new ones. We address this with an atom re-masking mechanism, which reopens committed sites during the generation process and allows alternative compositions to emerge. Across four electronic, photovoltaic, and mechanical objectives, our method improves the yield of SUN (stable, unique, novel) and property-on-target materials. We further demonstrate joint steering of three photovoltaic properties, showing that multiple physical objectives can be specified entirely at inference time without retraining.

</details>


### [306] [Universal scaling and measurability limits of thermal-conductivity measurement by a nanoscale point contact](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24846)
*Ram Munde,Raisul Islam*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文采用量纲分析（dimensional analysis）将扫描热显微（SThM）点接触法测薄膜热导率的六变量问题降为四个无量纲群，并揭示其由一个不变量Ω与两个控制参数支配，从而给出可分辨热导率的理论上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 亚100 nm薄膜热导率（thermal conductivity, κ）测量对异质系统热管理材料开发至关重要，但点接触法中薄膜、基底与界面贡献量级相当且未知量多，使κ提取成为高维难题。

Method: 采用量纲分析（dimensional analysis）减少变量数目，将针尖-样品总热阻（R_ss）由六变量函数约化为四个独立无量纲群，并构建描述R_ss对控制参数依赖关系的数学关系式；所提框架应用于SiO₂上生长的14±1 nm Al薄膜。

Result: 点接触针尖-薄膜-基底问题由单一无量纲不变量Ω = R_ss·κ_sub·t及两个控制参数（限域比t/b、热对比κ_sub/κ_ film）支配；由此导出的放大因子（amplification factor）仅由接触几何与界面决定，将测量不确定度映射为提取热导率的不确定度，并对可分辨的薄膜热导率给出界限（bound）。

Conclusion: 该框架可在既定界限内定位14±1 nm Al薄膜的测量结果，并以亚100 nm空间分辨率（spatial resolution）映射其热阻、反演局部热导率，为纳米尺度薄膜热物性表征提供物理边界与可行判据。

Abstract: Thermal conductivity ($κ$) measurement of a thin film is critical for developing thermal management materials in heterogeneous systems. A nanoscale point contact method based on scanning thermal microscopy (SThM) offers a unique approach to probe $κ$ with nanoscale spatial resolution in sub-100 nm thick films. Extracting $κ$ of a thin film from this method requires separating film, substrate, and interface contributions that are comparable in magnitude. Multiple unknowns make this extraction a high dimensional problem. In this work, we used dimensional analysis to reduce the number of variables to provide physical insights into the realistic bounds of $κ$ measurement. We show that the total tip-sample thermal resistance $R_{ss}$, originally a function of six variables, reduces to four independent dimensionless groups. These groups are used to construct a mathematical relation that captures the dependence of $R_{ss}$ on the governing parameters. The resulting analysis shows that the point contact tip-film-substrate problem is governed by a single dimensionless invariant, $Ω= R_{ss}κ_{\mathrm{sub}}t$, and two control parameters: the confinement ratio $t/b$ and the thermal contrast $κ_{\mathrm{sub}}/κ_{\mathrm{film}}$. From the invariant, we derive an amplification factor, set only by the contact geometry and the interfaces, that converts the uncertainty of a measurement into an uncertainty in the extracted conductivity. This factor scales with the ratio of the parasitic interface resistance to the film's sheet thermal resistance and bounds the film conductivity that a given contact can resolve. We apply the framework to a 14$\pm$1 nm Al film grown on SiO$_2$ to locate the measurement within the established bound. The framework then lets us map its thermal resistance with sub-100 nm spatial resolution and recover the local thermal conductivity.

</details>


### [307] [Strong Thermal Conductivity Suppression in Isotope Purified Single Crystal Diamond with Phonon Scatterings of Color Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24988)
*Jinwen Liu,Junliang Fan,Zimu Jiang,Zifeng Huang,Zhe Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究报告了两种经同位素纯化、氮-空位（NV）色心浓度不同的金刚石的热导率分别约为3200与2100 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹；修正Callaway模型分析表明，将氮与空位视为独立点缺陷的模型可能低估NV色心的声子散射强度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天然金刚石通过消除同位素杂质可获得约50%的热导率提升，但色心（color center）等缺陷如何散射声子并限制热导率的机制尚不清楚，尤其区别于孤立点缺陷（point defect）的情形。

Method: 采用光致发光（photoluminescence, PL）与二次离子质谱（secondary-ion mass spectrometry, SIMS）确认两种同位素纯化金刚石样品的NV色心浓度，测量其热导率，并借助修正Callaway模型（modified Callaway model）结合第一性原理（first-principles）导出的散射参数进行分析。

Result: 低与高NV浓度样品的室温热导率分别约为3200 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹与2100 W m⁻¹ K⁻¹；在假设空位浓度约占总氮浓度30%时，模型可重现高NV浓度样品的室温热导率，但无法重现其温度依赖关系。PL与SIMS结果显示两样品NV浓度相差约7.1倍、总氮浓度相差约92倍，暗示所设30%空位比例可能被大幅高估。

Conclusion: NV色心缺陷对声子的散射可能强于将氮与空位视为独立点缺陷的模型预测，为色心诱导的声子散射机制（phonon-scattering mechanism）提供了新认识。

Abstract: The thermal conductivity of natural diamond can be further improved by ~50% by eliminating isotope impurity to obtain pure 12C diamond. However, unlike isolated point defects, how color centers scatter phonons and correspondingly limit the thermal conductivity remain poorly understood. Here, we report the thermal conductivities of two isotope-purified diamond with different Nitrogen-vacancy (NV) color center concentrations, confirmed by photoluminescence (PL) and secondary-ion mass spectrometry (SIMS). Results show ~ 3200 W m-1 K-1 and ~ 2100 W m-1 K-1 for two samples of low and high NV concentrations, respectively. Modified Callaway model analyses show that first-principles-derived scattering parameters can reproduce room-temperature thermal conductivity of the high NV concentration sample under a vacancy concentration assumption (~30% of the total Nitrogen concentration), but fail to reproduce its temperature dependence. Compared to PL and SIMS results, which show ~7.1-fold NV concentration and ~92-fold difference in total Nitrogen concentration between two samples, suggesting the assumed 30% vacancy fraction may be greatly overestimated. Taken together, these results suggest that NV-color-center defects may scatter phonons more strongly than models treating Nitrogen and vacancy as independent point defects, providing new insights into color-center-induced phonon-scattering mechanisms.

</details>


### [308] [HEXRD and HEXRDGUI: a flexible toolkit for X-ray diffraction analysis in dynamic compression experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24989)
*Patrick Avery,Joel V. Bernier,Brianna Major,Saransh Singh*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: HEXRD 及其图形界面 HEXRDGUI 是开源 X 射线衍射分析工具，基于通用多探测器几何模型，支持任意仪器构型、多光源与多种标定/全谱拟合方法，已被多个动态压缩实验装置采用，并在跨程序与双光源标定验证中达到数值精度一致的散射角预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态压缩 X 射线衍射实验涉及多探测器、任意光束方向与多光源的复杂仪器构型，需要能适配任意配置并支持对未知相与多平台数据进行分析的通用工具。

Method: 基于通用多探测器几何模型构建，支持独立定位的平面或圆柱探测器面板、任意光束方向和多 X 射线源；利用粉末环、Laue 斑点或未知相衍射环进行仪器标定，并通过 Le Bail 或 Rietveld 精修进行全谱拟合，同时可导入 NIF 的 TARDIS、FIDDLE 及 PXRDIP 平台数据。

Result: 在单平面探测器相同环位置标定下，HEXRD 与独立程序 pyFAI 预测的散射角 2θ 在数值精度上一致；在 NIF 双光源数据上，同时以两个 X 射线源标定可将两组环拟合法向均方根（RMS）达 0.18°（约一个成像板像素），而仅拟合单一光源（板倾角取标称值）虽对自身环拟合接近，却对另一光源的环产生约 0.6° 的偏差。

Conclusion: HEXRD/HEXRDGUI 已从同步辐射高能衍射显微术扩展为动态压缩 X 射线衍射的通用分析工具，被 NIF、LCLS 的 MEC 端站、APS 的 DCS、LLE 的 OMEGA EP 及 European XFEL 的 HED 仪器等采用，并以 BSD 许可证免费开放。

Abstract: This article describes the application of HEXRD, an open-source Python library for X-ray diffraction analysis, and HEXRDGUI, its companion graphical application, to dynamic compression experiments.
