Built on a generic multi-detector geometric model, the toolkit supports arbitrary instrument configurations with independently positioned planar or cylindrical detector panels, arbitrary beam directions, and multiple X-ray sources. It calibrates instruments from powder rings, Laue spots or the rings of an unknown phase, fits whole patterns by Le Bail or Rietveld refinement, and imports data from the TARDIS, FIDDLE and PXRDIP platforms at the National Ignition Facility (NIF) and OMEGA EP. Although originally developed for high-energy diffraction microscopy at synchrotron sources, HEXRD has been adopted as an analysis tool for dynamic compression X-ray diffraction experiments at facilities including the NIF, the Matter in Extreme Conditions end-station at the Linac Coherent Light Source, the Dynamic Compression Sector (DCS) at the Advanced Photon Source, OMEGA EP at the Laboratory for Laser Energetics, and the High Energy Density (HED) instrument at the European XFEL. Calibrated from the same ring positions on a single planar detector, HEXRD and the independent code pyFAI predict the same scattering angle 2$θ$ to numerical precision. On a dual-source dataset from the NIF, calibrating the detectors against both X-ray sources at once fits the rings of both to an RMS of 0.18° in 2$θ$, about one image-plate pixel, whereas a fit to either source alone, with the plate tilts held at their nominal values, fits its own rings nearly as well but mispredicts the other's by about 0.6°. The software is freely available under a BSD license.

</details>


### [309] [Extrinsic Bulk Photovoltaic Effect from Asymmetric Defect Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25029)
*Zixun Wang,Andrew M. Rappe*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文建立从头算玻尔兹曼框架，将光学激发与电子-声子散射及对称、偏斜电子-缺陷散射统一处理，用于研究单层GeS中外禀体光伏效应；发现响应在有限缺陷分数处出现峰值，低温下峰值位置与增强倍数显著变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不对称电子-缺陷散射产生的 Sicherheit外禀体光伏效应（extrinsic bulk photovoltaic effect）对总光电流有重要贡献，且可高度诊断缺陷类型；需要对光学激发与多类散射通道进行统一的第一性原理描述。

Method: 提出从头算玻尔兹曼框架（ab initio Boltzmann framework），将光学激发与电子-声子散射、对称及偏斜电子-缺陷散射（symmetric and skew electron-defect scattering）结合，并应用于单层GeS。

Result: 产生电流的缺陷同时也会弛豫维持该电流的各向异性，因此响应在有限缺陷分数处达到峰值；在散射受限模型（scattering-limited model）中，降温至0 K使峰值移向更低缺陷分数，并在固定缺陷分数下实现六个数量级以上的最大增强；在选定低温缺陷分数下，总外禀电流可超过本征位移电流（intrinsic shift current）一个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 缺陷分数本身可作为可调参数（tunable parameter）；外禀电子-缺陷散射机制可在低温、特定缺陷浓度下主导光伏响应。

Abstract: The extrinsic bulk photovoltaic effect from asymmetric electron-defect scattering can make an important contribution to the total photocurrent and is highly diagnostic of defect type. We present an ab initio Boltzmann framework combining optical excitation with electron-phonon and symmetric and skew electron-defect scattering, and we apply it to monolayer GeS. Because the defects that generate the current also relax the anisotropy that sustains it, the response peaks at a finite defect fraction. Within the scattering-limited model, cooling to $0$~K shifts the peak position toward lower defect fractions and yields a maximum enhancement of more than six orders of magnitude at fixed defect fraction. At selected low-temperature defect fractions, the total extrinsic current can exceed the intrinsic shift current by more than an order of magnitude, so that the defect fraction itself becomes a tunable parameter.

</details>


### [310] [Electric Hidden Unconventional Collinear Magnetism in 2D \(\mathcal{PT}\)-Symmetric Antiferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25186)
*Mu Tian,Fanxian Pei,Zhi-Ming Yu,Yugui Yao,Run-Wu Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作对88个共线自旋层群（collinear spin layer groups, cSLGs）作完整对称性分类，指出垂直电场（\(\mathcal{E}_z\)）可解除\(\mathcal{PT}\)强制简并，将常规\(\mathcal{PT}\)对称反铁磁体驱动为三类非常规共线磁性，并给出候选材料与电控自旋响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电场诱导的隐藏非常规共线磁性（hidden unconventional collinear magnetism）此前基本未被探索，需要系统性的对称性框架与材料实现方案。

Method: 对与常规\(\mathcal{PT}\)对称反铁磁体相容的全部88个cSLGs进行完整对称性分类，并以常见、可剥离的二维候选材料为例说明各分类。

Result: 电场诱导出三类磁性：IV型磁性（Class-I，23个cSLGs）、交错磁性（altermagnetism，Class-II，34个cSLGs）与完全补偿铁磁（fully compensated ferrimagnetism，Class-III，31个cSLGs）；对应候选材料分别为Sr\(_2\)CaCr\(_2\)P\(_2\)O\(_9\)/Sr\(_2\)CaCr\(_2\)As\(_2\)O\(_9\)、BaMnGe/MnPS\(_3\)、Sr\(_3\)Fe\(_2\)O\(_5\)Cl\(_2\)/双层MnBi\(_2\)Te\(_4\)。三类呈现不同的自旋劈裂指纹与自旋相关响应：Class-I为电霍尔效应（electric Hall effect），Class-II为自旋极化输运（spin-polarized transport），Class-III为电可逆磁光克尔效应（magneto-optical Kerr effect）。

Conclusion: 研究闭合了二维反铁磁体中从常规到非常规共线磁性的完整内部循环，并为未来自旋电子学（spintronics）应用建立了可行的全电学补偿磁性平台与调控方案。

Abstract: Electric hidden unconventional collinear magnetism, in which a perpendicular electric field (\(\mathcal{E}_z\)) lifts the \(\mathcal{PT}\)-enforced degeneracy and selectively releases the hidden crystallographic constraints in conventional 2D antiferromagnets to drive emergent magnetic phases, has remained largely unexplored. Here we establish a complete symmetry classification of all 88 collinear spin layer groups (cSLGs) compatible with conventional \(\mathcal{PT}\)-symmetric antiferromagnets, and show that the \(\mathcal{E}_z\) drives the system into three distinct classes of unconventional collinear magnetism: type-IV magnetism (Class-I, 23 cSLGs), altermagnetism (Class-II, 34 cSLGs), and fully compensated ferrimagnetism (Class-III, 31 cSLGs). We illustrate these categories using the most common and feasibly exfoliable 2D candidate materials. These include Sr$_2$CaCr$_2$P$_2$O$_9$ and Sr$_2$CaCr$_2$As$_2$O$_9$ (Class-I), BaMnGe and MnPS$_3$ (Class-II), and Sr$_3$Fe$_2$O$_5$Cl$_2$ and bilayer MnBi$_2$Te$_4$ (Class-III). We further demonstrate that the \(\mathcal{E}_z\) generates distinctly different spin-splitting fingerprints across the three classes, yielding distinct spin-dependent responses: the electric Hall effect in Class-I, spin-polarized transport in Class-II, and the electric reversible magneto-optical Kerr effect in Class-III. Our work not only closes a complete internal cycle from conventional to unconventional collinear magnetism in 2D antiferromagnets, but also establishes a viable all-electrical compensated magnetism platform and control scheme for future spintronic applications.

</details>


### [311] [Electronic structure, magnetic order and anisotropic magnetoresistance in $\mathbf{Eu_{1-x}Gd_xCd_2P_2}$ (0 $\le$ x $\le$1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25194)
*Yogendra Singh Negi,Tulika Maitra*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过Gd替代Eu位进行电子掺杂，利用密度泛函理论（DFT）与蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）计算研究Eu$_{1-x}$Gd$_{x}$Cd$_2$P$_2$的磁性与庞磁电阻（CMR）行为，发现基态磁序随电子载流子浓度振荡，并预测x=0.125和0.5时存在巨大的各向异性磁电阻。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EuCd$_2$P$_2$的庞磁电阻（colossal magnetoresistance, CMR）机制似乎偏离锰氧化物（manganites）等经典CMR化合物，现有研究强调Eu空位导致的低空穴载流子浓度对铁磁（ferromagnetic, FM）序稳定及输运性质的作用，并以磁极化子（magnetic polaron）机制替代双交换（double exchange）机制进行解释；本文旨在探索电子掺杂对该化合物磁性转变与CMR行为的影响。

Method: 在Eu位以不同浓度x进行Gd替代形成Eu$_{1-x}$Gd$_{x}$Cd$_2$P$_2$，采用密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）结合蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）方法进行计算。

Result: 基态磁序随电子载流子浓度呈振荡变化：x=0.056、0.125和0.5时为A型反铁磁金属（A-AFM metal），x=0.33、0.67和1时变为铁磁金属（ferromagnetic metal）。研究预测x=0.125和0.5时存在巨大的各向异性磁电阻（anisotropic magnetoresistance），其机制类似于双交换，源于强的格点内4f-5d铁磁交换相互作用与杂化导电态中的巡游电子。转变温度随电子载流子浓度增强，x=0.67时可达150 K。

Conclusion: 基态磁序的振荡与转变温度随电子载流子浓度的增强为调控该化合物的磁输运（magnetotransport）性质提供了新途径，并为未来自旋电子学（spintronics）器件设计提供了优良平台。

Abstract: Recently, $\text{EuCd}_2\text{P}_2$ has attracted increased attention for its colossal magnetoresistive (CMR) property, the mechanism of which seems to deviate from that of known canonical CMR compounds such as manganites. Many studies underscore the role of low density of hole carriers caused by Eu vacancies, particularly in stabilizing ferromagnetic (FM) order and modifying electrical transport and magnetoresistive responses in this compound. A magnetic polaron based mechanism rather than double exchange based mechanism is being proposed to explain the CMR effect for this compound. In this work, we explore the effect of elctron doping on the magnetic transition and CMR behaviour via Gd substitution at Eu sites with varying concentration (x) in Eu$_{1-x}$Gd$_{x}$Cd$_2$P$_2$. Our Density Functional Theory (DFT) and Monte Carlo-based calculations reveal an oscillating ground state magnetic order as a function of electron carrier density. The ground state is found to be A-AFM metal for x=0.056, 0.125 and 0.5 while it becomes ferromagnetic metal at x= 0.33, 0.67 and 1. Most importantly, we predict colossal anisotropic magnetoresistance in this compound for x=0.125 and 0.5. The underlying mechanism of anisotropic CMR in this compound looks similar to double exchange like due to the presence of a strong intra-site 4\textit{f}-5\textit{d} ferromagnetic exchange interaction and an itinerant electron in the hybridized conduction states. The oscillating ground state magnetic order and the enhancement of transition temperature (as high as 150K for x=0.67) as a function of electron carrier density offers a new avenue to tune the magnetotransport property in this compound and offers an excellent platform for designing future spintronics devices.

</details>


### [312] [Long-lived nonequilibrium spin configuration induced by dual optical excitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25236)
*Sergii Parchenko,Arata Tsukamoto,Andreas Scherz*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过对称双泵浦（dual-pump）几何在非晶GdFeCo薄膜中激发相干磁化进动（coherent magnetization precession），发现窄激发窗口内铁磁共振（FMR）类模式的寿命显著延长、频率略微升高，指向长寿命的非平衡自旋相关电子构型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超快磁性（ultrafast magnetism）与光控磁性的核心在于产生并调控非平衡自旋态；但金属体系中电子-电子与电子-声子散射（electron-electron and electron-phonon scattering）极快，光激发电子分布迅速趋于准热态，难以实现自旋相关相互作用的持久改变。

Method: 采用两束飞秒激光同时激发的对称双泵浦（dual-pump）几何，使激发从单脉冲连续过渡到空间周期激发，从而区分激发图案效应与双泵浦同时激发效应；并结合时间窗口分析与唯象的Elliott-Yafet框架解读频率与衰减时间的变化。

Result: 识别出一个窄激发窗口，其中FMR类模式的寿命强烈增加、频率有小幅但可重复的升高；增强的寿命可持续数百皮秒，远长于周期激发图案相关的瞬态静磁效应（magnetostatic effects）。频率与衰减时间的联合变化无法仅用空间结构化加热解释。

Conclusion: 在Elliott-Yafet框架下，FMR衰减时间与频率的同时增加与有效自旋混合参数（spin-mixing parameter）的瞬态降低相一致；结果表明激发路径（而非激发能量本身）可作为自旋弛豫的控制参量，并获得单脉冲激发难以达到的非平衡自旋态。

Abstract: The ability to create and control nonequilibrium spin states is central to understanding ultrafast magnetism and to developing optical routes for manipulating magnetic materials beyond conventional thermal pathways.In metallic systems, photoexcited electronic distributions rapidly evolve toward quasi-thermal states through efficient electron-electron and electron-phonon scattering, making long-lived modification of spin-dependent interactions difficult to achieve. Here, we investigate coherent magnetization precession in an amorphous GdFeCo thin film driven by simultaneous excitation with two femtosecond laser pulses.Using a symmetric dual-pump geometry that continuously evolves from single-pulse to spatially periodic excitation, we separate effects associated with the excitation pattern from those arising from simultaneous dual-pump excitation. We identify a narrow excitation window in which the lifetime of the ferromagnetic-resonance-like mode increases strongly while its frequency exhibits a small but reproducible increase.Time-window analysis shows that the enhanced lifetime persists for several hundred picoseconds, substantially longer than transient magnetostatic effects associated with the periodic excitation pattern.The combined frequency and decay time changes cannot be explained by spatially structured heating alone and instead point to a long-lived nonequilibrium spin-dependent electronic configuration. Within a phenomenological Elliott-Yafet framework, the simultaneous increase in FMR decay time and frequency is consistent with a transient reduction of the effective spin-mixing parameter governing spin-flip scattering. Our results demonstrate that the excitation pathway, rather than excitation energy alone, can serve as a control parameter for spin relaxation and provide access to nonequilibrium spin states that are not readily reached by conventional single-pulse excitation.

</details>


### [313] [Instability-driven segregation of V group transition metals on HEB$_2$ grain boundaries influencing enhanced mechanical properties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25295)
*Daniil Alekseev,Viktor S. Baidyshev,Alexander G. Kvashnin*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合密度泛函理论（DFT）、COHP 键合分析与基于 MACE 势的蒙特卡洛/分子动力学模拟，揭示 (Hf,Ta,Ti,Nb,Zr)B$_2$ 高熵二硼化物中第五族金属 Nb、Ta 优先偏聚于晶界，从而降低晶界能（grain-boundary energy）并强化晶界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数高熵材料（high-entropy materials）为烧结多晶陶瓷，但哪种金属物种位于晶界（grain boundary）尚不清楚，该研究旨在解答高熵二硼化物 HEB$_2$ 晶界的成分归属问题。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论计算五种二元二硼化物及 HEB$_2$ 中四类晶界的晶界能，并以晶体轨道哈密顿布居（COHP）分析界面键合；同时用针对该体系微调的 MACE 势，结合蒙特卡洛（Monte Carlo）与分子动力学，对 HEB$_2$ 六个有序与一般晶界进行阳离子偏聚（cation segregation）采样。

Result: 晶界能按族分裂：NbB$_2$、TaB$_2$ 在四类晶界中的三类约为 0.8–2.7 J/m$^2$，约为第四族 2.5–4.8 J/m$^2$ 的一半；COHP 显示其 Me–B 键在体能带费米能级（$E_F$）处为反键、在晶界处转为成键。HEB$_2$ 中 Nb、Ta 在六个晶界中的五个富集，2000 K 下达 23–35 at.%，且偏聚后的 $Σ193$ 晶界拉伸强度为 26 GPa，高于随机晶界的 20 GPa（300 K）。

Conclusion: HEB$_2$ 晶界优先富集 Nb、Ta，这与二元化合物晶界能及其费米能级键合特征一致，共同导致晶界能降低与晶界强化。

Abstract: Five metals share a common sublattice in the (Hf,Ta,Ti,Nb,Zr)B$_2$ (HEB$_2$). Most high-entropy materials are produced as polycrystalline materials in the form of sintered ceramics. The question of which metal species are located at grain boundaries is still unknown. Here, we calculated the grain-boundary energy $γ_{GB}$ of four types of boundaries in five binary diborides and HEB$_2$ using density functional theory (DFT). We analyzed the bonding at these boundaries using COHP and sampled cation segregation at six boundaries of HEB$_2$, ordered and general, using Monte Carlo / molecular dynamics with a MACE potential that was fine-tuned for this system. According to our results $γ_{GB}$ splits by group: the group V diborides NbB$_2$ and TaB$_2$ form boundaries with about half the energy of the group IV at three of the four types of GBs, ranging from 0.8 to 2.7 compared to 2.5--4.8~J/m$^2$. COHP shows a significant difference at the Fermi level, namely the Me--B bonds of NbB$_2$ and TaB$_2$ are antibonding at Fermi energy ($E_F$) in the bulk but bonding at the GB, while their integrated strength hardly changes. In HEB$_2$ the segregation follows a similar pattern: Nb and Ta are enriched at five of the six grain boundaries, including general boundaries, up to 23--35~at.\% at 2000~K, although the size of the atoms would favor Zr and Hf in the expanded sites. A segregated $Σ193$ grain boundary can withstand 26 against 20~GPa for a random boundary in tension at 300~K. The grain boundaries of HEB$_2$ are composed of group V metals, as suggested by the boundary energies of binaries compounds and their bonding at the Fermi level. Grain boundaries of the (Hf,Ta,Ti,Nb,Zr)B$_2$ preferentially include Nb and Ta. This lowers the grain boundary energy and strengthens the boundaries. This consists of the lower boundary energy and changes Fermi level bonding in NbB$_2$ and TaB$_2$.

</details>


### [314] [NEP-kappa 2.0: A unified framework for phonon transport and nonequilibrium thermalization with machine-learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25477)
*Shixian Liu,Bin Liu,Fei Yin,Gang Wang,Yiming Dong,Yangjun Qin,Alexandra V. Kosyakova,Qiang Xian,Alexander A. Barinov,Ke Xu,Gang Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出 NEP-kappa 2.0 统一框架，整合密度泛函理论、材料特异性机器学习原子间势与预训练力模型，实现从谐性/准谐/随机自洽声子到三、四声子输运、Wigner 输运及含时声子动力学的多层级计算，并在 3C-SiC 等材料中揭示光学-声学声子间的超快能量再分配机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有声子计算工具通常彼此割裂，难以在统一流程中同时处理力模型比较、有限温重整化、高阶散射与非平衡能量动力学，因而需要一个可贯通平衡输运与微观非平衡过程的集成框架。

Method: 构建 NEP-kappa 2.0 框架，融合密度泛函理论（density-functional theory）、材料特异性机器学习原子间势（machine-learning interatomic potentials）与预训练力模型（pretrained force models），以统一工作流支持谐性晶格动力学、准谐与随机自洽声子（stochastic self-consistent phonon）、三声子与四声子输运、Wigner 布居-相干输运（Wigner population–coherence transport）以及含时声子动力学（time-dependent phonon dynamics）。

Result: 在 3C-SiC、BAs 与 Cs₂AgInCl₆ 上的应用展示了力模型的一致性比较、有限温重整化、高阶散射与模间相干效应。对选择性激发的 3C-SiC 的模式分辨模拟显示，约 3.75 ps 内一半注入光学声子能量经单光学-双声学散射（one-optical–two-acoustic scattering）转移至声学支；声学支响应时间相差达五倍，且竞争散射通道产生瞬态能量过冲。

Conclusion: 通道分辨的熵产生（entropy production）与持续非热占据（nonthermal occupations）可区分支间能量再分配与支内热化，表明该框架将平衡声子输运与微观非平衡能量动力学有效衔接。

Abstract: We present NEP-kappa 2.0, a unified framework that integrates density-functional theory, material-specific machine-learning interatomic potentials, and pretrained force models for advanced phonon calculations. A common workflow enables harmonic lattice dynamics, quasi-harmonic and stochastic self-consistent phonon treatments, three- and four-phonon transport, Wigner population--coherence transport, and time-dependent phonon dynamics. Applications to 3C-SiC, BAs, and Cs$_2$AgInCl$_6$ demonstrate consistent force-model comparisons, finite-temperature renormalization, higher-order scattering, and intermode coherence. Beyond steady-state transport, mode-resolved simulations of selectively excited 3C-SiC reveal that half of the injected optical-phonon energy reaches acoustic modes within approximately 3.75 ps, primarily through one-optical--two-acoustic scattering. Acoustic branches exhibit response times differing by a factor of five, with competing scattering channels producing a transient energy overshoot. Channel-resolved entropy production and persistent nonthermal occupations distinguish interbranch energy redistribution from intrabranch thermalization. The framework connects equilibrium phonon transport with microscopic nonequilibrium energy dynamics.

</details>


### [315] [Microscopic Compensation of Circular Optical Response in Altermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25489)
*Meysam Bagheri Tagani,Carmine Autieri,Sahar Izadi Vishkayi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 弱或消失的圆光信号可能源于大而相互抵消的微观贡献。作者在动量与跃迁层面解析Kubo响应，分离固定动量内的跃迁抵消与布里渊区跨区抵消，并以MnTe为例说明磁对称性决定响应是否允许、微观补偿决定残余大小与符号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱或为零的圆偏振光学信号常被直觉性地解读为螺旋性敏感跃迁本身弱，但这类信号也可能是大幅值、相反符号的微观贡献相互抵消后的残余，因此需要区分“是否允许”与“抵消后剩余多少”这两类问题。

Method: 在求和跃迁与动量之前，先解析有限频率的带间Kubo响应（interband Kubo response），将固定动量处的跃迁间抵消与整个布里渊区（Brillouin zone）上的抵消分开处理；以精确抵消的d波模型作为对称性基准，并对MnTe开展第一性原理Wannier计算。

Result: 在MnTe中发现存在对称性“暗”的Néel取向，其圆响应核（circular kernel）变号且体区积分消为零；改变Néel矢量取向可解除该消光，但允许的响应仍只是大正、负动量区间之间的小幅失衡，且该失衡随光子能量反转，从而使宏观符号翻转。

Conclusion: 磁对称性决定圆响应是否被允许，而微观补偿决定其残余幅度与符号；该区分为解释补偿磁体（compensated magnets）中弱、消失及变号的磁光信号提供了通用框架。

Abstract: A weak or vanishing circular optical signal need not imply weak helicity-sensitive transitions: it can be the residual of much larger opposing microscopic contributions. We resolve the finite-frequency interband Kubo response before summing over transitions and momentum, separating cancellation among transitions at fixed momentum from cancellation across the Brillouin zone. An exactly compensated $d$-wave model establishes the symmetry benchmark. In MnTe, first-principles Wannier calculations reveal a symmetry-dark Néel orientation with an extended sign-changing circular kernel whose bulk integral vanishes. Reorienting the Néel vector removes this extinction, yet the allowed response remains a small imbalance between large positive- and negative-momentum sectors; the imbalance reverses with photon energy and flips the macroscopic sign. Thus magnetic symmetry decides whether circular response is allowed, whereas microscopic compensation determines how much survives and with what sign. This distinction provides a general framework for interpreting weak, vanishing, and sign-changing magneto-optical signals in compensated magnets.

</details>


### [316] [Autonomous adaptive spectral analysis with an LLM agent for self-driving laboratories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25619)
*Yuki K. Wakabayashi,Yui Ogawa*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究实现了一个由大语言模型（large language model, LLM）监督确定性数值分析流程的自主自适应光谱分析代理（agent），用于在自驱动实验室（self-driving laboratory）中处理表征数据分析的异常情况。在203个β-Ga2O3外延薄膜透射光谱上，代理保留183个有效结果，对20个失败案例中16个通过有界参数调整恢复、4个返回NaN。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自驱动实验室中表征并不总是直接给出目标性质，光谱或图像需依赖物理假设和预设判据的自动分析，而此类流程在超出判据的测量上会失败，需要判断能否可靠恢复数值或应拒绝报告数值。

Method: 采用LLM监督确定性数值分析程序：对每个光谱执行预定义分析脚本，并结合实测光谱与拟合诊断评估结果；有效结果原样接受，可恢复失败通过预定义拟合参数的有界调整（bounded adjustments）重新分析，无可辩护数值时返回NaN。

Result: 在既往自驱动薄膜生长 campaign 中生长的203个β-Ga2O3外延薄膜透射光谱上评估：预定义分析脚本为183个光谱返回Urbach能量，20个失败；代理保留183个值，对20个异常案例中16个经有界参数调整恢复，4个返回NaN。审计记录（audit records）保存了光谱特征、参数变化及决策理由。

Conclusion: 结果展示了由LLM代理实现的自主科学异常处理（autonomous scientific exception handling），同时将定量性质提取交由确定性数值分析完成。

Abstract: Self-driving laboratories accelerate materials exploration through autonomous closed-loop integration of synthesis, characterization, and next-experiment selection. However, characterization does not always directly provide the target property; measured spectra or images often require automated analysis to extract quantitative descriptors based on physically motivated assumptions and predefined criteria. Such procedures can fail for measurements outside these criteria, requiring a decision on whether a reliable value can be recovered or no numerical value should be reported. Here, we implemented an autonomous adaptive spectral-analysis agent using a large language model (LLM) to supervise a deterministic numerical-analysis routine. For each spectrum, the agent executes the predefined analysis script and assesses the result using the measured spectrum and fitting diagnostics. Valid results are accepted unchanged, recoverable failures are reanalyzed through bounded adjustments of predefined fitting parameters, and NaN (not a number) is returned when no scientifically defensible value can be obtained. We evaluated the workflow on optical transmittance spectra from 203 epitaxial beta-Ga2O3 films grown during a previously reported self-driving thin-film growth campaign. The predefined optical analysis script returned Urbach energy, an optical-quality metric, for 183 spectra and failed for 20. The agent retained those 183 values and evaluated 20 exception cases, recovering 16 through bounded parameter adjustments and returning NaN for 4 when the spectral evidence did not support a reliable Urbach tail. The audit records captured spectral features, parameter changes, and rationale for each decision. These results demonstrate autonomous scientific exception handling by an LLM agent while leaving quantitative property extraction to deterministic numerical analysis.

</details>


### [317] [Crystal Structure Based Graph Neural Network And LLM Multi-Modal Approach for Material Magnetic Ordering Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25775)
*Adam Zheng,April Zheng,Sheng Ran*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于图神经网络（Graph Neural Network, GNN）与大语言模型（Large Language Model, LLM）的多模态（multi-modal）方法，通过信息融合预测材料磁有序类型，效果优于单一模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI/ML 在材料性质预测上进展显著，可规避昂贵耗时的实验室测试；但现有工作多依赖单一模态输入，本研究旨在利用晶体结构与语义文本两类信息提升磁有序（magnetic ordering）预测能力。

Method: 以材料晶体结构作为 GNN 输入、材料性质与结构的语义文本描述作为 LLM 输入，并对两者输出进行信息融合（information fusion），将磁有序分为非磁、铁磁、反铁磁、亚铁磁四类；数据集由 Materials Project、Magndata 及一组经实验室测定磁有序的铀化合物组合而成，按 80/10/10 划分训练/验证/测试集。

Result: 所提出的 GNN-LLM 多模态方法在三种融合策略下，整体准确率（overall accuracy）均优于单独使用 GNN 和单独使用 LLM 的方法。

Conclusion: 多模态融合可有效提升材料磁有序预测的准确性，表明结合结构化晶体信息与语义文本描述是有前景的材料性质预测途径。

Abstract: AI/ML models are useful for the prediction of materials' properties. Recently, there has been great progress in developing Neural Network models to predict the properties of solids, molecules, and crystals, avoiding expensive and time-consuming laboratory testing. This study presents a novel AI/ML-based multi-modal approach to predict material magnetic ordering using material crystal structure information as input to a Graph Neural Network model (GNN) and semantic text descriptions of the material's properties and structure as input to a Large Language Model (LLM), before performing information fusion of the GNN and LLM outputs to predict material magnetic ordering in four classes (Non-magnetic, Ferromagnetic, Antiferromagnetic, Ferrimagnetic). The model is trained, validated, and tested on a dataset which is a combination of Materials Project dataset, Magndata dataset, and a set of uranium compounds with magnetic ordering determined in laboratory testing, with an 80/10/10 (training/validation/testing) data split. The proposed GNN and LLM multi-modal approach achieves better overall accuracy than both the standalone GNN and LLM approaches with three fusion strategies.

</details>


### [318] [A Stress-Induced Tetragonal Distortion in K2OsCl6 under Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25913)
*Neha Bura,Peije Zhang,Ganesh Bera,Catalin Popescu,Caleb J. Bennett,Brendan J. Kennedy,Daniel Errandonea*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用同步辐射粉末X射线衍射将K2OsCl6加压至35.4 GPa，观察到立方→四方（约9.5 GPa以上）→单斜（约14.2 GPa起）的结构序列，并发现四方相可能源于非静水应力而非独立热力学相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究立方（Fm-3m）K2OsCl6在高压下的结构行为，并与其同族化合物（如K2IrCl6）的高压结构序列进行比较。

Method: 采用角散同步辐射粉末X射线衍射（angle-dispersive synchrotron powder X-ray diffraction），在最高35.4 GPa压力范围内采集衍射图谱，并结合衍射剖面、晶胞参数及状态方程（equation of state）分析。

Result: 立方相在9.5(3) GPa以上转变为四方相（P4/mnc），约14.2 GPa起出现单斜相（P21/n）；两相在14.2–17.2 GPa共存，17.9 GPa时单斜相为唯一相并稳定至最高压力。体积模量（bulk modulus）由立方相的26(2) GPa增至单斜相的71(3) GPa，表明跨越相变后压缩性显著降低。

Conclusion: 中间四方结构更可能由传压介质引起的非静水偏应力（non-hydrostatic deviatoric stress）导致的应力诱导对称性降低（symmetry lowering）所描述，而非独立热力学相。该结构序列与K2IrCl6类似但转变压力更低，且所有结构转变在卸压后均可逆。

Abstract: The structural behaviour of cubic (Fm-3m) K2OsCl6 under compression was investigated to 35.4 GPa using angle-dispersive synchrotron powder X-ray diffraction. Upon increasing pressure, K2OsCl6 undergoes a sequence of structural transformations from the ambient-pressure cubic phase to a tetragonal (P4/mnc) phase above 9.5(3) GPa, followed by a transition to a monoclinic (P21/n) phase beginning at approximately 14.2 GPa. The tetragonal and monoclinic phases coexist over the pressure range 14.2-17.2 GPa, while the monoclinic phase is observed as the sole phase at 17.9 GPa and remains stable up to the maximum pressure investigated. Analysis of the diffraction profiles and unit-cell parameters indicates that the intermediate tetragonal structure is likely associated with stress-induced symmetry lowering rather than a distinct thermodynamic phase. This distortion, which is adequately described by the tetragonal P4/mnc space group, is attributed to non-hydrostatic deviatoric stresses arising from the pressure-transmitting medium. Equation-of-state analysis reveals a pronounced reduction in compressibility across the structural transitions, with the bulk modulus increasing from 26(2) GPa for the cubic phase to 71(3) GPa for the monoclinic phase. The pressure-induced structural sequence is analogous to that reported for related compounds such as K2IrCl6, although the transitions in K2OsCl6 occur at lower pressures. All structural transformations are reversible upon decompression.

</details>


### [319] [Interfacial origin of magnetic compensation and topological-like Hall effect in crystalline lutetium iron garnet thin films.](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25965)
*Satakshi Pandey,Anne Forget,Sarah Clement,Christophe Gatel,B{é}n{é}dicte Warot- Fonrose,Gr{é}goire de Loubens,Jean-Baptiste Moussy*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 研究生长于SGGG(111)衬底上不同厚度的Lu3Fe5O12（LuIG）单晶薄膜，发现界面处存在约3 nm富Gd层并诱发约50 K的磁补偿温度，且LuIG/Pt体系的反常霍尔效应（AHE）呈现非单调特征，与Gd扩散引起的对称性破缺及DMI相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土铁石榴石（REIG）因高居里温度、低吉尔伯特阻尼和优良绝缘性而成为自旋器件重要材料，并被认为可承载手性自旋纹理；但LuIG薄膜的界面结构、化学与磁性行为及其对输运性质的影响尚需厘清。

Method: 在Gd3(Mg,Zr)Ga5O12(111)（SGGG(111)）衬底上制备不同厚度的LuIG单晶薄膜，表征其结构、化学与磁性；并在LuIG上覆盖超薄Pt层以测量反常霍尔效应（AHE），开展厚度依赖的AHE测量。

Result: 在LuIG/SGGG界面处发现约3 nm厚的富Gd LuIG层，导致约50 K的磁补偿温度；AHE回线除磁补偿点外还表现出显著的非单调特征，常被归因于自旋霍尔类拓扑霍尔效应（SH-THE）及DMI诱导的非共线自旋纹理；厚度依赖测量表明Gd扩散引起的成分变化破坏反演对称性，可能贡献于DMI。

Conclusion: 界面Gd扩散是调控LuIG磁性与AHE行为的关键因素，相关结果为磁性石榴石多界面的基础理解与工程化设计提供了依据，有利于先进自旋电子器件应用。

Abstract: Ferrimagnetic oxides such as rare-earth iron garnets (REIG) have emerged as essential components of spin-based devices owing to their high Curie temperatures, low Gilbert damping, and excellent insulating properties. Recently, REIGs have attracted immense interest for hosting robust chiral spin textures that can be potential information carriers in next-generation spintronic devices. Here we investigate the structural, chemical, and magnetic behaviour of single-crystal Lu3Fe5O12 (LuIG) thin films for different film thicknesses on a Gd3(Mg,Zr)Ga5O12(111) or SGGG(111) substrate. Our results demonstrate the presence of a 3 nm-thick Gd-rich LuIG layer at the LuIG/SGGG interface, which gives rise to a magnetic compensation temperature around 50 K. We further probe the anomalous Hall effect (AHE) in LuIG layers with an adjacent platinum ultrathin film. In addition to the magnetic compensation point, the AHE loops show pronounced nonmonotonic features, often attributed to the spin Hall topological-like Hall effect (SH-THE) and non-collinear spin textures arising from the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction (DMI). LuIG thickness-dependent AHE measurements reveal that the composition variation due to Gd diffusion breaks the inversion symmetry and could contribute to DMI. These findings shed light on fundamental understanding and engineering of the different interfaces of magnetic garnets for advanced spintronic device applications.

</details>


### [320] [Magnetoelectric Bulk Photovoltaic Effect in Centrosymmetric Solids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26057)
*Longjun Xiang,Fuming Xu,Lei Zhang,Jian Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作表明，中心对称固体可借助光电场与磁场分量的双线性耦合产生直流光电流，即磁电体光伏效应（MBPE），其源于Zeeman量子度规与Zeeman贝里曲率的偶极矩，并预言了单层Pt$_2$HgSe$_3$与交错磁体RuF$_4$中的可观测效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界普遍认为体光伏电流（bulk photovoltaic current）需要空间反演对称性破缺，本文旨在挑战这一传统认知，探索中心对称固体中产生直流光电流的可能性。

Method: 基于光电场与磁场分量的双线性耦合构建磁电体光伏效应（magnetoelectric bulk photovoltaic effect, MBPE）框架，揭示其量子几何（quantum-geometric）起源，并通过对称性分析识别四种MBPE机制及允许该效应的材料类别。

Result: 对称性分析表明MBPE在广泛类别的二维中心对称非磁与磁性材料中（正入射光下）均被允许；在单层Pt$_2$HgSe$_3$与交错磁体（altermagnetic）RuF$_4$中预言了约$1~\mathrm{mA/m}$量级的电流密度，且RuF$_4$中的类注入（injection-like）光电流在切换光螺旋度或Néel矢量时反号。

Conclusion: 研究将体光伏效应拓展至中心对称固体，并为交错磁序（altermagnetic order）的电学读出提供了新途径。

Abstract: It is commonly believed that a bulk photovoltaic current requires broken spatial inversion symmetry. However, in this work, we demonstrate that dc photocurrents can arise in centrosymmetric solids from the bilinear coupling between the electric and magnetic components of light. This magnetoelectric bulk photovoltaic effect (MBPE) has a quantum-geometric origin in the dipole moments of the Zeeman quantum metric and Zeeman Berry curvature. We identify four distinct MBPE mechanisms, including time-reversal-even and time-reversal-odd injection-like and shift-like photocurrents driven by linearly or circularly polarized light. Our symmetry analysis shows that the MBPE is allowed in broad classes of two-dimensional centrosymmetric nonmagnetic and magnetic materials under normally incident light. As representative examples, we predict sizable MBPEs in centrosymmetric monolayer Pt$_2$HgSe$_3$ and altermagnetic RuF$_4$, with current densities on the order of $1~\mathrm{mA/m}$. Notably, the injection-like photocurrent in RuF$_4$ reverses sign upon switching either the light helicity or the Néel vector. Our results extend the bulk photovoltaic effect to centrosymmetric solids and open new opportunities for electrical readout of altermagnetic order.

</details>


### [321] [Nanowire-Assisted Epitaxial Lateral Overgrowth of Planar GaN(0001) on Diamond(100)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26158)
*Brijmohan Paramanik,Mayur Shinde,Subhranshu Sekhar Sahu,Soumyadip Chatterjee,Prajwal Pandey,Anshul Jain,Himanjal Bhattacharyya,Alpesh Donga,Apurba Laha*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究采用等离子体辅助分子束外延（PAMBE）结合纳米线辅助外延横向过生长（ELOG），在金刚石（100）衬底上生长纤锌矿 GaN，并证明该策略可有效抑制位错传播并恢复接近体材料的晶格参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GaN-on-diamond 异质结构在高功率、高频电子器件中具有前景，但金刚石（100）上的直接外延生长受严重晶格与热失配限制，导致高位错密度，因此需要有效的缺陷抑制生长策略。

Method: 使用 PAMBE 在金刚石（100）衬底上通过纳米线辅助 ELOG 生长纤锌矿 GaN，设置两种纳米线模板（NW100、NW600）与无纳米线参考样（NW000）进行对比，并借助高分辨 XRD、φ 扫描、（0002）极图、倒易空间 mapping 及截面 TEM 进行表征。

Result: NW100 获得最低位错密度（5.51×10⁻⁴ nm⁻²）与微应变（2.736×10⁻³）、最窄的（0002）衍射峰，c 轴晶格参数为 5.1821 Å，与体材料相差 0.06%；NW000 则表现为 c 轴收缩（5.1633 Å）和更高位错密度（6.26×10⁻⁴ nm⁻²），与残余张应力一致。截面 TEM 证实了金刚石/AlN/纳米线/ELOG/平面 GaN 的层序，并观察到穿透位错弯折为近横向轨迹。

Conclusion: 纳米线辅助 ELOG 是抑制位错传播、恢复近体材料晶格参数的有效策略，为 GaN-on-diamond 异质外延提供了结构明确的器件集成平台。

Abstract: GaN-on-diamond heterostructures are promising for high-power and high-frequency electronics, however, direct epi- taxial growth on diamond (100) remains challenging due to severe lattice and thermal mismatches, which induce high threading dislocation densities. To overcome this challenge, we have used Plasma Assisted Molecular Beam Epitaxy (PAMBE) to grow wurtzite GaN layers on diamond(100) substrates using nanowire-assisted epitaxial lateral overgrowth (ELOG). Two nanowire templates (NW100, NW600) were benchmarked against a nanowire-free reference (NW000). High-resolution XRD, φ -scans, (0002) pole figures, and reciprocal space mapping show that NW100 attains the lowest dislocation density (5.51 x 10-4 nm-2) and microstrain (2.736 x 10-3), the narrowest (0002) reflection, and a c-axis lattice parameter (5.1821 Å) within 0.06% of the bulk value; NW000, in contrast, exhibits a contracted c-axis (5.1633 Å) and elevated dislocation density (6.26 x 10-4 nm-2), consistent with residual tensile stress. Cross-sectional TEM con- firms the intended diamond/AlN/nanowire/ELOG/planar-GaN layer sequence and shows threading dislocations bending into near-lateral trajectories, directly evidencing defect filtering by the nanowire template. Together, these results estab- lish nanowire-assisted ELOG as an effective strategy to suppress dislocation propagation and recover near-bulk lattice parameters in GaN-on-diamond heteroepitaxy, providing a structurally well-defined platform for device integration.

</details>


### [322] [Hidden piezo-Hall effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26293)
*San-Dong Guo,Shi-Hao Zhang,Bingwen Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出“隐藏压霍尔效应（hidden piezo-Hall effect）”：应变可在各扇区（sectors）中诱导有限的反常霍尔电导（AHC），而全局AHC保持为零，并以d波隐藏交错磁体（hidden altermagnet）RbV2Te2O为候选材料进行论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐藏自旋极化（hidden spin polarization）等隐藏现象已在量子材料中广泛确立，本文旨在将“隐藏物理”拓展至应变调控的输运领域，探索全局响应为零而分扇区响应非零的新型霍尔现象。

Method: 采用紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）并结合第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），针对实验已合成的材料RbV2Te2O建立d波隐藏交错磁体（hidden altermagnet）体系，考察单轴应变（uniaxial strain）下各扇区AHC及应变方向旋转（x轴至y轴）的响应。

Result: 零应变时全系统及各扇区均无反常霍尔效应（AHE）；施加单轴应变后，扇区AHC出现且符号相反、相互抵消。将应变方向从x轴旋转至y轴会使扇区AHC符号反转，但不产生净霍尔响应。

Conclusion: 该效应可推广至隐藏压磁光（hidden piezomagneto-optical）、压热霍尔（piezo-thermal Hall）、压能斯特（piezo-Nernst）与压埃廷斯豪森（piezo-Ettingshausen）等效应，并将隐藏物理扩展至应变控制的输运，同时认定RbV2Te2O为实验验证的有前景候选材料。

Abstract: Extensive studies of hidden spin polarization have established a broad class of hidden phenomena in quantum materials. Here, we introduce the hidden piezo-Hall effect, in which strain induces finite anomalous Hall conductivities (AHCs) in individual sectors while the global AHC remains zero. At zero strain, neither the full system nor its sectors exhibit an anomalous Hall effect (AHE). Under uniaxial strain, however, opposite sector AHCs emerge while their sum remains canceled. We establish this effect for a $d$-wave hidden altermagnet using a tight-binding model and first-principles calculations for the experimentally synthesized material $\mathrm{RbV_2Te_2O}$. Rotating the strain direction from the $x$ to the $y$ axis reverses the signs of the sector AHCs without generating a net Hall response. The same symmetry mechanism may also give rise to hidden piezomagneto-optical, piezo-thermal Hall, piezo-Nernst, and piezo-Ettingshausen effects. Our work extends hidden physics to strain-controlled transport and identifies $\mathrm{RbV_2Te_2O}$ as a promising candidate for experimental verification.

</details>


### [323] [Machine Learning-Assisted Discovery of Carbon-Related Defect Formation Mechanisms in Diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26301)
*Grace Huang,Christopher Fivecoat,Xun Xu,Mengen Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过微调面向金刚石缺陷的通用机器学习原子间势（machine learning interatomic potential, MLIP），揭示了中性双间隙（di-interstitial）与双空位（divacancy）在动力学驱动下的形成与解离机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金刚石是固态自旋量子比特等功能缺陷的重要宿主材料；辐照与热退火过程中产生的碳相关缺陷（空位、间隙原子）会迁移并形成新的缺陷复合物，其动力学形成机制尚不清晰。

Method: 利用少量密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算数据微调（fine-tune）通用机器学习原子间势，并以此模拟中性电荷态下双间隙与双空位的形成路径与势垒。

Result: 得到单间隙迁移势垒为1.84 eV；两个初始分离的单间隙仅需跨越更低势垒即可形成双间隙构型。此外，MLIP还识别出训练数据之外的一种新双间隙构型形成路径以及逐步式双空位解离机制。

Conclusion: 结果揭示了迁移辅助的缺陷复合物形成机制，并表明有针对性的MLIP训练可有效识别反应路径。

Abstract: Diamond is an important host material for functional defects such as solid-state spin qubits. During the defect creation by irradiation and subsequent thermal annealing, carbon-related defects, including vacancies and interstitials, are generated and can migrate to form new defect complexes. To investigate this kinetically driven process and reveal the formation mechanisms of the di-interstitials and di-vacancies in the neutral charge state, we fine-tuned a universal machine learning interatomic potential using a small dataset generated from density functional theory calculations. We obtained a barrier of 1.84 eV for mono-interstitial migration and found that two initially separated mono-interstitials only need to overcome a lower barrier to form di-interstitial configurations. Beyond the training data, the MLIP also identified the formation pathway for an additional di-interstitial configuration and a stepwise divacancy dissociation mechanism. These results reveal migration-assisted defect complex formation mechanisms and demonstrate how targeted MLIP training can identify reaction pathways.

</details>


### [324] [La14.33(CN3)8N, a Nitridocarbonate based on the (CN3)5- Ion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26307)
*Markus Ströbele,Mykhailo Brytskyi,Carl P. Romao,Scott Kroeker,Hans-Jürgen Meyer*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过固相复分解反应合成新型氮碳化合物 La14.33(CN3)8N，并借助热分析、单晶X射线衍射、中红外与多核NMR等手段完成结构与身份表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 含 (NCN)2- 的金属氮碳化合物已广泛建立，但含 (CN3)5-、(C2N4)6-、(C3N6)6- 等离子的化合物合成仍具挑战性，相关物种虽有报道却难以制备，本研究旨在拓展此类化合物的合成与表征。

Method: 以 LaF3、Na2(CN2) 与 Li3N 混合物为原料，在开放体系 550 °C 下经常规固相复分解反应（solid-state metathesis reaction）合成；结合热分析（thermoanalysis）研究反应条件及生成与分解行为，并以单晶X射线衍射（single-crystal X-ray diffraction）精修晶体结构，辅以中红外（mid-IR）与 23Na、7Li、13C 核磁共振（NMR）表征。

Result: 成功合成 La14.33(CN3)8N；其晶体结构经单晶X射线衍射精修，报道了结构中两种晶体学不等价 (CN3)5- 离子对应的中红外振动频率；23Na、7Li、13C NMR 结果为该化合物身份提供佐证；其晶体结构与 Ce15(BN3)8N 同型（isotypic）。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种在开放体系、相对温和温度下制备含 (CN3)5- 离子氮碳化合物的可行路线，并通过多手段表征确认其结构与组成，为拓展非 (NCN)2- 类氮碳化合物体系提供了实例。

Abstract: Metal nitridocarbonates containing the (NCN)2- ion are established for a multitude of metal atoms throughout the periodic table. The preparation of nitridocarbonate compounds containing a (CN3)5-, (C2N4)6-, or (C3N6)6- ion remains challenging, although corresponding species have been established. The new compound La14.33(CN3)8N was synthesized in a conventional solid-state metathesis reaction from a mixture of LaF3, Na2(CN2) and Li3N in an open system at 550 °C. The reaction conditions, including the formation and de-composition of La14.33(CN3)8N are analyzed on basis of thermoanalytic studies. The crystal structure of La14.33(CN3)8N was refined by single-crystal X-ray diffraction, and the mid-IR frequencies resulting from two crystallographically distinct (CN3)5- ions in the structure are reported. 23Na, 7Li and 13C NMR studies provide additional support for the identity of the compound. The crystal structure of La14.33(CN3)8N is isotypic to that of Ce15(BN3)8N.

</details>


### [325] [Investigating relative population partitioning in the presence of B$^-_\text{C}$ defects in diamond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26332)
*Rodrick Kuate Defo,Steven L. Richardson*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 基于已有电荷态衰变形式化理论，分析含B$^-_{\rm C}$、N$_{\rm C}$及醚终止表面的金刚石中电荷相对布居分配；通过NV$^-$与N$_{\rm C}$间电荷转移时标的实验数据推断必定存在第三种缺陷（即替位硼B$_{\rm C}$）调制N$_{\rm C}$的布居。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在含多种缺陷（施主与受主）的金刚石样品中，缺陷间电荷转移会改变各自电荷态的布居，但影响局域相对布居分配的因素尚需厘清，尤其是替位硼杂质在其中的作用。

Method: 沿用作者此前提出的电荷态衰变形式化框架：给定两个缺陷（一施主一受主）的电荷跃迁能级、相对布居和温度，即可完全确定两者间电荷转移的时标。结合Z. Yuan等（Phys. Rev. Res. 2, 033263, 2020）关于NV$^-$与醚终止表面杂化N$_{\rm C}$间电荷转移时标的实验测量及温度与布居数据进行分析。

Result: 仅用NV$^-$与N$_{\rm C}$两缺陷无法解释观测到的相对布居，需引入额外缺陷调制；N$_{\rm C}$（与醚终止表面杂化）局域费米能级的移动与替位硼杂质B$_{\rm C}$引起的效应一致。

Conclusion: 结果表明B$_{\rm C}$是调制N$_{\rm C}$相对布居的额外缺陷，且该方法可用于确定电离缺陷的局域相对浓度。

Abstract: Leveraging our earlier formalism for charge-state decay [see R. Kuate Defo et al., 108 235208 (2023)], we investigate relative population partitioning in the presence of singly negatively charged substitutional boron impurities (B$^-_{\rm C}$) in diamond samples, where these diamond samples also contain substitutional nitrogen (N$_{\rm C }$) impurities and have ether-terminated surfaces. Effectively, given any two defects, one donor and one acceptor, such that their charge-transition levels, their relative populations, and the temperature are known, the timescale for charge transfer between the two defects is completely determined. We use this insight and experimental measurements for the timescale for charge transfer between NV$^-$ centers and N$_{\rm C}$ hybridized with the ether-terminated surface of diamond, as well as the temperature and the relative populations for the NV$^-$ centers, [see Z. Yuan et al., Phys. Rev. Res. 2, 033263 (2020)] to argue that an additional defect must be modulating the relative populations of N$_{\rm C}$ hybridized with the ether-terminated surface of diamond. The shifts in the local Fermi level for N$_{\rm C}$ hybridized with the ether-terminated surface of diamond are consistent with the additional defect being a substitutional boron impurity (B$_{\rm C}$). Ultimately, our results allow for the determination of local relative concentrations of ionized defects.

</details>


### [326] [Atomic-level study of dose-dependent lattice thermal conductivity degradation in irradiated tungsten](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26502)
*Mary K Chessey,José Julio Gutiérrez Moreno,Sajjan Singh Mehta,Paolo Settembri,Arda Erbasan,Dominik Freinberger,Marina García Romero,Mervi J Mantsinen*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合非平衡分子动力学（Non-Equilibrium Molecular Dynamics, NEMD）与创建-弛豫算法（Creation Relaxation Algorithm, CRA），并引入机器学习（Machine Learning, ML）模型，定量研究辐照钨（tungsten, W）中缺陷特征与晶格热导率（lattice thermal conductivity, kappa）退化的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未来聚变堆面向等离子体部件（plasma-facing components, PFCs）需要钨等高性能材料，但中子辐照（neutron irradiation）产生的纳米尺度缺陷会严重削弱其热输运等关键性能，因此需要厘清缺陷类型与分布对热导率的影响机制。

Method: 采用NEMD模拟配合CRA算法，构建辐照剂量最高至1.8 canonical displacements per atom（cdpa）的多种缺陷结构（空位与自间隙原子（self-interstitial atom, SIA）团簇）；将空间分辨温度梯度与局部缺陷浓度关联，并用ML模型建立缺陷特征与热导率退化的定量关系。

Result: ML分析表明空位密度（vacancy density）是控制热输运的主导结构因素，kappa随缺陷密度增加呈显著非线性抑制；预测性多缺陷映射揭示了在0.05 at.%的临界空位阈值处出现阶梯式退化转变，超过该阈值后声子散射（phonon scattering）完全饱和，相对晶格热导率的最大退化被限制在约30 at.%。

Conclusion: 该集成模拟与机器学习的方法为量化辐照聚变材料中结构与热导率之间的复杂关系提供了具有预测能力的框架，可辅助PFCs材料的辐照耐受性评估与设计。

Abstract: Plasma-facing components (PFCs) in future fusion reactors must withstand extreme environments, making tungsten (W) a prime candidate material due to its high thermal conductivity, high melting temperature, low sputtering yield, low tritium absorption. However, neutron irradiation introduces complex nanostructural defects that severely degrade these critical properties. In this work, we use Non-Equilibrium Molecular Dynamics (NEMD) simulations coupled with the Creation Relaxation Algorithm (CRA) to investigate the lattice thermal conductivity (kappa) of W across a wide range of irradiation doses. Systems treated with different doses up to 1.8 canonical displacements per atom (cdpa) were simulated to generate and study diverse defect structures, including vacancies and self-interstitial atom (SIA) clusters. By correlating spatially-resolved temperature gradients with local defect concentrations, we show that even small changes in defect type and distribution significantly affect thermal transport. We employ Machine Learning (ML) models to develop a quantitative relationship between the defect features and the resulting thermal conductivity degradation. The ML analysis identifies vacancy density as the dominant structural factor controlling thermal transport. We found a significant, nonlinear suppression of kappa with increasing defect density. Predictive multi-defect mapping built upon our ML model resolved a step-like degradation transition at a critical vacancy threshold of 0.05 at.%, beyond which phonon scattering completely saturated and capped the maximum relative lattice thermal conductivity degradation at approximately 30 at.%. This integrated simulation and ML approach provides a powerful, predictive framework for quantifying the complex relationship between structure and thermal conductivity in irradiated fusion materials.

</details>


### [327] [Stabilization of Ferroelectric Domains via Interfacial Strain in HoFeO3- BaTiO3 Nanofiber Heterostructures for Enhanced Hybrid Energy Harvesting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26611)
*Smita Chaturvedi,Swathi Krishna,Shivshankar Jokare,Supriya Sahoo,Vikash Kushwaha,Priyank Shyam,Mandar Shirolkar,R. Boomishankar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过交替溶胶-凝胶静电纺丝制备 HoFeO3-BaTiO3（HFO-BTO）纳米纤维异质结构，利用界面晶格畸变稳定铁电畴并提升机电输出，器件功率密度达 24.31 µW/cm³，为纯 BaTiO3 器件的 2.4 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 压电纳米发电机（piezoelectric nanogenerators, PENGs）作为自供电机械能收集平台受限于退极化场（depolarization field）、电荷泄漏及应力向活性偶极子的传递效率低下，这一问题在以聚合物为主的复合材料中尤为突出。

Method: 采用交替溶胶-凝胶静电纺丝（alternating sol-gel electrospinning）制备 HFO-BTO 纳米纤维异质结构，使正交相 HoFeO3（Pbnm）与四方相 BaTiO3（P4mm）紧密共存；结合 Rietveld 精修 XRD、高分辨透射电镜（HRTEM）与拉曼光谱表征界面晶格畸变，并以极化-电场（P-E）回线评估铁电行为，最后将纤维封装于聚二甲基硅氧烷（PDMS）中在循环冲击下测试输出。

Result: 异质结构零场极化与 BaTiO3 相当（16.39 对 15.80 µC/cm²），但翻转电流峰值移向更高场强（约 7 对约 3.5 kV/cm），符合应变稳定铁电畴特征；器件呈现压电与摩擦电（triboelectric）混合输出，开路电压峰值约 +6 V 与 −8 V，在 4.7 MΩ 负载下功率密度为 24.31 µW/cm³，分别为同工艺 BaTiO3 器件（10.00 µW/cm³）的 2.4 倍与 HoFeO3 器件（2.78 µW/cm³）的 8.7 倍。

Conclusion: 研究将界面驱动的晶格畸变与机电输出提升建立关联，并表明全氧化物（all-oxide）纳米纤维异质结构是无铅（lead-free）能量收集中具有前景的技术路径。

Abstract: Piezoelectric nanogenerators (PENGs) are an attractive platform for self-powered mechanical-energy harvesting, but their output is often limited by depolarization fields, charge leakage, and inefficient stress transfer to the active dipoles, particularly in polymer-dominated composites. Here we report HoFeO3-BaTiO3 (HFO-BTO) nanofiber heterostructures prepared by alternating sol-gel electrospinning, in which orthorhombic HoFeO3 (Pbnm) and tetragonal BaTiO3 (P4mm) coexist in intimate contact. Rietveld-refined X-ray diffraction, high-resolution transmission electron microscopy, and Raman spectroscopy indicate interfacial lattice distortion. Polarization-electric field loops show that the heterostructure retains a polarization comparable to that of BaTiO3 (16.39 vs 15.80 microcoulombs per square centimeter at zero field) while its switching-current maximum moves to a higher field (about 7 vs about 3.5 kilovolts per centimeter), consistent with strain-stabilized ferroelectric domains. When the nanofibers are encapsulated in polydimethylsiloxane (PDMS) and excited under cyclic impact, the device produces a hybrid output: the piezoelectric response of the strained BaTiO3 is supplemented by a triboelectric contribution from contact electrification. The HFO-BTO device delivers open-circuit voltage peaks of about +6 volts and -8 volts and a power density of 24.31 microwatts per cubic centimeter at a 4.7 megaohm load. This is 2.4 times the value for an identically fabricated BaTiO3 device (10.00 microwatts per cubic centimeter) and 8.7 times that of the HoFeO3 device (2.78 microwatts per cubic centimeter). These results link interface-driven lattice distortion to improved electromechanical output and identify all-oxide nanofiber heterostructures as a promising route to lead-free energy harvesting.

</details>


### [328] [Vacancy and Helium-Cluster Thermomigration in Tungsten](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26667)
*Sanad Alturk,Enrique Martinez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作将原子尺度跳跃统计（jump-statistics）框架推广到钨中单个空位（vacancy）与 He$_1$–He$_4$ 团簇，定量给出其热迁移（thermomigration）的方向性偏置与输运热系数（heat-of-transport coefficient），并表明热迁移行为无法由团簇尺寸、扩散率或迁移能单独推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钨等离子体面向部件（plasma-facing components）中存在的强温度梯度（thermal gradients）会偏置辐照缺陷（irradiation-induced defects）的迁移，从而改变其再分布、相互作用与滞留行为；需在原子尺度量化各缺陷的热迁移，并为更高尺度模型提供参数。

Method: 基于非平衡分子动力学（non-equilibrium molecular-dynamics）在多个热梯度下求方向性跳跃比（directional jump ratio），结合几何推导的投影跳跃长度（projected jump lengths）将方向性偏置系数转化为输运热系数 $Q^*=\alpha kT^2$；另以 1000–1600 K 恒温模拟评估有效迁移能（effective migration energy）。

Result: 所有研究缺陷的 $\alpha$ 均为负值，对应净迁移朝向热区；He$_4$ 响应最强（$\alpha=-2.2457\times10^{-2}$ K$^{-1}$），空位次之，He$_3$ 最弱。恒温模拟中 He$_3$ 迁移性最低、$E_m^{\mathrm{eff}}=0.271$ eV，He$_4$ 迁移性高于 He$_3$ 且 $E_m^{\mathrm{eff}}=0.087$ eV 最小。跳跃统计预测的 He$_3$ 浓度剖面与长分子动力学轨迹结果一致，构成独立验证。

Conclusion: 热迁移不能由团簇尺寸、扩散率或迁移能单独推断；所得缺陷特异的输运热参数可支撑聚变相关热梯度下钨的更高尺度模型。

Abstract: Strong thermal gradients in tungsten plasma-facing components can bias the migration of irradiation-induced defects and thereby alter their redistribution, interaction, and retention. Here, we extend an atomistic jump-statistics framework previously developed for hydrogen to quantify the thermomigration of a single vacancy and interstitial He$_1$--He$_4$ clusters in body-centered-cubic tungsten. Non-equilibrium molecular-dynamics simulations performed over several thermal gradients are used to determine the directional jump ratio, while geometrically derived projected jump lengths convert the fitted directional-bias coefficient into the heat-of-transport coefficient $Q^*=αkT^2$, where $k$ is the Boltzmann constant, $T$ the temperature, and $α$ a constant. All investigated defects exhibit negative values of $α$, corresponding to net migration toward the hot region. He$_4$ has the largest response, $α=-2.2457\times10^{-2}$ K$^{-1}$, followed closely by the vacancy, whereas He$_3$ exhibits the weakest response despite its intermediate cluster size. Independent constant-temperature simulations over 1000--1600 K show that He$_3$ is also the least mobile cluster and has the largest effective migration energy, $E_m^{\mathrm{eff}}=0.271$ eV. In contrast, He$_4$ is more mobile than He$_3$ and has the smallest effective migration energy, 0.087 eV. Agreement between the He$_3$ concentration profile predicted from the jump statistics and that obtained from a long molecular-dynamics trajectory provides an independent validation of the method. These results show that thermomigration cannot be inferred from cluster size, diffusivity, or migration energy alone and provide defect-specific heat-of-transport parameters for higher-length-scale models of tungsten under fusion-relevant thermal gradients.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [329] [Self-organizing reinforcement learning: a swarm approaches the explore-exploit optimum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25113)
*Galen J. Wilkerson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 无学习者、无奖励信号的极简觅食群体（foraging swarm）通过重尾（Lévy）飞行、阈值级联招募与拥挤分散的循环，可动态趋近多智能体资源分配博弈的理想自由分布（ideal free distribution, IFD）均衡；其优势在于零样本预算，而非渐近性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文聚焦强化学习（reinforcement learning, RL）的核心问题——探索与利用的权衡（explore/exploit trade-off），旨在检验一个不依赖学习器与奖励信号的最小群体能否仅凭自组织机制逼近RL所追求的最优分配。

Method: 主体由重尾（Lévy）飞行探索、邻近网络上阈值级联（threshold cascade）招募邻居、以及斑块拥挤时分散三部分构成；通过比较移除“招募—移动”耦合的对照组、以及零训练群体与PPO和集中式评论家（CTDE）学习者的训练曲线来完成评估。

Result: 群体仅达到最优的形状而非精确均衡：已投入觅食者的数量随资源丰富度变化的斜率为0.94，且仍保持可测量的可利用性；约150步动态逼近。移除耦合后匹配度从0.94骤降至0.08。同具身环境中，零奖励群体取得经验证神谕（oracle）上限约65%，约4,000/15,000训练步后被PPO与CTDE超越（约74–75%，16个随机种子均高于群体均值）；更简单的非具身博弈中学习器更精确。

Conclusion: 该机制未评估收益、也未累积学习增益，故“RL”只体现在问题层面与所达分配上，而非微观规则；其应被视为一个强的零样本、无奖励运行点（operating point），表明极简群体的自组织物理可承载接近RL目标的集体行为，优势在于预算而非渐近上限。

Abstract: The explore/exploit trade-off is the core problem of reinforcement learning (RL). We show that a minimal foraging swarm approaches its optimum - the ideal-free / Nash allocation of a multi-agent resource-sharing game (an equilibrium, not a return maximum) - with no learner and no reward signal. Agents explore by heavy-tailed (Levy) flights, recruit neighbours to a discovery through a threshold cascade on their proximity network, and disperse as a patch crowds; this cascade cycle balances exploration and exploitation and drives foragers toward the ideal free distribution. The swarm attains the shape of this optimum (committed foragers track richness with slope 0.94) but not its exact equilibrium - it stays measurably exploitable - reaching it dynamically (~150 steps) through the coupling of recruitment to movement: remove the coupling and matching collapses (0.94 -> 0.08). The RL sits at the level of the problem and the allocation reached, not the micro-rule: no step evaluates payoff (reward-free by construction, though the crowd signal is informationally tied to payoff), and the swarm reaches the allocation without learning it (quality plateaus and re-settles after resources move, no cumulative gain). Against a trained baseline the comparison is two-sided: in an easier, disembodied game a learner is more precise, while in the same embodied environment the reward-free swarm reaches ~65% of a validated oracle ceiling at zero training and leads until trained PPO and centralised-critic (CTDE) learners overtake it (~74-75%, 16 seeds, all above the swarm mean) after ~4,000 of 15,000 training steps - its advantage is in budget, not asymptote. In this one setting we read it not as beating RL but as a strong zero-shot, reward-free operating point: a demonstration that the self-organizing physics of a minimal swarm can carry a collective close to the optimum RL seeks, with none of RL's machinery.

</details>


### [330] [Power-Law Correlations Without Apparent Temperature-Tuned Criticality in Fixed-Tree Hierarchical Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24334)
*Marcel Kühn,Bernd Rosenow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在受控的分层树状概率上下文无关文法（probabilistic context-free grammar）中研究采样温度是否引发相变，发现互信息始终由幂律包络描述，未观察到有序相特征的长程平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 采样温度（sampling temperature）会显著改变自回归生成（autoregressive generation），但温度引起的长程关联变化究竟反映真正的相变（phase transition）还是统计量的平滑变化，尚无定论；需在剥离训练与架构因素的受控环境中建立基线。

Method: 构建有限深度、固定分层树上的概率上下文无关文法，用信念传播（belief propagation）精确计算下一词条件概率，经 softmax 温度重标定后进行自回归采样，并测量两词互信息（mutual information）随间距的变化；另通过近似映射到树上的 Ising 模型进行解释。

Result: 在所测试的温度与文法系综（grammar ensembles）中，互信息均被幂律包络良好描述；温度改变关联强度与拟合指数，但在可达系统尺寸内既无有序相的长距离平台，也无向短程指数衰减的明确交叉（crossover）。

Conclusion: 树上的 Ising 模型近似映射表明，树距离的指数衰减可转化为词距离的幂律，因此幂律无需调至特殊解码温度即可持续存在；该基线厘清了分层结构本身可产生的温度效应，为后续引入训练、近似误差或神经架构提供参照。

Abstract: Sampling temperature can substantially reshape autoregressive generation, but it is unclear when temperature changes in long-range correlations reflect a genuine phase transition rather than a smooth change in statistics. We study this question in a controlled analogue of temperature sampling: a finite-depth probabilistic context-free grammar on a fixed hierarchical tree, where exact next-token conditionals are computed by belief propagation and then rescaled by a softmax temperature before autoregressive sampling. We measure two-point mutual information between tokens as a function of separation. Across the tested temperatures and grammar ensembles, mutual information remains well described by a power-law envelope. Temperature changes the correlation magnitude and fitted exponent, but within the accessible system sizes we find no long-distance plateau characteristic of an ordered phase and no clear crossover to short-range exponential decay. An approximate mapping to an Ising model on a tree explains how exponential decay in tree distance becomes a power law in token distance, allowing power laws to persist without tuning to a special decoding temperature. This controlled baseline clarifies which temperature effects can arise from hierarchical structure alone, before adding training, approximation error, or neural architecture.

</details>


### [331] [Emergent Sparsity: How Many Effective Variables Survive Coarse-Graining in AlphaEarth Embeddings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25258)
*Johannes Hirn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将AlphaEarth的64维像元向量重构为有序的全局非线性坐标，并把有效维度（effective dimension）刻画为重建容差与空间尺度构成的双控制曲面；在3%容差下，有效维数随尺度从10 m到78.9 km由24降至7，而PCA秩仅由46降至38。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AlphaEarth为每个10 m陆地像元提供64分量向量以支持多种全球制图任务，但许多科学应用运行在更粗的空间支持上，所需有效变量可能远少于64个；因此需要检验这些向量能否被重组为有序非线性坐标、在给定重建精度下需要多少维，以及该数量是否随空间尺度下降。

Method: 在每个空间尺度上，采用统一架构的β-VAE扫描（β-VAE scan）生成秩—失真包络（rank–distortion envelope），从而给出扫描族内在各重建容差下达到的最小保留维数；分析以容差与空间尺度为两个控制变量，并在单位范数（unit-norm）目标上重复验证。

Result: 在3%验证容差（validation tolerance）下，保留维数在尺度由10 m增至78.9 km时从24降至7；相比之下，对应PCA秩仅由46降至38。在单位范数目标上重复分析，该趋势依然保持。

Conclusion: 有效维度并非单一内在数值，而应表述为随容差与空间尺度变化的二维曲面；非线性方法（β-VAE）相比线性PCA能更明显地利用尺度增大带来的维度压缩，且该结论对目标归一化方式具有稳健性。

Abstract: AlphaEarth encodes each 10 m land pixel as a 64-component vector intended to support diverse global mapping tasks. Yet many scientific applications work at coarser spatial supports, for which fewer than 64 effective variables may suffice. We ask whether these vectors can be reorganized into ranked global nonlinear coordinates, how many suffice at a chosen reconstruction accuracy, and whether that number decreases with spatial scale. We thus describe effective dimension as a two-control surface over tolerance and spatial scale, rather than one intrinsic number. At each scale, a $β$-VAE scan with a common architecture produces a rank--distortion envelope, giving the smallest retained count achieved within the scanned family at each reconstruction tolerance. At 3% validation tolerance, this number falls from 24 to 7 when the scale is increased from 10 m to 78.9 km, whereas the corresponding PCA rank falls only from 46 to 38. Repeating the analysis on unit-norm targets preserves this trend.

</details>


### [332] [Dynamical Batching: How Class-Cycling Training Shapes the Internal Structure of Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26156)
*Nicolás Ratier-Werbin,Samuel Lozano,Miguel Ruiz-García*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出动态批采样（Dynamical Batching, DB），通过周期性轮换强调各类别来改变训练批次类别组成，从而在网络容量受限时改善拟合与测试精度，并重塑内部权重组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受非平衡系统（nonequilibrium systems）中"在相关子目标间循环可提升切换效率"的启发，研究训练批次类别组成的时间分布是否既能改变神经网络性能、又能改变其内部组织。

Method: 提出动态批采样（DB）协议：周期性强调每个类别，但保持损失函数与优化器不变；以均匀批采样（Uniform Batching, UB）为对照（每个小批量类别比例相同）。在合成数据集（如三臂螺旋）和 CIFAR-10 上，用容量受限的 MLP、通道缩减的 ResNet-10 及另一个容量受限卷积网络比较两种协议，并追踪单个权重的变化。

Result: 在标准训练失效的区域，DB 改变了结果：三臂螺旋上 UB 远低于完美精度的容量受限 MLP 在 DB 下能拟合训练数据。DB 下类别专用状态间的切换由更少的权重承担，且这些权重跨周期更具持续性，在所有合成数据集及两种协议均能拟合的更宽网络中均成立。CIFAR-10 上 DB 使通道缩减 ResNet-10 和另一容量受限卷积网络的测试精度略有提升。

Conclusion: 类别强调在时间上的分布方式可同时塑造网络达到的解与网络内部结构；上述权重组织是协议本身的特征而非其收益的预测指标，因为它同样出现在 DB 未改变精度的情形中。

Abstract: We investigate whether cyclically varying the class composition of training batches can modify both the performance and the internal organization of neural networks. Motivated by nonequilibrium systems, in which cycling between related sub-goals can make switching between their solutions increasingly efficient, we introduce Dynamical Batching (DB), a sampling protocol that periodically emphasizes each class while leaving the loss and the optimizer unchanged, and compare it with Uniform Batching (UB), in which every mini-batch has equal class proportions. In the regime where standard training fails, DB changes the outcome: on a three-arm spiral, a capacity-limited MLP that plateaus far below perfect accuracy under UB fits the training data under DB. Tracking individual weights, we find that under DB the change between class-specialized states is carried by a smaller set of weights than under UB, and that this set is markedly more persistent from cycle to cycle, on every synthetic dataset we test and in a wider network that fits the data under both protocols. This organization is a signature of the protocol rather than a predictor of its benefit: it appears equally where DB leaves accuracy unchanged. On CIFAR-10, the same protocol modestly improves the test accuracy of a channel-narrowed ResNet-10, a standard residual architecture, and of a second capacity-limited convolutional network. These results show that how class emphasis is distributed in time can shape both the solution a network reaches and the internal structure of the network.

</details>


<div id='math.PR'></div>

# math.PR [[Back]](#toc)

### [333] [Almost-optimal bulk regularity conditions in the CLT for G$β$E](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24211)
*Albert Zhang*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文研究高斯 β-系综（Gaussian β-ensemble）的线性统计量（linear statistics）$\sum_{k=1}^N f(x_k)$ 的中心极限定理（CLT），将 LaSo22 仅针对 Wigner 矩阵的结果推广至一般 β，并证明对任意 $f \in H^{1/2+\varepsilon}$ 的 CLT 成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作 LaSo22 仅处理 Wigner 矩阵情形，其结论未覆盖一般 β 的高斯 β-系综；本文旨在将该 CLT 结果推广到任意 β 的一般设定。

Method: 引入 LaSo22 未使用的两项新工具：协方差（covariance）的 Helffer-Sjöstrand 表示（该技术在随机矩阵语境中最早见于 ErYa15），并结合 Dyson 布朗运动（DBM）的特征流估计（characteristic flow estimates，最早见于 Bo22）。

Result: 证明了对于任意 $f \in H^{1/2+\varepsilon}$，高斯 β-系综线性统计量的 CLT 成立，从而在一般 β 下扩展了 LaSo22 的结论。

Conclusion: 通过 Helffer-Sjöstrand 表示与 DBM 特征流估计的组合，可将线性统计量的 CLT 从 Wigner 矩阵推广至一般 β 的高斯 β-系综，且所需正则性条件为 $f \in H^{1/2+\varepsilon}$。

Abstract: We consider linear statistics $\sum_{k=1}^N f(x_k)$ of a Gaussian $\b$-ensemble. Following the work of \cite{LaSo22}, which was concerned only with Wigner matrices, we extend their results for general $\b$. In particular, we show that the CLT holds for any $f \in H^{1/2 + \e}$. The novel input which was not used in \cite{LaSo22} is the Helffer-Sjöstrand representation for the covariance, first appearing the random matrix context in \cite{ErYa15}, coupled with characteristic flow estimates for DBM, first appearing in \cite{Bo22}.

</details>


### [334] [Berry--Esseen Bounds for High-Temperature Ising Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25746)
*Timothy M. Garoni,Aram Perez*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 本文研究 Z^d 上最近邻 Ising 模型（Ising model）在高低温区（high-temperature regime）的磁化强度（magnetisation）的正态逼近，利用 Stein 方法（Stein's method）分析连续时间 Ising heat-bath 动力学的半群，给出最优 Berry–Esseen 界（Berry–Esseen bounds），并揭示边界条件对收敛速率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献对 Ising 模型磁化强度的正态逼近缺乏在任意有限区域、任意边界条件下的尖锐刻画，尤其缺少最优 Berry–Esseen 型误差界以及对无限体积极性（infinite-volume susceptibility）为目标时收敛速率的精确分析。

Method: 采用 Stein 方法（Stein's method）量化正态逼近误差，核心手段是分析连续时间 Ising heat-bath 动力学（continuous-time Ising heat-bath dynamics）的半群（semigroup）；结论同时覆盖有限体 Gibbs 测度（finite-volume Gibbs measures，任意边界条件）与无限体 Gibbs 测度（infinite-volume Gibbs measure）。

Result: 建立了磁化强度与具有相应有限体方差的高斯分布之间的最优 Berry–Esseen 界；并给出有限盒内磁化强度收敛到方差由无限体磁化率（infinite-volume susceptibility）决定的高斯分布的尖锐收敛速率，证明在维数 d ≥ 3 时周期边界条件（periodic boundary conditions）的收敛严格快于自由边界条件（free boundary conditions）。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了高低温区 Ising 模型磁化强度正态逼近的尖锐且依赖边界条件的理论刻画，表明边界条件的选取（周期型 vs. 自由型）在 d ≥ 3 时对收敛速率有本质影响。

Abstract: We consider the nearest-neighbour Ising model on $\mathbb{Z}^d$, and establish sharp results for the normal approximation of the magnetisation throughout the high-temperature regime. In particular, we establish optimal Berry--Esseen bounds between the magnetisation in an arbitrary finite region and a Gaussian with the corresponding finite-volume variance. These results hold for finite-volume Gibbs measures with arbitrary boundary conditions, as well as for the infinite-volume Gibbs measure. We also determine sharp rates of convergence of the magnetisation in a finite box to a Gaussian with variance given by the infinite-volume susceptibility. For this fixed Gaussian target, we show that, in dimensions $d\ge 3$, periodic boundary conditions yield strictly faster convergence than free boundary conditions. Our approach uses Stein's method to quantify the normal approximation error by analysing the semigroup of the continuous-time Ising heat-bath dynamics.

</details>


### [335] [A connection inequality for the random-cluster model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26419)
*Jianping Jiang,Sike Lang,Yuxuan Sun*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该文针对有限图上自由边界条件下的随机簇测度（random-cluster measure），证明了一条关联概率不等式，将 Kozma-Nitzan 不等式从 q=1 推广到 q≥1，并据此给出亚临界情形下单臂与点到面连接概率尖锐上界的简洁证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kozma-Nitzan 不等式原仅在 q=1（即渗流（percolation）情形）成立，作者希望将其推广至一般簇权重（cluster weight）q≥1 的随机簇模型（random-cluster model），以覆盖更广的依赖结构，并借此简化亚临界（subcritical）区域连接概率上界的推导。

Method: 在有限图 G=(V,E) 上，取边参数为 (p_e) 的随机簇测度 φ、簇权重 q≥1 及自由边界条件（free boundary condition），对任意 x∈V 与非空 A,B⊆V 建立 φ(x↔B) ≥ φ(x↔A)·min_{a∈A}φ(a↔B) 的不等式；证明思路沿用 Blanc-Renaudie 近期对该不等式的证明方法。

Result: 所证不等式对 q≥1 成立，且在 q=1 时退化为（略作推广的）Kozma-Nitzan 不等式。作为应用，文中给出亚临界（subcritical）区域单臂（one-arm）连接概率与点到面（point-to-plane）连接概率尖锐上界（sharp upper bounds）的一个简洁证明。

Conclusion: 该工作把一项经典关联概率不等式推广到随机簇模型的一般 q≥1 情形，表明其证明技术可迁移，并为亚临界连接概率的尖锐上界提供了更简明的推导路径。

Abstract: For a finite graph $G=(V,E)$, let $φ$ be the random-cluster measure with edge parameters $(p_e)_{e\in E}$, cluster weight $q\geq 1$ and free boundary condition. For each $x\in V$ and all nonempty $A,B\subseteq V$, we prove
  $φ(x\leftrightarrow B)\geq φ(x\leftrightarrow A)\min_{a\in A}φ(a\leftrightarrow B)$.
  For $q=1$, this is the (slightly generalized) Kozma-Nitzan inequality. Our extension to $q>1$ is based on a recent proof of this inequality by Blanc-Renaudie. As an application, we give a simple proof of sharp upper bounds for one-arm and point-to-plane connection probabilities in the subcritical regime.

</details>


### [336] [Symmetric Polynomials Method for the Multiple Loewner Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26716)
*Sergio Andraus,Nicole Hufnagel,Jacek Małecki,Vlad Margarint*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 论文引入对称多项式（symmetric polynomials）方法研究由随机粒子系统族驱动的Loewner链（Loewner chains），并以对称多项式随时间的演化行为刻画临界参数κ=4处的相变（phase transition）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有Loewner链（尤其由Dyson Brownian motion等多变量根系统过程驱动者）的相变分析缺乏统一工具，本文旨在提供对称多项式这一新分析视角。

Method: 将对称多项式技术用于研究由随机粒子系统族（含但不限于Dyson Brownian motion）驱动的Loewner链，并推广至若干确定性多重Loewner演化（multiple Loewner evolutions）的初值情形。

Result: 以一类对称多项式的时间演化行为给出κ=4处相变的新证明，并获得与相关模型有关的新闭式表达式（closed-form expressions）。

Conclusion: 该方法被视为首次在根系统（root-system）驱动的多重Loewner链背景下得到分析，作者据此概述了该技术在相关模型研究中的潜在未来应用。

Abstract: We introduce the technique of Symmetric Polynomials in the study of Loewner chains driven by a family of stochastic particle systems including but not limited to Dyson Brownian motion. As a first application of this technique we obtain the phase transitions at the critical parameter $κ=4$ in terms of the behavior of a class of symmetric polynomials evolving in time. We obtain new proofs of the phase transitions, we also obtain new closed-form expressions relevant for the models. In addition, we apply this method for some initial deterministic multiple Loewner evolutions. Finally, as this is, to our knowledge, the first paper analyzing this method in the context of multiple Loewner chains driven by root-system based multivariate processes, we also give an overview of potential future applications of this technique in the study of these models.

</details>


<div id='physics.optics'></div>

# physics.optics [[Back]](#toc)

### [337] [Boosting fluorescence and polarization with nanophotonics for single-molecule chiral sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24746)
*Ershad Mohammadi,T. V. Raziman,Alberto G. Curto*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文提出基于高折射率介质纳米光子学（high-index dielectric nanophotonics）的手性传感方案，通过同时增强荧光与偏振，实现约1000倍荧光强度提升与10倍偏振提升，并强调角向发射中的外禀偏振效应对对映体区分不可或缺。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 圆二色性（circular dichroism）依赖手性分子对圆偏振光的差分吸收，但低浓度下吸收趋于消失导致灵敏度受限；荧光方法虽有望达到单分子极限，却受弱荧光信号与低偏振对比度（polarization contrast）制约。

Method: 利用高折射率介质纳米光子学同时增强荧光与偏振；提出基于互易性（reciprocity）的解析框架，描述手性偶极发射体与纳米天线（nanoantenna）的耦合，揭示圆偏振荧光增强的不同贡献与权衡关系，并据此设计无手性介质纳米结构（achiral dielectric nanostructures）。

Result: 所设计的无手性介质纳米结构实现荧光强度约1000倍增强与偏振约10倍提升；分析表明，在纳米光子结构角向发射图案（angular emission patterns）中计入外禀偏振效应（extrinsic polarization effects）对可靠的对映体区分（enantiomer discrimination）是必需的。

Conclusion: 该方案为单分子手性传感（single-molecule chiral sensing）提供了现实可行的路径。

Abstract: Detecting chirality is essential in chemistry and biomedicine. Circular dichroism identifies chiral molecules through their differential absorption of circularly polarized light. However, its sensitivity is limited by vanishing absorption at low concentrations. Fluorescence-based methods have already enabled other optical techniques to reach the single-molecule limit and could, in principle, also extend chiral detection to reach this sensitivity regime. They are marred, however, by the combination of weak fluorescence signals and low polarization contrast. To overcome these limitations, we leverage high-index dielectric nanophotonics to simultaneously enhance both fluorescence and polarization for chiral sensing. We propose a reciprocity-based analytical framework for a chiral dipolar emitter coupled to a nanoantenna and reveal the different contributions and trade-offs governing the enhancement of circularly polarized fluorescence. Guided by this insight, we tailor achiral dielectric nanostructures to reach a 1000-fold enhancement of fluorescence intensity together with a 10-fold increase in polarization. Crucially, we show that accounting for extrinsic polarization effects in the angular emission patterns of nanophotonic structures is indispensable for reliable enantiomer discrimination. Our approach opens a realistic path for single-molecule chiral sensing.

</details>


### [338] [Dynamic Hotspot Migration and Self-Induced Resonances in Ultrafast Laser-Irradiated Silicon Thin Films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.25079)
*George D. Tsibidis,Yi Wang,Emmanuel Stratakis*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 硅薄膜中由载流子诱导折射率调制（carrier-induced refractive index modulation）与自诱导法布里-珀罗共振（self-induced Fabry-Pérot resonances）共同决定飞秒激光辐照下能量的空间分布，并呈现非线性热点迁移与等离子体屏蔽转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在厘清飞秒激光在硅薄膜内部能量空间分布的形成机制，为激光纳米制造中的深度分辨改性（depth-resolved modification）提供物理依据。

Method: 针对绝缘体上硅（silicon-on-insulator, SOI）结构，分析100、230、350 nm三种厚度构型，划分界面局域（interface-localized）、双节点（binodal）与多节点（multi-nodal）三种特征激发区域，并进行数值模拟。

Result: 发现非线性热点迁移效应：脉冲期间折射率瞬态降低使内部驻波被拉伸，强度波腹向薄膜体区移动；高能量密度下等离子体诱导消光抑制内部反射，导致由共振介导吸收向等离子体屏蔽（plasma shielding）转变。

Conclusion: 研究确立了深度分辨激光改性的物理基础，具备表面图案化与次表面深度分辨改性并行的双重能力，可直接应用于激光辅助界面分离与三维集成光子制造。

Abstract: The spatial distribution of energy in silicon thin films under femtosecond laser irradiation is demonstrated to be influenced by the interaction between carrier-induced refractive index modulation and self-induced Fabry-Pérot resonances. We resolve three characteristic excitation regions such as interface-localized, binodal and multi-nodal regimes considering the analysis of silicon-on-insulator (SOI) configurations across thicknesses of 100, 230, and 350 nm. We report a nonlinear hotspot migration effect where the transient reduction of the refractive index stretches the internal standing wave, translating intensity antinodes toward the film volume during the pulse. Numerical results demonstrate that at higher fluences, plasma-induced extinction suppresses internal reflections, triggering a transition from resonance-mediated absorption to plasma shielding. These findings establish a physical basis for depth-resolved modification in laser nanomanufacturing, providing a dual-capacity for simultaneous surface patterning and depth-resolved subsurface modification with direct applications in laser-assisted interfacial separation and 3D integrated photonic fabrication.

</details>


### [339] [Optical vortex bullets with large angular momenta](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26289)
*José R. Salgueiro,Carlos F. Sánchez,Boris A. Malomed,Ángel Paredes,Humberto Michinel*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该文数值研究携带高阶轨道角动量的三维时空光涡旋孤子（spatiotemporal optical vortex solitons，即环形"光弹"），分析其存在性、结构与稳定性，指出工程化折射率分布可显著扩大其稳定区域，但方位角不稳定性最终限制其鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究在反常群速度色散（anomalous group-velocity dispersion）的Kerr介质中，能否借助横向局域的高斯型线性折射率分布承载高阶拓扑荷的三维时空涡旋光弹，并厘清其稳定性条件，为支持大轨道角动量光弹提供依据。

Method: 以三维非线性薛定谔方程（three-dimensional nonlinear Schrödinger equation）的数值解构造拓扑荷（winding number）1≤ℓ≤4的定常涡旋光弹，系统考察其存在性与结构随传播常数、导引分布深度与宽度及ℓ的变化，并以Vakhitov-Kolokolov（VK）判据与直接模拟评估稳定性，类比原子玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚中的物质波孤子。

Result: ℓ增大需要更强或更宽的波导势才能维持满足VK稳定性判据的局域态；满足与不满足VK条件的支系由退化的Townes支分隔；导引分布虽显著抑制坍缩并延长传播距离，但鲁棒性最终受方位角不稳定性（azimuthal instability）限制，且该不稳定性随ℓ增强。

Conclusion: 工程化折射率景观（engineered refractive-index landscapes）可显著扩大时空涡旋光弹的稳定域，并为承载大轨道角动量的光弹设计提供指导原则。

Abstract: We investigate three-dimensional spatiotemporal optical vortex solitons (light bullets with a toroidal shape) carrying higher-order orbital angular momentum in Kerr media with anomalous group-velocity dispersion and a transversely localized Gaussian profile of the linear refractive index. Stationary vortex bullets with topological charges (winding numbers) $1\leq \ell \leq 4$ are obtained as numerical solutions of the three-dimensional nonlinear Schrödinger equation, and their existence, structure and stability are systematically analyzed. In terms of atomic Bose-Einstein condensates, these solutions represent matter-wave solitons with embedded vorticity. We examine the dependence of the solution families on the propagation constant, the depth and width of the guiding profile, and $\ell $. It is found that the increase of $\ell $ requires one to use a stronger or wider waveguiding potential to sustain localized states satisfying the Vakhitov-Kolokolov (VK) stability criterion. VK-complying and non-complying branches of vortex-soliton families are separated by a degenerate Townes branch. Direct simulations reveal that, although the guiding profile substantially suppresses the collapse and extends the propagation distance of vortex bullets, their robustness is ultimately limited by azimuthal instabilities, whose growth is enhanced for values of $\ell $. These results demonstrate that engineered refractive-index landscapes significantly enlarge the stability domain of spatiotemporal vortex bullets and provide guidelines for supporting optical bullets carrying large orbital angular momenta.

</details>
